# The CLU Research Ledger
### From intuition to theory to experiment to revision — seven idea-threads, honestly scored

> **Who this is for.** You are a skeptical outside reader — an ML researcher evaluating whether the CLU program is a real contribution or physics cosplay. This document is the **companion to the HEP → CLU Primers** (`HEP_primers.md`): the primer tells you *what the physics means inside a learning system*; this ledger tells you, per idea, **what we believed, what we could prove, what we predicted, what we ran, what we measured, how the idea changed, and what we accidentally learned.** Read the primer for the mechanism; read this for the epistemics.
>
> **How to read.** Seven chapters, one per idea-thread, each with the same seven-part rubric: **Philosophy** (the intuition, attributed) → **Theory** (the formal statement, F5 proposition numbers) → **Expected** (what we predicted *before* running, where the record shows it) → **Experiment** (what actually ran) → **Measured vs expected** (numbers side by side) → **Verdict & revision** (validated / reframed / refuted / open, and how the idea moved) → **Emergent discoveries** (what we stumbled on and what it means for the math *and* the philosophy). Then a scorecard, positioning consequences, an ICLR gap analysis, and a plain list of shortcomings.
>
> **Provenance discipline.** Ideas are attributed to their origin: **Head** (Pratik-side raw research intuition), **Hub** (the research-lead agent's formalization), or a **named spoke agent** (a measured discovery). The record is `brainstorm_log.md` (the idea journal) and `handover_context.md §10` (the running log). Every number cites its output report + section. Status tags come in two layers: **program-level** — `[validated]` `[validated-with-scope]` `[reframed]` `[refuted]` `[open]` — and **claim-level** (imported from F5) — `[proven]` (derived) `[verified]` (also numerically confirmed) `[evidenced]` (standard-argument) `[conjectured]` `[design hypothesis]`.
>
> **⟲ Running-document protocol (Head directive, 2026-07-07).** This ledger is updated **after every wave** with a dated addendum section at the bottom recording, per chapter: what new evidence landed, which verdicts moved, and which numbers superseded which. Original chapters are never rewritten — the addenda form a natural **diff record of the experimental program**, essential for debugging larger ICLR-scale runs later ("what did we believe when, and on what evidence"). Current state = chapters (as of wave-3) + addenda below.
>
> **The one-paragraph program context.** The CLU (Causal Learning Unit, introduced as CHLU in Jawahar & Pierini 2026) is a recurrent primitive whose forward pass is *physics run forward*: latent state $z=(q,p)$ evolves under a learned Hamiltonian $H=T(p)+V_\theta(q)$ via a symplectic (leapfrog) integrator, with physical levers — inertial mass $M$, friction $\gamma$, temperature $T$, causal speed $c$ — as the interface. Three program principles frame everything: **P1** "physics is a design library, not ground truth" (every borrowed structure must earn an ML-measurable benefit); **P2** "the primitive is its lever interface, instantiations are swappable"; **P3** "fix the geometry, learn the content." All three are the Head's; the ML-benefit guard-rail on P1 is the Hub's.

# Chapter 1 — The mode-budget / Goldstone-memory idea (Thread 2 → V2)

### 1. Philosophy
The seed is the **Head's** (2026-07-04, `brainstorm_log.md` Thread 2): *"There are 3 Goldstone bosons — why not use all of them to propagate information? If one mode collapses, the others continue."* The instinct: physics guarantees certain directions are **massless** (flat, restoring-force-free), and a value written along a massless direction is never pulled back — that is memory that cannot decay. The Head explicitly invoked Welling et al.'s Goldstone-propagation philosophy but under **P1**: take the math, drop the obligation to physical fidelity.

The **Hub** generalized the "3" (2026-07-04): the count is not magic, it is group arithmetic — $\#\text{channels}=\dim\mathcal G-\dim\mathcal H=\dim(\mathcal G/\mathcal H)$ (HEP primer §4.4). So multiplicity becomes a **design knob**: choose the symmetry $\mathcal G$ of $V_\theta$ and the unbroken $\mathcal H$ to allocate exactly the number of protected parallel channels the task needs — *channel capacity by symmetry engineering*. The Hub also proposed the two parameterization routes (HEFT/coset vs SMEFT/equivariant, primer §4.6–4.7) and the "**EFT of memory**" framing (primer §4.8) as the collaboration surface with a Manchester HEFT theorist. This is V2's core, and the spine of the whole program.

### 2. Theory
The formal backbone is F5 §3–§4 (primer Part III–IV). The single load-bearing object is one mode under the damped Verlet map, solved exactly (primer §3.1):

- **Flat mode $\mu=0$ (Goldstone latch), F5 §3.2 / primer §3.2 [proven, verified]:** with friction $\gamma$, a flat direction's displacement freezes at
$$q_\infty=q_0+\frac{\varepsilon\,p_0}{M\gamma},\qquad p_n=(1-\gamma)^n p_0\to 0.$$
The Hub's slogan "dissipation kills Goldstone *momentum* but cannot erase Goldstone *displacement*" is upgraded by the theorist to something stronger: **friction converts a marginal integrator into a deadbeat latch** with *infinite* half-life. Memory lives in the coset coordinate; the **Noether charge $Q$ is its write current**, spent exactly as $(1-\gamma)^nQ_0$ (F5 §4.1, primer §4.2–4.3).
- **Massive/pseudo-Goldstone mode, F5 §3.3 / primer §3.3, §4.5 [proven, verified]:** a weakly-broken flat direction gets spectral mass $\mu^2\propto\delta$ (GMOR pattern), sitting overdamped, with retention
$$n_{1/2}\approx\frac{2\gamma\ln 2}{(2-\gamma)(\varepsilon\mu)^2}\;\propto\;\mu^{-2}\;\propto\;\delta^{-1},$$
**exponent exactly $-2$ in spectral mass**, saturating at a mass-independent floor $2\ln2/\gamma$ once $\varepsilon\mu>h^*\approx\gamma/2$.
- **Kinetic-isotropy constraint, F5 §4.1 / primer §4.2 [proven, verified]:** the Noether charge is conserved only if inertial masses inside a channel are equal ($M_1=M_2$); an SO(2) potential with per-coordinate learned mass **silently breaks its own symmetry** through the kinetic term (Schur's lemma).
- **Discretization protection, F5 Prop-8 / primer §1.7 [proven]:** discretization cannot generate a mass for an exactly-protected Goldstone direction — protected memory is step-size-robust; curvature memory is not.

One-equation essence: **$\mu=0\Rightarrow$ latch; $\mu^2\propto\delta\Rightarrow$ lifetime $\propto1/\delta$ with a floor.**

### 3. Expected (stated before the fact)
The falsifiables were on the record before the runs (brainstorm Thread 2, item 2; F5 v1.0 §3.3): (i) retention half-life per channel **$\propto1/\mu^2$**; (ii) a **floor** at $2\ln2/\gamma$ past crossover (the distinctive signature a naive continuum theory misses — F5); (iii) a designed flat direction should **latch** (freeze, infinite half-life); (iv) the Noether charge should decay **exactly** $(1-\gamma)^n$; (v) *falsifiable, sign uncertain:* does learned $M$ **isotropize** on symmetric data (self-repairing the §4.2 trap), or drift?

### 4. Experiment
Two builds and one full battery. **`v2-so2-build`** (engineer): SO(2)-invariant coercive potential, `TiltedPotential` GMOR wrapper (probe any checkpoint without retraining), `tie_channel_mass` kinetic isotropy, `chlu exp-d`, full measurement harness. **`v2-full-runs`** (analyst): the paper battery — GMOR $\delta$-sweep (5 seeds ×14 $\delta$), $\gamma$-sweep through $\gamma^*$, emergent-symmetry (mlp+tied) vs designed (so2+tied) vs broken-isotropy (so2+untied) variants, isotropization test, EP signatures. Laptop-CPU, seeds {42–46}, dim 4, `newtonian_learned`, dt 0.05, trained f32 then probed at f64. **Deviation, diagnosed:** battery trained at `train_epochs=150` not the 1000 default, forced by Finding 0 (see §7).

### 5. Measured vs expected
| prediction | measured | source |
|---|---|---|
| GMOR $\mu^2\propto\delta$ | $\mu^2_{\rm meas}/{\rm pred}=1.000000\pm5\times10^{-12}$ over **4.5 decades** | `v2-full-runs.md §3(a)` |
| retention $\propto1/\mu^2$ (exponent $-2$) | ratios 3.993, 3.998 vs 4.0; retention slope **$-0.985$** (pred $-1$ in $\delta$) | `v2-full-runs.md §3(b)`; F5 check (b) |
| mass-independent floor | saturation at exact floor **27.03 steps** | `v2-full-runs.md §3`; primer §3.5 |
| latch / frozen coset | designed flat μ² gap **5 orders**; coset freeze **≤1.2×10⁻¹⁵ rad**; latch transport ≤1% over 2 decades of $\gamma$ | `v2-full-runs.md`; handover §10 (w3) |
| Noether $(1-\gamma)^n$ | exact decay at f32 eps ($9.3\times10^{-16}$ in the F5 toy) | `v2-so2-build.md`; F5 check (g) |
| isotropization (v) | **NO** — kinetic breaking invisible to Hessian/latch, linear in $\lVert[M,X]\rVert$ in the charge law; **3rd corroboration** | `v2-full-runs.md`; handover §10 |
| designed vs emergent flat protection | gap **13–14 orders**; emergent self-breaking $\delta\approx0.01$–$0.06$, F5-priced at **+12–15%** energy | `v2-full-runs.md` |

Agreement quality is exceptional where the theory is exact (GMOR, latch, Noether — machine precision) and quantitatively strong where it is asymptotic (retention ratios within 0.2% of 4.0). The one *predicted-then-refuted* item is isotropization: the theory said "either $M$ self-repairs or it drifts" and the answer is **it does neither visibly — the breaking is simply invisible to the standard diagnostics** and shows up only in the charge law.

### 6. Verdict & revision — **[validated-with-scope]**
V2's mechanism is **validated**: designed flat directions latch, GMOR holds exactly, retention obeys the $-1/\delta$ law with its floor. Two revisions sharpened the idea:
- The Head's "use all three Goldstones / one collapses, others continue" **reframed** into the exact F5 statement: not "graceful failover" but *"friction is the write-settle mechanism; protected registers hold forever, imperfectly-protected ones leak predictably as $1/\delta$ with a floor."* The half-life conjecture went from vibe → **exact-with-scope** (only overdamped; saturates underdamped).
- **External positioning inverted** (scout `scout-goldstone-positioning.md`, `mo-deep-read.md`). Welling et al. are contrast-clean (their Goldstone is *time-independent/kinematic*; no Hamiltonian/energy/dissipation/EFT — 4/5 differentiators open). The real neighbor is **Mo 2026 (arXiv:2605.03338)**: a $\ge\dim(\mathcal G/\mathcal H)$ zero-Lyapunov theorem + a pseudo-gap→lifetime experiment. The Mo audit's verdict: *"Mo = kinematics of protection; V2 = constitutive theory of degradation."* His single-exponential law is **the overdamped face of our budget table** — measured directly: Mo's own protocol run on our mode gives ratio **1.001–1.012 overdamped** (his median 1.013), rising to **2.20±0.16 at the exceptional point**, collapsing to **0.309 deep underdamped** (`v2-full-runs.md`; F5 check (k)). This became V2's headline figure and forced a related-work reframe: lead with **(iv) EFT-of-memory + (i) energy-space**; reframe (iii) to *constructive allocation + Hamiltonian curvature mass-law*; **never headline the bare design-knob** "we choose $\mathcal G/\mathcal H$" (Mo's learned cell + Di Bernardo 2511.04802 own the *construction* — we own the **pricing**: "allocation with a price list", `di-bernardo-skim.md`).

### 7. Emergent discoveries
- **Sleep-phase vacuum erosion (`v2-full-runs.md §2`, Finding 0) — a result in its own right.** At the default 1000 epochs, the designed SO(2) vacuum is **destroyed 8/8**: the ring at $R=1$ inverts to a local *maximum* between epochs 300–600 ($r^*$: 0.967 → 0.873 → $\sim0$; ring depth +0.060 → +0.022 → −0.016 → −0.047 at 30/60/120/200 sleeps). **Wake-only is intact and data-pinned** (depth +0.074, deepest of all). The fix-pack-2 `lyapunov_penalty="max"` default is **exonerated** (max vs legacy differ at the 4th decimal). *Mechanism:* PCD negatives thermalize into the low-$V$ ring, CD keeps raising $V$ there, and nothing anchors $V$'s *value* at data (wake is MSE-on-trajectories only). **Math implication:** contrastive divergence on a symmetric landscape is not vacuum-preserving; the designed geometry is a transient, not a fixed point of the training dynamics. **Philosophy implication:** P3's "fix geometry, learn content" has a failure mode — *the training objective can erode the very geometry you fixed*; a designed symmetry needs an anchoring term or a wake-dominant schedule to survive. This is the training-dynamics twin of Chapter 7's "objectives must not legislate friction."
- **Metric bifurcation (F5 Cor-14, primer §3.5, `mo-deep-read.md`) — mandatory measurement discipline.** Envelope half-life $n_{1/2}$ and first-crossing time $n_\times$ agree overdamped but **bifurcate underdamped** (up to 3.2×, `v2-full-runs.md`): phase transport vs retention are different physics. Any lifetime claim must state which metric it uses — this is why single-exponential predictors (Mo's) fail past the crossover.
- **Exceptional-point spectroscopy (F5 Cor-15):** the crossover is a defective/Jordan point; EP onset $\varphi\propto\sqrt{h-h^*}$ measured at slope **0.5165** (pred 0.5). A free, sharp diagnostic that the mode structure is what the theory says.

> **⟲ w24 pointer (2026-07-24, doc-curator) — nothing here is deleted or superseded; a *use* of it moved.** The **write modes** this chapter's physics defines — (a) permanent latch (designed coset, `μ²≡0` exactly), (b) half-life write via a controlled spurion (`μ²(δ)` by GMOR), (c) fresh item at a packing-legal location — were filed under Ch. 1 by the w19 addendum. **They are now canonically homed in Ch. 8 §2 as the LIFETIMES dial**, where they carry **Prop C-N** (permanence is a *designed* flat coset, never a training outcome — R-4 ⇒ any finite-λ soft regularizer gives `μ²(λ)>0`; the designed-vs-emergent flatness gap is 12 orders) and their measured instance (`controller-mvp`: permanent + leaky wells coexisting in one store, decay exactly `exp(−0.35t)`). **Chapter 1 remains the home of the physics; Chapter 8 is the home of the dial.** → migrated to Ch. 8, w24.

# Chapter 2 — The mass-as-allocator idea (Thread 5)

### 1. Philosophy
The **Head's** raw intuition (2026-07-04, embedded in Thread 3's answer, then spun out as Thread 5): *"leverage the learned mass matrix to boost different regions differently — different particles with different masses within the CHLU."* The Head flagged it as half-formulated.

The **Hub** formalized it into the program's would-be unifier: read the $d$-dimensional latent not as one particle but as a **gas of $d$ particles**, each $(q_i,p_i)$ with its own learned $M_i$, and then **one object $M$ runs all three verticals** — it prices memory (V2: $\mu^2=M^{-1}\mathrm{Hess}\,V$), directs search (V1: mass = boost-stiffness = semantic inertia), and sets the timescale hierarchy (V3: shells *by mass, not size*). This was proposed as the F5 centerpiece and the ICLR spine: *"the mass matrix is the budget allocator of the primitive."*

### 2. Theory
F5 Def-2 / Def-3 (primer §2.1, §2.3) — and here the theory did something valuable: **it deflated the philosophy honestly.**
- **Def-2 [proven]:** there are *two* masses running in **opposite directions** — inertial $M$ (kinetic-term coefficient; large = slow, IR, kept) and spectral $\mu=\sqrt{k/M}$ (mode frequency = forgetting rate; large = fast, UV, integrated out). "Mass" unqualified is now **forbidden** in program docs. This corrected the Hub's own "integrate out heavy modes" to "adiabatically eliminate the *fast* (large-$\mu$) sector."
- **The honest deflation [proven, F5 §5 / primer §2.3]:** a *constant* $M$ is a symplectic coordinate convention — absorbable at linear order. Its **irreducible** content is exactly five things: (1) anisotropic causal cap $v_i^{\max}=c/\sqrt{M_i}$ (Prop-1); (2) relativistic regime assignment $p_i^*=m_0c\sqrt{M_i}$ (Prop-1); (3) relative pricing of a *shared* landscape ($\mu_i^2=K_{ii}/M_i$); (4) mass-weighted boost response (Def-6); (5) a learnability prior (Hyp-3, `[design hypothesis]`).

One-equation essence: **$\mu_k^2=\mathrm{eig}\!\big(M_{\rm eff}^{-1/2}KM_{\rm eff}^{-1/2}\big)$ — $M$ is half the object; you must measure it *with* the curvature $K$, never alone.**

### 3. Expected
Three falsifiables on the record (brainstorm Thread 5): (i) trained CLUs develop **non-trivial mass spectra correlated with feature timescales**; (ii) $S^{(M)}$-boosts search light modes preferentially (per-mode displacement $\propto M_i^{-1}$); (iii) mass-hierarchical lattices beat mass-uniform at matched parameters.

### 4. Experiment
- **`mass-spectrum-peek`** (analyst): read $M$ off 6 existing checkpoints (finalA exp_a/b, 4× mnist*), with the identity-mode exp_a as a bitwise pipeline control.
- **`v1-l0-gate`** (engineer): 23 fresh generative-PCD models, measured log-mass spread and the boost-displacement-vs-$M$ correlation.
- **`v3-lattice-build`** (engineer): banded-vs-uniform training smoke (falsifiable iii).
- **`v2-full-runs`** (analyst): a third independent look at whether learned $M$ isotropizes/differentiates.

### 5. Measured vs expected
| falsifiable | measured | source |
|---|---|---|
| (i) mass spectrum develops | signal **real, universal, data-aligned** but **weak**: every relativistic checkpoint moved 784/784 components (KS $p\approx0$); global lightening; ink-light/border-heavy pattern stable across variants (Pearson **0.40–0.93**; Spearman vs pixel-variance **−0.44…−0.72**). **BUT** $\sigma_{\rm struct}=$**2–23%** of init σ; max/min ≤1.6 | `mass-spectrum-peek.md §7,§tables` |
| (i) again, fresh models | log-std ≈ **0.08** across 23 PCD models — near-uniform | `v1-l0-gate.md`; handover §10 (w2) |
| (ii) boost searches light modes | **untestable** — with near-uniform $M$, Spearman$(\log|\Delta q_i|,\log 1/M_i)=$**0.03** ($n=4608$); operator response $\partial q'/\partial\zeta=p/M$ unit-verified to $\le10^{-10}$ | `v1-l0-gate.md`; F5 Def-3 |
| (iii) banded > uniform | banded wake loss **0.331** vs uniform **1.714**; eval MSE **1.362 vs 2.516** (300 ep, **1 seed**) | `v3-lattice-build.md §5` |
| control (exp_a identity) | bit-for-bit at init (0/2 moved) — validates pipeline | `mass-spectrum-peek.md §26` |

### 6. Verdict & revision — **[reframed → doctrine]**
The strategic ambition ("$M$ self-organizes into a hierarchy that unifies the program") is **refuted as an emergent phenomenon** and **reframed as a design doctrine**. Three independent data points (MNIST checkpoints, 23 PCD models, V2 trained models) agree: **a mass hierarchy does not emerge from current training at this scale.** F5 Hyp-3 now reads (v1.1): *"hierarchy is designed-in (banded $M$ — V3) or induced (mass-aware objectives, multi-timescale data) — not awaited."* The strategy "post-hoc analyze what $M$ learns" is declared **exhausted**.

What *survives* and is genuinely load-bearing: Def-2 (the nomenclature fix, now enforced program-wide), the five irreducible roles (which are what V1's boost and V2's timescales actually stand on), and falsifiable (iii)'s first positive evidence (banded > uniform, single seed → needs a seed sweep). The idea did not die; it moved from "the primitive self-organizes" to "**the primitive gives you a budget dial you must set** — and setting it well (banding) is a learnability prior, not extra capacity" (`v3-lattice-build.md §5`).

### 7. Emergent discoveries
- **The "mass runs everything" deflation is itself the finding.** The theorist's proof that constant $M$ is absorbable at linear order is what kept the program honest: it prevented an ICLR narrative built on a gauge artifact. **Philosophy implication:** P1's guard-rail (every borrowed structure earns an ML-measurable benefit) bit its own centerpiece — and the program is stronger for it.
- **Mass narrowness interlocks with the sampler thread (Chapter 6).** The FDT violation is *practically small today precisely because the mass spectrum is narrow* (`generative-studies.md §60`). Two threads that looked independent are one: the sampler bug becomes load-bearing exactly when V3 banding lands. This is a genuine cross-thread coupling nobody designed.
- **Architecture can imprint spurious mass structure:** the conv potential's checkerboard artifact on mnistFF's mass map (`mass-spectrum-peek.md`) — a caution that "learned $M$" measurements can read out the *architecture*, not the *data*.

> **⟲ w24 pointer (2026-07-24, doc-curator) — nothing deleted; the memory-side reading moved.** The w19–w23 addenda filed the **fiber results** under Ch. 2: **Prop F1** (*mass SELECTS, never STORES* — read depends on `(M,V,p₀)` only through `(M⁻¹∇V, M⁻¹p₀)`, verified 6.2e-16; **four independent nulls**, two of them pre-registered), the corrected composition rule **`B_total = K_spatial · B_fiber`** (slots × bits, never items × items), and the **parameter ceiling `B_total ≤ P·b_θ`**. **These are now canonically homed in Ch. 8 §2** as the theory of what an address can and cannot carry. **Chapter 2 keeps the allocator thread and its verdict** (`[reframed → doctrine]`) — Prop F1 is the *fifth* independent confirmation of this chapter's conclusion, arriving from the memory side rather than the training side. → migrated to Ch. 8, w24.

# Chapter 3 — The trash-region / trinity idea (Thread 1)

### 1. Philosophy
The **Head's** raw idea (2026-07-04, Thread 1): the three mode classes (flat / massive / expanding) map onto a **trinity** — creation / destruction / sustenance (Hindu-trinity framing for talks). Make **forgetting a first-class citizen**: add a **"trash region"** — a virtual black hole, Unruh-flavored, unconstrained by real physics (P1) — where information can be *actively and locally* destroyed, instead of the global $(1-\gamma)p$ friction that "forgets everything a little."

The **Hub** turned metaphor into mechanism: a **localized friction field $\gamma_\phi(q)$** — a sigmoidal event horizon around a learned center — giving *selective* forgetting; framed rigorously as a **classical Lindblad structure** (symplectic bulk + designed local non-unitary channels). Then the sharper move (round 2): make $\gamma_\phi$ **contrastively learned by the same wake–sleep signal** — wake pushes $\gamma\downarrow$ at data (protection), sleep pushes $\gamma\uparrow$ at persistent hallucinations (garbage attracts friction). One CD signal, two fields: $V_\theta$ learns *what things look like*, $\gamma_\phi$ learns *what deserves to die*. Fully P3-compliant.

### 2. Theory
F5 Prop-11 / Def-5 (primer §7.2) — **metaphor upgraded to theorem [proven, verified]:**
$$\det D\Phi=\big(1-\gamma_\phi(q_{n+1})\big)^d\quad\text{exactly, for any steepness of }\gamma_\phi.$$
Phase-space volume (information) is destroyed **exactly and only where $\gamma_\phi>0$**; outside the horizon the step is exactly conservative. What's *lost* relative to constant $\gamma$: conformality and the singular-value pairing wherever $\nabla\gamma_\phi\ne0$ (volume accounting survives perfectly, pairing doesn't). Then the Mo-audit corollary **Cor-13 (primer §3.5) [proven, verified]** hands the trash region a *derived optimal strength*: retention is minimized at critical damping, so **$\gamma_\phi(q)\approx2\varepsilon\mu(q)$** is the fastest-forgetting friction — the "destruction" vertex of the trinity now has an *optimum*, not just a knob. One-equation essence: **forgetting is a computable, learnable scalar field with a curvature-matched optimal value.**

### 3. Expected
Falsifiable on the record (brainstorm Thread 1, item 36): a signal-retention vs noise-rejection **Pareto curve** comparing (i) global $\gamma$, (ii) energy governor, (iii) horizon sink; **prediction: (iii) Pareto-dominates.** Plus the C1 spec: learned holes should sit near $2\varepsilon\mu$.

### 4. Experiment
**`gamma-field-build`** (engineer): `FrictionField` module (K learnable holes, noisy-OR combination, default `"none"` bit-compatible), contrastive wake/sleep training, **Experiment S1** (`chlu exp-s1`), 4 arms {global-γ sweep / governor / learned K∈{1,4} / oracle fixed hole}, retention (curve-coverage + KE survival) vs rejection (position-return + excess-energy dissipation of 16 paired injections), 3 seeds.

### 5. Measured vs expected
| arm | retention | rejection | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| (i) γ=0 ceiling | 0.713 | 0.488 | `gamma-field-build.md §tables` |
| (ii) governor | 0.713 | 0.669 | " |
| (iii) learned K=4 | 0.506 | **0.861** | " |
| (iv) **oracle hole** | **0.712** | **0.844** | " |

- **Mechanism validated via the oracle [validated at pilot scale]:** the position-gated horizon **Pareto-dominates the entire global-γ curve AND the governor** — retention at the conservative ceiling (0.712) with rejection 0.844 vs governor's 0.669. *"A horizon forgets garbage completely and memories not at all"* is a measured fact.
- **Prediction (iii)≻(i) HOLDS for K=4**; (iii)≻(ii) does **not** yet — the governor is a strong baseline only the oracle beats. Locus discovery 2/6 (gradient locality) ⇒ adaptive-K spawning queued.
- **C1 corroborated with a twist:** learned holes sit **2.6–13.2×** *above* $2\varepsilon\mu$ (over-damped — training prioritizes annihilation strength over forgetting-speed optimality); but the *pre-fix* MSE-legislated path equilibrated at **≈1.3× $2\varepsilon\mu$** — near critical damping, exactly as C1 predicts.

### 6. Verdict & revision — **[validated-with-scope]** (pilot, 3 seeds)
Thread 1 is the cleanest philosophy→theorem→measurement arc in the program: metaphor (Head) → Lindblad framing + contrastive $\gamma_\phi$ (Hub) → theorem $\det J=(1-\gamma_\phi)^d$ (theorist) → oracle Pareto-dominance (engineer). The idea changed in one important way: **F5 v1.1 Cor-13 added the honest scope clause** — *friction latches flat-direction content; it cannot erase it.* A friction-only trash region **cannot delete coset-coordinate (Goldstone) content** — that needs local curvature/tilt or noise (Hawking re-emission, study S2). The trinity's philosophical punchline sharpened: **"Shiva needs Brahma"** — destruction by friction alone is incomplete; erasing sustained content requires the creative/noise vertex.

### 7. Emergent discoveries
- **Design principle: "objectives must not legislate friction" (`gamma-field-build.md`).** Three gradient-hygiene lessons — stop-grad the field in wake+sleep rollouts, energy-gate the hallucination term, use a two-timescale learning rate — because letting the *accuracy* objective set friction converges it to critical damping (fast settling = fast forgetting), which is *wrong* for a protection field. **Math implication:** the friction field's loss geometry is adversarial to the MSE loss's; they must be decoupled. **Philosophy implication:** forgetting is not a byproduct of accuracy — it is a separate faculty with its own objective, which is why the trinity has three vertices, not two.
- **Latch-vs-erase distinction promoted to doctrine (Cor-13 scope clause):** the primer's table (§1.8) now distinguishes three columns of forgetting — conservative ($\gamma=0$), uniform contraction ($\gamma>0$), and local horizon ($\gamma_\phi(q)$) — and only the last destroys *locally* while leaving symmetry-protected registers exactly intact. Selective forgetting and dissipation-proof memory are **componentwise compatible**, which is the exact resolution of paper limitation §D.1.

> **⟲ w24 pointer (2026-07-24, doc-curator) — nothing deleted; the *deletion* verb moved, the *protection* verdict stayed.** w19 recorded that this chapter's own vault theorem **blocks friction as a deletion mechanism** (a friction hole is a **107.77 ± 4.78× memory vault** and erases nothing at T=0, drift ≤1.75e-12 over 200k steps), re-specifying deletion as **demote → overwrite → heat → remove, friction-as-eraser FORBIDDEN**. **The realized forgetting verb now lives in Ch. 8 §5.2 as the LIFETIMES dial** — leaky wells with per-item `A·exp(−λt)`, self-eviction below the depth floor, permanent wells untouched, all of it an **active edit by the controller**, which is exactly the "overwrite, never friction" prescription honoured. **Ch. 3's `[validated-with-scope]` protection verdict is unchanged and stays here.** → migrated to Ch. 8, w24.

# Chapter 4 — The boost / cascade / test-time-compute idea (Thread 3 → V1)

### 1. Philosophy
The **Head's** original design (Thread 3): the Lorentz boost was always for the *stacked* CLU — query the smallest shell; if the answer isn't good enough, **first boost within the shell** (semantically-clustered re-search in a transformed frame); only if that fails, **shell-jump** to a bigger layer. An adaptive-compute retrieval cascade with a physical confidence signal.

The **Hub** made it concrete: repositioned V1 from "attention" (scoop-exposed to LorentzNet) to **energy-gated escalation** (unowned); identified the confidence signal as **residual energy** ($H$ above the local floor); recognized a boost is a **symplectic squeeze in $Sp(2d)$** — "the retry that can't hurt you"; and (with the Head) made **calibration a training-time learned objective** (a per-instance margin $\tau$), since "good enough" is instance-specific. The mechanism ladder L0→L1→L2 (climb only as needed) was the Hub's plan.

### 2. Theory
F5 Prop-12 / Def-6 / Def-7 (primer §6):
- **Squeeze symplecticity [proven, verified]:** $S_\zeta^\top\Omega S_\zeta=\Omega$, $\det S=1$ (dev $\le2.2\times10^{-16}$).
- **Retry certificate [proven]:** (C1 structure) every retry stays in the conformally-symplectic class; (C2 cost) $H(S_\zeta z)\le e^{2|\zeta|}H(z)$ — bounded rapidity ⇒ bounded energy injection ⇒ governor re-absorbs. *The certificate says the retry is safe, not that it helps.*
- **The mass-weighted correction [proven, verified]:** the Head's "one global boost is automatically region-differential" is **false for raw squeezes** ($\partial q'/\partial\zeta=p$, mass-blind) and **true only** for $S^{(M)}=\mathcal N^{-1}S\mathcal N$ ($\partial q'/\partial\zeta=p/M$). Engineer must implement $S^{(M)}$.

One-equation essence: **retries are certified-safe ($e^{2|\zeta|}$ bounded) but their usefulness is an empirical question; the gate signal $\tau$ must be learned per-instance.**

### 3. Expected
Gate criteria (brainstorm Thread 3, item 84; scout `scout-adaptive-compute-prior-art.md`): (a) does residual energy **calibrate** with retrieval correctness? (b) do boost-retries **recover** answers plain relaxation misses, at cost ≪ retraining? (c) [certificate — already proven]. Anti-Hopfield abstention was the intended empirical differentiator.

### 4. Experiment
**`v1-l0-gate`** (single seed) then **`v1-pivot`** (5 seeds, 90 models). MQAR associative recall, CLU-EBM memory via `train_generative` PCD; arms: mass $S^{(M)}$ / raw squeeze / kinetically-matched random kick / margin-gated / relax-longer / modern Hopfield; kv∈{16,24,32}; training-time Platt/affine calibration head + LTT/CALM conformal wrapper.

### 5. Measured vs expected
| question | measured | source |
|---|---|---|
| (a) calibration within-model | AUROC **0.65–0.79** (qualified PASS) | `v1-l0-gate.md §Q1` |
| (a) calibration cross-model | raw R pooled AUROC **0.33** (anti-ranked!) → **learned per-instance τ mandatory** | `v1-l0-gate.md §Q1` |
| (a) powered (pivot) | pooled AUROC raw **0.431±0.038 → calibrated 0.869±0.015** (5 seeds) | `v1-pivot.md §1` |
| (b) retry recovery | $S^{(M)}$ pooled **0.140** vs relax-longer **0.149** ≈ random kick — **FAIL** | `v1-l0-gate.md §Q2` |
| compute allocation (2°) | learned-point **4.8×@kv16 above always-full accuracy**, never loses at any level; ≈8× steps saved | `v1-pivot.md §2`, `v1-l0-gate.md` |
| energy vs margin | ΔAUROC **−0.004…+0.024** — energy adds ~nothing over readout margin | `v1-pivot.md §4` |
| LTT certificate | **30/30 valid**, honest refusal at kv32 | `v1-pivot.md §5` |
| anti-Hopfield abstention | **LOST/unwinnable** — Hopfield 0.983–1.0 acc ⇒ AURC≈0 | `v1-pivot.md §3` |

### 6. Verdict & revision — **[reframed]** (pivot adopted)
The idea **survived by pivoting** (Head decision 2026-07-07). The headline moved from *"boost retries recover answers"* → **parked**, to *"calibrated energy-gated compute allocation on conservative memories"* — which **survives powered statistics decisively** in its three load-bearing parts (calibration transfer 0.43→0.87; compute allocation that never loses to always-full and saves ~5×; distribution-free risk certificates never violated). Two honest amputations: (a) **squeeze retries bought nothing** at single-unit scale (parked for V3-scale multi-shell / mass-banded experiments — "we own the mechanism; where it helps is open"; appendix-grade if they fail there too); (b) **energy ≈ readout-margin** as a gate feature ⇒ the claim is the *gate mechanism + certificates + allocation*, not energy-signal superiority. The anti-Hopfield framing is **dropped or must move to a harder regime** (kv≥128, noisy cues, capacity-stressed Hopfield) — Head to rule.

Why retries failed connects back to Chapter 2: $S^{(M)}\approx S$ operationally *because learned $M$ is near-uniform* (Hyp-3(ii) untestable) — the boost's mass-differentiation had no dynamic range to exploit. The two "failures" (mass hierarchy doesn't emerge; boost retries don't help) are **the same fact seen twice.**

### 7. Emergent discoveries
- **Residual energy is not cross-model comparable — a substantive negative result.** Each trained CLU has its own energy scale; a single global $\tau$ is meaningless (pooled 0.33 < 0.5). This *empirically mandates* the training-time learned-$\tau$ decision the Head/Hub had made philosophically — theory and experiment converged on the same design.
- **The τ-gated cascade is a genuine compute allocator even when retries can't fix hard queries** — it routes compute *toward* hard queries and *away* from easy ones (8× steps saved at ≥ full-budget accuracy). The adaptive-compute value survives the retry failure. **Philosophy implication:** the cascade's worth is *rationing*, not *rescue* — a cleaner, more honest story than the original "boost recovers answers."

> **⟲ w24 pointer (2026-07-24, doc-curator) — DUAL-FILED, nothing deleted.** The boost/retry is the only w19–w23 material that belongs to **two** chapters, and the split is deliberate: **Chapter 4 remains the home of the MECHANISM** (the squeeze's symplecticity and retry certificate, the energy-shell filtration semantics of Prop 2, the calibration/allocation/LTT pillars, and this chapter's own honest amputations — retries bought nothing at single-unit scale); **Chapter 8 §5.2 is the home of the CLAIM** — *compute-adaptive reads* as the fourth dial, with its measured curve (+6.6…+76.2 pp, auto-stop ×1.2–1.8), its load-bearing gate (ungated collapses 0.96→0.004 at 9×), its surviving mechanism attribution (kick and ensemble **dead flat, 8/8**) and its refuted dominance half (the feedforward-NN floor wins every cell, −3.5…−42.2 pp). **Any dial-level claim cites Ch. 8; any statement about why a boost is safe or what it does to the energy shell cites Ch. 4.** → dial migrated to Ch. 8, w24; mechanism stays.

# Chapter 5 — The lattice / scale-by-mass-and-size idea (Thread 5 → V3)

### 1. Philosophy
Two intuitions fuse. The **Hub's** Thread-5 extension: build the V3 hierarchy **by mass** — heavy sectors = slow coarse backbone, light = fast perception. The **Head's** critical caveat (2026-07-04): **"mass alone won't scale V3."** In a gas sharing one $V_\theta(q_{\rm joint})$, one particle's learning *overwrites* another's — **catastrophic interference in θ-space.** So **scale by mass AND size**: multi-unit modularity is the interference firewall, not optional decoration. The Hub formalized the interference as the **potential NTK** and cataloged firewalls (modularity = the only *hard* one).

### 2. Theory
F5 §7 (primer §8) — **one joint Hamiltonian, guarantees by construction [proven]:**
$$H_{\rm net}=\sum_i[T_i(p_i)+V_i(q_i)]+\sum_{(ij)\in E}V_c(q_i,q_j),$$
symplectic/conformal/Noether all inherit **at any $N$**, *provided* couplings are position-only, one global step, and damping discipline (uniform $\gamma$ keeps global pairing; per-unit keeps volume $\prod(1-\gamma_i)^{d_i}$ but not pairing). **Communication has a mass [proven at quadratic order]:** coupling two SO(2) memories gives a shared **latch** (sum mode, flat) and a relative mode $\mu_{\rm rel}^2=2\kappa_c/(Mf^2)$ ⇒ sync $\propto\kappa_c^{-1/2}$, disagreement-retention $\propto\kappa_c^{-1}$ — **coupling strength prices communication speed against relative-memory lifetime.** Interference is the off-diagonal potential NTK $\Theta(q,q')=\nabla_\theta V^\top\nabla_\theta V$ (F5 §6, primer §9); modularity makes it *exactly* block-zero across unit-owned parameters. One-equation essence: **width = $N$, depth = rollout time, architecture = graph; the memory taxonomy applies to the joint spectrum unchanged.**

### 3. Expected
(brainstorm Thread 5 falsifiable (iii) + F5 §7.2 pricing law): sync $\propto\kappa^{-1/2}$; $n_{1/2}\propto\kappa^{-1}$; $\mu_{\rm rel}^2=2\kappa/(Mf^2)$; shared latch frozen at every $\kappa$; **banded-mass lattices beat uniform at matched params.** Plus Thread-7's reversibility bet: at $\gamma=0$ the scan-pure rollout is exactly reversible ⇒ O(1)-memory BPTT.

### 4. Experiment
**`v3-lattice-build`** (engineer): `CLULattice` (position-only couplings structural; Spring/MLP/Gated-wormhole; duck-types CHLU ⇒ existing harness + `train_chlu` work verbatim; scan-pure ⇒ reversible-BPTT preserved). κ-sweep [0.003…0.3], probe γ=0.2, banded scales [4.0, 0.25], N=2/4/8, 300-epoch banded-vs-uniform smoke.

### 5. Measured vs expected
| prediction | measured | source |
|---|---|---|
| sync $\propto\kappa^{-1/2}$ | slope **−0.499** | `v3-lattice-build.md §1` |
| $n_{1/2}\propto\kappa^{-1}$ | slope **−0.986** | " |
| $\mu_{\rm rel}^2=2\kappa/(Mf^2)$ | $4\kappa/M$ to **6 decimals** | " |
| shared latch frozen | **bit-frozen at every κ** (drift exactly 0.0) | " |
| κ=0 reduction | **exactly bit-level** (`jnp.array_equal`, no tolerance) | `v3-lattice-build.md §2` |
| single-unit charge at κ>0 | **NOT conserved** (drift >1e-3) — communication *is* charge flow | " |
| banded > uniform (iii) | wake loss **0.331 vs 1.714**; MSE **1.362 vs 2.516** (1 seed) | `v3-lattice-build.md §5` |

### 6. Verdict & revision — **[validated]** (mechanism) / **[open]** (scale)
V3's *mechanism* is **validated**: the pricing law is measured on a working lattice to 6 decimals, the composition theorems hold bit-for-bit, and the shared latch survives coupling. The **communication-is-charge-flow** result is a genuine discovery (see §7). The Head's "scale by mass AND size" caveat is **vindicated in principle** (modularity = the only hard firewall, F5 §9) but **untested at scale** — the interference NTK $\Theta(q_A,q_B)$ measurement (single big unit vs modular lattice) is defined but not run. Banded > uniform is **single-seed evidence**, not a claim — the first positive data for Thread-5 falsifiable (iii), explicitly framed as "banding = learnability prior, not capacity."

### 7. Emergent discoveries
- **"Communication is literally charge flow" (`v3-lattice-build.md §2`).** The single-unit Noether charge is *not* conserved once $\kappa>0$ (drift >1e-3) while the *joint* charge is (>1e-10) — coupling doesn't just wire units, it opens a charge current between them. **Math implication:** inter-unit communication is quantified by the rate of single-unit charge non-conservation — a measurable communication observable. **Philosophy implication:** "connected CLUs propagate information" (paper §E) is now precise — information *is* the Noether current crossing the coupling, and its speed/lifetime is priced by $\kappa$.
- **Reversible O(1)-memory training preserved by design.** The lattice's scan-pure rollouts keep $\gamma=0$ exact reversibility (F5 Prop-6), so recompute-backwards BPTT works natively — a V3 systems claim (memory-vs-depth curves "for free") that other architectures buy with RevNet-style constraints. This was a Thread-7 frontier adopted into V3.

> **⟲ w24 pointer (2026-07-24, doc-curator) — nothing deleted; the capacity line moved.** The w20–w23 addenda filed the **address-space capacity results** under Ch. 5 / "Ch-8 candidate": designed **`K_max = 4·2^d` exactly over d=2–8** (R²=1.0000, 1024 items at d=8), the **falsified-with-repair packing bound** (`(1+2R/w)^d` wrong in form → `K_max=(2R/Δ_req)^{d_eff}`, `Δ_req ≈ 3.1·max(w,σ_query)`, N70), the **three capacity regimes**, and the learned law **`K_learned(d) = min(2^d, ~32)`** with its **d-independent write ceiling** (N92). **These are now canonically homed in Ch. 8 §2 and §5.3.** **Chapter 5 keeps the lattice/composition thread** — the joint-Hamiltonian guarantees, the communication pricing law, the reversibility claim — and gains one live question from Ch. 8: *does sharding K items across N units multiply capacity ~32·N without optimizer synchronisation, iff writes are local?* (tasked w24, `lattice-capacity-theory`). → migrated to Ch. 8, w24.

# Chapter 6 — The sampler-thermodynamics thread (FDT / Prop-9)

### 1. Philosophy
This thread has **no philosophical seed** — it is an agent discovery that *forced* a philosophical correction. The CLU's generative mode couples to Langevin dynamics to sample the Boltzmann distribution $P\propto e^{-H/T}$, anneal $T$, and "crystallize" noise into data (paper Exp III / MNIST). The implicit belief was: *the shipped sampler samples Gibbs, so temperatures mean what we say and mode statistics reflect the learned landscape.* The **theorist (F5)** falsified that belief.

### 2. Theory
F5 Prop-9 (primer §5.2) — **the shipped noise violates discrete FDT [proven, verified]:**
$$\sigma_{\rm code}=\sqrt{2\gamma T\varepsilon}\;\Rightarrow\;T_{{\rm eff},i}=\frac{2\varepsilon T}{(2-\gamma)M_i}\ne T,$$
a units-level slip — $M$ was swapped for $\mathrm{d}t$ relative to the correct $\sigma_i^\star=\sqrt{M_iT\gamma(2-\gamma)}$. Consequence: with learned non-uniform $M$, **each mode equilibrates at its own temperature ⇒ no single Gibbs invariant $e^{-H/T}$ exists at all.** One-equation essence: **the sampler samples a per-mode-tempered distribution, not Gibbs; the fix is one line but retrained temperatures shift.**

### 3. Expected
Two statements, opposite confidence levels. **[proven]:** the correction $\sigma_i^\star$ restores $\mathrm{Var}(p_i)=M_iT$. **[conjectured]** (F5 v1.0, flagged as testable): the per-mode temperature violation is a **candidate explanation for the MNIST 3/5/8/9 digit-mode imbalance** — testable on Exp-C checkpoints via the spread of $\mathrm{Var}(p_i)M_i$.

### 4. Experiment
**`generative-studies` Study A** (analyst): the FDT conjecture directly on 3 Exp-C checkpoints (mnist/FF/FFF) — measure $T_{\rm eff}$ vs mass; legacy vs fdt vs scale-matched-fdt generation comparison; γ=0.3, dt=0.05, T=0.5, 96k samples/mode.

### 5. Measured vs expected
| claim | measured | source |
|---|---|---|
| Prop-9 mechanism exact on trained models | legacy slope log Var(p) vs log M = **0.002** (mass-blind); ρ(T_eff, 1/M)=**0.994**; Var(p) vs closed-form ≤**0.06%** | `generative-studies.md §A.1` |
| fdt fix restores single T | slope **1.000**, T_eff flat to **0.8% CV** | " |
| $T_{\rm eff}\propto\mathrm{d}t$ | doubling dt **exactly doubles** T_eff | " |
| nominal T semantics | nominal T=0.5 is really T_eff≈0.045–0.048 (**~11× colder**, κ≈0.09) | " |
| **imbalance attribution** | **REFUTED**: scale-matched fdt χ²=**0.08, p=1.00**; **2/192 flips** (1.0%) | `generative-studies.md §A.2` |

### 6. Verdict & revision — **[validated] mechanism / [refuted] attribution**
A clean split. The **physics of Prop-9 is exactly right** on real trained models — legacy sampling has no single temperature; the fix restores Gibbs-consistency of the momentum marginal. But the **conjecture that it drives the digit imbalance is refuted**: scale-matched, correcting the per-mode violation changes essentially nothing about what gets generated (2/192 flips). The imbalance is a property of the **learned energy landscape** (basin depths/volumes + annealed relaxation from the centroid), not the sampler. *Why the violation is harmless today:* the mass spectrum is too narrow (max/min ≈ 1.5 ⇒ temperature anisotropy ≤±13%) — **directly connecting to Chapter 2's "latent tendency, not a hierarchy."** The revision is a **conditional promotion**: the `fdt` flag is cosmetic *now* but becomes load-bearing the moment V3 banding or mass-aware objectives create a real mass hierarchy. Sampler physics and mass doctrine interlock.

### 7. Emergent discoveries
- **No positional equilibrium (`generative-studies.md §A.1`).** Under either sampler, the momentum marginal is stationary but mean $H$ **falls monotonically** the whole 2000 steps and chains **exit the trained cube** (max|q|→3.66 at dt=0.05, →7.64 at dt=0.1). Direct consequence of the coercivity gap (F5 §7.7 / Prop-10): the Conv/Deep potentials are architecturally non-coercive; the confining clamp lives in the *training loop*, not the *unit*. **Implication:** any "energy-calibrated anomaly score" or long free-run generative claim on these checkpoints is only valid for the momentum marginal — the position distribution has no stationary state. This is a real limitation on the generative vertical and a concrete fix spec (put confinement in the unit).
- **EP-style temperature spectroscopy.** Because $T_{\rm eff}\propto\mathrm{d}t/M_i$ is exactly measurable, the sampler doubles as a diagnostic for the mass spectrum — an unplanned free measurement channel.

# Chapter 7 — The training-dynamics thread (Lyapunov degeneracy, sleep erosion, PCD null)

### 1. Philosophy
Also not a designed idea — three things the program *believed by default* about its own training loop, each of which an agent falsified or characterized. Belief A: the Lyapunov regularizer $\lambda_{\rm lyap}$ controls chaos (it's in every config). Belief B: the wake–sleep contrastive loop preserves the landscape it's shaping. Belief C: persistent CD (PCD) vs plain CD matters for the dynamics experiments (paper Algorithm 1 assumes persistence). These beliefs shaped experiments and paper claims — so falsifying them is load-bearing.

### 2. Theory
- **Belief A — F5 Prop-5 (primer §1.8) [proven, verified]:** the coded penalty $\mathrm{mean}_i\log\sigma_i(J)\equiv\frac12\ln(1-\gamma)$ by conformal symplecticity — **θ-independent**, and the wake phase calls it with $\gamma=0$ so it is **identically zero with zero gradient.** *It has never done anything.* Fix: $\max_i\log\sigma_i$ or $\sum(\log\sigma_i)^2$ (both θ-sensitive, verified).
- **Belief B — no theorem predicted it;** the theory (Prop-8) only guarantees discretization won't break protection — it says nothing about the *learning* process eroding a designed vacuum. Belief B's failure (Chapter 1 §7) is a *measured* dynamical fact, not a derived one.
- **Two impossibility results that reshape training (F5 §3.4, primer §3.4) [proven, verified]:** friction **never** stabilizes a saddle ($\lambda_+>1\;\forall\gamma$; $\min_\gamma(\lambda_+-1)=1.4\times10^{-5}$), and the governor is **blind** to isoenergetic saddle escape ($V\to T$ at constant $H$). Only curvature control of $V_\theta$ (a *working* Lyapunov reg) and the relativistic cap bound expanding modes.

### 3. Expected
Belief A predicted: $\lambda_{\rm lyap}$ ablations should show an effect. Belief C predicted: PCD should change the learned energy landscape vs CD (§7.4 discrepancy). Both are *retrospective* predictions the agents tested.

### 4. Experiment
- **`fix-pack-2`** (engineer): implemented the working regularizer (`lyapunov_penalty="max"` default) behind a `legacy_degenerate` flag; FDT σ*; seeding; env fixes.
- **`generative-studies` Study B** (analyst): PCD vs CD on the Exp-B dynamics path, 3 matched seeds ×2 arms (paired data/init/train keys), 500 epochs, + a 6-run `sleep_friction=0.2` supplement.
- **`v2-full-runs` Finding 0** (analyst): the sleep-erosion characterization (Chapter 1 §7).

### 5. Measured vs expected
| belief | measured | source |
|---|---|---|
| A: Lyapunov reg does something | $\equiv\frac12\ln(1-\gamma)$ to dev **≤2.1×10⁻¹⁶**; max-log-σ spread ~0.1 — **all past $\lambda_{\rm lyap}$ ablations were inert** | F5 Prop-5; check (d) |
| B: training preserves vacuum | **refuted** — ring inverts by epoch 600 (Chapter 1 §7) | `v2-full-runs.md §2` |
| C: PCD ≠ CD on dynamics | **characterized NULL**: Δloss −0.0004…−0.0006, ΔMSE ≤1e-3, relative divergence ~1e-5 across 3 seeds | `generative-studies.md §B` |
| saddle impossibilities | $\min_\gamma(\lambda_+-1)=1.4\times10^{-5}>0$; escape rate exact = fitted 1.000112449 | F5 check (b) |

### 6. Verdict & revision — **[refuted]** (A, retroactively) / **[characterized null]** (C) / **[validated as risk]** (B, erosion)
- **Belief A refuted with retroactive consequences:** $\lambda_{\rm lyap}$ has never regularized anything; any past claim that "Lyapunov regularization helped" is **meaningless retroactively**. Fixed on `fix-pack-2` (⚠ new runs behave differently by design — reproduce old runs with `legacy_degenerate`).
- **Belief C is a characterized null (§7.4 CLOSED):** at $\gamma=0$ the sleep phase has zero Langevin noise + isoenergetic walk ⇒ nothing to persist; at $\gamma>0$, k=500 over-mixes. **No empirical consequence for A/B; default `persistent_sleep_buffer=False` is safe; historical results need no asterisk.** A clean negative result that de-risks a paper-vs-code discrepancy.
- **Belief B is the sleep-erosion finding** — validated *as a risk*: long CD training destroys designed geometry (Chapter 1 §7). Fix specs written (exp-d default epochs, Noether $r^*\approx0$ guard, wake-dominant schedule).

### 7. Emergent discoveries
- **The dead regularizer is a methodological cautionary tale.** A quantity that *looks* like a chaos penalty (mean log-singular-value) is pinned by the symplectic structure theorem to a constant — it measures the *sum* of Lyapunov exponents, which conformality fixes; chaos lives in the *max*. **Philosophy implication:** structure-preserving priors can *hide* degrees of freedom you think you're controlling. The fix (penalize the max) is one line but changes the whole meaning of "we regularize the Lyapunov spectrum."
- **Sleep erosion + the saddle impossibilities together rewrite V3's trainability story.** The anti-explosion levers are exactly two — curvature control of $V_\theta$ (a working reg) and the relativistic cap — *not* $\gamma$, *not* the governor. Combined with erosion (training distorts the landscape), V3's depth-stability claims must specify *which* forgetting column (primer §1.8 table) they assume and *how* they anchor the vacuum. Honest, and load-bearing for the ICLR narrative's "honest correction" line ("symplectic ⇒ *paired* gradients, not stable — no vanishing, but explosion is real and only curvature + $c$ contain it").

> **⟲ w24 cross-reference (2026-07-24, doc-curator) — nothing migrated out of this chapter.** Chapter 7 remains **the** home of the program's epistemics (the collapse taxonomy T1/T2/T3, the prereg scalps, the instrument-validity family: w13 *instrument validity precedes inference* · w17 *a floating-point-exact order parameter is an overflow suspect* · w23 **N94** *diagnostics on `<40`-epoch CAFE fits are not properties of the shipped model*). **Chapter 8 §8 carries a chapter-scoped subset** — the laundering control, the mechanism controls, N94, and the w23 event in which a Hub overstatement was caught by a spoke's own pre-registration scorecard — because Chapter 8 is the program's best single exhibit for its own method. **Duplication is intentional; Ch. 7 governs on any epistemics wording.**

# Chapter 8 — Control over memory in time: the memory with dials

> **⭐ Chapter opened 2026-07-24 (wave-24) by the doc-curator, on a Head ruling recorded in `handover_context.md §10` (2026-07-24 later) and specified by the Hub in `tasks/doc-curator-w24-chapter-8.md`. It is the only chapter not authored at w3.** It gathers five waves (w19–w23) of material that the addenda below were forced to file as *"Ch-8 candidate"* and scatter across Chapters 1–5. **Nothing was deleted anywhere:** each originating chapter carries a dated `⟲ w24 pointer` block, and the w19–w23 addenda are untouched — this chapter is the *canonical home*, not the only copy.
>
> **The candidate title is retired.** From w19 to w23 this material was filed as *"the addressable-memory thesis"* with verdict `[split — physics validated, indexing refuted, controller unbuilt]`. That title names a claim the program **refuted** (indexing) and a component that is now **built** (the controller). The refuted indexing claim is recorded **inside** this chapter, as its opening negative — *it is part of the story, not the name of the story.*

### 1. Philosophy

Memory is not a buffer beside the network; it is **the shape of the network's own energy landscape**, indexed by launch conditions. A stored item is a valley; a query is a launch; retrieval is the trajectory the physics runs anyway. This much was the w19 thesis (**Hub** formalization of the **Head's** "CLU is a latent information carrier, not a model of the data's dynamics", `handover §10` w19).

⭐ **The w24 revision, and it is a change of thesis, not of emphasis (Head, `head-advisor-w23-direction.md` Part 1 close):** *the value of such a store is **not lookup — it is control.*** Lookup is ceiling-capped by the very operation settling approximates (§5.1), and five waves of measurement say so three independent ways. What no lookup table, no kNN datastore and no softmax memory offers is a set of **dials on memory in time**:

| dial | the verb | the question it answers |
|---|---|---|
| **admission** | *what may be written at all* | can the store refuse an item it would later corrupt? |
| **lifetimes** | *how long each item lives* | can half-lives be set per item, at write time, and met? |
| **isolation** | *what a write does to its neighbours* | can K sequential writes not destroy each other? |
| **compute-adaptive reads** | *how hard to think about this query* | can a read spend more and get more, and stop by itself? |

**One slide (Head, Part 3):** ***write = dig a valley · read = drop a ball · forget = let it fill in.*** ⚠ **Scope this last verb honestly** (w22 plain-language re-grounding, still binding): *a frozen surface does not decay on its own.* As shipped, "fill in" is either a within-rollout fade (a half-life in rollout steps) or an **active edit** — the controller lowering a well's depth on a schedule (`controller-mvp` §1(d)). The slide is the pitch; this sentence is the fine print, and it travels with it.

**Program principle it instantiates:** P3 (*fix the geometry, learn the content*) in its w23 architectural form — *learn around a designed core* (fixed physics + designed store, learned interfaces). ⭐ **The w24 doctrine adds the claim-level rule the architecture rule never gave us: every claim must be a claim about one of the four dials, each backed by a law.**

### 2. Theory

**The formal object.** `(V_θ, 𝒜)` — a landscape plus its address set; stated in `clu-memory-architecture` §1 as `(S, ρ, τ)` (store · read relation · retention) with the address roles **`q₀` = location, `(m, p₀)` = energy shell / scope, `m` = timescale**, and two read modes: **γ_r = 0** location-addressable and gradient-transparent; **γ_r > 0** content-addressable (the basin of attraction *is* the similarity metric — this is Hopfield retrieval) and gradient-opaque.

**The six propositions (`clu-memory-architecture` §2; all `[proven, verified]` unless noted).**
- **Prop 1 — mass = time dilation.** With `p₀=0` the continuum path image in `q`-space is exactly **M-independent**; only traversal speed changes.
- **Prop 2 — energy-shell gating.** The reachable set of address `a` lies in the connected component of `{q : V(q) ≤ E(a) − min T}`; crossing a barrier `h` from rest needs `p₀²/2M > h` ⇒ threshold `M* = p₀²/2h`. *A boost ladder is therefore an **energy-shell filtration**: retry k unlocks exactly the items behind barriers `< E_k`.*
- **Prop 3 — governed reach/latency** (the relativistic cap; inherited from Ch. 5's composition theorems).
- **Prop 4 — gradient dichotomy.** The γ=0 Verlet tangent map is symplectic ⇒ singular values pair `(σ, 1/σ)` ⇒ **‖J_N‖ ≥ 1 always: a conservative read has no vanishing-gradient problem, ever.** *(N62 — half a positive; the folk BPTT objection is inverted.)*
- **Prop 5 — contraction kills address gradients.** For γ_r>0 the read endpoint is the basin fixed point, locally **constant** in `(q₀,p₀,m)`; residual gradient decays as `((1−γ_r)|λ_int|)^n` (≈e^{−18} at γ_r=0.02, n=900).
- **Prop 6 — multiplexing lifts the D1 gauge.** With **per-item** masses only the overall scale stays gauge; all ratios become wake-visible and exactly learnable (**verified to 2.2e-14**). ⚠ As shipped, `m` is not an address component at all.
- **Prop 7 (promoted w20)** — exact robustness ⇒ zero first-order signal *through the read*; the strong "no learned addressing" form is **refuted**, which is why the two-phase design survives.

**Prop F1 (w21, `relaxation-fiber-capacity`) — mass SELECTS, it never STORES `[proven, verified]`.** The read depends on `(M,V,p₀)` only through `(M⁻¹∇V, M⁻¹p₀)` (verified **6.2e-16**) ⇒ `M` and `p₀` are **address-side**; the fiber's only storing channel is the local jet of `G = M⁻¹∇V` (8.6 bits coupled vs **0.20 separable** — *separability, not harmonicity, is the killer*). Composition: **`B_total = K_spatial · B_fiber`** — slots × bits, never items × items. Parameter ceiling: **`B_total ≤ P·b_θ`** (the shipped MLP's 1377 params vs ≥36 864 reals needed at d=8) ⇒ measured spatial capacity and per-well fibers are **jointly unreachable by the shipped learned MLP**. **Four independent nulls** (w20 · w21 · w22 pre-registered · w23 pre-registered: per-item mass Δstrict **+0.008 ± 0.011**).

**Prop C-N (w20, `relaxation-addressing-theory`) — permanence is DESIGNED, not trained `[proven]`.** No controller makes `τ=∞` in an unconstrained `V_θ`; and no finite-λ soft regularizer can produce a mode-(a) permanent latch (R-4 ⇒ `μ²(λ)>0` ⇒ finite half-life; the designed-vs-emergent flatness gap is **12 orders**, Ch. 1). **Permanent memory must be a designed flat coset.** *This is the theoretical licence for the "permanent + leaky wells in one store" result under dial 2.*

**Three capacity regimes (`clu-memory-architecture` §4) — capacity is never one number.**
1. **Barrier-protected** (`K ≤ K_pack ≈ 2πR/4.4s ≈ 8` on the ring): selectivity **1.00**, guaranteed by Prop 2 — a written particle *cannot* visit a foreign item. Long-horizon survival exact (N=20 000 ≡ N=2000).
2. **Washboard death zone** (`K ≈ 16`): partial well merger; **17.2%** of items transported ≥1 sector; selectivity crashes to **0.49–0.77**. *The worst regime is intermediate packing, not maximal packing* — and an **emergent** CHLU is stuck here (1–1.6 bits/register).
3. **Continuum register** (`K ≥ 24`): wells merge to a ring valley, the azimuthal direction is Goldstone-like, written angle retained exactly (drift ≥1 sector = **0.000** at K=24 and K=48); capacity becomes **resolution-limited**, `K_max ≈ circumference/(≈4·max(σ_write, ℓ_θ))`.

**The laws now attached to the dials.**

| dial | law | status | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| capacity (write) | **`K_learned(d) = min(2^d, K_ceiling ≈ 32)`** — budget-adequate walls {4, 8, 16, 32, 32, 32} at d={2,3,4,5,6,8} | `[verified]`, 3 seeds, 2-seed adequacy re-checks | `dimension-aware-budget` §1/§3 (N92) |
| capacity (designed) | **`K_max = 4·2^d` exactly over d=2–8** (log₂K_max = d+2, **R²=1.0000**; 1024 items at d=8); repaired packing bound **`K_max=(2R/Δ_req)^{d_eff}`, `Δ_req ≈ 3.1·max(w, σ_query)`, `d_eff ≈ 0.72–0.83·d`** | `[verified]` (designed landscape only) | `address-space-dimension-scaling` (N70) |
| admission | **`N_pack = πR²/((√3/2)·d_safe²)`** with `d_safe = 4.4·s`, and the sizing rule **`R = 0.808·√K`** (which inverts that formula to `N_pack ≈ K`) | `[verified]` at the geometry run | `controller-mvp` §0/§2 |
| lifetimes | per-item **`A·exp(−λt)`** — measured amplitude exactly `exp(−0.35t)`, half-life **1.98** | `[verified]` | `controller-mvp` §1(d) |
| isolation | interference is governed by the **C3 ratio `‖∇δV(q*)‖/λ_min(Hess V(q*))`** at the stored items — measured across 6 function classes and **4.6 decades** (meas/pred ∈ [0.73, 1.63] for 5/6) | `[evidenced]` | `sequential-write-interference`, `potential-function-class` (N74) |
| compute | a **monotone, self-limiting** accuracy-vs-compute curve: rises, then auto-stops when the low-confidence tail is exhausted | `[verified]`, seed 0 | `retry-compute-study` §1–§5 (N90) |

⚠ **Precision the chapter must carry (curator, w24):** "capacity doubles per dimension — exactly the designed rate" is a statement about the **rate**, not the value. The designed law is `4·2^d`; the learned law is `2^d`. Same doubling, **4× lower prefactor** for d≤5 (the designed/learned tax then plateaus at ~1/8 for d≥5). Never let "exactly the designed rate" be read as "equals designed capacity."

### 3. Expected (stated before the fact)

- **The Hub's w19 formalization** (`clu-memory-architecture`): the object `(V_θ,𝒜)`, six propositions, three regimes — with the **explicit bet that addresses could be learned** (item 4 of the vision table: *"learn masses + initial latents as access keys"*).
- **The Head's amendment** (w19): iterative, gauge-loose addressing — *"start arbitrary and restructure"* — recorded as R19-4, and still a **proposal under test**, in stated tension with six of the program's own negatives.
- **The original addressable-memory / indexing bet:** that a gradient could find which address retrieves which item. **Pre-registered and falsified** (§5.1).
- **The w23 phase doctrine** (Head): *"learn around a designed core"* — fixed relaxation physics + designed wells, learned `φ`/`ψ` interfaces + controller policy, with attention (fixed `softmax(QKᵀ)V`, learned projections) as the stated precedent. **This survives w24 as the architecture rule** (§6).
- **The consequence the doctrine predicted and the program bet on:** *with a real learned `φ`, the designed store will beat trivial baselines.* **Pre-registered; falsified on its own control** (§5.1(2)).

### 4. Experiment

Nine harnesses across five waves, plus the adjudication pass that cleaned the record:

`primitive-harness` (w20, CLU vs 3 primitives on 3 families) · `learned-landscape-write-read` (w20, 5 seeds) · `address-space-dimension-scaling` (w20, designed capacity d=2–8) · `relaxation-addressing-theory` + `relaxation-fiber-capacity` (w20/w21, theory + MVC-0 spec) · `sequential-write-interference` + `potential-function-class` + `gamma-read-sweep` (w21, the write-operator factorial) · `designed-mechanism-learned-content` + `gated-write-performance` + `hopfield-capacity-benchmark` (w22, the performance wave) · **`phi-read-in`** · **`retry-compute-study`** · **`controller-mvp`** · **`dimension-aware-budget`** (w23, the four instruments) · **`r19-r20-reconciliations`** (w23, three measurement conflicts closed). Every w22/w23 harness shipped a **pre-registration**; every w23 harness shipped its **controls**.

### 5. Measured — negatives first (the Head's framing, and the honest order)

#### 5.1 The three refutations

**(1) ⛔ Indexing/addressing fails — address-gradient search is dead (N61, tier A).** Four qualitatively different protocols, **two independent agents, two independent harnesses**: best **3/18 ≈ chance** on cross-basin capture; the engineer's ring harness **4.2%**; the damped arm **0/18 with the loss frozen at 7.9035443 to seven digits over 4000 steps** — Prop 5's zero-gradient plateau observed raw. *Escape and transport are learnable (9/18 basin-level, loss 7.90→2.65); **capture is not.*** Mechanism: separatrix roughness / chaotic scattering (cliff ratio 1.7e7). ⭐ **The candidate no-go this hands the field, stated as a proposition and not adjudicated:** *a dynamically-robust addressable memory has no useful address gradient* — a readable memory needs the retrieved state to be a fixed point, i.e. exactly `∂(final)/∂q₀ → 0`, and **the address gradient IS that derivative.** ⇒ addresses must be **derived from the landscape** (write-side bookkeeping), never searched by gradient.

**(2) ⛔ Static lookup is TRIPLE-refuted.**
- **w22, raw pixels (N81):** a trivial **nearest-neighbour pixel floor beats everyone** — CLU *and* modern Hopfield (MNIST ≥0.996 to M=512); *the visible half of a masked image already names it.* ⭐ CLU **wins the noise axis vs Hopfield** (MNIST σ=0.2: **0.92 vs 0.42**; CIFAR σ≤0.2: **0.86 vs 0.008**) — *rolling to the bottom of a valley IS denoising; one softmax step is not* — and still loses to NN.
- **w23, φ-space (N89, tier A — the decision-grade one):** with a real learned read-in in two independently-fair arms (frozen PCA-32; a separately-trained AE — both fit on a **disjoint** pool, **never through the store**), **the mandatory laundering control fires on all four (dataset × arm) cells, max CLU margin 0.000.** *"The win is φ's, not ours."* ⇒ **the "it only loses because there is no embedding" escape hatch — which had excused three waves of losses — is CLOSED.**
- **w23, retry (N90, tier A):** the matched-compute feedforward-NN control sits **flat at ceiling 0.996 (k0) → 0.926 (k8)** and beats CLU-gated retry in **all 8 cells** — **mask −5.5 / −13.3 / −3.5 / −17.6 pp; noise −7.8 / −26.6 / −11.3 / −42.2 pp.**
- ⚠ **The structural reading (Head, `head-advisor-w23-direction.md` Part 1) — recorded as the Head's reading, NOT as a proved theorem:** *settling **approximates** the nearest-neighbour lookup, and an approximation does not beat its target.* **Binding scope caveat, do not re-litigate:** on masked/static retrieval, **equalling a simple baseline is our best case**.

**(3) ⛔ The learned write caps at ~32, d-independently (N92, tier A).** Budget-adequate walls **{4, 8, 16, 32, 32, 32}** at d={2,3,4,5,6,8}: **K=64 is unwritable at d=6 AND at d=8 despite 4× different geometric room.** The ceiling belongs to the **WRITE OPERATOR** — not the terrain (**designed digs ≥256 under identical numerics**), not the parameters (d=6 K=64 gets *worse* at 2× atoms, **0.855 → 0.809**), not the dimension. And the smoking gun: **the write loss reaches zero while retrieval already fails** ⇒ joint-dig interference + an objective blind to crowding. *Different valleys fight over shared clay, and the objective declares victory.*
- **Two companions that belong with it.** ⛔ **CLU is the WORST of four primitives under sequential parametric writes** (K=64 retention: gru 0.57 · mlp 0.43 · attn 0.34 · **CLU 0.16**; joint criterion censored 0.00→1.00 between K=8 and K=32 — *what catastrophic forgetting looks like when you instrument the write loop*, N75). ⛔ **The controller's retention-per-OFFERED on a fixed address space is 0.081 — last of seven** (N91); it is an **abstention price**, not a malfunction (§5.2), but it is a loss and it travels with the win.

#### 5.2 ⭐ All four dials measured working

| dial | measurement | number | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| **admission** | retention **per admitted**, controller ON, K=1…64, 5 seeds paired | **1.000 flat to K=64 — best of five** (⚠ *five compared methods*, not five seeds; the run is 5-seed) | `controller-mvp` §2 |
| | the gate **demonstrably fires** (in direct contrast to N74's arithmetically-vacuous gate) | live-site spacing **1.61 ≥ d_safe 1.54**; intervention rate **0.20 → 0.97**; admitted **5.2 ± 0.4** of 64 vs `N_pack` **6.12** | `controller-mvp` §0/§2 |
| | sized to the load (`R = 0.808·√K`) | admitted **42.8/64**, per-offered **0.669 — beats all four primitives (gru 0.57)** | `controller-mvp` §2 |
| | cost | gate **0.006 ms**; refuse-and-relocate **3.1 ms ≈ 4× a 0.70 ms read** (N93) | `controller-mvp` §3(d) |
| **lifetimes** | per-item decay, leaky wells | amplitude **exactly `exp(−0.35t)`**, half-life **1.98**, `decayed_out = 6/6` | `controller-mvp` §1(d) |
| | permanent + leaky coexisting in one store | permanent wells **untouched** by the decay schedule; a full all-permanent store raises a **capacity alarm** instead of silently overwriting | " |
| | self-eviction | leaky wells evict themselves below the depth floor | " |
| **isolation** | masked (bit-local) write vs the joint global dig | **8474× (d=2) / 3434× (d=4)**; w22: **197× (d=2) → 599× (d=3) → 6332× (d=4)** — *the advantage AMPLIFIES with dimension* | `dimension-aware-budget`; `designed-mechanism-learned-content` §1 |
| | anchored write (codebook rehearsal) — the only arm that both preserves AND stores | **103×**; strict-A **1.000**, strict-B **0.975** | `sequential-write-interference` (N74) |
| | designed store + gate under K=1…16 sequential writes | item-1 retention **1.000 vs 0.16 ungated**, at the price of **6.0 ± 0.9 of 16 admitted** (= the packing bound 6.1 to 2%) — *refusal is the mechanism; always quote the capacity price* | " |
| **compute** | the gated retry ladder k∈{0,1,2,4,8} | rises **+6.6 … +76.2 pp** and **auto-stops at ×1.2–1.8** | `retry-compute-study` §1 |

| | the gate is load-bearing | ungated retry-all **collapses 0.96 → 0.004 at 9× compute**; τ-sweep optimum 0.99 → **0.961 @ 1.64×** vs no-gate 0.828 @ 1.81× | `retry-compute-study` §2 |
| | the w22 origin point | **+46.9 pp (0.492 → 0.961) at ×1.5 compute, with a −38.3 pp blank guard** (retrying the *confident* half destroys it) | `hopfield-capacity-benchmark` §4.1 |

#### 5.3 ⭐ Geometry vindicated where the write can reach

For **d ≤ 5, capacity doubles per dimension** — exactly the designed doubling rate (§2's precision note applies: rate, not prefactor). This resolves w21's sharpest open question and w22's suspicion in the primitive's favour: **the K=8 wall is geometry, not a learning ceiling** (N82), **H-LEARNING-at-8 is rejected**, and **w22's d=8 stall was budget starvation** (8 → 32 under a dimension-scaled budget; monotonicity restored). On the designed side the law is exponential and exact: **`K_max = 4·2^d`, R² = 1.0000, 1024 items at d=8** with the blank control passing every cell.

⚠ **What is NOT claimed:** none of this is a capacity *win* over anything. Per the Head's boundary ruling (w24), the capacity law is exempt from the masked-recall demotion **because it is a law about the primitive, not a competitive claim — and its figure must never be framed as beating anything.**

### 6. Verdict & revision — **`[split — lookup refuted, indexing refuted, control validated]`** (Head-ruled, w24)

The chapter's idea moved twice, and both moves were forced by measurement:

1. **w19 → w22: "addressable memory" → "designed memory."** The indexing bet died (N61) and the learned loop died with it (N67); what survived was a store whose physics works **when it is designed** and whose learned counterpart does not. This produced the w23 **architecture rule**: *learn around a designed core.*
2. **w23 → w24: "designed memory that wins lookups" → "control over memory in time."** The φ laundering result (N89) killed the *lookup* half on its own pre-registered control. What replaced it is the **claim rule**: *every claim is about one of four dials, each backed by a law.* **The architecture rule survives intact; the thesis it was serving does not.**

⚠ **Curator scope note (a note, not a re-verdict — the verdict line above is the Head's, recorded verbatim).** On this ledger's two-layer tag scheme, `control validated` is stated without the scope qualifier that Chapters 1 and 3 carry for better-replicated results (`[validated-with-scope]`). The scope that a reader must not have to reconstruct: **all four dials are measured on a *designed* store** (the spacing certificate is meaningless for a global-support learned write, N75); **one geometry family**, Newtonian, `p₀=0`, scalar payload; the compute dial is **seed 0**, the admission/lifetime dials are **5 seeds**, the capacity walls at d=4/5 rest on **2-seed** adequacy re-checks of a write the report itself calls *seed-fragile at the 0.9 rung*; and **zero external benchmarks have been won on their own headline metric.** Multi-seeding of the whole w23 frontier is tasked (`multi-seed-w23`, w24) and is a **precondition for any paper number**. Flagged to the Hub in `outputs/doc-curator-w24-chapter-8.md`.

### 7. Paper vehicle (the second blocker, now cleared)

The w19 refusal to open this chapter cited two blockers: *"a new chapter is a structural decision, not a curator one"* and *"the thesis has no named paper to attach to."* Both are now cleared by the Head's direction doc (Parts 3–4).

- **ICLR long (main text):** the primitive in the **hilly-landscape language** + **three laws** (capacity · lifetime · compute-scaling) + the **CL sweep** + the **unlearning demo**.
- **NMI forks at Phase 3** — same results, laws foregrounded (per the Head's filing rule: *performance with the explaining law → ICLR/NMI*).
- **Appendix:** the **NN-ceiling proximity result** (masked recall, permanently appendix-only — role: *competence, not victory*), the **negatives-registry material** (C-9), and the **RUD-C benchmark spec** (anchored verbatim to U-Hop `@cdac754`, four mandatory baselines).
- ⚠ **Status honesty (curator, w24):** of that main text, **the three laws exist** (§2), **the CL sweep and the unlearning demo do not** — they are R4 and R1 targets on the Phase-2/3 pathway, not results. The chapter names the vehicle; it does not claim the cargo.
- **Ambition it serves (Head, Part 3):** the transformer's **adoption** trajectory — *"what will researchers be unable to resist dropping into their own stack?"* — not a leaderboard. CLU's candidate answer: **it is the only memory with dials.**

### 8. Chapter epistemics — why this chapter is the program's best exhibit for its own method

- ⭐ **The laundering control.** A pre-registered trivial-store swap (*same features, dumbest possible store*) **killed the program's central bet on schedule** — the C17-3 lesson, executed: C17-3 → **N89**. It is now **mandatory on every performance claim**. *A control that can kill your headline, run before you write the headline, is the difference between a program and a press release.*
- ⭐ **The mechanism controls are what let a claim survive where its neighbour died.** Retry's kick/ensemble flatness (dead flat, 8/8) is *exactly why* the retry mechanism claim survives while the retry leaderboard claim died in the same report. **The controls did not weaken the claim; they are the only reason a claim remains.**
- ⭐ **The under-trained-instrument rule (N94, standing).** Two of the three w23 measurement conflicts dissolved into **training maturity**, not physics and not bugs: *diagnostics computed on `<40`-epoch CAFE fits are not properties of the shipped model; every CAFE diagnostic states its epoch count.* Joins the w17 rule (*an order parameter that hits a floating-point-exact value is an overflow suspect*) and the w13 family (*instrument validity precedes inference*).
- ⭐ **A Hub overstatement caught before it reached a draft.** The Hub's first-pass w23 summary wrote *"CLU-in-φ decisively beats closed-form Hopfield-in-φ"* unqualified. The instrument that caught it was **`phi-read-in`'s own pre-registration scorecard**, which marked that prediction **◐ — right on CIFAR, wrong on MNIST at high load (Hopfield 0.957 vs CLU 0.871)**; the w23 curator pass read the scorecard against the summary and flagged it; the Hub **confirmed the correction and made the CIFAR / hard-feature-geometry qualifier binding** (`handover §10`, 2026-07-24, ruling 1). *An agent's own scorecard corrected the agent that reviews it.*
- **The count, for the record.** Across w19–w23 this chapter's material produced **six overturned Hub hypotheses**, **four registered optimistic hypotheses reported dead by the agents who registered them** (w23), one agent **pre-registering its own headline loss and confirming it**, and one **declared deviation** (`c=2.0 → √2`, shown not to move the predictions). *A pre-registered prediction that survives is evidence; one that fails is a finding; an un-pre-registered agreement is neither.*
