---

# Positioning consequences (per short)

**V2 (Goldstone memory — the spine, and the naming debut of "CLU").** The strongest *honest* claim is: **"a constitutive theory of memory degradation in a dissipative Hamiltonian primitive — retention laws with a scope and a floor that single-exponential kinematic accounts (Mo 2026) provably cannot express."** The GMOR-to-4.5-decades law, the mass-independent underdamped floor, the metric bifurcation, and the exact latch are the assets; the head-to-head with Mo (his ratio 1.013 = the overdamped face of our budget table, breaking to 2.20 at the EP) is the figure. What V2 must *not* claim: that we invented Goldstone channel allocation (Mo + Di Bernardo own the construction) — lead with **EFT-of-memory + energy-space + the pricing/floor**. Sleep erosion is disclosed as a training-dynamics caveat with a fix, not hidden.

**V1 (test-time compute).** The strongest honest claim is **"calibrated, risk-controlled, energy-gated compute allocation on a conservative associative memory: a learned per-instance gate that never loses to always-full and saves ~5× on easy workloads, with distribution-free (LTT) refusal certificates."** It must state plainly that (a) squeeze retries bought nothing at single-unit scale (parked for V3), (b) energy ≈ readout-margin as a gate feature (claim the *mechanism + certificates*, not energy superiority), and (c) modern Hopfield dominates on accuracy at this scale — so the head-to-head is *risk management*, not accuracy, or moves to a Hopfield-erring regime. This is a smaller but *bulletproof* story.

**V3 (the lattice — ICLR anchor).** The strongest honest claim is **"one joint Hamiltonian whose stability, conservation, and memory guarantees survive width/depth scaling *by construction*, with communication that has a measurable mass (pricing law measured to 6 decimals) and native O(1)-memory reversible training."** The open flank is *interference at scale* (the NTK firewall is proven but unmeasured) and *the mass hierarchy is designed-in, not emergent* — V3 must present banding as a **design choice with a learnability payoff** (banded > uniform, needs a seed sweep), never as self-organization.

---

# ICLR gap analysis — what a rockstar long paper still lacks

**Claims resting only on synthetic/laptop evidence (→ the industrial eval):** every V2 retention law (SO(2) toy, dim 4), the trash-region Pareto (perturbed sine/lemniscate, 3 seeds), the lattice pricing law (2-unit toy), banded>uniform (1 seed). None has touched real data. **Recommendation:** run the F2 harness (`f2-eval-harness.md`) on **voraus-AD** (robot arm = literally Hamiltonian; headline), **SKAB** (iteration), **TEP** (label-perfect scale), with **SMD as negative control**; **VUS-PR primary, point-adjust forbidden, statistical baselines mandatory, unit-level splits** (binding F2 rules). This is the single biggest gap: the physics is proven, the *machine-learning benefit on real workloads* is not.

**Missing baselines (mapped to threads):**
- **V2:** the theory's neighbors are un-run head-to-heads. Add **coRNN / LEM** (oscillatory/Lipschitz RNNs — the closest "stable long-horizon RNN" prior art) and **Mo's own S¹ task** (his protocol on his setup vs ours — we have the overdamped-face result but not his task) to convert "we differ from Mo in principle" into "we beat/complement Mo on his own bench."
- **V1:** **capacity-stressed / noisy-cue modern Hopfield** (kv≥128, vocab≫d) — the *only* regime where the abstention head-to-head is winnable; also entropy-cascade / CALM-style early-exit baselines for the allocation curve.
- **V3:** **RevNet / Momentum-ResNet** for the O(1)-memory claim; an EWC/replay continual-learning baseline for the interference-firewall / sequential-retention eval (Thread-7 ICLR axis).

**Levers untested at scale (→ concrete runs):** (1) **banding on real data** (does the learnability prior hold beyond the 1-seed toy? — seed sweep + industrial task); (2) **fdt-under-banding** (the `fdt` flag is cosmetic only until a mass hierarchy exists — test it *after* banding, per `generative-studies.md §60`); (3) **reversible O(1) memory** measured (memory-vs-depth curves, not just "preserved by design"); (4) **interference NTK $\Theta(q_A,q_B)$** — single big unit vs modular lattice at matched params (F5 §6 measurement slot, defined but unrun); (5) **adaptive-K trash-region spawning** (locus discovery was 2/6 — the queued cure); (6) **squeeze retries at V3 multi-shell scale** (the parked V1 mechanism's only remaining shot).

---

# Shortcomings, plainly

- **Single-seed items** that are evidence, not claims: banded > uniform (`v3-lattice-build §5`); trash-region locus discovery (2/6); the original v1-l0-gate before the 5-seed pivot.
- **Laptop scale throughout.** Every result in this ledger is CPU, ≤ a few minutes wall. Nothing has run on CSF3/A100. The runbook exists (`csf3-runbook.md`); the scale-up hasn't.
- **Memory results are MQAR-only.** The V1 associative-recall story is one synthetic task; the Hopfield comparison is unwinnable there and the "harder regime" is a plan, not a result.
- **Narrow mass spectra everywhere.** Three independent measurements confirm learned $M$ is near-uniform (max/min ≤ 1.6). Every "mass does X" claim is therefore *about designed banding*, and banding's payoff is 1-seed.
- **Erosion horizon.** Designed vacua survive only to ~150–300 epochs before sleep-phase CD destroys them; the long-training regime is currently *hostile* to the V2 mechanism until the anchoring fix lands.
- **No positional equilibrium in generative sampling.** Conv/Deep potentials are non-coercive in-unit; free-run chains exit the trained region. Generative and energy-anomaly claims are valid only for the momentum marginal on current checkpoints.
- **The trinity's "destruction" is incomplete alone.** Friction latches but cannot erase coset content (Cor-13 scope) — true selective deletion needs the un-run S2 re-emission / tilt / noise machinery.
- **Two production bugs shaped historical results:** the Lyapunov regularizer was inert for the program's entire history (all $\lambda_{\rm lyap}$ ablations meaningless), and the shipped Langevin sampler never sampled Gibbs (temperatures ~11× off nominal, dt/mass-dependent). Both are fixed behind compat flags; both mean *pre-fix numbers must be read with the flag in mind*.

Audience = skeptical external reader. Drives short positioning (V2/V1/V3 strongest-honest-claim paragraphs) and ICLR eval/baseline choices.

Recommend a numbered §7 entry (parallel to §7.14 candidate) with the fix triplet: exp-d default epochs, Noether $r^*\approx0$ guard, wake-dominant/anchored schedule. Cross-links to the `sleep-erosion-study` task.
- **§8 open questions to promote:** (a) **interference NTK at scale** — F5 §6 measurement is defined but unrun and is now the single biggest V3 credibility gap (add to backlog as blocking for the ICLR anchor). All three unlock together only when V3 banding creates a real hierarchy. Worth a one-line "mass-narrowness is the pivot variable" note in the ICLR unifier framing.

# ⟲ Wave-4 addendum (2026-07-07, Hub) — first running-document update

*Written by the Hub after reviewing all six w4 reports (the ledger's original chapters were authored at w4-launch time, before five of the six reports landed). Sources: `fix-pack-3.md`, `seed-sweeps.md`, `sleep-erosion-study.md`, `v1-hopfield-stress.md`, `v1-wormhole-routing.md` (+ `di-bernardo-skim.md`, reviewed same day). Integrated code state: main @ 154 tests green.*

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 3 (trash region) — evidence upgraded, mechanism completed.** Adaptive-K spawning + compact-support gates now EXIST (`fix-pack-3` items 4–5: spawn at persistent-hallucination density, prune weak holes, exact-zero-beyond-r_k gate closing the tail-leakage retention gap). The pilot's two engineering gaps (locus discovery 2/6, sigmoid tails) both have implemented cures awaiting an S1 re-run. Verdict unchanged (`[validated-with-scope]`), scope now wider.

**Ch. 4 (test-time compute) — pillar 3 lands; verdict strengthens toward [validated-with-scope].**
- **Wormhole routing works** (`v1-wormhole-routing`): gated 0.875/0.812 overall vs 0.50 ceilings for local-only and dense; **direct 1-hop edge beats N−1-hop chain diffusion whose cost SCALES with N (750→1250) while the wormhole stays flat (500)**; gate selectivity AUROC 0.954–0.960; learned head routes perfectly at N=4; bounded gate-energy injection verified (F5 §7.4). Caveats: smooth z-gate mis-routes ~10–12% (raw-R-needs-calibration, third confirmation); learned head over-routes local at N=8 (impostor-composition study queued). V1 now has three pillars: calibrated gate ✓, certified retries (parked), energy-gated routing ✓.
- **The Hopfield regime map is in and honest** (`v1-hopfield-stress`): **no crossover at laptop scale — Hopfield dominant in all 26 cells**; both stress axes break Hopfield (1.0→0.47–0.67) but the CLU memory degrades faster; compute savings collapse 7×→1.3× when everything is hard. **Gap driver isolated: CLU-EBM storage fidelity, not the gate.** Superb for the "when to build with which" figure; converts Ch.4's open flank into a named prerequisite (memory training quality).

**Ch. 5 (lattice) — falsifiable (iii) now POWERED.** `seed-sweeps`: **banded > uniform 5/5 seeds × both budgets** (eval-MSE ratio 0.75@60ep → 0.49@300ep; crossover epoch = 1 — the learnability-prior signature; the optimizer never discovers the 16× hierarchy from uniform init). Confound stated: banding was matched to the data's true timescales — the claim is "a correct designed prior wins and is unreachable by gradient descent," not "any banding helps." **Trained couplings still price communication** (κ_eff extractor exact on reference; μ_rel²=4κ_eff/M to ≤1.2%). Scorecard row 5 moves: banding evidence single-seed → **5/5-seed**; "trained-V_c pricing" open → **validated**.

**Ch. 7 (training dynamics) — the erosion RISK is now a LAW with a cure.** `sleep-erosion-study`:
- **The demarcation:** wake–sleep CD inverts a designed vacuum **iff it has a flat (degenerate) direction unconstrained by the wake objective** — the non-degenerate Exp-B vacuum is IMMUNE (sleep even improves its noise gap). The V2 mechanism and its trainer are in tension *only* along the very flat directions the mechanism prizes.
- **The horizon law:** inversion is governed by **CD-update frequency racing the wake-clamp schedule, independent of chain length** (f1/f5/f20 → ~116/442/959 ep; sleep_steps 50 vs 500 identical to ±1 ep). PCD-vs-CD second-order (≤9 ep).
- **Mechanism quantified:** overlap (22–31% of shell negatives sit in the ring band at init) → raise → expel (negatives' radius 1.45→3.9 as the ring inverts).
- **The cure:** a cheap **V(data) energy anchor (λ=10)** — ring depth flat for 1000 epochs (zero erosion) AND noise gap +0.244, **beating wake-only (+0.199)**: preserves the prior and *improves* the contrastive benefit. Energy-gating on H fails (kinetic energy defeats the gate).
- **NEW silent knob (Ch.7 gains a third dead control):** `sleep_temperature` is a **no-op whenever `sleep_friction=0`** (σ∝√γ) — every historical Exp-A/B/D temperature setting was inert. Alongside the dead Lyapunov regularizer and the non-Gibbs sampler, the count of silently-inert training knobs found by this program is now three.
- Novelty (pending scout confirm): landscape distortion is classical (Fischer–Igel, Nijkamp); the *designed-vacuum inversion*, the *degeneracy-specificity demarcation*, the *frequency-not-work horizon law*, and the *anchor cure* appear novel.

**Ch. 1, 2, 6 — no new measurements; two ripples.** (Ch.1) V2's training recipe should adopt the anchor cure so long-training no longer destroys the object of study; erosion moves from "disclosed caveat" to "characterized + cured" in the short. (Ch.2/6) unchanged; the "mass-narrowness is the pivot variable" coupling stands — now with the banding side *powered* (Ch.5), the fdt-under-banding and S^(M)-under-banding unlocks are one designed-hierarchy experiment away.

# Positioning Charter (added 2026-07-07 — Head-approved; BINDING for all drafting)

> Source: the reviewer-hat critique audit, adopted in full (`.claude/critique_register.md`, Tier 3). These are **writing rules**, enforced at Hub draft review for every short, the ICLR paper, and the F5 arXiv note. The paper-writer agent inherits this section verbatim. Rule IDs cite the register.

**C-1 (G6) — REVERSED by Head, 2026-07-07: no audit confession.** ~~Own the falsifications first~~ — the legacy CHLU paper was a territory-claiming workshop poster; the papers people will evaluate are the shorts and the ICLR long. Rule now: (a) **no defensive audit paragraph in any paper**; (b) shorts describe their *current, fixed* mechanisms precisely (exact flags in provenance tables) and never assert the legacy paper's specific mechanism-numbers as evidence; (c) the class-level theory of the corrected mechanisms (regularizer degeneracy, FDT-consistent noise, anisotropic cap) lives in the **F5 note as neutral theorems** — that is the public record, in the right register; (d) cite J&P 2026 for the primitive's introduction only. Post-rename, the legacy paper points to the legacy CHLU codebase; the current work stands on the CLU codebase.

**C-2 (G1) — Designed testbeds verify; learned systems evidence.** Results on architecturally-invariant potentials with analytic tilts are labeled **verification of the theory's exactness** — never presented as discoveries. The evidence-grade results are the ones on *learned/anharmonic* vacua (laws holding to 2–15% **with the deviations themselves predicted**) and the Mo head-to-head on *trained* models. The constitutive-vs-kinematic contrast goes in the main text, foregrounded, not an appendix.

**C-3 (V2.2) — V2 reads ML-first.** The paper is "what memory in a trained network obeys," with physics as the derivation apparatus. The Mo head-to-head ("a published ML result is our overdamped face") leads the results. If a physics-literate reviewer can summarize the paper as "a damped-oscillator problem set," the draft has failed this rule.

**C-4 (V2.1) — No promissory notes in lead position.** Lead with measured (constitutive law, Mo separation, latch). EFT-of-memory and any unbuilt machinery appear only as horizon/future work until results exist.

**C-5 (G3) — Every generalizing claim carries its scale qualifier.** dim, N units, vocab, seeds, CPU-scale — attached in-sentence, not footnoted. No bare "CLUs provide…". Draft-review checklist item: grep for scope-free plurals.

**C-6 (G5) — Certificate language at honest altitude.** LTT's exchangeability assumption + the measured ECE≈0.10 shift, and Prop-12's compact-set scope, appear in the main text *next to* the claim they qualify. "Cannot destabilize" → "certified within [stated scope]." The fine print must never be invertible into the review.

**C-7 (M4) — Flag-provenance tables are part of the paper.** Every result inherits its training-config table from the agent reports (protocol §5 rule). Cross-section reproducibility is a reviewer path; apparent contradictions between differently-flagged runs must be impossible to construct.

**C-8 (M1/M2) — Hermetic shorts, one common ancestor.** No short cites or assumes another short. Citable common ground = the published CHLU paper + (once live) the F5 arXiv note, cited in third person. The Hub's claims-consistency matrix (built at wave-5 review) is checked against every draft: a reviewer who reads all submissions must find one coherent program, zero contradictions.

**C-9 (Head, 2026-07-07) — Negatives are documented, never dropped.** Every negative/null/refuted result across all verticals is recorded in the negatives registry (`.claude/negative_results.md`, doc-curator maintained); the most prominent negatives go in the **respective papers' appendices** as future-work anchors. A paper that hides its negatives fails Hub review.

**C-10 (Head, 2026-07-07) — Appendix maximalism until the pruning passes.** Main text = main results only; ALL corollaries, robustness checks, negative results, and extra plots go to appendices, fully written — nothing omitted at drafting time (short-paper reviewers aren't mandated to read appendices; the material is logging + ICLR/arXiv feedstock + the debugging record for V3/ICLR-scale runs). Titles and author lists are placeholders until the end. Dedicated pruning runs near each deadline scope the papers tight; cut text is banked for ICLR/follow-ups, never deleted.

# ⟲ Wave-5 addendum (2026-07-07) — the reviewer-proofing wave: all four Tier-1 critiques answered

> Wave-5 existed to answer the adopted critique register's Tier-1 items (`.claude/critique_register.md`) before drafting. All four answered; claims matrix v1.0 now live (`.claude/claims_matrix.md`). Chapter deltas:

**Ch. 2 (mass-as-allocator) — the "mass-narrowness pivot" is RE-SCOPED, its biggest caveat resolved (G4).** The three-time corroboration "learned M never differentiates (~0.08 ceiling)" was a **training-budget artifact**: at 1500 ep the uniform 2-unit lattice orders its masses correctly on 5/5 seeds (Spearman +0.89) even at base lr, and a 10× mass-specific lr amplifies spread to 0.52 (6.5× the ceiling) with eval-MSE 1.85→0.64. But no induced run reaches the designed band (designed fits 7–14× better), and 100× lr overshoots into an ordering-*inverting* global runaway; slow-first curriculum hurt. **Verdict shift: Hyp-3 "designed-in or induced, not awaited" → validated-with-control, sharpened to "ordering is inducible, magnitude must be designed."** Prior narrowness readings re-scope as budget-limited, not fundamental. New confound discipline: MSE rewards the runaway — hierarchy claims must read alignment/spread, never MSE.

**Ch. 4 (V1 / boost-cascade) — the gate's identity question is now answered honestly (V1.1).** The identical calibration/LTT stack on a Hopfield memory reproduces the calibration jump (raw 0.18→0.88 vs CLU 0.43→0.87) — **the gate mechanism is memory-agnostic**, confirming and extending v1-pivot's energy≈margin finding. What does NOT transfer: the *allocation payoff* — it requires an escalatable memory (graded compute with accuracy gains, full>base), which the one-shot Hopfield lacks and the CLU's staged relaxation has. **V1's honest headline: calibrated allocation on escalatable conservative memories + certificates + wormhole routing — never energy-signal superiority (now a forbidden claim, CM-3).**

**Ch. 1 & 5 (V2/V3) — the two missing comparisons now exist, and both favor the physics *structurally* while costing *fit* (G2, V2.3).** (a) Minus-the-physics: volume conservation buys BIBO boundedness (1.0 vs 0.33), a protected near-flat direction (μ² 0.008 vs 0.122), and **vacuum survival under CD training** (broken-volume arm collapses to r*=0 where CLU survives — a new Ch.7 coupling: erosion resistance is itself a symplecticity payoff); leapfrog structure buys the latch (coset drift 0.19 vs twin 1.15); the free twin fits 15× better raw MSE. The Pareto-not-podium frame is now *measured*, not asserted. (b) Learned baselines: coRNN/LEM/LSTM forget a stored phase in 5.6/56/69 map-steps, randomize >1.2 rad, and are perturbation-fragile (LSTM 69→2); CLU-emergent holds 263 bounded steps with the budget law predicting to +12–28% (item-4: the bias is settle-offset for 2/3 seeds, genuine anharmonicity on the softest mode); CLU-designed latches forever. **The structural triad — latch, μ⁻² law, bounded drift — is qualitatively absent in every learned baseline.** Honest gap kept on the record: CLU cannot enter the input-driven task-RMSE axis (no velocity ingestion; equivariant-control wrapper unbuilt).

**Ch. 3 (trash regions) — both queued cures validated at scratch (no code change needed):** adaptive-K spawns 1→8 and fixes locus discovery (2/3 vs pilot 2/6; best learned rejection 0.861); compact gates cut on-manifold leakage ~200× and put the oracle at the γ=0 retention ceiling *while rejecting*. Compact needs placement, adaptive-K provides it — combined arm is the queued next step. One-line engineer fix flagged (`exp_s1_gamma_field.py` doesn't forward `gate=`).

**F5 → citable ancestor:** the arXiv-note draft exists (all 14 checks re-run green; anonymization audit clean; 3 titles for the Head). Its §3–4 constants are now the canonical table every short must cite identically (claims matrix §1).

**Scorecard deltas:** Ch.2 mass-allocator: open-mechanism → **validated-with-control (ordering) / design-required (magnitude)**. Ch.4: 3 pillars → **3 pillars with the identity question answered** (mechanism general, payoff CLU-conditional). Ch.1/5: theory-vs-baselines gap → **closed** (CM-1, CM-4).

**Gap-list updates:** CLOSED — "no non-symplectic control," "no learned baseline," "mass-lr never tried," "gate CLU-specificity unknown." REMAINING (wave-6 targets): interference NTK at scale (P12, V3 gate), router-MLP baseline (P9, blocks CM-7 headline), band-selection recipe (P10, reframed per CM-5), anchor robustness = memory-fidelity intervention (P11+P14), pricing→task prediction (P13), real data (F2, unchanged, still the biggest).

### (curator supplement, wave-6 doc-curator — three genuine gaps in the wave-5 addendum vs the four w5 outputs)
*Coverage check: the Hub's w5 addendum above faithfully covers all four w5 outputs (mass-lr Ch.2; minus-the-physics A/B Ch.1/4/5; v2-prefreeze-baselines V2.3 Ch.1/5 + item-4 anharmonicity + item-3 γ-field extras Ch.3; F5 note). Three load-bearing precisions the addendum's compressed phrasing drops, added here per the ⟲ protocol:*
- **Ch.1/5 — "physics costs fit" carries a mandatory scope qualifier (CM-1) the addendum omits.** The measured cost is specifically **contraction-forbidden** (volume conservation; broken-volume arm recovers ≈2.4× fit by *leaking volume*) — **not** the causal cap, which was **inactive** in the `minus-the-physics` Part-A arm. And the fit gap is **short-horizon**: do NOT claim it persists at long horizon — the fit-vs-horizon crossing is *unmeasured* until `fit-gap-anatomy` (w6) reports. This is the CM-1 scope and the seed of the "two forbidden cheats / paid access" frame (brainstorm Thread 8; now primer §8.4). Reader takeaway: the twin's 15× fit edge is a *loan*, not a verdict.
- **Ch.1 — coRNN is an honest-weak baseline; do not lean on it.** At the fixed default oscillator hyperparameters (γ=ε=1.0, dt=0.1) coRNN is the weak entrant (train-horizon RMSE 0.82 rad); **LSTM/LEM (0.18/0.23 rad) carry the fair comparison** (`v2-prefreeze-baselines §2.1`). The "structural triad absent in every learned baseline" claim (CM-4) rests on the *well-trained* LSTM/LEM, not on coRNN.
- **Doc cross-reference:** charter C-9's negatives registry now **exists** (`.claude/negative_results.md`, 23 entries sweeping w1→w5, tiered A/B/C by paper-appendix prominence). The ledger's per-chapter negatives (retries N1, Hopfield-dominance N2, isotropization N4, erosion N5, mass-not-emergent N7, FDT-attribution N10, PCD-null N11, dead Lyapunov N17, non-Gibbs sampler N18) are indexed there for paper-writer appendix mining.

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 2 (mass-as-allocator) — the sweet-spot GENERALIZES; the "100× inverts" clause is sharpened to ratio-dependence.** `v3-band-selection` item 3 (48 cells, mult∈{3,10,30,100}×N∈{2,4}×ratio∈{4×,16×}, 3 seeds, 1500 ep) confirms CM-5's "≈10× induces ordering" as a **usable default across the whole grid** (align +0.80…+1.00, log-spread 0.44–0.61), not a single-cell artifact. High mult (≥30) is harmful with a **ratio-dependent failure mode**: at 16× it *inverts* the ordering (align −1.00), at 4× it triggers a *global-mass runaway that explodes the fit* (eval-MSE 35.3±47.3) without flipping the order. The CM-5 MSE-trap reproduces (mult=100 lowest MSE 0.332 yet align −1.00) — read align/spread, never MSE. Verdict unchanged (`reframed → doctrine`), sweet-spot now grid-validated.

**Ch. 3 (trash region) — both queued cures now VALIDATED (not just "at scratch"), and γ_φ is definitively the wrong tool for fit.** `fit-gap-anatomy` item 3 supplies the sharp scope: **+γ_φ learned field does NOT recover the fit gap (−24%)** — it is a *targeted-forgetting* mechanism (leakage↓200×), not a fit lever; the stationary vacuum has no trash region to forget. This is the cleanest statement yet of "the paid mechanisms are not interchangeable — match the cheat to the mechanism" (γ_global buys contraction fit at 92% recovery with BIBO/latch/μ² intact; γ_φ buys selective forgetting). Verdict unchanged (`validated-with-scope`, pilot); the composition negative (N12) and erase-scope clause (Cor-13) stand.

**Ch. 4 (test-time compute) — COURSE-CHANGER 1: the physics-free router WINS routing outright; and the regime map is PROVISIONALLY overturned.**
- **Router reversal (`v1-router-baseline`, 5 seeds, CM-7 REWRITTEN):** a 449-param physics-free learned router beats the energy-gated wormhole in **FLOPs AND accuracy at both N and every workload mix** (router 1.000/0.948 @8.81e7 vs gated 0.887/0.715 @1.18e8; the old 2-seed 0.812 was optimistic). **Energy-as-routing-signal is now forbidden** — the *third* instance of the CM-2/CM-3 pattern. Salvaged mechanism: the **1-hop edge is flat in N (1.18e8) where the N-hop chain scales (1.76e8→2.94e8) and loses distant accuracy (0.41→0.28)** — claim the edge, never the energy gating. Caveat: linearly-separable-cue ceiling drives router dominance; harder band untested.
- **Regime-map reversal-in-progress (`anchor-robustness` item 2, CM-8 PROVISIONAL):** the w4 "Hopfield dominant 26/26" map was **under-trained (500 ep)**. At 2000 ep, 3/3 sampled losing cells close completely — CLU-EBM fidelity 0.40–0.91→1.00, gated acc 0.05–0.53→0.99–1.00, matching/beating Hopfield at **9–10× compute savings** (incl. the kv64 "capacity collapse" = budget artifact). **DO NOT SHIP EITHER DIRECTION** — 2 seeds × 2 episodes × 3 cells; full ≥5-seed grid re-map at compute parity is the w7 `regime-remap-2000ep` blocker for V1 drafting.
- **Paid-access theory (`paid-access-theory`, CM-12):** V1's conditional **4th pillar** is now theory-complete — reach/escape dichotomy [proven], det-J=1 intra-unit wormhole + ledger, squeeze-cures-escape/cannot-beat-C_T, latch-transport pᵀXΔ, state-dependent-gate-breaks-volume guard, and the l0-gate-null-doesn't-test-access analysis (prevents a reviewer cross-section contradiction). End-to-end performance is the w7 gate. Verdict unchanged (`reframed`, pivot); the pillar count is now "3 shipped + 1 theory-complete-conditional."

**Ch. 5 (lattice) — COURSE-CHANGER 2 favorable: the interference firewall is MEASURED, pricing is PREDICTIVE, and banding is a METHOD. This is the strongest wave for V3.**
- **Firewall measured (`v3-interference-ntk`, CM-9):** cross-unit CD-update basin displacement R — shared-V_θ monolith ≈0.20 (spatially unstructured, **O(N)**, S=1.38 > self-signal by N=8); modular lattice ≈2e-5 (nearest-neighbour-only, **R_far≡0.0 exactly**, ∝κ² slope 1.99, **mass-independent** banded≡uniform, persists through training, **O(1) in N**). Ratio ≈1:9,000. *"Guarantees survive scaling only because of modularity"* — with the monolith as the measured foil. **Metric discipline: report basin-displacement R, NEVER the raw NTK cosine (≈0.99 for both, uninformative).** This closes the ledger's #1-flagged V3 credibility gap ("interference NTK at scale unrun").
- **Pricing predictive (`v3-interference-ntk` item 2, CM-10):** κ_eff read *blind* from learned coupling curvature predicts recall-horizon ranking (Spearman 1.0) and sync time (≤8%) across a 91× κ_eff decade — registered before measurement. Elevates the w4 "pricing map holds" to "the price list predicts the task."
- **Banding is a method (`v3-band-selection`, CM-11):** the V3.2 "we told the model the answer" confound is killed — mis-banded controls give matched (1.18) < uniform (2.42) < orthogonal (6.92) < anti (12.79), a wrong prior of equal spread worse than none; and a **spectral FFT selector recovers the oracle band exactly (MSE gap 0.000, 5/5 seeds) when timescales are spectrally separable.** Scorecard row 5 "open scale" partially closes: interference now measured; the remaining open flank is *real data* and *N beyond 8*.

**Ch. 7 (training dynamics) — erosion promoted from LAW+cure to the demarcation-as-THEORY, with a characterized anchor envelope.** `anchor-robustness` item 1 (5 seeds, 3000 ep): the V(data)-anchor holds the designed SO(2) vacuum through 3000 erosive epochs for λ∈{1,10,100} (λ=0 dies 5/5), with a **strength↔robustness envelope** — λ≈10 max noise-rejection (gap 1.85) but 1/5 seeds collapse (seed-45 pathology); **λ=100 bulletproof 5/5** (r*=0.911±0.016) at ~35× wake-MSE cost. The demarcation is now **theory**: μ²-witness (flat mode stiffens 0→+0.04–0.21 at λ=0, stays ≈1e-6 with anchor) + **tilt-immunity** (lifting the flat direction by δ≥0.05 immunizes with NO anchor — erosion ∝ flatness) + Exp-B non-degenerate control. And **anchor ⟂ volume conservation**: the anchor does NOT rescue the broken-volume vacuum (diverges regardless, wake 3.5k–176k, r*→∞) — CD-vacuum survival is a symplecticity payoff, the anchor an orthogonal cure (CM-1/CM-6 sharpened). Erosion now warrants primer §7.3. Placement: **V2 Appendix B default, pending Jul-11 novelty confirm.**

**Ch. 1 — the loan is CALLED (CM-1 crossing measured), with an honest boundary.** `fit-gap-anatomy` item 2 (3 seeds, matched ±0.2%): the twin leads 1–2 orders to ~500 steps, **crosses CLU at ~700, and diverges to 196 @5000** vs CLU's bounded 0.20–0.23 plateau — legacy Exp-I made quantitative. **Do claim the crossing + boundedness-by-construction; do NOT claim CLU has the lowest long-horizon plateau** (broken-vol 0.14, LSTM 0.13 < CLU 0.22 — CLU's asset is boundedness, not lowest steady MSE). Fills the wave-5 curator supplement's "crossing unmeasured" flag on CM-1.

**Ch. 4 (test-time compute) — V1's identity is DECIDED, and it is the program's most honest short.** Three waves of "physics buys nothing over a cheap baseline" now stack into one clear self-portrait, and one physics-specific win survives:
- **The three cheap-baseline results (stack, cite once):** the calibration/gate/LTT stack is **memory-agnostic** (w5, CM-2: Hopfield 0.18→0.88 ≈ CLU 0.43→0.87); the energy-gated router **loses to a 449-param physics-free MLP** in FLOPs *and* accuracy (w6, CM-7/N24); and now the CLU-gate is **not even cheaper than Hopfield** — modern Hopfield hits its accuracy ceiling in ~**1 matvec** (0.947–0.979, ≤0.003 over 10× iterations), so the celebrated "9–10× savings" is **intra-CLU** (gate vs full-budget CLU, ≥300 Verlet steps), **not** savings vs Hopfield (w7 `regime-remap-2000ep` Item 3, CM-8 refined, N32). Cost story **FINAL**; the accuracy-reversal (2000 ep closes the w4 "losing" cells) stays small-$n$ and **grid-pending w8** `regime-remap-complete`.
- **The one physics-specific, certificate-backed win — PILLAR 4 PASSED (CM-12 → PASSED).** `paid-access-experiments` (5 seeds, dim 2 & 4, 5 unit tests) met **all four** gate criteria: reach crossover present (squeeze bounded — landing 1→0 past the causal box; wormhole flat ≈1 at all $d$; Newtonian-squeeze control reaches $d>L$, confirming the relativistic cap is the operative constraint); certificates hold (**wormhole $\det J=[1.0]\times6$ & ledger $=[0.0]\times6$ exact**; squeeze $\det S^{(M)}=1.000$, $H_{\rm ratio}\le e^{2\zeta}$ on matched-quadratic H); latch **transported** by the exact bounded $p^\top X\Delta$ (across-coset $\Delta Q=0.25$ err 0.0); and it **beats the no-physics router** (which has no volume certificate) **and the dense/throat-V arm** (which fails reach for $d>L$). *Honest nuance (N34):* the squeeze crossover is a **bracket $[L, L+p_0\sinh\zeta/M_0]$**, not a knife-edge at $L$ (the squeeze also carries a displacement). **Scope: ORACLE placement — learned entrance-steering is out of scope and is "the true engineering crux / likely failure point at scale" (N35).**
- **Head decision (2026-07-08): pillar-4 ADOPTED; V1 SHIPS — as an ML4PS position/theory paper**, leading with the physics win (paid-access certificates) + honest framing; learned entrance-steering = explicit future work, addressed at scale in the ICLR long (Thread-9 MH-kernel is an optional physics-heavy fold that improves the ML4PS fit). **V1's honest thesis is now: *certified mechanisms, not benchmark-topping.*** Verdict on the scorecard moves `reframed → identity DECIDED (position/theory paper; pillar-4 the one physics-specific win)`.

**Ch. 5 (lattice) — reversible O(1)-memory training is MEASURED; ledger backlog #1 discharged.** The adopted Thread-7 systems claim (queued but never run since w2) is done (`v3-reversible-o1`, CM-13): the separable $H=T(p)+V(q)$ + leapfrog is analytically invertible, so γ=0 lattice training admits exact recompute-backwards BPTT — **gradients match standard stored-activation BPTT to ≤2e-6 rel (float32) / ~1e-15 (float64)** at $T\le1024$, **peak activation memory $O(1)$-in-$T$ vs $O(T)$: 6.3 KB vs 6.00 MB at $T=1024,N=2$ → 946×** (940× at N=8), at **≈0.9× wall-time (CPU/small-D only, not GPU-validated)**. **Exact ONLY at γ=0** (N33): γ>0 amplifies reconstruction error as $(1-\gamma)^{-n}$ → finite usable horizon (≈3.3e4 @γ=1e-3 down to 1.3e2 @γ=0.1, float64; ~100× shorter float32). *"Reversibly-O(1)-trainable memory = the conservative (γ=0) memory"* — dovetails the budget framing exactly. Caveat: **not yet wired into `train_chlu`** (gradient mechanics only, untrained model + final-state loss); position against gradient-checkpointing $O(\sqrt T)$. This closes the ledger's ICLR-gap item "reversible O(1) memory measured" and gives V3 its systems headline slot. Verdict row 5 gains `+ reversible O(1) measured (CM-13)`.

**Ch. 1 & 7 — no new science, two process notes.** (Ch.1) `v2-referee` **wired** the measured ≈700-step loan crossing into the V2 draft (MF-2; already CM-1 canonical) and caught a genuine **§3.4↔App D emergent-lifetime contradiction pre-submission** (MF-3: displayed +12–15% vs App D +5.2/+28.8% on a different number set — the M4/CM-7 cross-section defect, caught by the referee stage, N36 process record; fixed in w8 `v2-revision`). ## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 4 (test-time compute) — the CM-8 accuracy story is SETTLED, and it comes with a NEW dominant negative (the noise wall); Thread-9 makes V1 more physics-heavy.**
- **CM-8 SETTLED (`regime-remap-2000ep`, full 198-job grid, 0 failures).** The w6/w7 provisional accuracy-reversal resolves — but **regime-specifically, not as a general win.** The "Hopfield-dominant 26/26" map was an **under-training artifact** (500 ep: gate 0.02–0.31 vs Hopfield 0.95–0.98). At convergence CLU storage fidelity → ≈1.0 and gated accuracy **reverses Hopfield only on clean/correlated cues at kv≤64** (gate 1.00/0.99 vs 0.98/0.97, Δ +0.02, n=8; widens to Δ +0.08…+0.16 at ρ=0.9 **only because Hopfield collapses on correlated keys**, not because CLU rises). The wall beyond kv64 is an **epoch-budget wall, not capacity** (kv96 closes at 4000 ep +0.03; kv128 ties; required epochs scale with kv), and small cells **over-train** (kv32 1.00→0.89 at 4000 ep). **Tally 2000 ep: 6/15 close.** **THE NOISE WALL is the dominant negative and travels with every reversal claim:** under cue noise σ∈{0.3,0.6} **NO cell closes at any capacity, even kv32** (gate 0.36 vs Hopfield 0.71 @σ=0.6) *despite* fidelity ≈1.0 — the relaxation gate is markedly less noise-robust than one matvec (N37). **Cost stays Hopfield's** (~1 matvec, N32) and now **so does noise-robustness.** Net (approved CM-8): *escalatable accuracy under a rationing gate on clean/correlated retrieval at kv≤64; Hopfield keeps both cost and cue-noise-robustness.* This unblocks V1's §4.3 accuracy slot (was `[pending: regime-remap-complete]`).
- **Thread-9 ANSWERED — squeeze+MH is a certified kernel, with two sharp deflations (CM-14 new).** `thread9-mh-kernel` (4 float64 checks): squeeze+MH **is** a π-reversible HMC-family kernel (det J=1 ⇒ no Jacobian; verified L1=0.0095) — but only with sign-symmetrized ζ + momentum refresh (squeeze-only is a reducible one-parameter subgroup, non-ergodic). **Two things bound the pitch:** (i) **the governor destroys the invariant** — the squeeze-then-relax cascade is **Metropolis-within-annealing** converging to a colder MAP-seeking measure (T_eff 1.0→0.61 as γ:0→0.2), *not* Gibbs sampling; (ii) **certified retry erodes the latch** — the Gibbs measure is flat along the coset, so coset-tangent proposals are accepted w.p.1 and random-walk the register (D=½s², N_erode=(Δ_read/s)²; even charge-preserving isotropic squeezes erode coset *position* — N41). Recommended kernel: **½MALA(σ*)+½squeeze, γ=0 in-segment, governor outer, coset-projected** — the FDT-correct σ* (Prop-9) is load-bearing (MALA L1 0.0065 vs unadjusted 0.0995). **Honest deflation (CM-3, 4th instance):** MH's contribution is the **stationarity certificate + explicit latch-erosion accounting**, not parameter parsimony (the useful per-model T re-imports the learned gate) and not — on current evidence — performance. Head decision: **Thread-9 GO** — it makes V1 more physics-heavy (better ML4PS fit); V1 carries an optional fold-slot; the **w9 discriminating experiment is spec'd but on the BACKLOG** (freeze the math, run later).

**Ch. 1 (Goldstone memory) — SF-1/2/3 harden V2's headline laws AND retire an over-claim.** `v2-referee-experiments` (analyst) delivered all three referee runs:
- **SF-3 — the laws survive the cure at long horizon (CM-6 amended).** On anchored λ=100 **3000-ep** checkpoints (3 seeds, past the erosion horizon): GMOR μ²/δ = 1.00000±1.5e-12 over **4.6 decades**, retention slope **−0.956** (pred −1) with floor 27.03, latch machine-flat, **EP onset slope 0.5165 bit-identical to 150 ep**. So the retention/GMOR/EP laws are not artifacts of a short clean run — they hold under the anchored training the paper ships. The erosion cure (N5) is clean to headline.
- **SF-2 — the "≈4× longer retention" is RETIRED as a compute claim (CM-4 amended, N40, tier A).** Per-step CLU Verlet (h64) costs **6.2× LSTM / 3.1× LEM wall** (14–15× FLOPs; two ∇V backprops, not width-matched), so retention-per-compute **inverts** (23.5×/14.6× more wall). **Lead §3.3 with the qualitative structural triad** (latch / μ⁻² budget law / bounded drift) — compute-independent, the stronger claim.
- **SF-1 — the Mo predictor-substitution gap is closed, and a draft number is corrected (N42).** Mo's OWN λ̂(T=128) tracks the retention budget overdamped (corr 0.9995, meas/pred 0.86–1.03) and fails ballistically past EP (0.30 @δ=4). The 44% λ̂ deviation is a **near-EP transient (gap·T≈3.1)**, not gap·T≲0.1 as the draft stated — writer must fix the attribution.

**Ch. 5 (lattice) — no new science; the modular-interference related-work bib gap is CLOSED.** `scout-modular-interference` returned **CLEAR at the specific-claim level, CROWDED at the neighbourhood level.** The two sharp foils to name in V3 §4: **Doan et al. 2021** (interference *measured* but as a data-dependent NTK-*overlap* on a monolith, not a coupling-law) and **Boopathy et al. 2025** (modularity ⇒ *sample-complexity* separation, not a *cross-talk* separation). CLU's defensible novelty is the **conjunction** none owns: a *measured* cross-module interference **kernel** with a **∝κ² coupling-law**, **exact-zero off-graph**, **O(1)-vs-O(N)-in-width**, on a **physics-structured lattice**. 11 verified refs + a lift-ready guard-railed paragraph delivered (metric discipline: report basin-displacement R, never the NTK cosine ≈0.99).

**Drafts — all three shorts now exist and build.** V1 debuts (`v1-short-draft`; ML4PS position shape, C-1 REVERSED — no audit ¶; pillar-4 leads; 2 pending slots [CM-8 accuracy now fillable, Thread-9 optional] + 5 editorial Qs). # ⟲ Wave-9 + Wave-10 addendum (2026-07-09, doc-curator — standing ledger-addendum duty)

> Waves 9 and 10 are the **revise → referee** waves, and they are folded together here because **neither produced new science**: w9 was three paper-writer revisions + a headline figure; w10 was three adversarial referee passes + a banding figure. **No code either wave** (main stays `37dc664`, 194 green, clean). What changed is **what we are entitled to say** — two headline sentences were falsified *from our own appendix data* by our own referees, and one contribution was found to be **definitional rather than demonstrated**. Matrix → **v1.5** (CM-9 UNDER RE-SCOPE; CM-4 misprediction canonical). **All three shorts: weak-accept.** Written from handover §10 (the 2026-07-08/09 WAVE-9 and WAVE-10 review entries) + the w9/w10 outputs {`v1-revision`, `v2-revision-2`, `v3-revision-2`, `v3-scaling-figure`, `v1-referee`, `v2-referee-2`, `v3-referee`, `v3-banding-figure`}. ⟲ protocol respected: chapters untouched; deltas appended. Companion numbers now in `HEP_primers.md` (§3.3 CM-4 canonical, Part IX CM-9 re-scope, §10.3 ledger), `negative_results.md` (**N43–N45**), and — first curator pass — `future_work.md` (V3 referee-named interference channels; V1 certificate-payoff; V2 width-matched per-step).
>
> **The one-line epistemic lesson of these two waves:** *our referee stage now catches, pre-submission, exactly the class of defect our matrix was built to prevent* — and in all three cases the defect was **an over-strong verbal framing sitting on honest, reproducing numbers.** Zero number-mismatches were found in V1 (every claim traced to the digit); the failures were **claims, not data.**

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 4 (test-time compute → V1) — the position paper is honest and internally consistent; its central *comparative* claim is definitional (N45).**
- **V1 referee = weak-accept.** Every quantitative claim spot-audited **reproduces its source report to the digit** (a 12-row trace table: reach battery, gate AUROC/4.8×/LTT, router, the 198-job regime map, MH-kernel constants); scale qualifiers in-sentence (C-5); certificate fine print next to its claim (C-6); **zero mismatched/unbacked/scope-widened numbers.** Charter-clean, matrix-clean, CM-3 not slid into even in hedged form.
- **The sharpest finding (F3 → N45, tier B): "the wormhole beats a no-physics router" is DEFINITIONAL, not demonstrated.** Both arms land at rate **1.0 at every distance**; the wormhole's only edge is the **det J=1 label the router lacks** — a label with **no shown downstream consequence anywhere in the paper**. Meanwhile the *same* 449-param router **beats the energy-gated edge in both FLOPs and accuracy** (1.000/0.948 @8.81e7 vs 0.887/0.715 @1.18e8; CM-7/N24). The certificate's value (BIBO, latch preservation) is **argued (App B), never measured as a failure the router incurs.** Referee: *the one addition that lifts V1 above weak-accept* is a **measured guarantee-violation** — router erases the latch / blows up BIBO where the wormhole preserves it. → **`v1-certificate-payoff` (w11)**, with the instruction to **check the `paid-access-experiments` logs first** (may be a wiring note, not an experiment).
- **Why this matters epistemically (and why it is not a retraction).** The certificates themselves verify exactly (det J=[1.0]×6, ledger=[0.0]×6, latch ΔQ=0.25=pᵀXΔ err 0.0) — **CM-12 is untouched.** What N45 exposes is that a *landing-rate* observable **cannot express a volume-certificate advantage**: on this testbed the physics can only tie. This is a **new flavour of the program's recurring deflation** — the earlier four (N3/CM-3 energy≈margin, N21, N24 router-wins, N41 MH-parsimony) are "a cheap baseline **equals or beats** the physics signal"; N45 is "the physics receipt is **real and exact**, and its **consequence is unmeasured**." Fifth instance, distinct mechanism. State once, cite.
- **Craft failures that make the paper read weaker than its honesty warrants** (all in-scope fixes): **F1** the headline figure draws wormhole/router/Newtonian as three indistinguishable flat lines at 1.0 — *the visual opposite of the thesis*; **F2** the foregrounded noise wall (N37) appears in **no figure** while two figures show the favourable clean axis ("you foreground your worst result in prose and bury it in an appendix table"); **F4** §5's MH block is one ~400-word wall; **F5** the BIBO-coercive-exit caveat sits in App B, not next to its §3.1 claim (the one C-6 placement gap); **F6** the "4.8×" is **kv16-only**, sold as "the easy band"; **F7** an internal source-report section number ("§7.1") leaked into a figure title.
- **w9 delivered the substance the referee then audited:** `v1-revision` filled both slots — §4.3/App C.4 with **CM-8 SETTLED and the noise wall foregrounded in main text** (0/6 under cue noise; *"the gate rations clean retrieval, noise-robustness is Hopfield's"*), plus the intra-CLU cost caveat attached to **every** accuracy sentence; and §5's **CM-14 certified-kernel design-rule** subsection (*"even the certified retry carries a receipt: coset diffusion"*), with "no runs on trained CLU checkpoints are claimed" stated explicitly.

**Ch. 1 (Goldstone memory → V2) — near-final; two number-hygiene defects, both of the M4 class the matrix exists to prevent.**
- **V2 referee-2 = weak-accept, "submission-ready modulo two cheap MUST-FIX + pruning + the F5 arXiv id."** The **entire prior punch-list is CLOSED** (MF-1…5, SF-1…6): audit-¶ deleted, ≈700 crossing + recovery ladder wired with the correct non-overclaim, the §3.4↔App D contradiction (N36) resolved by explicit attribution, figures embedded, F5 note arXiv-clean with the Cor-3 de-anon clause cut. Positioning discipline judged **"well above venue median."**
- **MF2-A → N43: the "5×" is a MISLABEL, not a result.** The abstract and §3.2 claimed misprediction "up to **5×**" while the §3.2 table, Fig 2 and Fig 2b captions bottom at **0.309 / 0.31 / 0.30 at δ=4 (≈3.2×)** — and the **full 14-δ sweep contains no deeper underdamped row** (censored rows are the *overdamped* side). Traced to **`v2-full-runs` line 79**, which annotates the `0.309±0.012` row as "*(5× failure, calculable direction)*." The **genuine 5× exists only on the exact analytic map** (F5 check (k), γ=0.1: ratio 0.19 = 5.3×) — a different γ and a deeper δ than any trained run. **CM-4 now carries the canonical form** (≈3.2× trained / ≈5× exact-map-only; **quote both or neither**). *Provenance-hygiene lesson:* the number lived in a **parenthetical aside** of a source report — a locus the flag-provenance rule (which governs tables) does not cover.
- **MF2-B: the recovery-ladder wiring created a NEW cross-section contradiction** (the same M4 class as MF-3 last wave, introduced *by* fixing MF-2). §3.4's table shows CLU **0.190** vs twin **0.0128** (≈15×, `minus-the-physics`, seeds 42–44 @`b41410f`), while the abstract/App-G say licensed damping "recovers **92%** of it" — but App-G's ladder is a **different experiment** (`fit-gap-anatomy`, seeds 0–2 @`9a13455`, wake-only) with a **different twin (0.0047)** and a +γ=0.05 unit at **0.0216**. Three constructible reader traps: the §3.4 CLU is **unlabelled γ=0**; two twins/two gaps are cross-quoted; and **"92% of the gap" is absolute-MSE** — the +γ unit still trails the twin **~4.6× by ratio**. Fix is labels + one non-comparability caveat + "92% of the **absolute** gap (still ~5× by ratio, **bounded by construction**)." **No experiments.** → `v2-revision-3` (w11).
- **The honest-but-quotable residue (accepted, not a defect):** App-H's own compute-inversion numbers ("**23.5×** the wall, **54.8×** FLOPs per unit of retention") are quotable *against* the retention contribution. This is the right trade — the fragile "4×" (N40) was retired for the robust qualitative triad — and §3.3 now leads with the triad, which is the compute-independent claim.

**Ch. 5 (lattice → V3) — the headline was a two-point scaling claim (N44); the *result* is real, the *framing* was not. This is the wave's most consequential correction.**
- **V3 referee = weak-accept, "borderline → REJECT if MF-1 is left as written."** Meta-review otherwise strong: the short **answers the standing V3.1 attack** (interference NTK unmeasured — V3's own named firewall) with a **measured foil** (parameter-matched shared-potential monolith) rather than an assertion, and its **metric discipline is exemplary** (pre-empts the 0.99-NTK-cosine cheat by name, reports basin-displacement R). Scope qualifiers, negatives, flag-provenance all present.
- **MF-1 → N44: "O(1)-in-N vs O(N)" is a 2-point claim whose two points SHARE A SLOPE.** Measured at **exactly N∈{4,8}**: modular S **6.79e-5 → 1.74e-4 (×2.56)**; monolith **0.635 → 1.384 (×2.18)** — the "O(1)" curve grew **faster in relative terms** than the "O(N)" one; **R_nn itself doubles** (4.53e-5 → 9.92e-5) with non-overlapping seed error bars. On the log-y headline figure (which `v3-scaling-figure` just delivered) this renders as **two roughly-parallel lines four decades apart**. *"Flat in N" is false as an empirical growth-rate statement* — and it is falsifiable **from our own Appendix A.**
- **The defensible core is STRUCTURAL, and it is strong:** (a) **R_far ≡ 0.0 exactly** ⇒ per-unit received interference is bounded by the chain **coordination number (≤2), independent of N**; (b) **~4 decades absolute separation** (≈2e-5 vs ≈0.20; 1:9,000); (c) ∝κ² (slope 1.99), mass-independent (banded≡uniform), persists through training. **Ship:** *"structurally O(1): interference confined to graph neighbours (R_far≡0 exactly), coordination-bounded, four decades below an unconfined monolith."* A **scoped growth claim survives only if** `v3-interference-extra` (N∈{2,6} + **block-structured-monolith control**) returns **divergent slopes**. **CM-9 → ⚠ UNDER RE-SCOPE; wording finalizes at w11.**
- **The conceptual point worth keeping (primer Part IX, w10 block):** we measured a **support property of the kernel** (exact block-zero off the coupling graph — exact, N-independent) and wrote it up as an **asymptotic growth exponent** (a claim needing several N-decades). *Locality is not scaling.* The distinction is the same one that separates "the propagator has finite range" from "the correlation length scales as…" — structure vs phenomenology.
- **Also caught (SF-1):** *"the monolith interferes with itself more than it stores"* (S=1.38>1) **reifies a normalization reference line** — S=1 is where summed cross-unit ΔF equals one unit's own ΔF, **not a measured storage capacity**. Licensing the storage gloss requires the (unmeasured) **dynamical interference half-life**. Soften; cross-ref App C.
- **Assets landed:** `v3-scaling-figure` (headline Fig 1, per-unit S_B vs N, 3-seed error bars, monolith crossing S=1 between N=4 and 8) and `v3-banding-figure` (Fig 2: matched **1.180±0.216** < uniform **2.416±0.345** < orthogonal **6.924±1.883** < anti **12.791±0.698**; FFT-selector–oracle gap **0.000**, 5/5 seeds). **Note the ordering constraint:** the scaling PNG **visually exposes MF-1** — the reframe must land **before or with** the figure swap.

**Drafts — all three shorts are now drafted → refereed → revising, all three weak-accept.** V1 (v0.2, 335 KiB, both slots filled) · V2 (v0.3, 596 KiB, 5 figs, all SF-1/2/3 folded) · V3 (v0.3, 530 KiB, 4 figs, modular bib spliced with 10 verified refs; Doan/Boopathy the named foils).

## Positioning/gap ripples
- **The referee stage is now demonstrably load-bearing, and it is catching a *class*, not incidents.** Across w8→w10 it has caught: a genuine §3.4↔App D contradiction (N36), a mis-attributed cause (N42), a mislabelled headline constant (N43), a two-point scaling claim (N44), a definitional comparative (N45) — and, notably, **a new contradiction introduced by the previous wave's own fix** (MF2-B, spliced in while closing MF-2). **All five are framing/number-hygiene defects sitting on numbers that reproduce.** The program's data discipline is holding; its *prose* discipline is what the referees keep correcting. (Curator note: this is an argument for the matrix's canonical-wording mechanism, and for extending flag-provenance discipline to **prose asides in source reports** — N43's origin.)
- **V1's self-portrait, sharpened once more.** The Head's "certified mechanisms, not benchmark-topping" framing survives the referee — indeed the referee calls the candor "its strongest scientific asset at these venues" — but the referee also states the hostile core plainly: *"the physics buys a demonstrated, CLU-specific advantage only where the authors control the testbed."* Three of five contributions are boundaries/negatives on learned memories. **N45 is the precise, fixable version of that complaint**, and `v1-certificate-payoff` is the one experiment that could answer it. **The M3 portfolio question (Head-owned, still not adjudicated here) is unchanged in shape but sharper in evidence.**

- **V3 is the wave's lesson.** It did the single most important thing a paper can do — answered its own named credibility attack with a **measured foil** — and then **overstated the answer by one word ("flat")**. The fix costs nothing scientifically (the structural claim is *stronger*, being exact rather than asymptotic) and everything rhetorically (the most quotable sentence goes). **Take the structural framing; it is the true one.**
- **Charter/citation:** C-1 holds across all three drafts; C-8 hermetic (V3 cites only J&P 2026 + the theory note); C-9 registry now at **N45**; C-10 appendix-maximalism unchanged — with three referee-flagged **pruning** debts (V1 Fig 3 + §5 MH block; V2 §3.1 enumerations + triple-reported lifetimes; V3 §3.5/§3.6 → appendix-forward), all correctly deferred to the post-Jul-11 pruning pass when venues and page limits are known.

The w11/w12 science (`f5-corrigendum` — F5 unblocked, and a **retracted number**; `f1-gmor-condensate` — CM-15 GMOR proper; `t-lever-forgetting` — CM-16, V5 conditional-GO; `v1-revision-2`; `v3-revision-3`) reached the running log and the matrix but **never reached this ledger.** Flagged as curator debt, not silently backfilled — the ⟲ protocol forbids rewriting, and a fabricated retrospective addendum would be worse than an honest gap. **The Hub should either commission a w11–12 catch-up addendum or record the gap as accepted.**
>
> **Wave-13 is the heaviest evidence wave since w6, and it is a wave of *corrections*.** Two proven code defects (one ⛔ **blocking**), one proven theory defect (a **no-go**, not a bug), **two dictionary errors in a thesis the Hub itself wrote**, a **wrong number this program had been quoting and propagating**, and — against that backdrop — a clean **V5 GO** and an exact XY reduction. Written from handover §10 (2026-07-10 WAVE-13 review) + the w13 outputs {`v5-gate`, `xy-lattice-theory`, `v2-symmetry-deepdive` v1.1 §7bis, `fix-pack-5`, `v2-revision-4`} + `claims_matrix.md` **v1.8**. ⟲ protocol respected: **chapters untouched; deltas appended.** Companion numbers now in `HEP_primers.md` (new **§4.9**, **§5.3**, **§8.6**; §4.4/§5.2 corrected in place; §10.3 ledger +9 rows), `negative_results.md` (**N46–N51**; **N10 re-opened**, **N18 amended**, **N4's "2.6" retracted**), and `future_work.md` (Thread-10 rebuilt; §7bis boundaries folded; V5 = GO).
>
> **The one-line epistemic lesson of this wave:** ***instrument validity precedes inference.*** We have now twice inferred physics from a sampler we had not validated — and the second time, we had already drawn a **tier-A negative** from the broken instrument. The defects were not in our reasoning; they were in the derivative nobody tested.

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 5 (mass-as-allocator → V3) — the slogan acquires a candidate conservation law, and it is now under test.**
- **R5, the causal retention–bandwidth bound**, is the most consequential *positive* theory result of the wave. Overdamped retention gives `n₁/₂ ∝ F²` while the causal cap gives bandwidth `θ̇_max = c√m₀/F ∝ 1/F`, so **the decay constant cancels from the product**:
  `n₁/₂ · θ̇_max² = 2γ ln2 · m₀c² / ((2−γ) ε² · δΣ)` — **independent of `M` and `r*`.**
  Verified on the exact damped relativistic map (`M ∈ {0.5,1,2,4}`): products **681.7 / 696.0 / 702.9 / 706.4** vs predicted **709.7**, with the residual *exactly* the deep-overdamped correction `(εμ/γ)²` (deficits 3.95/1.94/0.96/0.48% vs 4.00/2.00/1.00/0.50%).
- **Why this promotes the chapter's verdict.** Ch. 5 has carried "**M is the budget allocator**" as a *doctrine* (reframed at w4, sharpened at w5 by `mass-lr-doctrine-test`): a design rule with measured consequences but **no invariant**. R5 supplies the invariant's candidate: *a register's robustness × its bandwidth² is fixed by the causal budget `m₀c²` over the forgetting drive.* **`F` buys robustness and pays in bandwidth; only raising `c` (or `m₀`), or lowering the breaking, buys both.**
- **The honest half, and it is half the result.** ⚠ **In Newtonian mode `θ̇` is unbounded ⇒ there is no bandwidth constraint and no bound. This trade-off is *created by the causal governor*.** So the "conservation law" is a **relativistic-mode statement** — and every trained checkpoint this program owns is `newtonian_learned`. The bound is therefore **THEORY-ONLY on toy exact maps** and **untested on any trained model.** → `rb-bound-trained` (F-12, Head-promoted to w14). **The Newtonian null is not a control; it is the boundary of the claim.**
- **Scorecard consequence:** the chapter's *mechanism* verdict is unchanged (**validated**); its *scale* verdict is unchanged (**open**). What is new is a **third axis** — a candidate **conservation law**, status `THEORY-ONLY → TASKED`. Do not upgrade Ch. 5's verdict until `rb-bound-trained` reports.

**Ch. 7 (training-dynamics / instrument validity) — ⭐ the wave's defining lesson, and it will happen again.**
- **A second, independent sampler defect was found *after* the first was fixed, and *after* a tier-A negative had been drawn from the broken instrument.** The sequence is worth writing down exactly, because the *shape* is the lesson:
  1. **w1–w2:** N18 — the shipped Langevin never sampled Gibbs (per-mode `T_eff`, ~11× off nominal). Mechanism proven (F5 Prop-9), fix derived (`σ*ᵢ`).
  2. **w3:** N10 — *"the FDT violation does NOT drive the MNIST imbalance"* — concluded by comparing `legacy` vs scale-matched `fdt` **on Exp-C checkpoints**. Registered **tier A**.
  3. **w12:** a *third* defect (`effective_mass()` ignores `tie_channel_mass`) → `fix-pack-5` → ff-merged at **`d6f8bac`**, suite **217 green**, contamination audit: **zero shipped results contaminated** (four independent lines, matching the Hub's own cross-check).
  4. **w13:** the theorist proves (R8) that **Exp-C is `relativistic`**, and that **no `σ` whatsoever** gives the coded relativistic Langevin a Gibbs invariant — the O-step is an autonomous *linear* OU recursion (stationary law exactly **Gaussian**) while the relativistic Gibbs marginal is **Maxwell–Jüttner** (exponential tails). ⇒ **Both arms of N10's comparison were non-Gibbs samplers**, compared with a **variance-level instrument blind to a tail-shape error.** At Exp-C's operating point `T/(m₀c²) = 1`, `KL(MJ‖Gauss) = 0.384 nats` and `Var_MJ/(M_eff T) = 2.6995`.
  5. **Independently, the same wave:** ⛔ the `fdt` noise has a **NaN gradient at γ=0** — so **no FDT-correct model has ever been trainable at repo defaults.** The "correct" sampler was, for its entire existence, *unusable* in training.
- **The epistemic content, stated as a rule rather than an anecdote.** *A negative result about a mechanism is only as valid as the instrument that measured it, and an instrument's validity is a **separate empirical question** that must be settled **before** the inference, not after.* We treated "the sampler is fixed" as a *property of the code* (it was — for Newtonian modes) rather than as *a property of the experiment we ran it in* (it was not — Exp-C is relativistic). **N10 is therefore re-opened: UNDER RE-TEST, not refuted, and not deleted (C-9).** → `relativistic-gibbs-expc` (F-9), cheap, checkpoints exist, and the fix if it lands is one free config line.
- **Why it will happen again, and the cheap prophylactic.** Both w13 code defects are the **same defect in different clothing**: **the value was right and the derivative was wrong.** `sqrt(0)` has a correct *value* and a NaN *gradient* (N47); `GatedCoupling` has a correct *energy* and a sign-reversing *force* (N48 — the shipped wormhole **repels its endpoints** at `v < 0.8020`). Both survived because every test checked a **forward value**. N51 is the same failure one level up in abstraction: an **estimator** whose central value is quotable and whose **bias** silently carries the claim. **Standing prophylactic, cheap, and this chapter's contribution to the program's method: *test the derivative of everything you gate with, the gradient of everything you differentiate, and the bias of everything you fit.*** Budget one matched control **in the arm you are not testing** — that is precisely what caught N51.
- **Chapter verdict:** unchanged in letter (**[refuted]** A, retroactively / **[characterized null]** C / **[validated as risk]** B), but the chapter now owns a **fourth, generalized finding**: *instrument-validity failures are this program's dominant error class, they are not detected by the referee stage (which reads prose), and they are cheap to detect and expensive to inherit.* Where the w9/w10 addendum concluded *"our data discipline is holding; its prose discipline is what the referees keep correcting"* — **w13 corrects that: our data discipline was holding only where the instrument was valid, and we did not know which instruments were valid.**

**Ch. 6 (sampler thermodynamics / FDT / Prop-9) — the mechanism sharpens into a no-go; the attribution verdict is suspended.**
- **Prop-9 → Prop-9′.** F5's `σ*ᵢ = √(M_eff,i T γ(2−γ))` is *"the exact discrete-FDT noise"* — **for the Newtonian kinetic modes and those only.** In `relativistic` mode **no `σ` exists** (proof: a linear OU recursion driven by Gaussian noise has an exactly Gaussian stationary law; Maxwell–Jüttner is not Gaussian). Verified two ways: dynamically (`Var(q)/(T/k) − 1` = **−0.0036** Newtonian at *every* T, the `O(ε²)` shadow floor, vs −0.073/−0.115/−0.313/−0.538/−0.729 relativistic at `T/(m₀c²)` = 0.056/0.1/0.5/2/8 — and the two configs sharing `T/(m₀c²)=8` give **bit-identical** −0.7290074, so **the defect is a function of `T/(m₀c²)` alone**), and on the real lattice code path (`⟨cosΔθ⟩/Gibbs` = 1.006/1.040/**1.134**, **drift ≈ 0** ⇒ a genuinely different stationary measure, not a transient).
- **The subtlety this chapter must never get backwards:** the **exact Gibbs measure is relativity-insensitive** — the momentum integral factorises for any `T(p)`, so the configurational marginal is exactly `e^{−V/T}` regardless of kinetic mode or mass matrix. ***The failure is in the sampler, not in the thermodynamics.*** (Corollary, independently useful: **the inertial mass matrix and the kinetic mode do not affect the equilibrium statistics at all.** `tie_channel_mass` is a *dynamical* — Noether — requirement, not a thermodynamic one.)
- **Verdict move:** *[validated] mechanism / **[refuted] attribution*** → *[validated, and strengthened to a no-go] mechanism / **[attribution UNDER RE-TEST]***. The chapter's original framing — *"the mechanism is exactly right and the attribution is wrong"* — is now *"the mechanism is exactly right, sharper than we knew, and **we never had the instrument to judge the attribution.**"*
- **As with the first corrigendum, the corrected statement is stronger than the false one:** *"no `σ` exists"* is a sharper theorem than *"here is `σ*`."* F5 is re-blocked pending Prop-9′ (→ `f5-corrigendum-2`); the Head's parking of the arXiv push bought exactly the time this required.

**Ch. 1 (Goldstone memory → V2) — the register is an *architectural* object; CM-16 splits in two, and the split is the result.**
- **❌ The `T=0` face (Cor-13 / CM-16(a)) does NOT generalize.** On emergent (MLP) checkpoints the coset is not a near-Goldstone — it is a **mid-spectrum massive mode**, only **1.7–4.9× softer than the *stiffest* direction in the whole spectrum** (`1−|λ_coset|` = 2.963e-3 / 1.060e-3 / 2.780e-3 vs designed −6.7e-16 ⇒ **~12 orders**). Any written `δ ∈ {0.1, 0.3, 0.5}` rad relaxes **completely** (`|retained| ≤ 2.1e-3`); the designed net retains `δ` exactly. Capacity: **1–1.6 bits** (2–3 washboard minima), not a continuum. **⇒ "CHLU's flat directions" do not emerge; they must be designed in.** (N46; Hyp-3 again, on a new axis.)
- **✅ The unification (the deepest CM-16 claim) generalizes *exactly*.** The emergent `n₁/₂(γ)` is the predicted **V-curve**: minimum at **0.902 ± 0.003 × γ_crit**, log-slopes **−1.0020 ± 0.0003** below and **+1.116 ± 0.011** above, **3/3 seeds** — *one damping-optimum curve across **eleven orders of magnitude** in `μ²` (1.7e-12 → 7e-2), the designed coset being its `μ→0` corner.* **This is now the strongest, most portable claim in CM-16**, and it is an *emergent-arm* result, not a designed-only one.
- **✅ The `T>0` face generalizes above a crossover `T* ≈ 3e-3`** (`∂n₁/₂/∂γ > 0` in **10/10** emergent conditions; bias-corrected `n₁/₂^obs/n₁/₂^Goldstone` = 2.75, 3.40 at `T=2e-3` → 0.94–1.25 at `T ≥ 4e-3`). Below `T*` the pseudo-Goldstone mass **protects** the register; above it, the mass is thermally invisible.
- **A conjecture of our own, cleanly falsified.** The task predicted the γ-slope *"flips back to negative below `T*`"*. **It does not, and structurally cannot,** for a register written **at** the vacuum: the restoring force *protects* it, so both channels give `∂n₁/₂/∂γ > 0`. The negative slope belongs to a register written **off** the vacuum — where it is exactly the V-curve's `γ < γ_crit` branch. **Two observables, two signs. A "sign flip vs γ" figure that does not name the initial condition is meaningless.** *(This is a good failure: we predicted, we measured, the prediction was wrong for a reason we now understand.)*
- **⚠ And the finding that nearly cost us a false claim (N51).** A **matched designed control on the same (γ,T) grid** — an arm the task never asked for — shows the **raw exponents are not discriminating**: designed gives `d log n₁/₂/d log T` = −0.534/−0.599/−1.044 and `d log n₁/₂/d log γ` = +0.777/+0.633/+0.546, i.e. **the same shallow slopes as emergent.** Those deviations are CM-16(d)'s `ℓ_θ/Δ` boundary-layer bias (reaching **2.03** on that grid), present in *both* families. **Any designed-vs-emergent claim quoted from raw exponents would have been an artifact.** Standing rule, now binding on every draft, table and caption: ***never quote `n₁/₂` without `Δ` and `ℓ_θ/Δ`***; the `D̂` estimator is the unbiased instrument.
- **Matrix action the curator cannot take:** **CM-16 must be formally split** — (a) designed-only; unification + `T>0` face universal. Matrix v1.8 anticipates it (*"CM-16 will need a further split at w14 review"*). **Until the Hub splits it, no draft may cite CM-16 as a single claim.**

**Ch. 3 (trash-region / trinity) — the friction hole is a *refrigerator*, and a number we were quoting is wrong by 7.94×.**
- **The correction, and it propagated.** `t-lever-forgetting` §7 predicted a `γ_φ` friction hole is a **≈13.9× memory vault**; that number entered the handover running log. **It is wrong** — it assumed the hole is a **locally thermalized bath**. The shipped `FrictionField` is **absorb-only**: it damps `p` but does **not** rescale the FDT noise (`p ← (1−γ_φ)(1−γ)p + σ(γ)ξ`, `σ²` built from the *scalar* γ). Hence `T_local = T·γ(2−γ)/(γ_eff(2−γ_eff))` and **`D_θ ∝ γ_eff⁻²`** (not `γ_eff⁻¹`), so **`n₁/₂ ∝ γ_eff²`** and the vault is `(γ_eff/γ)²`. ***A friction hole is a brake AND a refrigerator.***
- **Superseded number, named** (per ⟲ protocol): ~~"≈13.9× vault"~~ → **107.77 ± 4.78×** (D̂-based, 3 seeds; pre-registered **110.25×**). The coupled-bath hypothesis (**13.88×**) is **rejected by a factor 8.11 ± 0.37** against its own dedicated control — a scalar `γ = 0.525` at the *same* `γ_eff`, no field, which measures **13.28 ± 0.12×** and leaves `Var(p) = M_iT` intact. **`13.88×` is now the measured value of the scalar-γ control, not of the field.** `T_local = 1.26e-4` inside vs `1e-3` outside (a **7.94× refrigerator**, confirmed to 0.2%: `Var(p_i)/(M_iT) = 0.12600 ± 0.00031` vs absorb-pred `0.12591`, vs coupled-pred `1.0`).
- **⚠ Design consequence with teeth: the absorb-only design is now LOAD-BEARING and must NOT be "fixed" or "tidied".** The integrator's docstring calls the field *"a pure sink"*; it is a sink **and a refrigerator**, and that is exactly what makes the hole a 110× vault instead of a 14× one. Recorded as a **DO-NOT-FIX** note in `fix-pack-6`. *An undocumented physical property of a shipped operator became the mechanism of a headline result — which is its own lesson about what "cleanup" can destroy.*
- **`γ_φ` was never the wrong lever — it was the wrong lever for the wrong job.** The chapter has carried N25 (*"the learned friction field does NOT recover the fit gap — the wrong-tool lesson"*) since w6. Refined: **`γ_φ` is the wrong lever for *erasure* and the right lever for *protection*.** At `T=0` a hole erases **nothing** (drift ≤ 1.75e-12 rad / 200k steps, `||λ|−1| ≤ 2.0e-15` over 27 cells **including at the hole edge where `|∇γ_φ| = 3.0`** — the `∇γ_φ` term multiplies `p* = 0` at the latched vacuum and drops out identically). And a *second*, independent mechanism surfaced: **the hole attenuates the WRITE, not the stored value** (`θ_settle(in)/θ_settle(out) = 0.09576` vs the transport prediction `γ/γ_eff = 0.095238`, **ratio 1.0054 ± 0.0002**) — which retro-explains the `γ_φ` rung of the fit-gap ladder recovering −24% for a *second* reason.
- **Chapter verdict:** **[validated-with-scope]** (pilot, 3 seeds) → **[validated-with-scope, mechanism corrected]**. The trinity's `T_φ(q)` half — the "shredder" — remains a **prediction**; the build is **HELD** (Head, this wave).

## New chapter candidate (not opened — Hub call)

**The lattice as a thermodynamic object (Thread-10).** `xy-lattice-theory` establishes, *exactly*, that a **designed** CLU register lattice **is** the classical XY model on its coupling graph (`V = Σ 2κr*²(1−cos Δθ)`, pure first harmonic, `J = 2κr*²`, verified to **2.2e-16**; `⟨cosΔθ⟩/Gibbs = 1.00028 ± 0.00218` over 9 cells and a **20× range of γ**). It carries the wave's third and fourth corrections — **two dictionary errors in the Hub's own Thread-10 write-up** (`ρ_s = J = 2κr*²`, **not `F²`**, which is an *inertia* and cancels from the Gibbs measure; and **`n = dim(G/H) + 1`**, not `dim(G/H)`) — and it replaces the loose *"a 2-D array remembers"* with the true and much sharper statement: **equilibrium forgets any generic written pattern in every dimension; what a 2-D array below `T_KT` remembers is the winding number, and the KT transition *is* the memory transition** (`τ_winding ∝ L^{πρ_s/T−2}`, exponent vanishing exactly at the Nelson–Kosterlitz jump `ρ_s/T → 2/π`; `T_KT = 1.786 κ r*²`). The best sentence in it: **aliasing and vortices are the same winding** — an `S²` bank has `π₁ = 0` ⇒ **no aliasing and no 2-D memory phase**; capacity and thermodynamic stability trade through one topological invariant.
This is a *chapter's worth* of idea (philosophy → theory → expected → measured → verdict), but it has **not yet had its experiment**: `τ_winding` was never simulated in 2-D, `L=4` (`N=16`, our largest lattice ever) is **13.5% off `T_KT`** and cannot see the jump, and the whole framing is gated on a **blocking code fix**. **Recommendation: do not open Ch. 8 until the parameter-free 1-D control returns** (`ξ = −1/ln(I₁(J/T)/I₀(J/T))`, nothing to fit — the task whose purpose is to stop us buying a CSF3 run to discover a laptop-scale mistake). Held for w15 by the Head. *Recorded here so the ledger's chapter list does not silently omit the program's leading Nature-MI thesis.*
