## Gap-list updates
- **⛔ NEW #1 CODE ITEM, and it outranks everything on this list:** the `fdt` NaN gradient at γ=0 (**N47**). **No FDT-correct model has ever been trainable at repo defaults.** It blocks Thread-10, the `T_φ(q)` build, and **every Gibbs-sampling claim**. 3-line fix, verified. → `fix-pack-6`. *This is not a hygiene item; it retroactively bounds what "we ran FDT-correct training" could ever have meant.*
- **F5 is BLOCKED again** — Prop-9 must be re-scoped to **Prop-9′ (a no-go)** at all three sites before the arXiv push (→ `f5-corrigendum-2`; blast radius includes CM-14's `MALA(σ*)` consumer and V2 App-F's fine print). **The Head's parking of the push bought exactly the time this needed.** *Note for the record: this is the **second** F5 corrigendum, and like the first, it makes the note **stronger** than the version it replaces.*
- **N10 resolves at w14** on `relativistic-gibbs-expc` (F-9). Two exits: the sampler *does* drive the MNIST imbalance (N10 **inverts** into a positive result, and the fix is one free config line — raise `c` or `m₀`), or it does not (N10 **re-closes**, now on a valid instrument). **Neither outcome is assumed here.** Until it reports, **no paper may cite N10 as a settled refutation.**
- **CM-16 must be split by the Hub** (designed-only (a) vs universal unification). Until then, **no draft may cite CM-16 as a single claim.** The curator recorded both halves and did not reinterpret the verdict.
- **The "13.9×" sweep is DONE and was, in the transfer docs, a no-op** — the string appears in **none** of the four docs this curator owns (verified by grep). Its live occurrences are in **Hub-owned files**: `handover_context.md` (§10, which already self-flags *"Curator is sweeping it"*), and the number's *mechanism* is now corrected wherever the vault is discussed in `future_work.md`. ⚠ **`research_roadmap.md`, `brainstorm_log.md` and `claims_matrix.md` were NOT swept** (out of scope) — the Hub should grep them for `13.9` / `13.88` and for the two dictionary errors (`ρ_s ↔ F²`, `n = dim(G/H)`).
- **A retracted number was still live in this program's docs for two waves.** `f5-corrigendum` (w12) retracted check-(g)'s **"2.6"** (a running-supremum excursion: 2.62@1e5 → 2.96@1e6 steps; non-reproducible — 2.625 vs 2.734 for two algebraically identical spellings of `∇V`) and explicitly assigned *"N4 needs wording + drop the 2.6"* to `negative_results.md`. **It was never done until this wave.** Now discharged (N4 + primer §10.3). *Recurrence vector: a retraction delivered as a downstream-reconciliation list in one agent's report, with no owner and no wave-boundary check.*
- **REMAINING (unchanged priority):** **real data** (still the single biggest gap, and now confirmed as the **shared gate for BOTH flagships**, not just the ICLR one); reversible-BPTT in `train_chlu` + accelerator; learned entrance-steering (N35); noise-wall diagnosis (N37); **F5 arXiv push** (parked by the Head until days before submission; the corrigendum-2 must land first); venue calendar + erosion placement + pruning (gated on the **Jul-11** scout, fires tomorrow).
- **Scope guards for drafters (carry verbatim, w13 additions):** **(q) CM-16** — cite it **split**: the latch *register* is **designed-only** (N46); the damping-optimum *unification* and the `T>0` law generalize. **(r) `n₁/₂`** — **never** quote it without `Δ` and `ℓ_θ/Δ`; **never** quote a designed-vs-emergent claim from **raw** exponents (N51); the `D̂` estimator is the unbiased one. **(s) the vault** — **107.77 ± 4.78×** (D̂) / 86.97 ± 2.94× (raw FPT, `ℓ_θ/Δ = 0.079`); **"13.9×"/"13.88×" is the *rejected* coupled-bath hypothesis and the *measured* scalar-γ control** — never the field. **(t) `σ*`** — the exact-FDT noise is **Newtonian-only**; in relativistic mode **no `σ` exists** (CM-17). **(u) the lattice dictionary** — `ρ_s = J = 2κr*²`, **never `F²`**; `n = dim(G/H) + 1`; O(n) universality **only for spherical cosets**. **(v) 2-D memory** — the stable stored quantity is **topological (winding)**, not a written pattern; and `D_Θ = D_θ/N` holds in **every** dimension and **must never be sold as a phase transition**.

## Positioning/gap ripples
- **The referee stage does not catch this class, and that is the wave's structural news.** Waves 8–10 established that our referees catch **framing/number-hygiene defects sitting on numbers that reproduce**. Wave-13's defects **reproduce perfectly and are still wrong**: a NaN gradient, a sign-reversed force, a Gaussian sampler for a non-Gaussian target, an estimator bias that mimics a physics claim. **No amount of adversarial *reading* finds these.** They were found by (i) a theorist re-deriving the code's actual step from scratch, and (ii) an analyst running a **control in the arm he was not testing**. *The prophylactics are structural, not editorial — and they should be budgeted as such.*
- **Two *good* process artifacts, and both deserve to be program policy.**
  1. **`v5-gate` pre-registered its predictions before measuring** (`PREREG.md`, both hypotheses, differing by 7.942× in two independent observables) — and thereby **caught a wrong number this program had been quoting and propagating**, converting what would have been a "fit" into a **decisive 8.11 ± 0.37 rejection against a dedicated control.** *Pre-registration did not slow the wave; it is what made the result sharp.* **Recommend: pre-registration becomes mandatory for any task whose acceptance criterion is a measured ratio.**
  2. **The theorist reported two of his own refutations rather than dropping them** — **X4** (*"I first said relativistic aliasing protection is exponentially harder to reach. Only conditionally"*: `sinh(ζ*)/ζ*` = **48.86×** at γ=0.05 but **1.288×** at γ=0.01, **→1 as γ→0**) and **X5** (*his own Maxwell–Jüttner "fix demonstration" was **under-converged** and is discarded as evidence*; the R8 defect rests instead on the free-particle proof plus the `T/(m₀c²)` scaling). **The no-go stands on a proof, not on the harness he had to throw away.** *This is what the C-9 culture is for, and it worked without prompting.*
- **V5 "Forgetting" = GO, with an honest scope that is *better* than a flat confirmation.** Both pre-registered gates passed. The short's thesis — ***"friction preserves, temperature shreds — and the operator you reach for first does the opposite of what you want"*** — survives, and R1 delivered **three** emergent-arm results rather than one. **The scope sentence the short must carry:** *the vault, the sign flip and the V-curve unification **generalize**; the latch register **does not** (designed-only).* The emergent case is the **interesting** one, not the embarrassing one: it shows the coset register is an **architectural object**, and it hands us a naturally-occurring washboard (a free `tilt_delta`) for the Arrhenius leg. (Head decision pending the Hub brief; `T_φ(q)` build **HELD**.)
- **The program's recurring deflation gains a sixth instance — and it is a new species.** The five prior instances (N3/CM-3 energy≈margin, N21, N24 router-wins, N41 MH-parsimony, N45 receipt-without-consequence) are all *"the physics buys less than we said."* **N46 is the opposite shape: the physics buys exactly what we said — but only when we *build* it.** The designed/emergent gap is 12 orders. *That is not a deflation; it is the program's clearest positive statement of what "designing the geometry" is worth,* and it is the cleanest available answer to the standing hostile question *"is this physics or is it just a well-chosen architecture?"* — **it is a well-chosen architecture, and the physics is what tells you which one to choose, and by how much it will win.**
- **Charter/citation:** C-1 holds; C-9 registry now at **N51**, with **N10 re-opened rather than deleted** (exactly the case C-9 exists for); C-10 unchanged. **G7 (LONGS mandate) held throughout this wave** — R5/R7/R3 and the entire XY reduction are Nature-MI + ICLR-long material, and **not one short was widened.** The only short-side change is a **correction**, not an addition: V2 App-F's `σ*` statement needs the kinetic-mode qualifier.

# ⟲ Wave-11 addendum (catch-up — written 2026-07-19 by doc-curator; covers wave-11 science)

> **On the date.** The ⟲ protocol forbids *rewriting* chapters and treats addenda as **dated records**. This entry is written on 2026-07-19 and honestly dated so; it **covers wave-11's science** (reviewed 2026-07-09), which reached the running log and the claims matrix but never this ledger. The w13 addendum flagged the gap and refused to backfill silently; the **Head approved the catch-up on 2026-07-10** (*"shouldn't be too hard given all agents write their individual output reports"*), so it is now written from those reports, not reconstructed. Ordered after w13 to keep the append-only spirit; the header names the wave it covers. Sources: handover §10 (2026-07-09 WAVE-11 review) + `v3-interference-extra`, `v1-certificate-payoff`, `v2-revision-3`, `transfer-docs-sync-w10`; `claims_matrix.md` v1.6.
>
> **Wave-11 is the wave that RETIRED a near-reject and turned a definitional claim into a measured one.** Its shape: two *strengthenings* of headline claims (V3's firewall, V1's certificate), each achieved by the same move — **replace a weak empirical statement with an exact structural one**.

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 5 (lattice → V3) — the interference firewall becomes an EXACT IDENTITY, and the near-reject is retired.**
- **The w10 near-reject (N44) is discharged by measurement.** `v3-interference-extra` (12 seeds × 6 N, N∈{2..16}, three topologies + block controls) bit-exactly reproduced the old table and then **retired the "×2.56 vs ×2.18 parallel" as 3-seed noise** (per-seed CI ±2.27). The structural claim is upgraded from a growth-rate to an **exact identity**: cross-unit basin displacement is **0 off the coupling graph** (0/4,656 nonzero entries, 72 runs) ⇒ **`S = deg·R̄_edge` to 6 decimals**; at fixed degree S is **statistically flat in N** (ring, deg≡2: b=+0.07±0.18, p=0.39); circulant-4 = 2× ring.
- **The slopes DO diverge — but as the honest, powered statement.** Modular b=+0.46±0.31 vs monolith b=+1.18±0.17 (Welch p=3.3e-4, non-overlapping CIs, 11/12 seeds); the chain's residual is its **degree ramp** (1.5→1.88), which vanishes under degree-normalization. Separation ×20,649 at N=16.
- **The firewall's mechanism is named: PARAMETER SEPARATION, not block structure.** A block-diagonal single V with *shared* params still interferes (deep-sets/tied per-pair R̄=0.97 — **6× worse than the naive monolith**, a candidate *primary foil*); with *separate* per-block params → **S ≡ 0 exactly** (1,185-param tied = worst, 18,960-param untied = zero). Capacity decoupled from parameter count.
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 5's mechanism verdict (**validated**) is unchanged; **CM-9 moves from ⚠ UNDER RE-SCOPE (w10) to RESOLVED** — *"degree-bounded vs width-linear," never "flat in N" for a chain.* Metric discipline noted for the record: report **basin-displacement R**, never NTK cosine (≈0.99 both).

**Ch. 4 (boost / cascade → V1) — the certificate acquires a MEASURED downstream consequence; "beats the router" stops being definitional.**
- **The w10 F3 near-reject (N45) is DISCHARGED, and measured.** `v1-certificate-payoff` measured the router map `(q,p)↦(b,p)` to have **det J = 0 exactly** (correcting the prior "None") — volume-annihilating information destruction: it maps a capture ball of stored states to **one point**, **erasing** the Goldstone spread (`std(Q_out)=0`), where the wormhole **transports** it (`std(Q_out)=std(Q_in)=0.0803`, `ΔQ=pᵀXΔ` to 1.2e-7). **PAYOFF A** (latch preservation) is now a *result*.
- **The honest fine print that must travel (the claim does NOT discharge cleanly).** Volume alone isn't the latch receipt — a det-J=1 random shift *also* scrambles `Q`, so the **matched channel** is what preserves it; and **PAYOFF B (BIBO)** needs the caveat *"the receipt buys it, not the jump; router ≡ blind-wormhole; a free ΔH=0 exit still escapes ⇒ coercive-component membership is the operative clause, not the energy ledger."* The task ceiling (linearly-separable cues) still drives the raw router win (N24 stands).
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 4 stays **[reframed]** (pivot adopted); the F3-lift converts the pillar-4 story's one un-measured link into a measured one. CM-7 updated (F3 DISCHARGE row).

**Ch. 1 (Goldstone memory → V2) — the drafting hygiene, not the physics.**
- `v2-revision-3` closed V2's two w10 MUST-FIX with no new experiments: MF2-A (the **"5×"→"≈3.2× trained / ≈5× exact-map-only"** canonical wording, N43) and MF2-B (§3.4↔App-G ladder-wiring — γ=0 labelled, non-comparability caveat, "92% of the *absolute* gap, ≈4.6× by ratio, bounded"). > **Same dating discipline as the w11 addendum above** (written 2026-07-19, covering wave-12 science reviewed 2026-07-09, Head-approved catch-up). Both are recorded here as the *origin* of recurrence vectors the program has since named.

**Ch. 6 (sampler thermodynamics / FDT) — F5 is unblocked, and the corrigendum makes the note STRONGER than the version it replaces.**
- `f5-corrigendum` re-derived Prop-9's core independently and proved a **stronger** form: not just rank but **inertia** preserved (Sylvester); the latched physical direction is **`ker K` itself** (not even rotated by `M`); a **global** statement (vacuum × {p=0} are exact fixed points for *any* `M` ⇒ the latch survives anisotropy non-perturbatively); and no-secular-drift is **elementary** (H conserved + coercive V ⇒ compact orbit ⇒ |Q| bounded). F5's arXiv push is unblocked (all three sites edited, PDF rebuilt clean, CHANGELOG v0.4).
- **A number RETRACTED (and this is the time-bomb).** check-(g)'s **"2.6"** is a *running-supremum excursion*, **not reproducible** — two algebraically identical spellings of `∇V` give **2.625 vs 2.734** (the off-ring orbit is a chaotic rosette), and it is window-dependent (2.62@1e5 → 2.96@1e6). ⇒ *bounded O(1) charge non-conservation — an excursion, not a drift.* The corrigendum **delivered a downstream-reconciliation list** (matrix clean; `negative_results.md` N4 "needs wording + drop the 2.6"; three V2 draft sites → `v2-revision-4`). **The N4 item had no owner and was not executed until w13** — the recurrence vector the program later names (a reconciliation list buried in one report, no wave-boundary check).
- **Verdict:** Ch. 6 stays **[validated] mechanism / [refuted] attribution**; the "stronger-when-corrected" pattern is now on its **first** instance (the second is w13's Prop-9′ no-go).

**Ch. 1 (Goldstone memory → V2) — GMOR PROPER on trained checkpoints; the condensate is resolved.**
- `f1-gmor-condensate` (CM-15): with a *linear ambient* spurion (`LinearSpurionPotential`, V→V−δ(u·q)), the three ChPT objects are measured **independently** and satisfy **μ²F² = δΣ to machine precision** (max abs dev **1.33e-15**, 8 trained SO(2) checkpoints × 10 δ), where **`F² = M_ch·r*²`** (the decay constant — *not* the orbit radius) and **`Σ = r*(δ)`** (the condensate). The leading LEC is **resonance-saturated by the radial (σ) mode** (LO-GMOR rel. error ratio → 0.99999607). Records the subtlety that the shipped *angular* tilt **cannot see Σ** (radius-independent ⇒ measures only the product μ²F²=δn²) — it remains the clean power-law *verification* (C-2); the linear spurion resolves the condensate.
- *Ledger note (this survives w13's corrections):* the `F² = M_ch r*²` identity is the **temporal** stiffness of a single register — it is exactly the object w13's Thread-10 work later distinguishes from the spatial stiffness `ρ_s = J = 2κr*²`, which is the dictionary error `ρ_s ↔ F²`. GMOR's `F²` was right all along; only the *lattice* label was wrong.

**Ch. 3 (trash-region / trinity) — temperature erases where friction cannot; V5 is floated; and a wrong number is born.**
- `t-lever-forgetting` (CM-16) on trained designed-SO(2) (fdt, retied, 5 seeds): **(a) Cor-13 exact as an operator identity** (‖|λ_flat|−1‖ ≤ 1.7e-14 ∀γ∈[0.002,0.5]; drift ≤ 4.9e-12 rad / 200k steps) — **friction cannot erase a Goldstone register**; **(b) D_θ law verified to 1.0068±0.0219** over 25 (γ,T) cells; **(c) sign flip 10/10** — γ 0.05→0.2 *lengthens* coset half-life **3.77±0.23×**. **UNIFICATION beyond the deep-dive:** the massive mode's n₁/₂(γ) is **non-monotone with a minimum at γ_crit=2εμ**; the flat mode is its **μ→0 corner** (γ_crit→0, permanently overdamped) — *one damping-optimum curve at two μ, not two laws.* New qualifier: `Δ ≫ ℓ_θ = ε√(T/M)/(γr*)`, the thermal persistence length.

It reached the running log as fact. **`v5-gate` (w13) rejected it by 8.11×** (the shipped `FrictionField` is absorb-only ⇒ the vault is **107.77×**, the coupled-bath 13.88× is the *rejected* hypothesis). *Recorded here so the ledger's chapter-1/3 history shows exactly where the number entered and why it was wrong: it lived in a §7 **prediction**, which the flag-provenance rule (tables) did not cover.*
- **Verdict:** Ch. 3 stays **[validated-with-scope]** (pilot); V5 "Forgetting" is **floated (conditional-GO)** pending R1 emergent-replication + R3 the free γ_φ-vault falsifiable → both TASKED to `v5-gate` (w13).

# ⟲ Wave-14 addendum (2026-07-19, doc-curator — the catch-up pass completes the w11→w14 ledger)

> Written 2026-07-19 from handover §10 (2026-07-10 WAVE-14 review) + the w14 outputs {`f5-corrigendum-2`, `relativistic-gibbs-expc`, `rb-bound-trained`, `causal-memory-floor`, `fix-pack-6`, `lattice-xy-prereqs`} + `claims_matrix.md` **v1.9**. Companion numbers now in `negative_results.md` (**N52–N58**; **N10 UPHELD**, **N18 sharpened**, **N45 DISCHARGED**) and `future_work.md` (R5/R7/O8 → SHOWN; R3/R6 corrected). ⟲ protocol respected: chapters untouched; deltas appended.
>
> **Wave-14 is the wave that turned two slogans into laws, produced the healthiest falsification the program owns, and — most importantly — is the wave where THREE agents re-derived rather than trusted, and two of them corrected the Hub's own claims matrix.** Where w13's lesson was *instrument validity precedes inference*, w14's is its constructive twin: ***re-derive, don't trust — and write attack tasks that invite their own falsification.***

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 5 (mass-as-allocator → V3) — "M is the budget allocator" goes from SLOGAN to CONSERVATION LAW, on trained checkpoints.**
- **R5 is SHOWN on trained models** (`rb-bound-trained`, F-12, Head-promoted). Across a 16× inertial-mass band, `n₁/₂` moves **16.6×** (342.4→5690.3) and `θ̇_max` moves **4.0×** (1.4102→0.3526) in opposite directions, while the **product moves 1.0386× vs pre-registered 1.0388×, 5/5 seeds**, every band's `P/P₀` matching prereg to **≤4e-11**. *If `M` bought both, `P` would span 16×; it spans 3.9%.* The w13 addendum's promise — *"do not upgrade Ch. 5's verdict until `rb-bound-trained` reports"* — is now discharged: **the candidate conservation law is measured.**
- **⚠ The scope R5 never stated, and it is load-bearing (Head-flagged, and the task's own sharpening).** (1) It is a **SATURATED-WRITE law:** *below* the causal cap the relativistic arm reproduces Newtonian `P ∝ 1/M` (at fixed finite impulse the Newtonian product spreads **15.40×**; the relativistic spread collapses `15.40× → 1.0386×` **as the write saturates the cap**). **The governor CREATES the conservation law** — it is not a property of the potential. (2) The residual coefficient is **`((2−γ)/2)²(εμ/γ)²`**, not bare `(εμ/γ)²` (the deep-dive quoted the γ→0 limit — *and its own analytic table already contained the correction*; N55, caught by the prereg). The Newtonian null is sharper than "undefined": `θ̇(p₀)=p₀/(M_ch r*)` exactly ⇒ `sup_p P = ∞`.
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 5 keeps **[validated] mechanism / [open] scale**, and its **third axis** (candidate conservation law) moves `THEORY-ONLY → TASKED → **SHOWN (saturated-write, relativistic-mode only)**`. *Any paper use must carry the saturated-write scope; it is the boundary of the claim, not a control.*

**Ch. 3/4 (trash-region + boost/cascade — the relativistic governor's memory side) — a FALSIFICATION WITH A REPAIR, the healthiest outcome the program has produced.**
- **R7's causal memory floor, as written, is FALSE — and the report returned the correct, stronger object.** `causal-memory-floor` attacked its own proposed bound `n ≥ Δ/(ε·θ̇_max)` with an adversarial impulse train and **broke it in 332/787 cells, up to 3.14×**: an adversary that *dives toward the origin* trades radius for angular speed (`θ̇ = v/r`, `r` its to choose). The **assumption-free repair** is a **Cartesian light-cone bound `|q_{n+1}−q_n| < ε·c/√M_min`** — never violated (0/787), verified to **1 ulp at `|p|=1e18`**, and **exactly attained**. Newtonian mode has no such bound (a single step erases the register). (N52.)
- **Why this is the healthiest outcome, and how the task INVITED it.** The task was written as an **attack** — *"the attacked version (adversarial impulse train) is the experiment"* (w13 future-work, R7 entry). It did not ask the analyst to confirm the bound; it asked him to break it. He did, and in breaking the *angular* form he found the *Cartesian* form that cannot be broken. **A falsification that returns a stronger object is worth more than a confirmation** — the paper now ships an assumption-free light-cone bound instead of an adversary-defeatable angular one. *Record this as a task-design principle: when a bound is load-bearing, task its falsification, not its verification.*
- **Two further self-refutations from the same report** (C-9 culture, unprompted): the analyst **refuted his own** `γ_c/γ` refinement to R6 (N53 — the `sinh(ζ*)/ζ*` form needs none), and found **R3's rapidity-transport law is a stiff-ring idealization that fails at 1% by `ζ₀ ≈ 0.19`** on the *actual trained* ring, not "0.2% at ζ≤3" (N54 — the natural failure mode of a companding register).
- **Verdict move:** the relativistic-governor material (which straddles Ch. 3's forgetting story and Ch. 4's test-time-compute story) gains a **measured, assumption-free memory-lifetime guarantee** — the governor's *memory-side* payoff, never claimed by the published paper. **[validated-with-scope], repaired.**

**Ch. 6 (sampler thermodynamics / FDT) — Prop-9′ is finalized; the attribution verdict RE-CLOSES on a valid instrument.**
- **The no-go is sharpened and F5-ready** (`f5-corrigendum-2`). The free-particle proof is superseded by a **characteristic-function argument** needing neither `V=0` nor linear damping: any chain whose last substep is a fixed-covariance Gaussian kick has a Gaussian-smoothed invariant momentum marginal, and Maxwell–Jüttner is not one (`e^{−m₀c|t|}` tails). **Prop-9 → Prop-9′** landed at all three F5 sites. **The control parameter is `d·Θ`, not `Θ`:** at Exp-C (`d=784`) the defect is **`785×` at `c=1`, `31.4×` at `c=5`**; `KL = 3.24e6 / 3.05e5` nats — *not* the `0.384` the Hub's v1.8 matrix inferred from a `d=1` table (N57). An **exact cheap fix nobody had** (F2, the latent-mass thermostat: MJ is a Gaussian scale mixture ⇒ draw `s|p ~ InvGauss`, run the same Gaussian O-step at variance `M/(2s)`; bias `−0.727 → +0.0011`). Blast radius: **CM-14 / Prop-MH1 step 1 is FALSE in relativistic mode** (one-line repairable).
- **N10 RE-CLOSES on a valid instrument — UPHELD and STRENGTHENED** (`relativistic-gibbs-expc`). This is the direct payoff of Ch. 7's w13 lesson. The imbalance survives `c=5`, `m₀=25`, *and* an exactly-Gibbs MJ momentum law; two configs with numerically identical MJ defect give maximally different digit histograms (**χ²=216.0, p=2.7e-42, 80.7% flips**) ⇒ **the digit law is not a function of the defect.** Exemplary discipline: the analyst **bit-reproduced Study A's histograms first** (earning the right to overturn premises), then corrected three of the task's own premises before running anything.
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 6 goes from *[validated → strengthened to a no-go] mechanism / [attribution UNDER RE-TEST]* (w13) to *[validated, no-go, F5-ready] mechanism / **[attribution RE-CLOSED on a valid instrument, UPHELD]***. The imbalance is a **landscape** property, not a sampler artifact — now established, not conjectured.

**Ch. 7 (training-dynamics / instrument validity) — the w13 prophecy comes true, and a NEW epistemics entry is opened.**
- **"It will happen again" — it did, and correctly this time.** The w13 addendum wrote *instrument validity precedes inference* and predicted the pattern would recur. At w14 the **re-test on a nearly-correct instrument UPHELD N10** — the discipline worked: *a negative that was challenged and re-closed is stronger than one never challenged.* The challenge (running F-9) was worth it **even though it confirmed the original**, because it moved N10 from resting on a variance-level comparison of two non-Gibbs samplers to resting on an exact-MJ law. **Record the rule: re-test a negative drawn from an instrument you later learn was invalid, even when you expect confirmation — the re-test is what earns the tier-A status.**
- **⭐ NEW epistemics entry — "re-derive, don't trust," with a counterfactual.** *Three separate w14 agents re-derived rather than trusted, and two of them corrected the Hub's own claims matrix.* (i) `f5-corrigendum-2` **independently derived the `d·Θ` control parameter**, exposing that matrix v1.8's CM-17 quoted a `d=1` table against a `d=784` experiment (N57). (ii) `relativistic-gibbs-expc` **measured `d·Θ` on the real Exp-C path** and **refuted the Hub's "raise `c` is the free fix"** (N56). (iii) `rb-bound-trained`'s **pre-registration corrected a residual coefficient** before the harness ran (N55). **The counterfactual, recorded per the task:** had the shorts trusted matrix v1.8, they would have shipped a benign `KL = 0.384 nats` for a `KL = 3.05e5` reality, a "free `c` fix" that is refuted, and a bare `(εμ/γ)²` residual. *The Hub being wrong twice and caught twice is not a failure of the Hub — it is the program's cross-check culture working exactly as designed. The matrix is authoritative precisely because agents are licensed to overturn it with a derivation.*
- **The two w13 process proposals are now BINDING PROTOCOL (Item-3 propagation).** Protocol §5 carries, as of the w13/w14 reviews (Head-approved): **(a) the pre-registration rule** — any acceptance criterion that is a *measured ratio, exponent, slope, or law* must write `PREREG.md` (predicted value + derivation) *before* running the harness; and **(b) the reconciliation-list-owner corollary** — a report's downstream-reconciliation list must be flagged in its first 10 lines and converted by the Hub into an explicit owner-task at the accepting review. **Both rules came out of this program's own failures** (the "13.9×" prediction outside the flag-provenance net; the "2.6" retraction that sat un-owned for two waves) and both have now *paid*: pre-registration caught N55 (this wave) and the "13.9×" (w13); the owner rule is what would have prevented the "2.6" living for two waves. *This is the ledger's methods-chapter update: the program's validation procedure is now three-layered — flag-provenance (tables), pre-registration (measured laws), and reconciliation-owner (downstream changes).*
- **Chapter verdict:** unchanged in letter; the chapter now owns a **fifth generalized finding**: *the program's dominant error class (instrument/derivative/dimensionality defects that reproduce perfectly and are still wrong) is countered structurally by three procedures — a control in the arm you are not testing, a pre-registered prediction, and a re-derivation that is licensed to overturn the matrix.* None of the three is editorial; all three are cheap.

## Positioning/gap ripples
- **The program's method story for the longs is now complete and demonstrated.** Three defect-classes met by three structural procedures (w13/w14: instrument-invalidity → a control in the untested arm; wrong asides → pre-registration; un-owned downstream changes → the reconciliation-owner rule) are now joined by a **fourth stance — the funded adversary**: a scout tasked to *kill* a paper idea (N59), an attack task tasked to *break* its own bound (w14 R7). **The common thread: the program spends compute and agent-time trying to be wrong, and keeps only what survives.** That is the cleanest available answer to *"are your positive results just un-attacked conjectures?"* — no, and here are the scalps.

# ⟲ Wave-19 addendum (2026-07-21, doc-curator `w19-w20-sync`) — **the program named its own architecture, and then measured which half of it works**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope flags (read first).**
> **(1) The Hub reviewed w19 and wrote a §10 entry** (handover, 2026-07-21, WAVE-19: *"REVIEWED 4/4 + INTEGRATED → main GREEN 392"*) — **but did NOT write a ledger addendum.** This addendum is therefore a **full curator addendum, not a `(curator supplement)`**. It is written against **both** the handover §10 entry **and** the four source reports, which I cross-checked; **I found no output↔handover disagreement on any number**, and I note the two places where the handover's shorthand understates a report (both in the deltas below).
> **(2) ⚠ THE LEDGER HAS A GAP: waves 17 and 18 have NO addendum.** The ledger runs …w14 · w15 · w16 · **[w17 MISSING] · [w18 MISSING]** · w19. The w19 handover references w18 work as delivered (`scout-hepa-predictor`, `cmapss-fd002-004-fetch`, `hepa-protocol-*`) and w17 as scoped. **This is a real hole in the program's epistemic record and it is NOT mine to fill from a task scoped to w19/w20** — reconstructing two waves' verdicts from reports the Hub has already reviewed would risk exactly the reinterpretation this document forbids. **Flagged for the Hub as an owed catch-up pass.**
> **(3) ⟲ protocol respected:** chapters untouched; deltas appended; superseded numbers named. Companion edits this pass: `negative_results.md` (**N61–N66, W2, §Open reconciliations R19-1…R19-4; N7 AMENDED not deleted**), `claims_matrix.md` (**v2.0** — CM-18/19/20/21 new, CM-5 amended, CM-16a/b sharpened, CM-3 new instance, D1 partition corrected), `HEP_primers.md` (**new Part XI**), `future_work.md` (w19/w20 sweep).
>
> **Wave-19 is the wave in which the program stopped being a collection of mechanisms and became an architecture with a named missing piece — and the same wave in which four of its own load-bearing beliefs were measured to be wrong about *why* they were right.**

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**⭐ NEW FRAMING ABOVE ALL CHAPTERS — the Head rejected the Hub's diagnosis, and the rejection reorganizes the whole ledger.**
- **What the Hub proposed (WRONG, now WITHDRAWN):** a *"primitive–task mismatch"* — that CLU's conservative/symplectic prior is mismatched to a dissipative, monotone-drift task; *"a symplectic integrator asked to model a ramp wants to orbit."*
- **The Head's correction (CORRECT; adopt everywhere):** *"CLU's dynamics has nothing to do with the task, but rather the flow of information in the latent space… CLU is only conserving the information that 'the system dissipates at rate X'."* ⇒ **CLU is a LATENT INFORMATION CARRIER, not a model of the observed system's dynamics.** Symplectic conservation is a **memory** property (latent information is not lost over long horizons), not a claim that the observed process is conservative. **The error was conflating "CLU models the data's dynamics" with "CLU's latent dynamics carries information about the data." Only the second is claimed.** ⛔ **Never diagnose "prior mismatch" again.**
- **Why this belongs at the top of the ledger and not in one chapter:** it changes the *reference class of the whole program*. Every chapter's verdict was scored against an implicit question — "does the physics help this task?" The correct question is **"does the physics carry latent information faithfully, and can we write into and read out of it?"** Chapters 1–7 are unaffected in their *measurements*; several are affected in their *meaning*.
- **The Head's summary, now the program's organizing problem:** *"We have basically built a novel latent dynamics unit but we have no idea how to actually use it for different tasks."*
- **⭐ THE ARCHITECTURAL GAP (the diagnosis that replaces the withdrawn one).** A transformer does not operate on raw data — a learned embedding lifts data into a representation space and *then* attention operates there. **We have been running the equivalent of attention on raw pixels.** Measured, not asserted (`clu-latent-io-audit` §Item 2): read-in `φ: data → (q₀,p₀)` is **≈ the IDENTITY** (`q₀` = a literal z-scored sensor frame, `p₀` = a literal scaled first difference), read-out `ψ` is **HANDCRAFTED**, and **all representational burden falls on `V_θ` alone.** **This single gap predicts most of our negatives.**

**Ch. 2 (mass-as-allocator, T5) — 🔴 THE MECHANISM WAS WRONG FOR THE PROGRAM'S ENTIRE HISTORY; THE CONCLUSION SURVIVES.**
- **The chapter's verdict (`[reframed → doctrine]`, "3× no hierarchy emerges") is CONFIRMED — and its stated mechanism is refuted.** On the eval/CAFE path **`log_mass` is NOT frozen: it moves +3.56** (M 0.67 → 3.56), travels **4.28× more per-parameter than `V_θ`**, and receives a gradient **8,200× larger per-parameter** ⇒ **N7's "masses stay at init" is WRONG there, and the theorist's D1 premise is INVERTED** (the mass gets the *rich* gradient, not the starved one).
- **What actually produces the flat spectrum — three named causes, all new:** (i) **99.8% of the mass gradient comes from `E_reg`**, whose only lever is inflating `M` *uniformly* (differential:common = **1:39**); (ii) ⭐ **the EBM contrastive term has EXACTLY ZERO mass gradient** — negatives perturb `q` only, so kinetic terms cancel identically in `H(data) − H(neg)`: **the objective's representational half is structurally blind to the mass**; (iii) the **softplus trap** — `M = softplus(log_mass)` is linear for `x ≫ 0`, so a *log*-scale spread stops buying *exponential* range (Std(log M) = 0.56 → ratio only **1.28**).
- **The sharpest single number in the chapter's history:** **`M_max/M_min` ends at 1.153 — BELOW its random init of 1.265.** ***Training makes the mass spectrum MORE uniform than random.*** The primitive has arguably never been run in its intended configuration.
- **The escape is architectural, and it is proven:** Prop 6 — with **per-item masses**, only the overall *scale* stays gauge and **all ratios become wake-visible and exactly learnable** (verified to **2.2e-14**). ⇒ **masses must be PER-ADDRESS codebook entries, not a global model parameter.** ⚠ **As shipped, `m` is not an address component at all.**
- **Superseded number named:** the D1 lr-partition constant **`1/11` is WRONG for multi-item settings**; the correct law is **`η_m/(η_m + K·η_k)`** (K=2 → **1/21**, matched to **13 digits**). This was a **pre-registered prediction that failed** (P6b) and returned an exact corrected law.
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 2 → *`[reframed → doctrine]`, **conclusion re-confirmed on a third code path; mechanism REPLACED** (common-mode runaway + a mass-blind objective + a softplus ceiling, not frozen parameters or gradient starvation); **the fix is now known and architectural** (per-address masses)*. ⚠ **Scope: the amendment is measured on the eval/CAFE path only — the original `train.py` lattice measurements are neither re-run nor retracted, and the mechanism is entangled with the `dt` bug below.**

**Ch. 4 (boost / cascade / test-time compute, T3) — the retry mechanism is PROMOTED from parked luxury to load-bearing replacement.**
- Chapter 4's honest weak point has always been that **retries bought nothing** (N1: 0.140 vs 0.149 ≈ random kick) — and the chapter itself diagnosed why: `S^(M) ≈ S` *because learned inertial M is near-uniform* (Ch. 2's pivot variable).
- **W19 gives the boost ladder its first *structural* role.** A boost is an affine symplectic re-address `a ↦ S·a`: volume-preserving (so it does not change the read's information capacity) **but `H` is not boost-invariant, so it changes `E(a)` and hence the reachable component** (Prop 2). ⇒ **a boost ladder is an ENERGY-SHELL FILTRATION: retry k unlocks precisely the items behind barriers < E_k.** *Memory is hierarchically organized by barrier height, and retries walk up the hierarchy.*
- ⭐ **And because gradient-based cross-basin address search is dead (below), retry-as-capture is not a luxury on top of gradient capture — it is the REPLACEMENT for it.** Derivative-free selection over energy shells is **immune to separatrix roughness by construction**.
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 4 → *`[reframed]` (pivot) + **the retry mechanism acquires a principled semantics and a structural role it never had; still UNMEASURED in that role***. ⚠ The scout independently confirms **novelty (c) — symplectic structure-preserving test-time retry — is genuinely unoccupied prior art**, while warning it is *"a mechanism in search of a demonstrated benefit."* Both halves travel together.

**Ch. 1 / Ch. 3 / Ch. 5 (Goldstone memory · trash region · lattice) — the components are re-read as parts of ONE architecture, and one of them is re-specified.**
- ⭐ **The retroactive unification.** Every mechanism the program built now has a named role: latch → *permanent memory mode* (`μ²≡0`); pseudo-Goldstone → *half-life writes* (`n½ ∝ 1/μ²`); SSB vacua → *symmetry-broken point sets*; governed rollout → *stable trajectory*; `γ_φ` field → *trash*; `M` → *access key / budget*; boost ladder → *retry*; `IntraWormholePotential` → *wormholes*; `CLULattice` → *multiple particles*. **We have been building the components of one architecture without naming it.**
- 🔴 **Ch. 3's trash region is BLOCKED by the program's own theorem and must be re-specified.** A friction hole is a **107.77 ± 4.78× memory vault**, erases **nothing** at T=0 (drift ≤ 1.75e-12 over 200k steps), and the learned field never localizes (N12/N13). ⇒ **deletion must be re-specified as "overwrite or heat, NEVER friction."** Three admissible operators: `γ → γ_crit = 2εμ` locally (the V-curve minimum is the *fastest possible eraser*, for **massive** content only); a **T_φ hot spot** (still unbuilt); or **overwrite via the equivariant write current** (deletion as a write — no thermodynamics required). *The vault theorem, a Ch. 3 result, is what forbids Ch. 3's own headline mechanism from doing the job the architecture asks of it.*
- **Ch. 1's write modes are now concrete** — mode (a) permanent latch (designed coset, `μ²=0` exactly), (b) half-life via a controlled **spurion** (`μ²(δ)` by GMOR — *R-4's "defect" as a latch-maker is exactly the feature here*), (c) fresh item at a packing-legal location. ⚠ **The locality problem, stated precisely:** an MLP potential has **global support**, so *every* write perturbs *every* stored item (measured instance: CM-6 erosion). Fixes in program order: structural blocks / dictionary atoms (exactly local) → anchor stored items during writes (R-2/R-3, envelope λ=100 holds 5/5) → NTK-orthogonalized write currents (expensive, unproven).
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 3 → *`[validated-with-scope]` **for protection; BLOCKED as a deletion mechanism** — its own vault theorem forbids it.* Ch. 1/Ch. 5 unchanged in verdict, **re-read as architecture components**.

**Ch. 7 (training dynamics / epistemics) — ⭐ THE RICHEST CHAPTER OF THE WAVE. Four beliefs were right for the wrong reasons, and pre-registration caught every one.**
- ⭐ **A new named pattern: *the mechanism was wrong, the conclusion was right*.** Twice in one wave: **(a)** no mass hierarchy — confirmed, but *both* proposed mechanisms (frozen parameters; D1 null direction) refuted; **(b)** address learning fails — confirmed, but the mechanism **exactly inverted** (the Hub pre-registered *exploding* gradients from non-contraction; the engineer pre-registered *"gradient norm grows with rollout length"* — **the gradient VANISHES, falling 2.6 orders**). *A conclusion that survives the death of its own mechanism is stronger — but only if someone checks.*
- ⭐ **Pre-registration earned its keep four more times, all reported not buried.** Theorist: **P5a failed as registered** (2/18 strict vs ≥50% — *"the wave's most important negative"*), **P5c failed** (Adam 1/18 < GD 2/18), **P6b failed** (returning the exact corrected K-law), **P4b failed** (the *derivation* was right; the *registered instrument* was wrong — a pointwise probe phase-overfit at train acc 1.0, while the probe-free physics selectivity was 1.00). Engineer: **P5b falsified** (the wave's mechanism), **P4 too pessimistic** (0.992 at K=8 vs predicted <90%), and **P2's number right for the wrong reason** (3 robust items as predicted, but corr = **−0.07** — *not monotone*, mechanism falsified). **Two post-hoc repair arms also committed thresholds before running and both failed** (naive hybrid 3/18 ≈ chance vs a committed interpretation rule that fired; Toy F 0/18 vs an in-script ≥6/18).
- ⭐ **Self-caught errors, both of which would have produced wrong conclusions.** The engineer fixed **five** mid-run defects, three of them silent-wrong-answer class: `barrier=0.05` could not hold a jittered query (would have reported a 2-item interference ceiling that was really a settling failure); the one-hot probe is **estimator-limited on a 1-D non-monotone code** (0.484 at K=4 while the payload was retrieved to 7e-4 — *"I nearly reported a decoder artifact as interference"*); the half-life measured the **first zero crossing of an oscillating envelope** (6 steps instead of 66.9). ⭐ **And a retraction of his own report draft:** he claimed an existing config test failed to catch an omission, then **verified by reverting the fix that the test DOES fail as designed** — and recorded the retraction *because an unverified "the test doesn't catch it" would have invited a redundant guard.* **This is N51's lesson (instrument validity precedes inference) operating at the level of a single agent's afternoon.**
- **A new epistemics entry: two agents, two harnesses, one verdict.** The cross-basin address negative was reached **independently** by the engineer (4.2%, ring landscape, GD on `q₀`) and the theorist (2/18 strict, Toy-A landscape, GD on `(q₀,p₀,log M)`), with **the same mechanism** identified from different directions (a staircase with cliff ratio 1.7e7 / separatrix roughness with fractal basin boundaries). **Independent convergence is the strongest form a protocol-level negative can arrive in** — the same standard the program applied to the SDR/VICReg demarcation at w17-adjacent work.
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 7 → *`[refuted]` (Lyapunov) / `[characterized null]` (PCD) / `[validated as risk]` (erosion) **+ two new epistemics exemplars: "the mechanism was wrong, the conclusion was right" (×2 in one wave) and "independent convergence on a protocol-level negative" (×1); + 7 prereg scalps and 5 self-caught instrument defects in a single wave***.

**⭐ NEW CHAPTER CANDIDATE (not opened — Hub call): Chapter 8 — the addressable-memory thesis.**
- **Philosophy:** memory is not a buffer beside the network; it is the *shape of the network's own energy landscape*, indexed by launch conditions. **Theory:** the formal object `(V_θ, 𝒜)`, six propositions, three capacity regimes. **Expected:** the Hub's formalization plus the Head's iterative/gauge-loose amendment. **Experiment:** two independent harnesses. **Measured:** write/read/durability/locality **work**; **addressing fails**. **Verdict:** *`[split — physics validated, indexing refuted, controller unbuilt]`*.
- **I did not open it.** The ledger's chapters are Hub-authored and a new chapter is a structural decision, not a curator one — **and the thesis has no named paper to attach to** (see the gap list). **Recommended to the Hub as the natural home for the w19 science**, since scattering it across Chapters 1–5 (as this addendum necessarily does) loses the fact that it is *one* result.

## Gap-list updates

- **⭐ THE GAP LIST'S TOP ITEM CHANGES.** Since w15 the top gap has been *real data*. **It is now the CONTROLLER.** Every verb in the architecture — *decide, triage, add, create, trash, select, combine* — is a controller verb, **and none is built**. The physics is demonstrated; the thing that would *use* it does not exist. ⚠ Real data remains the top *credibility* gap; the controller is the top *architectural* one, and they are not the same gap.
- **⭐ NEW, and it subsumes several older items: the I/O gap.** `φ ≈ identity`, `ψ` handcrafted ⇒ all representational burden on `V_θ`. **This one gap predicts physics-scalars-at-chance (11/16 at ≤0.55), best-config-is-least-dynamics, and the rollout-below-persistence result.** ⚠ **And the hoped-for escape is NOT supported by evidence:** moving to representation space made CLU look **worse** (feature-space persistence loses at every horizon by 20–56×, 30/30 features at n=1). *That is an argument for the design task, not evidence for it — hold it as a hypothesis about a future learned `ψ`, never as a defence of the current one.*
- **⛔ NEW BLOCKING GATE: the `dt` units bug.** `dt=0.05` on cycle-indexed data inflates K by **400×** ⇒ `E_reg` is **99.2% of the loss** / **99.8% of the mass gradient**, the training rollout is **98.3% ballistic free-streaming** (`V_θ` contributes **1.7%**), and **`H` ≡ `K` (corr 0.999996)** — *the potential barely participates in its own Hamiltonian*. ⇒ **ALL SDR/anti-collapse work is gated on this one-line fix, and ALL FD00x numbers are PROVISIONAL.** ⚠ **The fix is UNTESTED end-to-end**, and comes with a caution: at `dt=1.0` the potential is strong enough to collapse `q*` under damping, so **the relax budget must be retuned simultaneously — do not vary `dt` alone.**
- **SDR moves from side-quest to PREREQUISITE — with a hard limit.** The division of labour is **forced, not a compromise**: existence/non-degeneracy is wake-invisible (D3) so it must be *supplied*; assignment is wake-visible and restructures under the loss; **T3 forbids the regularizer from doing assignment anyway.** ⛔ **But mode-(a) permanent latches cannot come from ANY finite-λ soft regularizer** (R-4 ⇒ `μ²(λ)>0` ⇒ finite half-life; the designed-vs-emergent flatness gap is **12 orders**). **Permanent memory must be designed-in structural blocks.** ⚠ **And SDR is itself gated on the `dt` fix** (R-1 is *provably invisible* at `dt=0.05`).
- **⭐ The cheapest decisive follow-up in the program: the d-dimensional address-scaling test.** The measured ceiling of 8 is a **ring** property (`K_max ≈ 0.2·2π/σ_θ`); the packing bound is `(1+2R/w)^d`, **exponential in `d`**. *It decides whether "a handful of items" is a property of the 2-D toy or of the primitive — the difference between an interesting memory and a non-starter.* **Both agents named it independently.**
- **The named minimal falsifier now exists: the *derived-address retrieval gate*** — K=4, payload ≥16 reals, address dim 5; derived `q₀` (damped-relaxation endpoint from the query), within-basin-only refinement, boost-retry ladder as the sole cross-basin mechanism. **Pre-register ≥80% at K=4.** ⚠ **Do NOT gate it on gradient-based cross-basin address search — that is measured dead and gating on it would re-test a known negative.**
- **🔴 Superseded / retired numbers and claims named (per ⟲ protocol):** the D1 partition **`1/11` → `η_m/(η_m+K·η_k)`** (K=2 → 1/21, 13 digits); **"masses stay at init" (N7) is wrong on the eval path** (mechanism only — conclusion stands); **"exploding gradients from non-contraction"** (Hub prereg) and **"gradient norm grows with rollout length"** (engineer P5b) are **both falsified — the gradient vanishes**; the **"primitive–task mismatch" hypothesis is WITHDRAWN**; and **four positioning claims are RETIRED** (see below). ⚠ **The "8-item ceiling" is NOT retired but must NEVER be quoted as CLU's capacity** (watch-item W2).
- **⛔ FOUR POSITIONING CLAIMS RETIRED, three by external prior art — this is the sobering half of the wave.** (a) *"a retention guarantee attention has no analogue for"* — **HiPPO-LegS has one** (`O(tL/√N)` error, gradient `Θ(1/t)` polynomial). (b) *"retrieval is a rollout, not a weighted sum"* as our novelty — **Kong, Brewer & Lai, *Nat. Commun.* 2024**, must be cited. (c) *"continuity is our NTM/DNC escape hatch"* — **false; both were already fully soft and differentiable**, and the precedent transfers as a **warning**. (d) *"competitor to the transformer"* — **indefensible** at 1–1.6 bits/register with no baseline win. ⚠ **The unanswered challenge to carry:** HiPPO-LegS Prop 3 is **exactly timescale-equivariant with no `dt` at all** — *a reviewer will ask why a learned mass spectrum beats provable timescale invariance, and we have no answer.*
- **⚠ Capacity is now formally the program's most exposed flank** (scout §3). Modern Hopfield's headline is **exponential-in-dimension**; ours is **order-one bits**. ⇒ **decide at program level that CLU does not compete on capacity, and say so in every write-up before a reviewer asks.**
- **Scope guards for drafters (verbatim, w19 additions):** **(hh)** every retrieval number is **DESIGNED, zero learning** — never let it read as emergence (N46 precedent); **(ii)** any retrieval claim must report the **blank-landscape control**, and **a full-state read scores 1.000 on a blank landscape** ⇒ *any claim reading the address plane is decorative*; **(jj)** capacity is **three regimes**, never one number, and the **8-item ceiling is a ring artifact**; **(kk)** **all FD00x numbers are provisional** pending the `dt` fix; **(ll)** *"start arbitrary and restructure"* is a **proposal under test**, in direct tension with N46/N7/N64/CM-5/CM-16a and D1/D3 — **label it as such**; **(mm)** **never diagnose "prior mismatch."**

## Positioning/gap ripples

- **The program's honest posture changed shape this wave, and it changed for the better.** Before w19 the negatives were **diffuse** — physics scalars at chance, no mass hierarchy, no win over a trivial baseline, real data unmeasured. After w19 they are **concentrated and localized**: *the physics of the memory works and is guaranteed; the indexing, the I/O and the controller do not exist or do not work.* **A program that knows which of its four subsystems is broken is in a far stronger position than one with a diffuse malaise** — and it is what makes a one-line units bug (N63) worth fixing before anything else is re-measured.
- **The wave's two results point in opposite directions and both must be carried.** ⭐ **The best news the program has had in months:** the memory loop *works* — payload to 9.98e-4, durability flat to 1200 steps, zero cross-corruption, and **a hard read-isolation guarantee that attention structurally lacks** (a sub-barrier particle *cannot* read a foreign item; softmax mixes every key). ⛔ **The most sobering news:** the thing that would make it an architecture — learning which address retrieves which item — is **measured dead across four protocols and two independent agents**, and three of our positioning claims are refuted by published work. **A ledger that reported only one of these would be lying by selection.**
- **The redirection is more aligned with the Head's vision, not less** — and that is the wave's most important interpretive point. Derived addresses (*the writer knows where it wrote* — make the codebook a **function of the landscape** so cross-basin moves happen in θ-space as continuous deformations rather than separatrix crossings), write-side restructuring, and retry-as-capture are **all closer to "encode, then restructure by the loss"** than blind read-side gradient search ever was. **Plus a concrete training story:** the address selector is an **amortization of content-addressable relaxation** (Hopfield-exact at γ>0) and **can be trained by DISTILLATION from it** — avoiding backprop-through-relaxation entirely, which Prop 5 forbids anyway.
- ⚠ **The tension the Hub explicitly asked not to be smoothed, restated here as ledger record.** *This program's strongest negatives say structure does NOT self-organize* — **N46** (emergent has no coset register), **N7/N64** (no mass hierarchy), **CM-5** (hierarchy designed-in), **CM-16a** (designed-only latch), **D1** (rich-gradient levers absorb the signal), **D3** (exact structure is measure-zero). **"Start arbitrary and restructure" is in direct tension with all six.** The working resolution — loss-driven restructuring **plus** designed structural scaffolding, since **T3 proves a regularizer can restore *diversity* but can never choose *assignment*** — is a **proposal under test**, and the theorist's confirmation that the division of labour is *forced* strengthens it without settling it. **It is not a result and this ledger does not score it as one.**
- **Charter/citation:** C-1 holds; **C-9 registry now carries N61–N66 + W2 + four open reconciliations, with N7 AMENDED rather than deleted** — the full "conclusion right, mechanism wrong" trail is the point. Matrix at **v2.0** (CM-18/19/20/21 new; CM-5/CM-16/CM-3 amended). G7 (LONGS mandate) unaffected. ⚠ **New charter-adjacent question for the Head, raised by the wave and NOT resolved here: the addressable-memory family has no named paper.** CM-18/19/20/21 currently point at a vehicle that does not exist in the portfolio.

# ⟲ Wave-17 addendum (2026-07-23, doc-curator `ledger-w17-w18-catchup`) — **the collapse taxonomy arrives, and CLU's first public-benchmark number arrives with it**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope (read first).** Written as a **Head-capped catch-up** (*"do the w17, w18 work, but briefly"*) to close the ledger's two-wave hole flagged in the w19 addendum. Sources: handover §10 **WAVE-17 REVIEW** + **WAVE-17 REVIEW PART 2** (Hub, 2026-07-20) and the headline sections of `anti-collapse-characterization`, `clu-cafe-integration`, `scout-cafe-hepa`, `kt-csf3-tranche`. **Deliberately compact — this is a catch-up, not a reconstruction.** Both Hub review entries record **all four outputs ACCEPTED**. **Nothing here is re-adjudicated**; where w18/w19/w20 later overturned a w17 item, the later verdict is *named* and the w17 text is not defended. ⟲ protocol respected: chapters untouched, deltas appended, superseded numbers named.

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 7 (training dynamics / epistemics) — ⭐ THE WAVE'S REAL CONTRIBUTION: the program's four measured collapses become one principle with three drivers.**
- **T1 (demarcation).** *A structural lever collapses under training iff its order parameter is **wake-invisible*** — movable at zero task cost — and the collapsed value is then chosen by one of three drivers: **D1** lr-partition/laziness · **D2** CD-sleep adversarial bias · **D3** measure-zero non-formation. **This unifies N7/CM-5 (mass narrowing), CM-6/CM-16 (vacuum erosion), N46 (coset non-formation) and CM-16b under one statement** (`anti-collapse-characterization` §1–§2).
- **T2 (anti-collapse = gauge fixing).** Adding `λ·G(O(θ))` with minimum at a healthy `O*` **costs zero fit at the optimum** (one-line proof; the Hub records it as correct as stated). Honest corollary retained: the **finite-training-time** price is **optimization stiffness, not representability** — the measured anchor envelope at λ=100 sits at **35× the wake-MSE floor** while holding the vacuum 5/5 seeds.
- **T3 (diversity ≠ assignment) — the honesty arm, and the one with teeth.** Permutation-symmetric spectral statistics restore *spread* but **cannot choose which channel gets which timescale**: warm-started, the final ordering equals the sign of the **1e-3 init jitter, 10/10**. ⇒ **binding design rule: structural regularizers ON FROM EPOCH 0, never bolted onto a warm start; prefer the Var-form** (gradient ∝ Δ ⇒ the collapsed point becomes an *unstable* fixed point) **over a Std-hinge.** This retro-explains CM-5's schedule sensitivity (10× induces ordering; 100× inverts it, N8).
- **Pre-registration, again earning its keep:** 5 predictions committed pre-run, **5/5 landed**, two to 4 decimal places against closed-form derivation (P1: predicted `Std(log m) = 0.12603`, measured **0.1264 ± 0.0009**); one non-converged run **voided and archived** per protocol. **The Hub independently re-derived the central collapse law and confirms it** (for a q-only loss with `p0=0` the trajectory depends on `(k,m)` only via `ω²=k/m`, so the slow lever receives exactly `η_m/(η_k+η_m)` of the required `Δlog ω²` — landscape-independent).
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 7 → *unchanged verdicts **+ a unifying collapse theory (T1/T2/T3) that the chapter's own scattered negatives now instantiate**, and a new design rule with a measured failure mode for its violation*.

**Ch. 2 (mass-as-allocator) — no verdict move; the chapter acquires the *language* (D1) that w19 then partially inverted.**
- 🔴 **Superseded number named:** the D1 partition constant **`1/11`** (w17, two-lever gauge case) is **corrected at w19 to `η_m/(η_m + K·η_k)`** (K=2 → **1/21**, matched to 13 digits), and the **D1 premise itself is inverted on the eval/CAFE path** (N64: `log_mass` receives the *rich* gradient there, not the starved one). **The w17 derivation is right in its own two-lever setting and must not be quoted outside it.**

**Ch. 1 / 2 / 3 / 5 — the SDR family is *specified*, not built.** R-1 mass-spread · R-2 energy anchor *(already exists)* · R-3 Fourier-anchor · R-4 orbit-variance/symmetry-inducer · R-5 band-coverage · R-6 coercivity floor, plus `R_specW` (sorted-Wasserstein spectral pin), each with a task-nullity (free vs priced) classification and a lever→CAFE-task fatality map. **Held by the Head behind horizon-conditioning at w17, then gated on the `dt` fix at w19 (N63); the gate OPENS at w20** (R-1 relative gradient **1.77e-6 → 7.5e-2, 42,000×**, with a stability cost). Folded to `future_work.md` as a live untested family.

**⭐ The eval/CAFE thread (no chapter — Chapter 8 remains a Hub call): CLU'S FIRST NUMBER ON A PUBLIC BENCHMARK, AND ITS HONEST HEADLINE.**
- FD001 h-AUROC, 3 seeds, CAFE loader → CoxPH probe → `evaluate_event` (`clu-cafe-integration` "Answer first"): CLU default **0.6554 ± 0.0017**; best single lever **0.7168 ± 0.0015**; `basin_coords` only **0.7230**; ⚠ **56-d raw summary statistics through the identical probe: 0.7486**; `raw_last`/`raw_mean` (14-d) **0.7203 / 0.7068**; **CLU physics scalars only: 0.5887 (near chance)**.
- ⛔ **The honest headline is NOT "CLU trails HEPA" — it is "CLU trails a 56-d raw-statistics baseline, and its physics features are at chance."** **Deleting the physics scalars IMPROVES CLU** (0.7230 > 0.7158) and **CLU is not additive with raw stats** (0.7486 → 0.7420). **The raw-stats reference must travel with the number in every downstream doc and draft.**
- ⭐⭐ **The diagnosis, and it is a T1 instance: the HCD objective is DEGRADATION-AGNOSTIC.** Epochs 150→600 is a **null lever** (0.7158→0.7147, within seed noise) ⇒ **"training length" is FALSIFIED as a top-3 lever.** HCD learns *"is this a plausible C-MAPSS window"*, never *"how far along is this engine"* ⇒ **degradation phase is a wake-invisible order parameter for HCD**, and the theorist's own **arm D** (*when the order parameter is wake-visible there is no collapse and no regularizer is needed*) prescribes the cure: **horizon-condition the objective BEFORE reaching for SDR** — a different prescription than the pre-run plan.
- **⭐⭐ The HEPA target is a PROTOCOL question, not a number question** (Head, with App. G/H in hand): four distinct C-MAPSS-1 numbers — README **0.918** (⛔ **DISCARD**, placeholder) · paper Table 1/6 survival-CDF head **0.81 ± .03** · paper Table 5 matched 198K dt-conditioned MLP head, encoder-isolated **0.73 ± .02** · CAFE `encode()`→CoxPH wrapper **never measured**. ⇒ **CLU's 0.7168 has NO apples-to-apples comparator**; the head alone is worth **≈0.08 h-AUROC**. **Never compare across rows.** **⭐ And the claim is re-shaped by the Head into an ENCODER-SWAP claim** — HEPA-with-a-transformer-encoder vs HEPA-with-a-CLU-encoder, everything downstream fixed — which is more portable and dissolves the Table-5-vs-Table-1 worry.

Sources: handover §10 **⭐⭐ WAVE-18 REVIEW (2026-07-20)** and the headline sections of `scout-hepa-predictor` and `cmapss-fd002-004-fetch`. **Both outputs ACCEPTED.** The Hub's own entry calls this *"the most valuable output of the program so far because it falsified us."* **Nothing re-adjudicated.** ⚠ **One w18 headline (the persistence gate) is in an OPEN conflict with w19's `clu-latent-io-audit` — recorded as R19-1 in `negative_results.md`; neither number may be quoted.**

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 7 (training dynamics / epistemics) — ⭐ THE RICHEST DELTA: three instrument-validity lessons, one of them about the Hub itself.**
- ⛔⛔ **RETRACTION 1 — "single-basin collapse measured on real data" was NUMERICAL OVERFLOW.** `encode` ends with `np.nan_to_num(...)` and the dissipative step is `p ← (1−γ)p`, so **`γ > 2` amplifies momentum every step** until the rollout overflows; zero-filling then makes every coordinate exactly 0.0 — visually identical to a basin collapse. Holding budget=64 fixed and varying only the (γ, steps) split: **γ=0.5 → 0% non-finite, spread 0.819**; γ=2.0 → 0% non-finite, spread 0.856; **γ≥5 → 100% non-finite, spread exactly 0.000.** ⭐ **STANDING LESSON (the Hub's own accountability note, kept verbatim in force): an order parameter that hits a floating-point-*exact* value is an overflow suspect until proven otherwise.** **What survives:** the theorist's `anti-collapse-characterization` theory is **untouched** (self-contained numpy toy, verified to 4 dp, independent of this code path) — what is gone is its *real-data corroboration*. ⚠ **Later contested:** w19's audit **reinstates** a single-basin claim in a different form (**R19-2, open**).
- ⛔ **RETRACTION 2 — "the relaxation budget γ·steps·dt is the knob" is FALSIFIED; γ alone controls it.** ISO-γ (γ=0.5), budget varied **400×**: h-AUROC **0.7230 / 0.7230 / 0.7230 / 0.7230** at steps 6/64/512/2560 — *inert to 4 decimals.* ISO-budget (=1.6), γ varied 0.05/0.10/0.20/0.50: **0.6109 / 0.6744 / 0.7132 / 0.7230** — a 0.11 spread. **The +0.061 "budget lever" was a γ lever.**
- ⛔⛔ **AND THE REASON γ WINS IS THAT IT TURNS THE PHYSICS OFF.** h-AUROC rises monotonically with γ, and so does `corr(q*, q_last)`: **0.559 (γ=0.05) → 0.972 (γ=0.20) → 0.920 (γ=0.50)**, while `q_last` *with no CLU at all* scores **0.7203**. **CLU's best arm beats "take the last sensor reading" by +0.0027.** *The best configuration is the one where the Hamiltonian rollout does the least.*
- ⭐ **A process finding that became program policy: the scout caught its own tool hallucinating and quarantined the output.** An early WebFetch confidently "quoted" Appendices L and M; the scout then **proved those appendices are not retrievable by any renderer it has** and struck the text as fabricated. **None of it reached the handover.** ⇒ **policy: re-ask every appendix extraction with an explicit "say if absent" guard.** Method notes worth keeping: **`arxiv.org/html/<id>` silently truncates long papers; `ar5iv.labs.arxiv.org/html/<id>` carries the full appendices**; there is **no poppler on this machine** (no agent can render PDF pages).
- **Pre-registration scored honestly:** P1 **4/4 on sign**, FD002/FD004 bands ✅, **FD003 missed high** ❌ (0.771 vs predicted ≤0.76), **FD002/FD004 ordering swapped** ❌ — all reported, none redefined.
- **Verdict move:** Ch. 7 → *unchanged verdicts **+ two Hub-promoted ⭐ findings retracted by the next wave's engineer, with the exact-zero-is-an-overflow-suspect rule and the hallucinated-appendix quarantine as new epistemics exemplars***.

**Ch. 5 / the multi-basin thesis — ⭐ THE CLEANEST MEASURABLE FORM THE MULTI-REGIME CLAIM HAS EVER HAD.** Reproduced FD001 **bit-for-bit, seed-for-seed** against w17 (which is what licenses treating its retractions as authoritative), then measured all four sets (`cmapss-fd002-004-fetch`):

| set | op cond | fault modes | raw_stats 56-d | CLU best | **gap** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FD001 | 1 | 1 | 0.7486 | 0.7230 | **−0.0256** |
| FD003 | 1 | 2 | 0.7971 | 0.7710 | **−0.0261** |
| FD002 | **6** | 1 | 0.6908 | 0.6541 | **−0.0367** |
| FD004 | **6** | 2 | 0.7361 | 0.6734 | **−0.0627** |

**A second fault mode costs −0.0005; six operating conditions cost 1.4×–2.4× more** — multi-regime ≡ multi-basin, in its cleanest measurable form. ⚠ **The op-condition/fault-mode decomposition is POST-HOC — pre-register it before quoting.** ⚠ And note what it is a decomposition *of*: **CLU loses to raw statistics in all four cells.**

**Ch. 4 / the rollout story — ⛔ THE LOAD-BEARING NEGATIVE, and it is worse than the collapse anyone expected.** Rollout MSE vs *"predict `q(n)=q(0)`"*, FD001 — persistence **0.600 / 0.580 / 0.591 / 6.17** at n=1/8/64/256 vs CLU γ=0.5 **0.825 / 1.096 / 1.054 / 6.50** (γ=0.0: 0.825 / 4.256 / 166.7 / 483.7). **The failure is not that the rollout collapses — it is that the rollout is not predictive at all**, while remaining perfectly "bounded" at γ=0.5. ⭐ **This vindicates demanding a bounded-vs-informative diagnostic *before* the rollout experiment**: an error-and-stability score would have reported *"stable, bounded CLU rollout"* and buried the fact that predicting *no change* is uniformly better. **New reusable asset:** `chlu/eval/rollout_diag.py` (+16 tests), collapse length `n* = min{n : S_rel(n) < τ}` at τ=0.01, with documented failure modes (spread is *necessary not sufficient*; blind to rank collapse; `S(0)` anchor-dependent). Measured **`n* > 4096` for every γ on both datasets** ⇒ the pre-registered collapse budget **γT* ∈ [3,30] is REFUTED**, and **boundedness does not discriminate** (every configuration is "bounded", including the γ=0 rollout whose error reaches 484). ⚠ **CONTESTED at w19 (R19-1): the audit measures CLU *winning* at n=1 under a different protocol. Neither persistence number may be quoted until the Hub reconciles them.**

**Benchmark integrity (no chapter — a finding about the world, not about CLU): ⭐ THE CAFE LABEL BUG, QUANTIFIED.** `cmapss.py::_load_split` sets `rul = max_cycle − i` and `e=1` for all windows; **`RUL_FD00x.txt` is never opened**, and the same code runs for train and test. Train recordings run to failure (**train error exactly 0.00, all four sets**); **test recordings are truncated `RUL_unit` cycles early ⇒ 100% of test labels under-estimate, by exactly `RUL_unit`**: mean error **+62.5 / +58.9 / +62.7 / +71.7** (FD001/2/3/4), `P(event by h=125)` CAFE-vs-true **0.891 vs 0.497 · 0.850 vs 0.498 · 0.720 vs 0.376 · 0.699 vs 0.343**. **Worth ≈ +0.19–0.21 h-AUROC.** Under corrected labels on FD001: raw_stats **0.9545**, CLU basin **0.9093**, CLU ALL **0.9054** ⇒ **a 56-d summary-statistics baseline scores 0.9545, ABOVE the README's HEPA 0.918** — the README cells are unusable as a bar. ✅ **Ranking is label-robust** (raw > basin > ALL under both labellings) ⇒ **this program's *relative* conclusions survive — but no CAFE C-MAPSS number is externally comparable.** Forwardable write-up: `.claude/outputs/cmapss-fd002-004-fetch/CAFE_BUG_REPORT.md`.

## Gap-list updates

- **⭐ The 198K head is fully recovered and verifies to the digit** — `Linear(257→256)` 66,048 + `Linear(256→256)` 65,792 + `Linear(256→256)` 65,792 = **197,632**, plus `LayerNorm(256)` 512 + `Linear(256→1)` 257 = **769**, total **198,401 ≈ 198K** (Hub independently re-derived 197,632). The **257 input width pins Δt as ONE raw scalar concatenated to `h_t`**. **The Head's supplied `HEPA_predictor.py` is the ground truth** and closes three gaps: **activation = GELU**, **Δt raw and UNNORMALIZED in native time units**, architecture confirmed. ⇒ **the encoder-swap experiment is now specifiable; it is still unwritten.**
- **⭐ The rollout experiment is RETARGETED.** *"HEPA-iterated"* is dimensionally constructible (`ĥ, h_t, h* ∈ ℝ²⁵⁶`) but **a straw man** — `ĥ` regresses a **bidirectional, attention-pooled read of the FUTURE**, while `h_t` is a **causal read of the PAST**. **The correct experiment is "HEPA-extrapolated": evaluate `g_φ(h_t, Δt)` at `Δt > K`** — a one-line experiment the predictor accepts, testing the intended claim, violating no distribution. **HEPA ships no multi-step path and no beyond-horizon evaluation ⇒ we are not preempted.** ⚠ Beyond-H starts at **Δt=151 (paper)** or **126 (CAFE hard-codes `horizon_max=125`)** — state which.
- **⛔ NEW, and larger than the head or the aggregation: HEPA and CAFE are NOT THE SAME BENCHMARK.** Context **full engine history** vs **`window=30` cycles**; normalization **per-context instance norm** vs **per-channel z-score on train stats**; horizons **K=150** vs **125**. ⚠ **The window difference may specifically handicap CLU** — *a 30-cycle window out of a ~200-cycle degradation gives a dynamical model almost nothing to integrate.* → `future_work.md` (carried as a live confound on the headline number).
- 🔴 **Superseded / corrected this wave (per ⟲ protocol):** the w17 **single-basin collapse** (overflow; ⚠ reinstated-and-contested at w19) and the w17 **budget lever** (γ alone) are **withdrawn at source**; **Table 6 is HEPA 6 of 7, not "8 of 9"** (a Hub error); **TEP is excluded because the public SC-JEPA release has no chemical-process benchmark**, not encoder non-convergence (also a Hub error); **Table 5 shows HEPA winning only 4 of 9 under its own matched-head protocol** ⇒ **soften any "beat the leader" framing**; **Tables 5 and 6 are different protocols (0.73 vs 0.81) and must never appear in one table**; and **MTS-JEPA has been RENAMED to `SC-JEPA`** (arXiv:**2602.04643**, **SDM 2026**, peer-reviewed).

> **⚠ Provenance & scope (read first).** The Hub reviewed w20 (§10 entry, 2026-07-21 night: *"REVIEWED 8/8 + INTEGRATED → main GREEN 515"*) but wrote **no ledger addendum** — this and the two addenda below it are **full curator addenda** for the three-wave arc w20→w22, written against the three §10 review entries and the source reports (headline sections read in full for the ten load-bearing reports). **No output↔handover numeric disagreement found in any of the three waves.** ⟲ protocol respected: chapters untouched, deltas appended, superseded numbers named. ## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 5 / the Chapter-8 candidate — ⭐ THE HEADLINE POSITIVE: capacity is EXPONENTIAL in d; the 8-item ceiling is DEAD.**
- `address-space-dimension-scaling`: **`K_max = 4·2^d` exactly over d=2–8** (log₂K_max = d+2, **R²=1.0000**; 1024 items at d=8, 128× the ring ceiling), blank control passing every cell; **selectivity 1.000 at every K, every d; no washboard death zone at any d** (regime 2 is a 1-D-ring property). **Watch-item W2's promote-trigger resolved in CLU's favour — W2 is CLEARED** (struck, kept as provenance).
- ⚠ **The theorist's packing bound `(1+2R/w)^d` is FALSIFIED as a law** — wrong in form (measured β = 0.96/1.28/1.65 vs required β = d; overestimates 12.7×→1.7e6×). **Replacement, measured:** `K_max = (2R/Δ_req)^{d_eff}` with **`Δ_req ≈ 3.1·max(w, σ_query)`**, `d_eff ≈ 0.72–0.83·d` (predicts base 2.964 to ~10%) — **N70, a falsification-with-repair** in the N52 mold. Caveats that travel: d=12/16 censored at the compute cap; the d=2 prefactor is precision-sensitive (float32 16 / float64 8), the exponent robust.
- 🔴 **And the ceiling on the celebration, named the same wave (sharpened at w21):** the exponential was measured on a **hand-designed analytic landscape**. The learned counterpart fails (below), and w21's parameter ceiling (`B_total ≤ P·b_θ`, N76) makes the designed capacity **jointly unreachable with per-well fibers by the shipped learned MLP**.

**Ch-8 candidate (cont.) — ⛔ THE HEADLINE NEGATIVE: learning destroys everything design provides.**
- `learned-landscape-write-read` (5 seeds): **no learned rung clears 0.9 at both K=4 and K=8**; minimum viable design = **essentially the whole designed landscape** (rung 1, marginal). Write locality designed **0.000** → learned **2.9e-2…5.0e-1**; the best rung is **destroyed by ONE subsequent write (1.000 → 0.000)**. w19's 4.17e-7 is **designed-only, always**. (⚠ w21 amends the *curve*, not the point: a better learned family passes rung 1 cleanly and w20's `local_rbf` failure was an init artifact — "what an **MLP** learned landscape can do," N67.)
- ⭐ **Method finding, program-wide (N68):** the w19 blank-control protocol is too weak — nearest-centroid blanks score **0.992–1.000 on empty landscapes** (a 1e-4 leak = a perfect item code). Blanks over the strongest read; value-recovery over classification.
- `primitive-harness`: **CLU wins 0 of 3 families** (recall mid-tier vs attention flat 0.996; adding at the no-mixing floor 0.182 vs 0.183; parity at chance 0.538), cost 1.41× GRU wall-clock at 0.48× attention FLOPs (N69).

**Ch. 7 (epistemics) — ⛔ THE HUB'S OWN PREDICTION FALSIFIED, AS THE AGENT PRE-REGISTERED.** The task file the Hub wrote asserted *"attention degrades from interference and CLU does not (Prop 2)"*; measured: **attention drop 0.001, CLU drop 0.137**. **Prop-2-as-a-recall-advantage-over-attention is RETIRED** (survives as the fiber answer to Ramsauer + partial support for the capacity axis). ⭐ **The one genuinely CLU-favourable measurement is on the capacity axis:** GRU 0.997→0.008 (chance) vs CLU 0.525→0.154 over kv=2→16, crossover kv≈8. ⛔ **The "19×/17×-chance" gloss on that number is CORRECTED AT W22 to 2.1× (un-rescued GRU; N78) — never quote this addendum's crossover without the correction.** Other Ch. 7 entries: the dim-scaling agent **retracted its own draft caveat** (censored points invert the headline) and reported its own failed consistency check; the learned-landscape agent **overturned its own single-seed answer with its own 5-seed check**; the Hub broke the merge (absorbed `@dataclass` decorators) and caught it.

**Ch. 4 + the architecture — the two-phase mechanism is ACCEPTED, with three mandatory amendments; the controller is SPECIFIED.**

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 7 (epistemics) — ⛔ FOUR HUB PREDICTIONS FALSIFIED IN ONE WAVE, all pre-registered against, all by different agents.**
1. *"γ=0.05 is too dissipative ⇒ the 0-of-3 is an artifact"* — **REFUTED** (`gamma-read-sweep` §1): adding flat to **0.5σ** over γ∈[0,0.1], no transition anywhere incl. the derived γ\*=0.0108; 16/19 preregs clean, 0 falsified. **N73.**
2. *"H-SUPP: a transformer potential is more global ⇒ fails worse than the MLP"* — **REFUTED** (`potential-function-class`): sharp modern-Hopfield corrupts **26.7× LESS**; attention's support is `exp(−β·gap)`, a tunable locality knob.
3. *"The admission gate suppresses interference ~5 orders"* — **REFUTED** (`sequential-write-interference`): the gate **cannot fire** on the w20 geometry (spacing 1.4142 vs d_safe 1.10 ⇒ gated ≡ ungated to all digits); on a learned landscape it preserves items **only by refusing to store** (C3: 0/300 commits, strict-B 0.000). **N74.**
4. *"Anharmonicity separates mass from landscape"* — **REFUTED to 1e-15** (`relaxation-fiber-capacity`): the V↔M degeneracy is an **exact gauge of the whole trajectory for any V**. **N76.**
⇒ **Six Hub claims now overturned by agents who checked (w20: 1; w21: 4 + the trajectory-read prediction).** Registered as a cross-thread pattern in the registry.

**⭐⭐ The convergent replacement — the WRITE OPERATOR (three independent tasks, one law).** Masked/local write **70×** (identical class/params/budget) · **anchored write (codebook rehearsal) 103×, the ONLY arm that both preserves AND stores (strict-A 1.000, strict-B 0.975)** · function class 27× · spacing gate **1.00×**. **The transferable law:** interference is governed by the **C3 ratio `‖∇δV(q*)‖/λ_min(Hess V(q*))` at the stored items** — measured across 6 function classes and 4.6 decades (meas/pred ∈ [0.73,1.63] for 5/6); the registered support-radius statistic (Spearman 0.143) falsified; evaluate at the relaxed fixed point. **Two routes to safety: shrink the numerator (local write, 1 070×) or inflate the denominator (stiff wells, 13–29×).** ⭐ The gate IS decisive on a **designed** store: item-1 retention **1.000 vs 0.16** at the price of **6.0±0.9/16 admitted** (= packing bound 6.1 to 2%) — *refusal is the mechanism; always quote the capacity price.*

**Ch-8 candidate — ⛔ CLU is the WORST of four primitives under sequential parametric writes** (K=64 retention gru 0.57 / mlp 0.43 / attn 0.34 / **CLU 0.16**; joint criterion censored 0.00→1.00 between K=8 and K=32 — *what catastrophic forgetting looks like when you instrument the write loop*). The Head's "wasted compute reorganising" hypothesis **REFUTED as stated** (compute-to-criterion flat in K, 0.77–1.82×) and replaced by **infeasibility**. ⚠ "CLU degrades more gracefully than the GRU" = contextual-MQAR-only; both results carry the label. **N75.**

**⭐ The real cause of the 0-of-3 — found by a 90-second information probe** (`gamma-read-sweep` recon 3): the write current `p += W_in x_t` is **unconditionally LINEAR in the token** ⇒ the state carries `Σv_t` (R²=1.000) and marker positions (R²≈0.99) at every γ while the value×marker **conjunction sits at R²≈0.02–0.09** — *the history is not lost; the conjunction was never representable.* A multiplicative gate buys **65× (0.1816→0.0028)** — ⚠ **catching up to an ingredient every baseline already has, not a win**; its pre-registered follow-up became w22's `gated-write-performance`. Also: the shipped read-mode axis is **DEGENERATE** at `clu_steps=1` (endpoint ≡ trajectory bit-exactly), and the trajectory read **never helps**.

**Ch. 2 — ⭐ THE FIBER, CORRECTED: mass SELECTS, it does not STORE (Prop F1).** The read depends on `(M,V,p₀)` only through `(M⁻¹∇V, M⁻¹p₀)` (verified 6.2e-16) ⇒ mass contributes `d−k` observable dimensions, **ZERO for any separable V** (8.6 bits coupled vs 0.20 separable — *separability, not harmonicity, is the killer*). **The fiber's only storing channel is the local jet of `G = M⁻¹∇V`**; `M`/`p₀` are address-side; γ = 0 per-item bits; temperature = noise floor. **Composition corrected: `B_total = K_spatial · B_fiber` (slots × bits, never items × items).** ⛔ **The parameter ceiling `B_total ≤ P·b_θ`** (MLP 1377 params vs ≥36 864 reals needed at d=8) makes measured spatial capacity + per-well fibers **jointly unreachable by the shipped learned MLP**. ⭐ New quotable role for the program's headline physics: **the relativistic governor is what gauge-fixes M against V, making inertia observable** (∝(v/c)² slope 1.989; ∝N slope 1.03; +2 bits/octave of v/c). Fiber numbers only dressed with `(σ_rel, N, launches)`; **read length is exponential in bits**. **N76.**

**Positioning (scout) — three strategic claims dead, one target found, three prior-art debts closed.** ⛔ **Capacity-per-parameter: we LOSE (≈1.1–1.6 bits/param vs the transformer's 2 — always carry the denominator).** ⛔ **Retrieval-cost-independent-of-items: FALSE for shipped code (O(steps·K·d)) and occupied where true** (PKM √K; Memory Layers at Scale 128B at zero FLOPs). ⛔ **MQAR is structurally unwinnable (Arora Thm 3.1, Ω(N))** — cite once, move on. ⭐ **The one admissible benchmark: the modern-Hopfield capacity protocol** (nothing learned on either side ⇒ the w20 blocker does not bind) — became w22's `hopfield-capacity-benchmark`. ⭐ Hu–Wu–Liu: optimal Hopfield capacity = optimal spherical codes `M* ≍ c^D` — **positioning gift + novelty risk: the exponential is the field's own headline; ours is the measured constant/mechanism.** ⚠ **Titans (NeurIPS 2025) = the nearest published neighbour to CLU's write** (test-time GD-with-momentum on an associative-memory loss = discretized damped second-order dynamics) — must be cited. **UnICORNN verified: "symplectic ⇒ long-horizon stability" is OCCUPIED** (with a `(NΔt)³` theorem we lack). ✅ SRNN closed, not a preemption; Ramsauer's constant closed (Lambert-W via Hu–Wu–Liu). **N77.**
