This pass owns all six.

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**A closing wave should convert and it should kill, and C1W27 did both.**

**(1) R2 closed — and the mechanism inverted underneath the wave's own name.** w26's unclamping was not a d=4 artefact: `K_learned` goes **32 → 128 at d=6** — *pinned*, bracketed above by a measured **K=256 FAIL at 0.7358** against a designed arm at 1.0000 — and **32 → ≥128 at d=8**, against a `K_designed(d) = 4·2^d` that **re-measures exactly at d = 4, 6 and 8 on one metric with zero free parameters.** But the arm that carries d ≥ 6 is **arm (b), the annealed read — zero extra bytes, dimensions, parameters or Verlet steps** — while **the m=4 multi-channel code the wave was named for is a d=4-only win** that at d=6 buys nothing and *destroys* K=128 (strict **0.0906**, basin 0.4448). ⭐ **And the engineer measured why, on the wrong side of the system:** strip the payload excursion and the write loses the pressure that keeps wells narrow, so it widens the atoms **2.3–2.7×**, `2·w_atom` exceeds the site separation, neighbouring wells merge, and **the address collapses** — *a write-side side-effect of a read-side change*, invisible to a laundering control because the designed arm has no write. The two arms are **perfect substitutes** (`I = −0.1509`, **18.3 SE**; arm (b) buys **+0.1507** on m=1 and **−0.0002** on m=4) ⇒ **the combination is strictly dominated by arm (b) alone.**

**(2) The last win-route died by measurement, and the program is better for it.** With byte accounting pinned, the learned store reaches **0.0243 bits/param** at its best cell — **~47× below the designed construction (1.14)** and **~82× below a transformer's 2.** *Moving the wall 4× moved only the numerator.* ⇒ **all three contested-win candidates are now closed.** ⚠ And the Hub was wrong twice in its own prior entry and the engineer corrected it: **w21's "~1.3 vs 2" is the DESIGNED construction's number — it was never the learned store's and it was never "stale"**, and the ranking of Candidate 3 as *"now the strongest of the three"* is **refuted.**

**(3) R1 lost its capacity qualifier entirely.** A ~20-line waitlist makes canonical deletion byte-exact at **every load measured, 0.29×–1.71× lattice capacity** (AUC **0.5000 ± 0.0000** on all six statistics, byte-equal **1.0000**), with the exact replacement-scope sentence delivered and its three conditions named. And **`R₅₀` survives the gated-stiffness fix** (1.135 → 0.771 vs baseline 1.146 → 0.752) — so the lifetimes dial keeps its one adversary-caveat-free differentiator. ⛔ **B3 is falsified as stated**: payload-independence is an **effect size** (retention std **0.274 → 0.016**, worst codeword **0.398 → 0.972**), not a correlation (`|r|` only 0.846 → 0.667) — *a correlation is the wrong statistic once the thing it correlates with stops varying.*

**(4) A causal test succeeded and immediately narrowed its own theory.** `learned_confine` **0.05 → 0.022** gives `Δstrict = +0.0293`, **3/3 seeds**, inside the registered band — so **(★) is causal, out of sample, on a knob nobody had moved.** ⚠ **And the same run corrected the theory twice**: the `2α|c|` product claim is **approximate** (a 2.27× α cut needs only a **1.64×** `|c|` cut, because `|c|` also enters via `L`), and **`D_fit` is α-contaminated (≈0.37α)**, so w26's `D = 0.910 / 0.459` are **≈0.89 / 0.44**. ⭐ The dial itself is **logarithmic**: 2.27× buys **+9.3 %** reach and the `|a| = 1.0` rung is **unreachable at any α ≥ 1e-4.**

**(5) And a positioning pass found a citation defect in our own foundations.** ⛔⛔ **"Guo et al., ICML 2020, Def. 1 / Def. 2" DOES NOT EXIST** — the arXiv source has **no `definition` environment at all**; ε-certified removal is **§3 Eq. (1)**, inline. Ginart **Def. A.5** and Sekhari **Def. 3** are confirmed verbatim — and **A.5 is about amortized TIME**, while the shipped `_canonical_sync` is a full **O(n)** rebuild per op ⇒ **no Ginart-A.5 efficiency sentence is supportable** until **M1**.

**The rhyme.** w26's was *the controls did the science.* **C1W27's is: the closing wave audited its own citations and found the defect in the definition it had been leaning on for four waves.** The three most consequential things this wave produced — the write-side mechanism behind a read-side change, the α-contamination of a depth, and a definition that does not exist — were all found by **checking something the program already believed.**

# ⟲ C2W1 addendum — the wave that assembled the whole thing and measured its dividend at zero (2026-07-31, doc-curator `c1w27-c2w1-sync`, **[C2W3]**) — **the full CLU runs and does not collapse, and the dividend at v0 is ZERO OR NEGATIVE ON EVERY TASK FAMILY MEASURED — the charter's own null hypothesis, met honestly**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope.** Sources: the six C2W1 reports (`full-clu-harness`, `memory-gym-v0`, `trainability-spike`, `trainability-spike-theory`, `controller-doctrine`, `rival-recon`) and **both** `[C2W1]` §10 entries (2026-07-30; **6/6 ACCEPTED**, integrated → `main @ 233fd9e`, **880 green**, zero shared files between branches). **Every headline was re-derived by the Hub from raw artifacts digit-for-digit** and is transcribed here with its source. ⟲ protocol respected: no chapter rewritten, nothing deleted. **This is C2W1's primary ledger record.**

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**C2W1 asked two questions and got two answers, and the second one is a negative that the charter had already written down.**

**Can the full CLU be assembled and run without collapsing? Yes.** The harness runs the full system on a learned `V_θ` (**57 KB of atoms, no per-item arrays in the read path**), with masked local writes, admission, lifetimes, deletion and the controller v0 over the designed verbs, on a 10-offer stream with real capacity pressure and a deletion demand — tripping **only #9 lifetimes**, which the engineer's own PREREG had declared **uncleanable by any verb**. ⭐ And the annealed read works as a **controller verb** inside it: acquisition **0.828 → 1.000**, decode **0.906 → 1.000**, clearing modes #5 and #9 simultaneously at **+0 bytes**.

**What is its dividend? Zero, or negative, everywhere anyone looked.** ≈0 or negative on **all four** independent gym families; **exactly `0.0000` with `D = 0`** at the one configuration where the store works perfectly — the settle and the arg-min launder agree on essentially every query. That is **Prop D2a, reproduced three times independently** (harness R1/R3 · the gym's shipped anchor · the gym's `D`-vs-dividend analysis). ⛔ **Worse for the program and better for the science: the trivial-substitute audit is 0-for-4** — a **+0 B** substitute of the launder's own table matches or beats the CLU on every family, and wins outright on two (insertion order **0.776** vs 0.302; echo **1.0000** vs −0.180). ⛔ And **at `sep/σ_q` 3.07 the CLU reads 0.479 while its own launder holds 0.854** — *the physics is not buying anything separability was not already providing, and it loses more than the table does when separability is removed.* The engineer did **not** roll that stage back to flatter the acceptance table.

**And pillar 1 — the highest-novelty pillar — failed a pre-registered liveness gate before its own ablation was allowed to run.** 0 qualifying cells of **588 per seed × 3 seeds = 1764**, at healthy geometry (`sep/σ_q` 6.83/6.76/6.92), across `γ_read ∈ [0, 0.2]` **including `γ_read = 0` where Liouville forbids an attractor and the trajectory is all there is**; the only cross-seed-replicating effect is **31–63 % reproduced by a store with nothing in it.** ⭐ **The Hub's blocking Stage-0 amendment is the reason this is a finding and not an artefact:** launched as written, the ablation would have swept a dead axis, produced a flat result and filed it — under its own *"does NOT falsify"* clause — as *"pillar 1's first honest datum."*

**The wave's one unambiguous positive is structural, not empirical.** **`∂q*/∂q₀ = 0` exactly** — `‖∂L/∂φ‖` is **0.0** (implicit) / **2.654e-9** (unroll) / **6.421e-3** (trajectory), ratio **2.42e6** — so **a settled-point read sends no gradient to its read-in.** It is the exact form of N61, it retro-explains w19's learned-addressing death, and it is a **benchmark-free** argument that a trajectory read is the only trainable read. ⭐ **Alongside it, a theorem the program will keep after everything else is spent:** `ratio = 1.4·A + 0.8`, **independent of K**, verified to **1e-9 in all 28 cells** ⇒ **matched bytes is unreachable by construction** at `≥ 2.20×`. Not a caveat. A theorem.

**The rhyme.** C1's rhyme at close was *the controls did the science.* **C2W1's is: the gate did the science.** A pre-registered axis-liveness gate stopped a dead sweep from becoming a datum; a mandatory substitute audit turned a "0.0000 dividend" into a *0-for-4*; a byte ledger turned a caveat into a theorem; and the one clean positive of the wave came from a **gradient norm**, not from a task.

# ⟲ C2W2 addendum — the wave that fired its own gate (2026-07-31, doc-curator `c1w27-c2w1-sync`, **[C2W3]**) — **the write was asked, explicitly and in two independent ways, to put information somewhere other than the endpoint; it refused in both, monotonically; and the charter mechanism designed to make it possible was refuted in SIGN on a learned store**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope.** Sources: the five C2W2 reports (`traj-write-objective`, `ssb-shell-atoms`, `phi-particle-head`, `doctrine-repairs`, `track2-admissibility`) + `track2-admissibility/PREREG-Bprime.md`, the **live 2026-07-31 `[C2W2]` §10 review entry** (**5/5 ACCEPTED**, integrated → `main @ 6ff4c1d`, **984 green**, **fourth consecutive zero-conflict wave**, every headline re-derived by the Hub from raw artefacts), and charter **ADDENDUM 2 §A7–§A11**. ⛔ **Scope taken from the §10 entry and Addendum 2, not from any single spoke report**, per the curator task. ⟲ protocol respected. **This is C2W2's primary ledger record.**
> ⛔ **C2W3's own results are NOT folded** — they are not measured yet.

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The gate fired, and the grade is not close.** Charter §A5 asked: *if writes that **explicitly ask** trajectories/paths to carry information still give dividend ≤ 0 on every family, both routes, multi-seed, substitute-audited ⇒ **B′ activates**.* **Route 1: `any_family_clears = False`** — 9 `le_zero_vote` arms; `aggregate` and `manifold` **ABSTAIN 0/12 each** after spending their one bounded escalation. **Route 2: `any_family_clears = False`** — **90 cells, 16 `le_zero_vote` · 12 `abstain` · 2 `under_powered_grid`**, best multi-seed arm **−0.0072**, best single cell **+0.0005 (n=1)** against an `echo` substitute of **1.0000**. ⛔⛔ **`B′` ACTIVATES; the grade is a clean ≤0, and the §A6 weak-proceed caveat is moot because nothing cleared.**

**(2) And the honest reading is sharper than "no effect": it is a MONOTONE COST.** On the one family where the store demonstrably works, the dividend goes **−0.0278 (endpoint-only control) → −0.1111 → −0.1806 → −0.1944 → −0.2639** across a three-decade coefficient grid; the **path** term costs more than the **traj** term; the two together cost **−0.4722.** ⭐ **With a measured mechanism, not a shrug:** the recorded write loss is **exactly `L_endpoint + 0.55·λ` at every λ from 0.03 to 300** — *a constant the optimiser never reduces* — because a **60-step** path reaches only **0.27–0.50** of a payload the read climbs over **400** steps, so the path read-out is dominated by the near-zero early path and is nearly θ-independent. ⚠ **The engineer declared the scope without being asked: "the null is for `rollout_steps = 60`."** ⛔ **And the Advisor's ruling travels with it: the read-length re-price is REPLACED, not deferred** — §A8.1 rules the read-length requirement *an artifact of the payload convention, not intrinsic*, because if the latent is the visited state itself the store acts from the first step (`−M⁻¹∇V`, and **`∇V` IS the store**), with **momentum at small t almost pure store** while position at small t is almost pure query. **The store-attribution curve replaces rollout length as the design-deciding quantity.**

**(3) A charter mechanism was refuted in SIGN — and the refutation is more useful than the mechanism would have been.** §A4.2 said a small tilt makes `λ_min = ε > 0`. Measured: tilt **monotonically REDUCES** `λ_min` — **+0.0994 → −8.28** (envelope), **+0.3291 → −1.198** (depth) — on **two independent implementations** and **every family**. ⭐ **And the cause is measured, not guessed: a designed degeneracy does not survive superposition.** `tilt_vacuum_residual` is **0.140–0.343** against an ideal of 0 and a *random-orientation* baseline of **`1/dim = 0.167`**, with a group's atom-centre spread **1.19–1.95× the designed shell radius** ⇒ **a written site is not a common vacuum of its own atoms; it is at or WORSE than random.** ⭐ **`λ = ε` holds only in the single-atom geometry it was specified in** (unit-tested to 5 %). ⇒ **the pseudo-Goldstone ruling survives as GEOMETRY; its shipped instantiation does not.** And the lifetime half dies with it: the damped-mode floor **`τ = 2/Γ = 5.0` is CONFIRMED (4.23–6.58)** and the above-knee slope **0** confirmed, but **the `1/ε` branch is unreachable** because the shipped confinement floors `λ_soft` at **2α, 2.5× above the knee** ⇒ **`τ_max = Γ/2α = 4.0`; α is the ceiling, and lowering α breaks the write.**

**(4) A third failure mode entered the taxonomy, and it is the quiet one.** Learned shell radius `r` moves **0.500 → 0.501** in 300 steps. **w20 said free learning *erases* design. C2W2 adds: design the objective cannot SEE is neither erased nor used.** The parameter is trainable, reachable and correctly initialised — and no term in the loss reads it, so it simply does not move. ⚠ Route 2's failures are therefore **objective-visibility and geometry, not optimizer quality** (§A8.4), and the write objective is now **declared open to redesign** under three invariants: coefficient-zero bit-identity · a liveness anchor · per-slot laundering.

**(5) But the wave is not empty, and both positives are PRECONDITIONS rather than dividends — which is exactly how they must be filed.** ⭐⭐ **§A2.2 is SUPPORTED, for the first time in 29 waves:** a trajectory read sends **1.74e-3 / 1.17e-2 / 1.88e-3** to a per-query **mass** and **2.4e-2 / 4.1e-2 / 3.3e-2** to a per-query **friction**, while the settled-point arm sends **8.7e-9 / 4.0e-8 / 1.1e-8** (unroll) and **exactly 0.0, BITWISE** (implicit) ⇒ ratios **1.7e5–2.9e5** and **2.6e5–4.9e5**, 3/3 seeds, cross-checked against float64 finite differences. **"Mass as selector" is live**, the point arm's zero is **structural** (Prop Q1.1: `∇V` contains neither `M` nor `γ`), and **friction is the ~14× stronger channel.** ⭐⭐ **Filed jointly with C2W1's `∂q*/∂q₀ = 0` as ONE statement: a settled-point read is untrainable end-to-end, IN BOTH DIRECTIONS.** ⭐⭐ **And §A2.5 was adjudicated, both ways at once, by a structural discovery nobody had made:** the shipped harness sets **`d_safe := 2s_max + κ′σ_q`**, so **the admission radius IS the certificate radius** — the two "mutually exclusive" bands are **one object counted twice.** Decoupled (`d_safe = 0.6·sep`), **11 of 12 configs clear all 13 bands while violating the certificate by 1.5–32.9 % of `sep`**, `ρ_ex` up to **6.3×** at a `λ_min` price of 2.2–6.0×. ⛔ **And the theorist read it honestly in the same breath: the dividend there stays ≈0 (+0.0043 … −0.0067), exactly as §A2.1 predicts. The non-separable region is a precondition, not a result.**

**(6) And the instrument got materially more honest in one wave.** A **harness defect** found and fixed (`queries_recency` grades a **K-way** answer on a **2-way** curve; the CLU's answer falls outside its own pair **19.4 %** of the time ⇒ the "below-chance recency null" was a **scoring-domain artefact**, post-fix **−0.0028 ± 0.0619**). The **last escape hatch closed** (6× the write budget moves decode by **−0.014 / +0.056** against a registered **≥ +0.25**, plateau **0.20–0.22** unmoved ⇒ *"the store was never written"* becomes **a capacity statement**). An **`AttentionPsi` leak caught before it could contaminate a headline** (`q0_only` **0.35–0.45** vs a bar of **0.19**, at every stride). **31 of 58** monitor-#6 trips shown to be ε-artefacts with a **10-order** separation and a threshold whose decision is **invariant over 12.8 orders** (58 pre-repair → **27**, **0 new trips**, every other monitor bit-identical over 28 cells). **Monitor #3's validity leg replaced, not repaired** (spearman **+0.914 vs +0.412**, 0/12 sign flips vs 1/12, at zero extra cost). And a rig proven **bit-faithful across two independent branches** (the `gauss` control reproduces `memory-gym-v0` digit-for-digit; the r=0 gate is bit-identical on 12/12 cells in f32 **and** f64, including at `q == c`).

**The rhyme.** C2W1's was *the gate did the science.* **C2W2's is: the wave that fired its own gate had already written down what it would do next, and what would kill that too.** `PREREG-Bprime.md` — **P1–P5 with its own falsifiers FB1–FB5** — was **written before the gate was adjudicated.** *That is the difference between a fallback and a retreat.*

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

# ⟲ C2W3 addendum — the wave that built its own kill-condition early and then had it fire (2026-07-31, doc-curator `c2w3-sync`, **[C2W4]**) — **a protocol gate cleared the instrument on one family out of four; the anti-lookup kill fired on the route the whole wave was built around; and the frame under which any of it counts as evidence was rewritten the same day**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope.** Sources: the four C2W3 spoke reports (`bprime-fb4-gate`, `route3-stage1-plus-2x2`, `bprime-theory`, `bprime-fb1-recon`) + the curator pass `doc-curator-c1w27-c2w1-sync`; the **live `2026-07-31 (later still)` `[C2W3]` §10 review entry** (**5/5 accepted**, both branches integrated → `main @ d4f56c8`, **1061 green**, **fifth consecutive zero-conflict wave**, every decision-grade number re-derived by the Hub from raw artefacts); the **`[C2W4]`** scoping entry; and charter **ADDENDUM 3 §A12–§A15**. ⛔ **Scope taken from the §10 entry and Addendum 3, not from any single spoke report.** ⟲ protocol respected — **no chapter body edited, nothing deleted.**
> ⛔ **THE HUB WROTE NO ⟲ ADDENDUM FOR C2W3** (verified: the file ended at ⟲ C2W2 before this pass). **This is C2W3's primary ledger record, not a `(curator supplement)`.**
> ⛔⛔ **AND THE FIRST FACT, BECAUSE EVERYTHING ELSE IS READ AGAINST IT: C2W3 IS NOT A COMPLETE WAVE.** `bprime-rivals` — the audit paper's spine — was **released by FB4 and FB1 and never spawned.** **Not one rival memory was built; B′'s cross-family table still reads `need` in every non-CLU row.** ⛔ **Do not read "5/5 accepted" as "the wave delivered B′."**

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The instrument was put on trial, and it half-passed — which is the useful outcome.** FB4 asked the question a referee would ask before reading a single number: *is the +0 B substitute at ceiling on every family, including full attention?* If yes, the protocol measures the task rather than the memory and the whole audit paper is void. **It is not yes.** `S(f)` = **1.0000 / 0.5068 / 1.0000 / 1.0000** — pre-registered before the run and hit exactly, `S(aggregate)` **to three decimals**. ⭐ **So the honest sentence is not "everything is at ceiling" and not "the protocol is fine": it is *on three of four families something costing ≤4 B sits at the metric's exact maximum, and it is never the CLU.*** On the one family where the store demonstrably works, the CLU reads **0.9722 ± 0.0139** while **three different readers of a table costing 1/478th of its bytes** read **1.0000**. ⭐ **And the survivor survives for a reason that generalises into a build rule:** `aggregate`'s target is a convex combination **dropped at construction if it lands on a stored payload** — *the answer is provably not in the table*. ⭐⭐ **That is the only family property that has survived a +0 B audit in two waves (0-for-4, then 1-of-4), and §A3.7's four design rules are now a standing construction standard.**

**(2) The wave's own route died, and it died in the cheapest possible place — because someone moved the kill-condition forward.** Route 3 asked whether an item's information can live at strided waypoints of the trajectory. Stage 1's unlock bar **cleared**: `unlock = true`, **18 clearing rows**, both channels, 3/3 seeds. ⛔⛔ **And §A9.5's anti-lookup kill — which was a stage-2 obligation until a Hub scoping decision moved it into stage 1 — fired in the same run: a table of `K = 6` time-indexed rows, 478× cheaper than the store, reproduces 37 of 38 slots, all 18 clearing slots, and the read beats it by >0.10 at exactly ZERO slots.** ⭐⭐ **And the kill has a measured mechanism rather than a shrug, which is what makes it a finding instead of a disappointment: the flow map is contractive within an item's launch cloud from the first slot (plateau 0.218, 4.6× noise suppression) while between-item separation GROWS (+15 %), so the Fisher ratio climbs six orders — 6.3 → 1.47e7. A map that is contractive within an item and separated across items sends every query of an item to one value per slot, and that is exactly what K rows express.** ⭐ ***The kill is a consequence of the medium being in the productive regime, not of it being broken.*** ⛔ **Independently and fatally: slot count buys no per-item capacity** — measured rank **13 = 3d+1** at `d=4` for every `S ≥ 2` (and **4 = d** under the shipped read) ⇒ **a per-slot table can hold strictly MORE per-item content than the CLU can generate.**

**(3) A charter claim was confirmed at one integrator step and refuted in the same breath, and both halves are exact.** ✅ **The store acts from the first step**: at `t = 0.05`, `D = 0.559` against a store-deleted launder of **0.268** and a launch-noise floor of **0.154** — **`∇V` IS the store and it enters immediately.** ⛔ **But §A8.1's q/p split is refuted as stated, and the reason is arithmetic rather than statistical**: the shipped read launches at `p₀ = 0` with `q₀`'s payload channels zeroed, so Verlet gives `q₁ − q₀ = dt·p₁/m` — the first position increment is a **positive multiple** of the first momentum, hence rank-identical (**0.5590 both, to four decimals**). ⭐ **Where the Advisor is right is the ADDRESS block, and more strongly than claimed**: position's address block is **pure query at ALL t** (dividend **exactly 0.000** at every slot), while momentum's is **destroyed by the store** (0.319–0.556 vs 1.000) because `p ∝ (c_i − q)` collapses every query of an item onto one vector. ⚠ **Declared post-hoc secondary diagnostic — and that label travels with it forever.**

**(4) The 2×2 closed C2W2's most-flagged unrun cell, and the unrun cell was the worst cell.** Interaction **−0.1250**: the two routes are **sub-additive** — combining them costs *more* than the sum of their separate costs — so *"an objective that can see a path makes the designed degeneracy pay"* is **refuted in sign**. ⭐ **And C2W2's headline gets sharper rather than repeated:** the path term **does** move the learned shell radius, **0.49854 → 0.50252 (+0.0040, 16 SE of the endpoint arm's spread — it IS visible)**, but that is **12× below** the registered *"the objective can see it"* bar while the same term costs **0.209 of dividend** and **0.9–1.1 of `λ_min`** ⇒ ⭐ ***the path term is ~50× more effective at damaging the write than at shaping the design.*** ⭐ The rig reproduced C2W2's control **digit-for-digit across a third independent branch**.

**(5) The theory wave was the one that paid, and it paid three times.** ⭐⭐ **T3 holds as ONE statement in both directions** — `Fix(T_θ)` contains no transient parameter, so a settled-point read is untrainable end-to-end — **and it retro-explains w19's death with zero fitted parameters**: five decades of the C1 γ-scan reproduced to within **0.92–8.9×**, and at C1's 3000-step probe the address gradient is **10^−33.4, twenty-six orders below float32 eps**. ⭐ *Gradient descent was optimising round-off, and every "weak encoder" failure of the last year was a `C ≈ 18` failure.* ⭐⭐ **T1 made the byte floor into a trade with an exchange rate**: matched bytes needs **`S* = 2387`** items per shared atom at the shipped anchor, and at matched bytes **at most 0.042 % of an item's parameter mass can remain byte-exactly deletable** ⇒ ***the property that makes deletion exact is the property that makes compression impossible.*** ⭐⭐ **And T5 answered the question stage 2 could not have answered for itself**: the one coupling a per-slot table provably cannot express is **third-party store attribution** — and it is suppressed as `exp(−½(d/s)²)`, i.e. **7.0e-4 at the gate we designed, 0.111 in the soft-certificate region, and 0.69–0.80 at the rig we actually run.** ⛔ **A 1089× span set by one configuration flag — and the §A9.5-satisfying measurement was therefore available all wave, at zero build cost, and was not made.**

**(6) The instrument got more honest again, and once it caught us.** SC-1…SC-7 landed default-off, and the **SC-1 demonstration reproduced the `d_safe_override` hack's admissions bit-for-bit** — ⭐ ***the override WAS the soft certificate, undeclared*** — showing that arm had been running at **6.0× the declared violation budget with nothing saying so**. Monitor #3's correlation leg was **retired** across a 15-cell diff in which **every `full`/`launder`/`dividend` number is bit-identical and zero trips were added**. `AttentionPsi` was **quarantined in code — it raises, it does not warn**. ⛔ **And the trajectory launder fired on 2 of 24 cells** — which is how we discovered that **`to_race_cell` had silently dropped that launder in C2W2, so every Route-2 race cell reported `fired = False` by absence, not by measurement.** ⭐⭐ ***An absent measurement recorded as a passing control is the exact failure mode this ledger exists to catch, and it was caught by a control firing rather than by an audit.***

**(7) And then the frame moved.** ⛔⛔ **Charter §A13's claim architecture v3 supersedes the claim framing of §2.1/§A5 wherever they conflict** — and it does so by naming a **mis-specification that has been silently present in every dividend number this program has ever reported**: ⭐⭐ ***the settle-deleted launder tests whether inference-time dynamics beat a table GIVEN THE ORGANIZATION — both arms inherit the same placement. It has NEVER tested whether physics-trained organization beats non-physics-trained organization.*** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **a CLU that organizes the latent space well and then reads like a cheap table at inference scores dividend ≈ 0 BY DESIGN — and that is a FEATURE (an inference-cost win), not a failure.**

**The rhyme.** C2W1's was *the gate did the science*. C2W2's was *the wave that fired its own gate had already written down what it would do next*. ⭐⭐ **C2W3's is: the wave built the thing that could kill it before it built the thing, and then read the result without flinching — and on the same day discovered that the ruler it had been holding for thirty waves measures a different quantity than the one the program cares about.** *Both of those are wins. Only one of them feels like one.*

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 8 — ⭐⭐ verdict UNCHANGED, but for the first time the chapter's SUBJECT is explicitly superseded.** `[split — lookup refuted, indexing refuted, control validated-with-scope]` stands. What is new is not a dial moving but the **frame around all four dials**: §A13's three tiers, **each with its own control, and controls move UP a level, they never relax** (tier i the settle-deleted launder · tier ii the **organizer swap** · tier iii the **system-level swap**). ⛔ **The chapter is now, unambiguously, a Campaign-1 chapter with four Campaign-2 addenda attached, and its governing document is the charter.** ⭐ **The chapter's honest sentence acquires its sharpest form yet:** *for thirty waves we measured whether the dynamics beat a table at reading a store; we never once measured whether the dynamics beat anything at building one.*

**Ch. 5 (the lattice / scale idea) — ⭐ the byte floor stops being a caveat and becomes a TRADE.** **N177**: `ratio(S) = A_tot(D+2)/[S(d+m)] + d/(d+m)`, `S* = (D+2)A_tot/m` (**7** at one atom per item, **2387** at the shipped anchor), and the byte-exactly-deletable fraction is capped at **0.042 %** at matched bytes, with exchange rate **`dp/dr = 2.10e-3`**. ⭐ **The transferable sentence — *the property that makes deletion exact is the property that makes compression impossible* — belongs to this chapter, and it is the first time the byte floor has said something about a DIFFERENT capability rather than about itself.** ⚠ **And a dated correction lands with it: the published closed form was wrong on 4 of 28 cells** (**N167**) — ⭐ **conservatively wrong, so nothing was inflated.**

**Ch. 4 (boost / cascade / test-time compute) — ⚠ a pointer, and it is a boundary rather than a movement.** The waypoint idea — read the trajectory at strided points, spend compute to get more out of one store — is **closed for inference-read claims** (**N173**), and **closed by arithmetic for per-item claims at any budget** (**N174**). ⭐ **What survives is the shape of the only possible successor: cross-item, and gated on a coupling that our own admission gate suppresses by 1089×** (**N180**). ⚠ **No chapter body is edited.**

**Ch. 1 (mode budget / Goldstone) — no movement.** C2W2's refutation-in-sign stands; the tilt-direction probe that could separate *"§A4.2 is wrong"* from *"§A4.2 was instantiated wrong"* is **still unrun**, and is now **demoted from a claim venue to a mechanism check** by §A14.8.

**Ch. 2 (mass-as-allocator) — ⚠ one scope note, no movement.** T3 makes the C2W2 positive **exact**: the mass gauge dissolves because `Fix(T_θ)` contains no `M` — **a statement about the READ, not about `V`** — so ⭐ ***"mass as selector" is live ONLY under a trajectory read***, and doctrine I-7's gauge orbit is **`newtonian_learned`-ONLY** (residual **2.52e-7** vs **0.2505** under `newtonian_identity`, **0.0274** relativistic). ⚠ **Prop F1's "mass selects, never stores" remains untouched.**

**Ch. 7 (epistemics) — ⭐ five exemplars, and this is the wave's densest yield in the whole ledger.** (i) ⭐⭐ **A kill-condition deliberately built BEFORE the thing it could kill — and it fired, saving a build for hours of measurement.** (ii) ⭐⭐ **A theorist who published his own DECLARED-NOT-DERIVED list**, including the one item that gates the transfer of every domain statement he wrote. (iii) ⭐ **An engineer who corrected his own task file's premise** and shipped a test for the *real* hazard plus its passing partner — *the task was wrong, and the report says so in the section that implements it.* (iv) ⭐ **An engineer who declined to land a two-line fix he was authorised to make**, because it would re-score another agent's published number mid-wave — ⭐ *and filed the omission as a reconciliation instead.* (v) ⭐⭐ **A control that fired and, in firing, revealed that the same control had never run at all in the previous wave.** ⚠ **And one anti-exemplar, recorded because it is the wave's real cost: the escape the kill demanded was measurable on the existing rig, at zero cost, all wave — and nobody measured it.**

**Ch. 3, 6 — untouched.**

# ⟲ C2W4 addendum — the wave that gave the competition every advantage and won the argument anyway, then put its own memory inside a real model and found it empty (2026-08-01, doc-curator top-up, **[C2W4]**) — **the audit survived being tuned against itself; the store did not survive being asked to do a job**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope.** Sources: the eight C2W4 spoke reports (`bprime-rivals`, `bprime-c6`, `harness-debt`, `orgdiv-prereg`, `doc-curator-c2w3-sync`, `bprime-rivals-f3`, `cluformer-pilot`, `bprime-draft`) and **both `[C2W4]` §10 review entries** (first review: 4/4 accepted, three branches merged, `main @ 21a6dc4`, **1136 green**; second review: 4 more accepted, two merges, **`main @ 29fc22b`, 1189 green, seventh consecutive conflict-free wave-batch**, every decision-grade number re-derived by the Hub from raw artefacts), plus charter **ADDENDUM 3**. ⟲ protocol respected — **no chapter body edited, nothing deleted.**
> ⛔ **THE HUB WROTE NO ⟲ ADDENDUM FOR C2W4** (verified: the file ended at ⟲ C2W3 before this pass). **This is C2W4's primary ledger record, not a `(curator supplement)`.**
> ⛔⛔ **AND THE SCOPE FACT THAT EVERY TIER-iii SENTENCE BELOW IS READ AGAINST: the pilot ran at TOY SCALE — 0.16 M parameters, 409 600 tokens, three seeds — and S4, the 26–47 M cluster run, WAS NOT RUN.** Every number from it is an **instrument reading**, formally **non-promotable under N94**, and ⛔ ***"needs > 500 M" is not available and appears nowhere.***

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The wave began by handing the competition every advantage the program could afford, and the headline got stronger.** C2W3's spine — five rival memories at iso-state bytes under one uniform protocol — finally ran, and then the Head funded the attack a referee would make anyway: *you hobbled them on three learning rates.* So the rivals got the **full grid, five times the budget, and a held-out selection rule that genuinely picks the new points**. ⭐⭐ **At byte-matched state, 0 of 5 rivals beat a zero-extra-byte reader of a RAW table holding the same bytes — pooled over nine seeds, every margin at least 4.4 standard errors below zero — and neither does the CLU.** The widened grid is selected in **0 of 45** cells; tuning moves any arm's score by **≤ 0.031**; a **64 % cut in fit-split loss buys less than one standard error** of eval. ⭐ ***The binding constraint is not the optimisation budget. It is the fit→eval generalisation gap, and we now have that as a measurement rather than an argument.***

**(2) But the pre-commitment cut the other way too, and it was honoured.** The Head's condition was explicit: *if proper tuning changes any outcome, the paper's claim changes with it.* It did. ⛔ **The per-arm RESCUE verdicts — which of the five rivals are even alive enough for a margin against them to be quotable — are n = 3 artefacts.** The gate's own control (a rival's blank read) has a seed-to-seed spread **larger than the lift it gates**; C2W4's rescued set was `{ttt_linear, gdn, gdn2}`, the tuned run at the same power gave `{}`, and at nine seeds both code paths agree on `{deltanet, gdn, gdn2}`. ⇒ ⭐ **one of our own never-quotes — *"no margin against DeltaNet is quotable"* — is RETRACTED by our own measurement**, and the standing rule that replaces it is sharper than what it replaces: ***quote a verdict only at the power where it is stable.***

**(3) The wave's methodological finding is on the RIVAL side of the audit, and it is the strongest thing in the wave.** Our own pre-registration's launder — read a memory's byte-matched table **through that memory's own projections** — is **not a neutral control**. It costs the table **0.26–0.94** against a raw table holding the same bytes, for two stateable reasons: those projections are trained for the **recurrence**, and a single stored value is **out of distribution** for a head trained on kernel-averaged reads. ⇒ ⛔ **a paper running only the registered launder would have published dividends of +0.88 to +1.02 that do not exist.** ⭐⭐ ***This is launder by omission — the exact failure the +0 B callers were built to prevent — appearing on the rival side for the first time. It is a contribution, not a caveat.***

**(4) Then the store went inside a real model, and the first-order finding is that nothing was in it.** The full C2W1 store — no lever turned off, by standing Head direction — ran as the memory of a streaming byte-LM block on enwik8. ⭐ **The structural positive is real and it is a first: gradients flow `token → φ → store → trajectory-ψ → loss` end-to-end, and the settled-point arm's γ and M gradients are `0.0` BITWISE — T3, confirmed in-system inside a language model rather than on a probe.** ⛔⛔ **And then the decisive row: the live store and a store that was never written give identical held-out loss to float32 round-off — `0.00e+00 / −5.96e-08 / 0.00e+00`.** Self-probe acquisition sits **exactly at chance**. **Sixteen times the write budget buys 1.33× well depth and ZERO acquisition**, while the read at the shipped budget reproduces the old harness exactly. ⇒ ⛔ ***"the write was starved of steps" is refuted; neither the read nor the write is starved of STEPS.*** The named suspect — untested, and correctly not chased mid-run — is **atom placement at initialisation**: 128 atoms scattered at scale 1.0 in a three-dimensional ball, which a few unrolled steps cannot gather into a well.

**(5) And the honest scoreboard at toy scale, reported by the engineer who pre-registered its direction.** ⛔ **The memory is a NET COST against a memory-deleted control (+0.0070 ± 0.0055, 3/3 seeds) and loses to a matched-params GRU (+0.0106 ± 0.0057, 3/3).** It does beat the trivial echo reader. Dynamic evaluation improves **every arm by the same −0.002**, so ⛔ **DF3 is MOOT, not passed — there was no advantage for it to erase.** The anytime curve over a 16× compute range is **flat to 7.5e-4 bpc**, which is the same fact said a third way: ⭐ ***a memory that carries nothing cannot be read better by reading it longer.*** ⭐ **The comparison itself produced a permanent result: a GRU cannot be matched on parameters and state bytes at once** (its cell is quadratic in its state; matched-state costs **79.5 M parameters**), **while a TTT-class cell matches both axes to under 0.1 %** — so the two-sided control at tier iii is now decided by arithmetic rather than by preference.

**(6) The instrument wave, again — and this time the instrument corrected our own theory by a factor of fifty.** ⛔⛔ **Our theorist's own table placed our own rig on the `d/s` axis wrongly, by 45–52×, because it read an admission gate — a radius at which a write is REFUSED — as if it were the spacing the store achieves.** Measured, the shipped cell sits at **`d/s = 4.34` with a coupling of `1.53e-2`**, and ⛔ **the widely-quoted "1089× span" retires to a measured 525×.** ⚠⚠ **And the direction matters: this makes the C2W3 kill and its scoping STRONGER, not weaker, because it puts the rig we already run INSIDE the designed-gate regime where the table-inexpressible coupling is smallest.** ⭐⭐ **In the same measurement the law held on a learned multi-atom store (`R² = 0.995`) and handed back the well width — `s = 0.40`, two independent estimates 0.7 % apart — which discharges, for this store, the modelling question the whole registry has been calling "unsolved and gating every domain transfer."**

**(7) And a wave that had already killed one route pre-killed the next one's default operating point, for three minutes of toy compute.** Tier ii was **pre-registered, not run**. Two theorems came out of it. ⛔ **Under a settled-point read the reachable set is exactly the store's minima, so a table with one row per minimum reproduces the read for ANY reader — composition cannot live in a settled point.** ⛔⛔ **And the physics partition is a power diagram, so at the designed gate the organization is EXACTLY nearest-centroid vector quantisation (`D = 0.0000`) — an organization experiment run there has a structurally zero dividend whatever the physics does.** ⭐⭐ **Two spokes that never spoke to each other met here: one MEASURED that our shipped store sits at `d/s ≈ 4.3`, the other DERIVED that 4.4 is the void point. Our shipped operating point is the void one for tier ii — established before tier ii exists.**

**The rhyme.** C2W3's was *the wave built the thing that could kill it before it built the thing*. ⭐⭐ **C2W4's is: the wave gave its competitors every advantage it could afford and its headline got stronger — and in the same breath it put its own memory inside a real model and found that the model could not tell whether the memory was there.** *Both of those are the same discipline. Only one of them is a good day.*

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 8 — ⭐⭐ verdict UNCHANGED; the chapter acquires its first TIER-iii datum, and it is a mechanism rather than a score.** `[split — lookup refuted, indexing refuted, control validated-with-scope]` stands. What is new is that the chapter's object — *control over memory in time* — has now been **instantiated inside a full model on a real stream** for the first time, and the result is neither a win nor a collapse: ⭐ **the system trains end-to-end through the trajectory read (the design rule the chapter has carried since w19 is confirmed in-system), and its stored content changes the model's output by exactly nothing.** ⛔ **The dials do not move.** ⚠ **The compute-adaptive dial in particular gains a flat curve — but as a statement about an inert store, not about compute-adaptive reads in general** (N199). ⭐ **The chapter's honest sentence gains a second clause:** *for thirty waves we measured whether the dynamics beat a table at reading a store; when we finally put the store inside a model, the model could not tell it was there.*

**Ch. 5 (the lattice / scale idea) — ⭐ the byte floor becomes a LEDGER that decides an architecture, and it is now correct in code.** **N167** closed: the corrected law is **28/28 exact**, 24 cells bitwise unchanged, with the regression test that never existed. ⭐ **And its first architectural consequence landed at tier iii (N198): at the store's own state bytes a plain table gets 1 714 rows where the store gets 8 items — 214× per item.** ⭐ **The chapter's transferable sentence is now two-sided:** *the property that makes deletion exact is the property that makes compression impossible — and the same accounting decides which rival cells can be matched on both axes at all.*

**Ch. 4 (boost / cascade / test-time compute) — ⚠ a boundary hardens.** The waypoint route stays closed for inference-read claims; **the only surviving successor shape (cross-item, via third-party attribution) is now MEASURED rather than derived — and it is `1.53e-2` at the geometry we ship, two orders below the query's own launch noise** (N180/N204). ⭐ **The chapter's dial keeps its shape claim and loses its tier-iii instance: the in-block anytime curve is flat because there is nothing to read.**

**Ch. 3 (the trash-region / trinity idea) — ⚠ a pointer, first movement in many waves.** ⛔ **`B = 0.33`'s outer edge is not merely mislocated — under the correct ruler it is not located at all (`B ≥ 0.542` unrefuted), and the corrected inradius that was supposed to fix it does not transfer to the anisotropic store the certificate work actually uses** (N205). ⚠ **No chapter body is edited; the budget's number is unchanged in code and unquotable as derived.**

**Ch. 2 (mass-as-allocator) — ⭐ one confirmation, in-system.** T3's consequence is now measured **inside a 2-layer language model**: the settled-point read's **γ and M gradients are `0.0` bitwise on 3/3 seeds** ⇒ ⭐ ***"mass and friction as trainable selectors" is live ONLY under a trajectory read*** — no longer a probe result. ⚠ **And a pre-registered prediction of the pilot's own FAILED honestly: γ did NOT move ≥3× further than M** (ratios **1.67 / 0.52 / 1.56**; M wins on seed 1) ⇒ **the "14× channel" does not reproduce as a parameter-movement statement in-system.**

**Ch. 7 (epistemics) — ⭐ four exemplars and one governance anti-exemplar; the wave's second-densest yield.** (i) ⭐⭐ **A Head pre-commitment that cut both ways and was honoured in both directions** — the tuning pass was funded *with* the promise that a changed outcome changes the paper, and it changed one, and the paper changes. (ii) ⭐⭐ **An engineer who pre-registered the direction of his own failure and then reported it happening** — *the matched-state GRU wins, and I say so* — and who reported a **13 h against 8 h budget overrun** rather than absorbing it, because two self-caught correctness defects each forced a full re-run. (iii) ⭐ **Two defects caught by the engineer who would have benefited from missing them:** an inner write that was **arithmetically** inert (the read moved 7.5e-9, below float32) and eviction bookkeeping reporting **133 evictions from 64 offers** — ⭐ *reporting an inert memory caused by one's own optimiser would have been a false first-order finding.* (iv) ⭐ **A cross-branch tripwire that worked:** an engineer who found a bug he did not own **asserted the bug**, so the fix's owner collided with it at the merge — the assertion fired at integration and was flipped there. ⚠ **And the anti-exemplar, which is governance rather than science: a Head ruling reversed on 2026-07-07 was still live, in its pre-reversal form, inside the paper-writer's own brief and inside the claims matrix's citation section** — caught only because the writer **followed the Charter over its own instructions and said so** (N210).

**Ch. 1, 6 — untouched.**

- ⚠ **NEW GAP, and it is the tier-iii critical path: WHY the store is inert.** The cause is **named and untested** — atom **placement at init** — and the three candidate fixes (`atom_local_radius > 0`, a payload-carrying ψ residual, a trajectory term in the write objective) are **design changes, deliberately not built mid-run.** ⭐⭐ **The decisive next experiment is therefore NOT more scale: the mechanism was isolated at 0.16 M for 1.35 CPU-hours, and reproducing it at 26–47 M would cost ~108 A100-hours** (**N196**).
- ⚠ **NEW GAP — tier ii's read and its band, both fixed by derivation before the tier exists.** ⛔ **A settled-point read cannot show composition (an `N_min`-row table reproduces it for every reader), and at the designed gate the organization is exactly VQ (`D = 0.0000`)** ⇒ **the read must be multi-particle occupancy, the band must be the soft certificate, `γ ∈ [0.05, 0.1]`, and the scoring must be OFF the training support** (**N206/N207**).

## ⟲ C2W4 addendum — **(curator supplement, `doc-curator-c2w4-fold`, 2026-08-01, [C2W5])**: the STANDING PRACTICES the wave produced, ratified after the addendum above was written

> **⚠ Why a supplement and not an edit.** The C2W4 addendum above was written by the top-up pass **before** charter **ADDENDUM 4 (§A16–§A19)** was ratified. ⟲ protocol: **nothing above is rewritten or deleted.** This block carries only what post-dates it — **the Advisor's interpretive findings (§A17) and the Head's rulings (§A18) that convert this wave's experience into standing practice.** ⛔ **No new measurement is reported here; every number is the addendum's own, unchanged.**

### The four standing practices, in the order the wave earned them

1. ⭐⭐ **POWER BEFORE VERDICT — `A17.4`, ratified (Advisor §A17.4; Head 2026-08-01).** *"Any gate whose control is a single learned-init draw is underpowered at n = 3"* generalises to a **binding practice: paired or multi-init controls, or n ≥ 9, on any gate whose control has learned-init variance.** ⭐ **The epistemics of this are the wave's sharpest lesson and they are not about seeds.** The rescue gate did not fail because three is a small number; it failed because **the CONTROL was noisier than the effect the control was gating** (`gdn2`'s blank swung **−0.962 / −2.634 / −1.390**). ⇒ ***when the control is noisier than the lift, you do not raise the threshold — you fix the control.*** ⛔ **And the practice has already spent its first coin:** it retracted one of our own never-quotes (**N193**) and it is the reason `ttt_linear` is printed **UNPINNED** rather than resolved by choosing a reading.
2. ⭐⭐ **BUILD THE KILL-CONDITION BEFORE THE THING IT CAN KILL — adopted at `A12`, and it has now paid in TWO CONSECUTIVE WAVES.** C2W3 built the FB4 protocol gate and the anti-lookup kill, and both fired. **C2W4's `orgdiv-prereg` pre-killed tier ii's default operating point for three minutes of toy compute — before tier ii existed.** ⭐⭐ **And the wave's most striking corroboration is that two spokes which never spoke to each other met at the same number: one MEASURED our shipped store at `d/s ≈ 4.3`, the other DERIVED that `d_safe = 4.4 s` is the point where the settled-point organization is EXACTLY VQ (`D = 0.0000`).** ⇒ **our shipped operating point is the void one for tier ii.** ⚠ **The reconciliation that must travel with that sentence** (Hub, for C2W5's lead, so it is not misread against the Head's standing *"no full-CLU feature turned off"* direction): **tier ii runs in the soft-certificate band (`d/s ≈ 2.5–2.9`) — that is NOT turning admission off, it is §A9.8's licensed band, and the soft certificate is precisely what makes a non-separable measurement legal.**
3. ⭐ **A MEMORY THAT CARRIES NOTHING CANNOT BE READ BETTER BY READING IT LONGER.** The in-block anytime curve is **flat to ≤ 7.5e-4 bpc over a 16× compute range**, an order below the seed s.e. ⛔ **This is a statement about an INERT store, not about compute-adaptive reads in general** (N199) — and that distinction is the whole reason the sentence is a lesson rather than a retraction. ⭐ **Its generalised form, which is what future waves should carry: before you scale a read, establish that the thing being read is non-empty — an effect that is ABSENT and an effect that is SMALL look identical at every budget.**
4. ⭐ **THE TIER'S OWN CONTROL IN THE SAME PARAGRAPH — CM-27(b)'s adjacency rule, now standing.** *"A CLU that organizes well and then reads like a cheap table scores dividend ≈ 0 by design"* is **TRUE at tier ii and a RATIONALISATION at tier i.** ⛔ **A draft that states the feature-reading without the ORGANIZER-SWAP control in the same paragraph is non-compliant** — the adjacency is the rule, not the presence of the control somewhere in the paper. ⭐ **Tier ii has now acquired a second precondition of the same kind: the organizer swap must be run somewhere the physics is not ALREADY VQ**, or the control is vacuous by construction.

### Ch. 7 (epistemics) — three more entries, from the rulings rather than the runs

- ⚖ **A HEAD RULING SUPERSEDING A HUB RULING, IN PUBLIC AND IN ONE LINE.** The `[C2W4-CLOSE]` Hub ruling made `draft-vN` the convention; **A18.5 (Head) made B′'s revisions `r2/r3`** because *"v2"* collides with the **V2 short**. ⭐ **The precedence was recorded rather than assumed, the supersession was scoped (B′ only, non-B′ papers keep `vN`), and the rename carried NO content change with a CHANGELOG line.** *A convention that is written down is cheaper than a convention that is remembered.*
- ⭐⭐ **PERSISTENCE RULED ON SCHEDULING, EXPLICITLY NOT ON CONTROLS (`A18.4`).** The Head ruled the CSF3 scale run **non-negotiable** and attached a standing direction: **arms are not killed on early failures; nulls RE-PRICE a route, they do not close it.** ⚠ **And the Advisor's note is the part that makes it epistemically safe, so it is recorded here verbatim in substance: the invariants are UNCHANGED — the score is earned under the tier-iii system-level swap protocol, multi-seed, with the probe informing the submitted config; *persistence governs scheduling, never controls.*** ⭐ **This is the cleanest statement the program has of the difference between stubbornness and self-deception.**
- ⚠ **THE ANTI-EXEMPLAR OF ITEM 4 ABOVE GOT WORSE ON INSPECTION, AND IT IS RECORDED HERE BECAUSE IT IS A LEDGER MATTER, NOT A REGISTRY ONE.** The reversal sweep that fixed the paper-writer brief **fixed the document and not the filesystem**: a duplicate brief, **`.claude/agents/paper-writer 2.md`, declaring the same `name: paper-writer`, still carries the PRE-REVERSAL C-1** (found this pass; **N210** addendum). ⛔ **The defect survived its own fix.** ⭐ **The sharpened rule: a reversal sweep ENUMERATES every file an agent can load — duplicates, backups and same-`name:` collisions included.** ⚠ *This is not a curator fix: agent briefs belong to the Hub.*

# ⟲ C2W5 addendum — the wave that killed its own vehicle, then proved the task was solvable all along (2026-08-01, `doc-curator-c2w5-fold`, **[C2W5]**) — **the family was never the problem; the READ was, and we can say which part of it**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope.** Sources: the seven C2W5 spoke reports (`orgdiv-cat-test`, `pilot-placement-probe`, `bprime-referee`, `bprime-cite-check`, `doc-curator-c2w4-fold`, then `orgdiv-null-arms`, `bprime-referee-closures`, `bprime-mamba2-arm`, `plan-pass-vectorise`, `bprime-draft-r3`) and **both `[C2W5]` §10 review entries** (first review: 5/5 accepted, two merges, `main @ eaecc91`, **1238 green**; second review: 5/5 accepted, three merges, **`main @ 9b2d4db`, 1289 green**, zero conflicts, program total **21 branches / 6 waves / 0**), plus charter **ADDENDUM 5 §A20** (filed 2026-08-01, binding). Companion edits this pass: `negative_results.md` (**N212–N224** + addenda on N193/N196/N204/N211), `future_work.md` (C2W5 fold), `claims_matrix.md` (**v2.11 proposed**), `HEP_primers.md` (**§11.21, Records 20–22** + a dated Record-17 update), and a new **`ERRATA-TierII.md`** beside the untouched prereg. ⟲ protocol respected — **no chapter body edited, nothing deleted.**
> ⛔ **THE HUB WROTE NO ⟲ ADDENDUM FOR C2W5** (verified: the file ended at the C2W4 curator supplement before this pass). **This is C2W5's primary ledger record, not a `(curator supplement)`.**
> ⛔⛔ **AND THE RE-LABELLING EVERY SENTENCE BELOW IS READ AGAINST (charter §A20.2, binding): the refuted object is "the `P`-particle occupancy read protocol at `P = 4`". NEVER "the compositional family". The physics is NOT adjudicated — the organizer swap was never reached.** ⛔ **The 0.272 figure is always a *reference ceiling from an out-of-class decoder*, never an arm's bar.**

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The wave's vehicle died before it could carry anything, and the honest form of that is narrow.** Tier ii's factored store was built to the pre-registration verbatim and put through its own five kill-conditions. It survived `K1` only at 2.7× the registered atom budget, and then at the one passing operating point it read **0.0008 ± 0.0008** against a chance of **0.0004** — ⛔ **and so did every control, including the very table `K5` exists to beat (margin 0.0000 on all four readers).** ⭐ *A kill by tie at zero is not a kill of the hypothesis; it is a kill of the instrument.* The arm also ran **COLLAPSED** (`S_eff` 34–51 against a registered band of [8, 16], 3/3 seeds) and **UNTUNED** (one config; the grid a declared NOT-RUN). ⇒ ⛔⛔ ***"Physics loses" was never available. The only quotable form is "physics, untuned and collapsed, does not clear its own K5."*** ⭐ **And the Head's response to it is the wave's governing sentence, generalised from a ruling into a standing direction: *nothing the main vision demands is killed on a vacuous result — a kill must come with deep analysis that informs the next iteration on the surfaced problem.***

**(2) So the wave went and bought that analysis, and it is sharper than the thing it was asked to replace.** The null-arms audit was re-scoped from *"do the nulls beat the physics?"* (unanswerable — there was no physics arm to beat) into a **family-solvability audit**. Five matched-capacity organizers, the full 584-configuration registered grid, F3-equal tuning, held-out selection, four internal-validity anchors — and ⛔ **`null*` = 0.00117 against a bar of 0.05039: 43× short, computed rather than estimated, the first computed `null*` in the program.** ⭐⭐ **And then the fact that turns "nothing works" into an attributable finding: the same frozen launch points every arm sees are 27.2 % exactly decodable by a combinatorial matched filter, and 100 % from the noiseless code.** ⇒ ***The information was there the whole time. What does not exist is a read that can use it.***

**(3) The named defect is geometric, it is measurable in seconds, and it was never measured.** The metric requires a sum over **four distinct wells**. The frozen `P = 4` launch occupies **2.20**; `≥ 4` distinct wells are reachable on **5.0 %** of queries; **exact-set occupancy is 0.0000 out of 2 560.** ⭐⭐ **And the physics settle makes it worse: 2.20 → 1.70, because the settle MERGES particles into shared basins** — the same sign as the cat test's `−0.109` occupancy dividend, now with its mechanism named. ⇒ ⛔ **You cannot sum four vectors you never visited.** ⭐ **The pre-registered §7.3 branch — *ceiling high + arms at zero ⇒ PROTOCOL refutation, not family refutation* — was committed before the harness ran, which is the only reason this is a finding and not a re-frame.**

**(4) The sharpest single measurement in the wave is one line long.** N1 — gradient-placed atoms, **the identical store parameterisation, five times the bytes, 28 672 free floats** — fits **1.0000** of what it was trained on and scores **0.0000** on held-out combinations, on all four readers × five seeds. ⭐ ***It memorises perfectly and composes not at all.*** ⛔ **And because it did not clear chance, the pre-registered revival target it would have set does not exist: no target is set by this wave.**

**(5) Meanwhile the audit paper closed its own symmetry, against us.** MF-1 — *the CLU row printed ✅ RESCUED while reading below its own blank* — was closed **by measurement, not by drafting**: at n = 9 the CLU's lift over its own blank store is **−0.0465 ± 0.0406**, |t| = 1.14 ⇒ ⛔ **NOT RESCUED.** ⭐ **The honest replacement is stronger than the tick it removes: on the one family that survives protocol validation, the written content does not lift the read above an empty store — which is the paper's own thesis, previously unstated about ourselves.** And Mamba-2 landed at iso-state ±0 B: **the audit's best `full` score, still −0.2563 ± 0.0416 below a zero-extra-byte reader of a raw table (6.2 SE, negative 9/9)** ⇒ ⭐ **the headline becomes "0 of 6", and §5.2's "SSMs named, none measured" limitation retires.**

**(6) And the instrument wave, again — this time it corrected a compute story and a ruler.** ⛔ **The pilot's "77–84 % of wall-clock is the Python plan pass" was right about the plan pass and wrong about the Python: 1.6 % was the controller and 98.4 % was an eager JAX forward** sitting beside a jitted train step. Jitting it: **21.7×** on the plan pass, decision-identical. Then the remaining Python term was cut **4.93×** with all seven `WritePlan` fields bit-equal. ⚠ **And a ruler moved under us for the second campaign running: the effective-`s` estimator inflates by 1.44× if it does not subtract the confinement term (0.438 vs 0.304), so `bprime-c6`'s `s = 0.40` is FLAGGED FOR A CHECK — not refuted, not re-measured, and every `d/s` in the program is quoted in units not yet shown to be the same unit.**

**The rhyme.** C2W3's was *the wave built the thing that could kill it before it built the thing*. C2W4's was *the wave gave its competitors every advantage and its headline got stronger.* ⭐⭐ **C2W5's is: the wave's kill fired vacuously, and instead of accepting a verdict it could not have earned, it spent a second spoke proving the task was solvable and naming exactly which component was not.** *A kill that cannot say what failed is not a result. This one can, and the thing it names costs seconds to check next time.*

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 8 — ⭐ verdict UNCHANGED; the chapter's tier-ii datum arrives, and it is a PROTOCOL result rather than a physics one.** `[split — lookup refuted, indexing refuted, control validated-with-scope]` stands. What is new: the chapter's compositional question was asked for the first time with a built store and matched organizers, and ⛔ **the answer is that the read protocol could not express any answer at all** — 2.20 of 4 required wells, exact-set occupancy 0.0000/2560 — **while the task itself was 27.2 % decodable from the same inputs.** ⭐ **The chapter's honest sentence gains a third clause:** *we measured whether dynamics beat a table at reading a store; we put the store inside a model and the model could not tell it was there; and when we finally asked the store to compose, the question could not reach it through the read we had built.* ⚠ **The dials do not move. The physics is not adjudicated.**

**Ch. 3 (the trash-region / trinity idea) — ⭐ the chapter acquires its first scheduled USE, seven waves after the mechanism was built.** `γ_φ(q)` has existed since w3 and has never been used for anything. §A20.3(d) schedules it: **over-dig freely → consolidate to the designed well budget → prune BELOW budget as a controller decision, with pruned wells routed to the trash region** — ⭐ **and the discipline attached to it is the chapter's own doctrine restated: spurious shallow wells are TRASHED, never merged into meaningful ones.** ⚠ **Unbuilt; task-file-scoped, spawn-gated.**

**Ch. 2 (mass-as-allocator) — ⚠ a boundary sharpens on the write side.** The wave adds a measured statement about **when** placement gradients exist at all: ⛔ **the organizer is untrainable until wells exist — gradient norms move `1e-10 → O(1)` across the write** (`orgdiv-cat-test`). ⭐ **That is the mechanistic reason §A20.3(c) requires a SOFT-occupancy training signal and staged store-then-launch co-training: hard assignments do not backprop, and there is nothing to backprop into before the write.**

**Ch. 4 (boost / cascade / test-time compute) — ⚠ one lever named, none measured.** ⭐ **Learned `p₀` enters as a registered lever for the first time**: ballistic reach across **informationally dead inter-well gaps** (which carry exponentially dead atom gradients plus only the confinement bowl). ⚠ **It complements wormholes and it is unbuilt.** ⛔ **The chapter's compute-adaptive dial gains nothing measurable this wave** — the `P`-sweep is a diagnostic, not a compute curve.

**Ch. 5 (the lattice / scale idea) — ⛔ a scope fact, not a movement.** The wave's only scale event is **CSF3 run 1, in flight, and pre-registered as the BASELINE LEG of a decoder ablation** ⇒ ⛔ **no run-1 number is ever the scale verdict.** ⚠ **The chapter is unchanged and will stay unchanged until run 2 exists.**

**Ch. 7 (epistemics) — ⭐⭐ the densest yield of any wave in the ledger; five exemplars, listed below.**

**Ch. 1, 6 — untouched.**

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices and doctrine this wave earned (the ⟲ practice list, continuing C2W4's four)

5. ⭐⭐ **BUILD THE KILL-CONDITION'S *PRE-CONDITION* TOO — `K0`, adopted.** The doctrine *build the kill-condition before the thing it can kill* (N189, paid in three consecutive waves now) gains a sharper sibling: ⭐ **`P(≥ F distinct wells occupied)` is computable from the launch geometry ALONE, store-free, in seconds — and it caps every arm at 0.05 before any organizer exists.** ⛔ **Nobody computed it until after the wave had run.** ⇒ **`K0` joins K1–K5 as a pre-condition for any future compositional cell.** ⭐ ***A kill-condition tells you when to stop. A pre-condition tells you not to start — and it is usually the cheaper of the two.***
6. ⭐⭐ **AN ORGANIZER AUDIT NEEDS AN IN-CLASS FIT ANCHOR, OR "NOTHING WORKS" IS A STATEMENT ABOUT YOUR OPTIMISER.** The null-arms verdict is credible **because of L1–L4**, not because of the headline: **N1 fits 1.0000 of its own training items** (registered ≥ 0.50), **N4 `k=1` memorises SEEN at 1.0000**, the **shuffle-φ launder is at chance on every arm**, and **capacity is flat to 0.0000 over a 5.3× byte range.** ⇒ ⭐ ***Before you report that no method clears a bar, show that at least one of your methods can fit something. Otherwise you have measured your own harness.***
7. ⭐⭐ **QUOTE THE DECODABILITY CEILING BESIDE EVERY NULL-ARM NULL (Hub ruling, standing).** ⛔ ***"No matched-capacity organizer clears" is never quoted without the 0.272 reference ceiling in the same sentence.*** ⭐ **Because the two together are a different claim from either alone:** *the information is present and the read cannot use it* is a design brief; *nothing works* is an epitaph. ⚠ **And the ceiling carries its own label every time — a REFERENCE ceiling from an OUT-OF-CLASS decoder, never an arm's bar** (§A20.2).
8. ⭐ **A SEEN-VALIDATION SPLIT INHERITS THE FAMILY'S OWN HELD-OUT RULE.** The naive seen-holdout is **not the same problem** as the unseen split: two written items may share `F−1` wells, so selecting on such a split rewards near-neighbour interpolation. The engineer held out 32 rows satisfying `|A_val ∩ A_train| ≤ F−2` against **every** retained training row (**32/32 on all 5 seeds, asserted in tests**) — stronger than the protocol asked for. ⚠ **And the honest companion: at grain 1/32 every config scored 0.0000, so selection ran on the MSE tie-break** ⇒ ⭐ ***a validation statistic needs grain finer than the effect, or it is a coin the tie-break is really flipping.***
9. ⚖ **A17.4 EXTENDS FROM POWER TO SELECTION RULES (Hub ruling, C2W5 second review).** *"Quote a verdict at the power where it is stable"* becomes ***"…and under the selection rule where it is stable."*** ⛔ **A third code path (held-out `f3_val`, n = 9) un-rescues `deltanet` and `gdn2`; only `gdn` and `mamba2` clear under all three registered selections** ⇒ **both are labelled SELECTION-DEPENDENT and no flattering margin against them is quotable.** ⭐ ***An instrument has two knobs. Reporting stability on one of them is reporting half an instrument.***

## Ch. 7 (epistemics) — five exemplars from this wave

- ⭐⭐ **A RE-SCOPE THAT MADE A NULL SHARPER INSTEAD OF SOFTER.** The Hub's re-scope offered the engineer an easy inference: *none clears ⇒ the family is refuted.* ⛔ **The engineer measured that the inference does not follow, said so in his first ten lines, and handed back a narrower and more useful verdict than the one he was authorised to write.** ⭐ *The path of least resistance was a bigger claim. He took the smaller true one.*

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The wave was built to kill a mechanism, and the mechanism had already left.** C2W6's whole premise was N223: the outer loss reaches the store's depth-determining leaves through a differentiably-unrolled write, and 200 steps drive well depth to ~1e-63. The wave built the shield (**P1**, a stop-gradient partition at the write boundary), built its kill-conditions as **tests first** (18 of them, green before any science cell), and then measured the baseline it was protecting against. ⛔⛔ **Over 1000 outer steps × 3 paired seeds the unprotected arm does not decay — it dips and recovers: 9.782 / 0.9035 / 0.5305 × its own step-200 depth against a registered rule of ≤0.3×. E1 is REFUTED 0/3.** The banked collapse was measured at the **shipped** config (no placement, `write_margin = 0.15`, pre-ψ-fix); **placement + margin + the ψ residual have between them removed it.** ⚠ **And the honest form of that sentence is narrower than it wants to be:** *"there is no erosion at the run-2 config"* is **estimator-dependent** (final/untrained arith **0.708 ± 0.57×** vs geo **0.327×** — two seeds lost ~7× depth, one gained 1.85×). ⭐ **The quotable form is *"N223's monotone collapse does not reproduce at the run-2 config."***

**(2) So the gate failed on the wrong leg, and the honest instrument was to say so rather than to ship past it.** `aggregate()` applied the pre-registration verbatim and printed **`FAILS_FLATTEN` at both budgets** — ⛔ **and the failing leg is E1, the baseline: there was no erosion to flatten.** Every leg *about P1* passes: **K1 exact** (bitwise 0.0 on all six store-parameter gradients, loss bit-identical at `5.764082908630371`, read channel alive) · **K3 no harm** (−0.004853 ± 0.000780 at w4, 6.23 SE, 3/3 better; +0.005676 ± 0.005613 at w40, n.s.) · **3.628× geometric-mean deeper wells at N94's undemoted floor, 3/3 seeds** · **K4 clean** · **K2 bit-identical off-path against a pre-build sha256 fingerprint**. ⭐ **The Hub refused to ship past a binding gate and escalated it; the Head re-scoped the gate whose premise had evaporated (§A23.2) and `erosion_partition=True` ships in run 3.** ⛔ **The verdict is SUPERSEDED, not overturned — it stands as the record of the registered rule, and may only be quoted with its re-scope attached.**

**(3) The finding that will outlive the wave was not the wave's subject.** ⭐⭐ **`write_sign`'s docstring is false at the configuration we ship.** It argues that `jnp.sign`'s zero derivative severs `d(store state)/d(phi)` *"by construction as well as by theorem"* — true of the inner loop, and **false whenever `atom_place_radius > 0`**, because H1b's placement assignment `centers[:, :addr] = z[:addr] + jig` is a plain differentiable path **outside** the sign-gated loop. Measured: **27 % of φ's layer-0 gradient (0.0908 → 0.0659)** flows through the write, and the partition is what closes it. ⇒ ⛔ **every *"the write cannot train φ"* statement in the program now needs the placement qualifier** (§A23.3). *A claim that was true of the code that motivated it, quietly false of the code we run.*

**(4) And the §A20.6 question — *is erosion intrinsic to a differentiable write, or a symptom?* — is ANSWERED, robustly, by a rider that was never a claim cell.** With the ψ payload residual **OFF** — the store useless to the loss — retention falls to **0.141 ± 0.095×** untrained (geo 0.077×) against **0.708 ± 0.57×** with it on, and destructive rewrites rise to **51.2 ± 23.8 %** against **22.8 %** (run-2) and **0.9 %** (P1 on). ⭐⭐ ***Erosion is a symptom of uselessness, not an intrinsic property of a differentiably-unrolled write.*** ⛔ **DIAGNOSTIC label travels — labelled 2×2 corner, never a claim cell.** ⚠ **And the partner sentence is not flattering: the memory is still a NET COST (memory-deleted margin positive on 6/6 cell-seeds), so "protected" was never upgradable to "useful" here.**

**(5) The operationally urgent item was found by replaying an old rule against new curves, and it changes what a babysitter does.** The C2W5 pre-registered CSF3 abort rule, replayed on the exact arm the cluster is running: **seed 0 trips on 15 of 41 readings, first at step 25, reaching 4.12e-4 — below the 1e-3 immediate-escalation floor — then RECOVERS 15.2×; seed 2 trips 10/41 and recovers 2.6×; only seed 1 never trips.** ⇒ ⛔⭐ **the rule is a measured false-positive generator at this config, and the Head SUSPENDED it entirely (§A23.1) — stronger than the amendment the Hub proposed.** ⭐ **The Head's earlier run-1 seed-0 override is now recorded VINDICATED BY MEASUREMENT** — backed by a 3-seed replication and a mechanism, not a judgement call. ⚠ **Accepted risk, stated knowingly: a genuinely destroyed store now burns its allocation to completion.**

**(6) And the process failure of the wave is the Hub's own, reported by the Hub.** The analyst was spawned on a **prose** gate rather than a mechanical one, found no build, ⭐ **verified liveness and mtimes before concluding, took no recovery action, fabricated nothing**, and spent its time re-deriving all nine banked anchors from raw (**all nine reproduce**) and auditing the prereg — returning **F1** (the censoring erratum), **F2** (the P-residual row is near-unpassable as written) and **F3** (depth must be adjudicated on the log scale: the same banked data gives **1.60 SE linear vs 4.33 SE log**). ⛔ **Those arrived at 11:39; the first science cell ran at 13:17; no Hub was in session to fold them.** ⇒ **they are POST-HOC with respect to the cells and are applied as labelled reporting-side estimators, NEVER as a re-scoring.** ⭐ **The lesson is now standing program doctrine: a gated spoke needs a MECHANICAL precondition — a named file that must exist — never a prose gate.**

**The rhyme.** C2W3's was *the wave built the thing that could kill it before it built the thing.* C2W4's: *the wave gave its competitors every advantage and its headline got stronger.* C2W5's: *the wave's kill fired vacuously, so it went and proved the task was solvable and named the component that failed.* ⭐⭐ **C2W6's is: the wave measured its own premise before claiming its own product — and the premise was gone.** *We built a shield, discovered the blow had stopped landing, said so, refused to ship past our own gate, and shipped only after the rule was re-scoped in the open. The two things we keep are a mechanism we had documented backwards and a number we had been quoting as an estimate when it was a censoring statement.*

What is new: **a training-time isolation mechanism that is exact (bitwise-zero gradient to store parameters, loss bit-identical, read channel alive), costs no bpc at the 2-SE bar, and protects depth 3.63× at the undemoted write budget** — and, in the same breath, ⛔ **the store it protects is still a net cost against deleting it.** ⭐ **The chapter's honest sentence gains a fourth clause:** *we measured whether dynamics beat a table at reading a store; we put the store in a model and the model could not tell it was there; when we asked it to compose, the read could not carry the question; and when we finally built the protection its lifetime story demanded, the thing we were protecting against had already stopped happening at the configuration we ship.* ⚠ **The dials do not move. `isolation` gains an exact mechanism; `lifetimes` gains a scope correction, not a result.**

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices and doctrine this wave earned (continuing C2W5's 5–9)

10. ⭐⭐ **A GATED SPOKE NEEDS A MECHANICAL PRECONDITION — A NAMED FILE THAT MUST EXIST — NEVER A PROSE GATE.** (§A23 process lesson, **standing program doctrine**.) C2W6's analyst was gated in prose (*"GATED on the build's artifacts landing"*), was spawned in parallel anyway, and its pre-cell findings went stale by 98 minutes. ⭐ ***A gate you cannot check with `ls` is a wish.*** ⚠ **Already applied: this curator task's own item 5 was gated on `.claude/outputs/c2w6-phi-leak-docstring.md` existing — and it did not, so item 5 is reported `BLOCKED-PENDING` rather than guessed.**
11. ⭐⭐ **A POSITIVE QUANTITY THAT SPANS DECADES IS NEVER SUMMARISED BY AN ARITHMETIC MEAN — AND THE CORRECTION IS REPORTING-SIDE ONLY.** Depth's within-arm seed spread is **52–128×**; the same banked data gives **1.60 SE linear (a null) and 4.33 SE log (a result)**. ⇒ **every pooled depth aggregate reports geometric alongside linear.** ⛔⛔ **And the discipline that makes this safe rather than corrosive: estimator corrections that arrive AFTER the cells are POST-HOC and may never re-score a registered verdict.** ⭐ *You may improve how you report a number. You may not improve how it was scored, after seeing it.*
12. ⭐ **A CENSORED MEASUREMENT IS REPORTED AS A CENSORING STATEMENT.** *"4.95e-63"* was an arithmetic mean over **114.8 orders of magnitude** — ⭐ *a single seed wearing a mean's clothes*. **The approved form states the bound and the per-seed values: *"below 1e-62 on 3/3 seeds (1.4e-87 / 1.5e-62 / 2.5e-177)."*** ⚠ **The qualitative claim survived intact; only the arithmetic did not.**
13. ⭐ **AN ABORT RULE IS AN INSTRUMENT AND MUST BE REPLAYED AGAINST REAL CURVES BEFORE IT IS TRUSTED.** Replayed, the C2W5 rule pauses **2 of 3 healthy seeds**. ⇒ **suspended, telemetry kept.** ⭐ ***A monitor that fires on healthy runs does not protect an allocation; it spends attention and manufactures false mechanisms.***
14. ⭐ **KILL-CONDITIONS AS TESTS, BEFORE THE SCIENCE CELL — the C2W3 doctrine, now executed to its strongest form yet.** 18 tests green before any cell; **K2 fingerprinted by sha256 against the pre-build base**; three self-refutations reported as findings; and a self-caught artifact-hygiene bug (`--aggregate-only` globbing 3 smoke runs into the aggregate: 24 records, not 21) **declared, regenerated, verdict unchanged.** ⚠ **The Hub's own note: this is the standard the wave record should cite.**
