## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The blocker named at the end of iteration 1 was CARDINALITY, and cardinality was solved.** Iteration 1's read had no way to say *how many* wells the answer contains: its asserted set averaged **5.79 ± 0.90** members and was never 4. C2W7 built a multiplicity read — the head estimates `F_hat`, allocates its `k = 12` particles, and the read asserts `{j : m_j ≥ 0.5}` — and the mechanism works: **`F_hat` = 4.542 ± 0.122**, asserted-set size **4.194 ± 0.224**, exactly `F` on **39.4 %** of queries, and ⭐⭐ **`R1` (exact-set occupancy) goes 0.0023 → 0.065625 ± 0.023876, clearing its pre-registered bar for the first time in the programme** (mean − 2 SE = **0.0417** > 0.02; **28×**). *After three waves of "the read cannot express the answer", the read can express the answer.*

**(2) And it does not matter, because the whole gain is on the launch side.** ⛔ **`G1` — the read against **this cell's own trained head** with the landscape deleted — is **−0.007031 ± 0.001562, negative on 5 of 5 seeds** (mean − 2 SE = −0.0086). ⛔ **The designed, untrained head beats the trained one** (best reader **0.0695 vs 0.0539**; `R1` **0.0883 vs 0.0656**) — **w20's "free learning erases design", now measured at the claim budget on the same organized store.** ⇒ **the gate fails 2 of 4 legs and the organizer swap was NOT run** (`SWAP_RUNS = false`, pre-registered as P17 and hit exactly), so ⛔ **no `OD_min` / F1 / F2 / F5 number exists this wave and the tier-ii verdict is UNREACHED for a third consecutive wave — not refuted.**

**(3) The mechanism is measured, and it points at the write.** The head allocates onto **4.834 ± 0.125** distinct wells; the settle delivers **5.668 ± 0.396**. ⭐ **Particles committed to the same well end in different basins**, because (iteration 1 §7) **the store's attractors sit 1.09 — about 1.4 well-spacings — from its designed anchors.** A counting code is exactly the representation that scattering corrupts: `3/3/3/3` becomes `3/2/3/2/2` and `m_j` stops landing on 1. ⚠ **And the registered falsifier for this could not see it:** `R2` fires only if settled **<** launched, and this failure has the **opposite sign**. ⇒ ⭐⭐ **the deficit is store-side, and the Advisor's §A25.2 reading follows: no third READ design can produce `G1 > 0` on this vehicle.**

**(4) The wave's highest-value loose thread reached backwards three waves — and it was closed in a day.** C2W7 measured a **0-parameter** reader scoring **0.0539** where a **72-parameter least-squares** reader scored **exactly 0.0000** on the same latent, which put **every fitted-reader zero in the programme** in question, `null*` = 0.00117 included. ⭐⭐ **The audit ran a reproduction gate FIRST — three cells re-scored with the EXISTING fitted readers, bit-for-bit, before any identity number was looked at (two of them on non-zero, seed-asymmetric published values) — and then re-scored through zero-parameter identity readers: `null*` UNMOVED, `OD_min` identical, N1 unmoved, largest gain anywhere +0.0023, 21× below the bar. VERDICT: SURVIVES.** ⭐ **The mechanism is CONFIRMED, not refuted** (`diag(W)` 0.13–0.45 everywhere; the `tol` crossing fires on two cells, where the identity reader keeps **every** correct query and the fitted reader keeps **none**) — ⭐⭐ **it simply had nothing to destroy: the population it acts on is 2/2560 · 3/1280 · 0/2560 at C2W5's cells against ~18 % at C2W7.** ⛔⛔ **And the hope that the artifact was hiding a physics dividend is measured and refuted: with the identity readers included, iteration 1's `G1` gets WORSE (−0.0016 → −0.0023).**

**(5) Three instruments failed this wave, in three different ways, and all three were caught by arithmetic rather than by a result.** ⛔ **A band whose lower half is unreachable** — `S_eff = 512/W` with `W ≤ 32` means `S_eff ≥ 16` always, so the registered `[8, 16]` had exactly one attainable value and its "COLLAPSED" label described **30.6-of-32 wells visited** with the same word C2W5 used for genuine concentration. ⛔ **A bar that a broken head passes perfectly** — a collapsed head scores `K0 = 1.000` while every reader reads 0.0000 (and the claim cell's own `K0` PASS is a **mean**: 3 of 5 seeds clear it). ⛔ **A falsifier blind by sign** — `R2`, above. ⭐ *Each was found by checking what the instrument could possibly say, not by a number coming out wrong.*

**(6) And one thing worked exactly as doctrine said it would, un-forced.** The launch-collapse monitor (#15) was built **before** the anti-collapse regularizer it authorises; it stayed quiet on the claim cell, so the regularizer stayed **OFF** (turning it on moves nothing: ΔS_marg −0.90, Δbest −0.0016) — ⭐ **and the same monitor tripped on a REAL training run at `head_lr = 3e-2` (`S_marg ≈ 7.9`, Hub-verified from raw) before any regularizer existed in the loop.** ⭐⭐ **The marginal/per-query distinction is now load-bearing with a number: per-query perplexity 3.94 (≈ `F` — the design working) against a marginal of 21.22.** *A regularizer that penalised per-query concentration would have attacked the mechanism it was built to protect.*

**The rhyme.** C2W5's was *the wave's kill fired vacuously, so it proved the task solvable and named the component that failed.* C2W6's: *the wave measured its own premise before claiming its own product.* ⭐⭐ **C2W7's is: the wave fixed the thing it was told to fix, cleared the bar nobody had cleared, and reported in its own first ten lines that the fix changed nothing about the verdict.** *We repaired the read, we measured that the repair was entirely launch-side, we found the mechanism, and we said out loud that the next iteration is not another read.*

**Ch. 7 (epistemics) — ⭐⭐ three exemplars and two instrument lessons, listed below.**

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices and doctrine this wave earned (continuing C2W6's 10–14)

15. ⭐⭐ **BEFORE YOU FIT A READER, REPORT WHAT A ZERO-PARAMETER READER WOULD SCORE — AND REPORT THE FRACTION OF QUERIES THAT ARE ALREADY EXACTLY RIGHT (`K6`).** It needs **no store, no fit and no reader**, it is the **closed-form ceiling of every zero-parameter reader**, and it is the **exact headroom of any reader-fitting artifact**: **18 % ⇒ the artifact can be worth 28×; 0.1 % ⇒ it is worth two queries.** ⭐ *It would have answered a three-wave-deep question in seconds.* **Companion rule: every reader class carries a zero-parameter member — added, never substituted, never reported alone** (it can be **strictly worse**: +0.0109 on a real cell, > 0.99 on a designed control, so substituting would itself launder).
16. ⭐⭐ **CHECK WHAT YOUR INSTRUMENT CAN POSSIBLY SAY, NOT ONLY WHAT IT SAID.** Three registered instruments failed this wave on arithmetic rather than on data: a band whose lower half needs 64 wells in a 32-well store; a precondition a broken head passes perfectly; a falsifier that fires on only one sign of a two-sided failure. ⭐ ***A bar that cannot fail and a band that cannot be entered are the same defect wearing different clothes.***
17. ⭐ **A METRIC AND ITS FITTING CRITERION MUST AGREE, OR THE FIT IS AN ADVERSARY.** Least squares minimises a continuous residual; exact-set accuracy is a threshold. The mismatch shrinks right answers past `tol` (`diag(W)` 0.13–0.45) **and can inflate them (+46.5)** — ⛔ **so a fitted reader's residual is never evidence that a store is "close."**
18. ⭐ **A FALSIFIER INHERITED FROM A PREVIOUS PROTOCOL IS NOT AUTOMATICALLY A FALSIFIER FOR THE NEXT ONE.** `R2` was written for a read whose failure was *merging*; the new read's failure is *scattering*. **Re-register two-sided, or state in advance which sign the instrument cannot see.**
19. ⭐ **MONITOR FIRST, REGULARIZE ONLY IF IT FIRES — and this wave is the first time the rule was exercised by an accident rather than by a design.** M15 tripped on a real training run before any regularizer was in the loop; the regularizer then measured inert on the quiet cell. ⭐ **And the statistic's *level* was load-bearing: marginal 21.22 vs per-query 3.94** — the charter's insistence on marginal-only is vindicated **by measurement**.

## Ch. 7 (epistemics) — three exemplars

- ⭐⭐ **A WAVE THAT PUT THE SENTENCE THAT UNDERMINES ITS OWN PRODUCT IN ITS FIRST TEN LINES.** `c2w7-read-cardinality`'s reconciliation list opens with *"every arm this programme has scored at ≈ 0 may have been scored through a reader that destroys its own signal"* — a finding that questions the wave's own instrument **and three waves of published zeros**, filed as `AMENDMENT-C2W7` by the agent that would have benefited from silence. ⚠ **The sentence was too broad and is now re-scoped by measurement — but the wave that raised it is the reason the measurement exists.**
- ⭐⭐ **AN AUDIT THAT PASSED ITS REPRODUCTION GATE *BEFORE* IT WAS ALLOWED TO LOOK AT ITS OWN NUMBER.** Three published cells re-derived **bit-for-bit with the existing readers**, two of them on non-zero seed-asymmetric values, **before a single identity-reader score was computed** — and the PREREG *derived in advance* that a zero-parameter reader's score **is** the asserted set's exact-set accuracy, which then held **to the last digit** (`gated_well_identity` = published `R1`, per-seed `[0, 0, 0, 0.01171875, 0]`). ⭐ *A pre-registered derivation that survives is evidence; this one also made the whole task cheap.*
- ⭐ **A HUB THAT CHECKED THE ARITHMETIC OF ITS OWN REGISTERED BAND.** The `S_eff` finding is not a measurement — it is `512/W` with `W ≤ 32`, noticed at review, retiring a leg of the very gate the Hub was adjudicating. ⚠ **And it was reported as *not changing the outcome***: the gate fails on `G1` alone as substance, which is the honest version.

## ⭐⭐⭐ THE FINDING: read the curves, not their endpoints

C2W6's own addendum above records the wave's premise as **retired** — *"N223's monotone collapse does not reproduce at the run-2 config."* ⛔⛔ **That sentence is now NEVER-QUOTE, and so is *"the premise is retired"*.** The analyst plotted the **per-reading depth series** rather than the endpoint statistics every table in the wave had been built from, and found what every endpoint had hidden: **a large TRANSIENT TROUGH.**

| `p1_off` seed | untrained | **trough (step)** | **min ÷ untrained** | at step 200 | final | final ÷ min |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | 4.63e-02 | 4.12e-04 (**step 150**) | **0.0089 — a 112× loss** | 6.39e-04 | 6.25e-03 | 15.2× |
| 1 | 1.62e-01 | 1.62e-01 (step 0 — **no dip**) | 1.000 | 3.31e-01 | 2.99e-01 | 1.85× |
| 2 | 2.49e-01 | 1.32e-02 (**step 275**) | **0.0532 — a 19× loss** | 6.59e-02 | 3.50e-02 | 2.64× |
| | | | **geo 0.0779, log-mean/SE −1.85** | geo 0.196 | geo 0.327 | **geo 4.20×** |

⭐ **It is not a minimum-of-noise artifact:** reading-to-reading noise over the last 400 steps is **1.33× / 1.10× / 1.05×**, two orders of magnitude below the 112× and 19× excursions, and seed 1's argmin is at step 0.

⭐⭐ **And then the reconciliation nobody had stated: the banked N223 anchor was taken at 200 outer steps — INSIDE the trough** (troughs at 150 and 275 on the two seeds that dip). **So the registered E1 statistic, `final ÷ step-200`, had its DENOMINATOR sitting in the trough on 2/3 seeds and was structurally measuring RECOVERY-FROM-THE-TROUGH, not decay.** That is *mechanically* why it read 9.782 and 0.5305.

✅ **The defensible record, and the only approved form: *"N223's mechanism FIRES at the run-2 config but is NOT TERMINAL."*** ⭐ **This is an UPGRADE, not a retraction — it RESTORES N223 as a live mechanism** instead of leaving "the premise the wave was built on is retired" as the record. ⛔⛔ **Nothing is re-scored: E1 stays REFUTED 0/3 raw AND decay-netted, `FAILS_FLATTEN` is unchanged, and §A23.2's re-scope premise — the BASELINE failed E1, not P1 — is independently confirmed.** ✅ **The estimator-robust survivor is unchanged: *"erosion tracks uselessness"*** (residual-off geo **0.0775×**, log-mean/SE **−3.10**, significant; run-2 geo 0.327×, n.s. — a **4.2×** separation).

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices this pass earned (continuing 10–14 above)

15. ⭐⭐⭐ **QUOTE THE CURVE, NOT THE ENDPOINT — and this is the SECOND time the program has had to learn it.** The register already carries *"quote the curve, not the endpoint"* as a **wave-26 standing rule**; C2W6 then built an entire wave on two endpoint ratios and a registered statistic whose denominator was a single early reading. ⭐ **The generalisation the trough forces: an endpoint statistic is only meaningful if you have looked at the path to it — and a RATIO of two endpoints is two chances to be wrong.** ⛔ **The concrete design rule: any decay/flattening gate must fix its reference reading OUTSIDE the transient** (`final ÷ max over run`, or a registered late-window baseline), *because a fixed early step is coupled to a trough whose location moves with the config.*
16. ⭐⭐ **A DECISION RULE THAT THRESHOLDS *AT* THE EFFECT SIZE CANNOT CONFIRM THAT EFFECT — AT ANY n.** I2's registered rule (*confirm iff mean ρ ≥ +0.5*, against a hypothesis of exactly +0.5) has **P(confirm | true ρ = +0.5) = 0.424 at 6 wells × 3 seeds and 0.468 at 100 × 3.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ ***buying data against such a rule is money for nothing; the rule is the binding constraint, not the sample.*** ⭐ **Pre-registration protects against p-hacking; it does not protect against registering a rule that cannot fire. Price the rule's operating characteristics when you register it, not after it returns null.**
17. ⭐⭐ **A PROXY WITHOUT MEASURED RELIABILITY IS NOT A NULL — IT IS NOT A MEASUREMENT.** ρ(LOO) = **+0.0667 ± 0.1627** looked like a clean null and was **undefined**: the proxy's **ICC(1,1) is negative on 3/3 seeds** ⇒ attenuation ceiling **0.000**, so no correlation of any size could have been observed. ⭐ ***Report an instrument's reliability alongside every correlation it produces, or the correlation's absence means nothing.*** ⚠ **And the companion: two proxies for one construct must be shown to agree — ours were ANTI-correlated (−0.505 ± 0.125, 4.04 SE).**
18. ⭐ **AN ESTIMATOR THAT DISAGREES WITH ITSELF IN *DIRECTION* IS NOT REPORTING A DIRECTION.** *"0.708 ± 0.57× — it does not decay, it recovers"* dies not because the arithmetic is wrong but because **arith 0.708 (0.51 SE from 1) and geo 0.327 (−1.29 SE) point opposite ways on the same three seeds.** ⭐ ***When two defensible estimators of the same quantity disagree in sign, the finding is "unresolved", and any sentence asserting a direction is an artifact of the choice.***

## Ch. 7 (epistemics) — two more exemplars

- ⭐⭐⭐ **AN ADJUDICATOR THAT FOUND NO ERRORS AND STILL CHANGED THE RECORD — the best epistemic work in the campaign so far.** It re-derived **21 records × 5 curve scalars** (worst relative deviation **0.00e+00**), all **12** cell-seed ρ triples, every gate leg and `aggregate()` — **and found nothing wrong.** ⭐ **A lesser report stops there and files "confirmed."** Instead it asked what the *estimators and the instrument* were doing, and produced the trough, the ICC, the self-capping rule and the netting audit. ⭐ ***Verification that only checks arithmetic can certify a wave whose sentence is wrong.***
- ⭐⭐ **AN AGENT THAT REPORTED ITS OWN PRE-REGISTRATION REFUTED, AND DIAGNOSED THE ERROR AS THE ONE IT HAD JUST FLAGGED IN SOMEONE ELSE.** The analyst registered *"|ρ| < 0.3 on 2/3 seeds"*, measured **1/3**, reported it as a finding, and wrote: *"my own prereg made the same n-blind error I flagged in F2"* (the exact null sd of a single-seed ρ at n = 6 is **0.447**). ⭐ **Second n-blind estimator error caught in two waves, by two different agents, each on its own prereg.** ⚠ **And it declined to over-claim its own mechanism: the derivation is *consistent* with the result but not evidenced by it, because the mechanism row (ρ_grad −0.1619 ± 0.2806) is itself a null.**
- ⚠ **A ONE-LINE ANTI-EXEMPLAR, AND IT IS THE HUB'S OWN, SELF-FILED:** the `[C2W6]` never-quote list **forbade one wording and recommended a replacement that is now itself forbidden.** ⭐ **It was corrected in place with the chain visible rather than silently swapped** — which is the right repair, and the reason a future reader can audit how the sentence moved.

# ⟲ READ ITERATION 1 addendum (**CATCH-UP** — written 2026-08-06 by `doc-curator-tierii-read-fix-catchup`, opened at **`[C2W7-CLOSE]`**; covers the science of `tierii-read-fix`, the wave that sits between C2W5 and C2W7) — **we repaired the component we had diagnosed, it worked completely, the answer did not improve at all — and the throwaway measurement in §7 turned out to be the finding**

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The diagnosis was acted on exactly, and the repair worked completely.** C2W5 said the read could not reach the rooms the answer needed (2.20 of 4). This wave rebuilt the read around that: **12 particles instead of 4, `d` = 8 instead of 4, a successive-suppression launch head, a descent gate**. ⭐ **`K0` — the probability that at least `F` distinct wells are reachable — goes 0.0477 → 1.0000 (bar 0.90); distinct wells occupied go 2.20 → 11.8156 at launch and 11.2688 ± 0.5189 settled; the settle's destruction of distinct-well occupancy falls from −23 % to −4.6 %, and the descent-gated occupancy precision turns it into a GAIN: 0.3502 ± 0.0193, +0.076 over its own launch and +0.054 over the null's static read (2 SEs disjoint).** ⭐ **The instrument even reproduces the cell it refutes to 3 digits** (2.2023 / 0.0477 / 0.4053 vs the published 2.202 / 0.050 / 0.4106).

**(2) And the answer did not improve.** ⛔ **Exact-set occupancy — the statistic that *is* the answer — is 0.0023 ± 0.0047 against a clearing bar of 0.02, carried by ONE seed with the other four at exactly 0.0000: not a clear, not a fire, INDETERMINATE.** ⛔ **`OD_min` = −0.0008 ± 0.0016 is an explicitly VACUOUS tie** (every arm, every reader and the launder within a single query of zero — the report says so itself and refuses to call it a tier-ii datum). ⛔⛔ **And `G1` fires: the full read scores −0.0016 ± 0.0019 against the same head's own launches with the landscape deleted.** ⇒ ***the store adds nothing over its own front door, measured for the first time with the A1-strong live launder.***

**(3) The blocker it named was CARDINALITY, and it named it with three measurements rather than an argument.** The gated set had **5.79 ± 0.90** members and was never 4; `coverage_gated` was **0.0531 ± 0.0186** (the gate discards correct wells with the wrong ones); and a π-sharpening sweep over `β ∈ {1,2,4,8,16}` left `top-F(π) == A(x)` at **0.000 for the settled read and 0.016 for the launder at every β** ⇒ ⭐ **the launch's own ranking was already the better set estimator, and sharpening provably could not supply the commitment.** *C2W7 built the commitment; `R1` cleared 28×; `G1` fired harder.*

**(4) ⭐⭐ THE FINDING WAS IN THE STAGE NOBODY WAS WATCHING.** Stage (d) — consolidate-to-budget + trash — was built to test a *lifecycle*, and it was **not adopted** (+0.030 raw precision, **−0.202 gated**). But to consolidate you must first find the store's minima, so it descended 512 probes and printed where they are: ⭐⭐ **the kept attractors sit 1.0871 from the nearest DESIGNED anchor — about 1.4 well-spacings — and 476 of the 508 candidate "minima" have depth 0.0000: confinement-bowl resting points, not wells.** ⇒ ***the map and the ground disagree.*** **One wave later this is the mechanism behind C2W7's scattering result (4.834 → 5.668 distinct wells on a committed allocation) and the reason the next lever is WRITE-SIDE.** *The wave's most valuable number came out of a stage it declined to use.*

**(5) Two of its own registered results were later corrected, and both corrections are demotions it could not have known about.** ⛔ **Its guard-4 capacity claim (`k` 12→24 buys +0.2383 coverage) is REFUTED at a committing head (0.0000 at exactly 2× ledgered flops) — `k` is a RESOLUTION dial.** ⛔ **Its `S_eff` = 16.00 "in band" was the ONLY attainable value in a band whose lower half needs 64 wells in a 32-well store.** ⛔⛔ **And the one correction that could have rescued it went the other way: adding a zero-parameter reader — the very artifact that made C2W7's fitted zeros suspect — makes THIS cell's `G1` WORSE (−0.0016 → −0.0023), while `OD_min` reproduces identically.** ⇒ ***the fitting artifact was not hiding a physics dividend here.***

**(6) It also lost a run to a failure mode that looks exactly like success.** ⛔ **Reverse-mode AD through an un-`jax.checkpoint`-ed 300-step `lax.scan` settle (192 particles × 1024×16 atoms, ~7 GB taped) OOM-killed the process with EXIT CODE 0, no traceback and no partial output** — and separately, a well-width ruler measured at the wrong `payload_radius` silently put the first completed matrix outside its own certificate band (`d/s` = 3.386). ⭐ **Both were self-caught, both were reported, and the off-band matrix was retained as a labelled diagnostic whose conclusions are identical in sign and magnitude.**

**The rhyme.** C2W5's was *the kill fired vacuously, so it proved the task solvable and named the component that failed.* ⭐⭐ **Read iteration 1's is: we fixed exactly the component we had named, we fixed it completely, the score did not move — and the honest report of that is what made the next two waves possible.** ⚠ **And the program's own failure alongside it: this wave went unfolded for two waves, so its numbers were quoted, corrected and re-scored in documents that had never recorded them.**

## ⭐ Standing practices this wave earned (retro-numbered as 15′–17′ so they do not collide with C2W7's 15–19)

**15′.** ⭐⭐ **BUILD THE PRE-CONDITION, NOT ONLY THE KILL-CONDITION — and adjudicate it FIRST, before the thing it can kill exists.** `K0` was measured **store-free, in seconds, before the module was written**, and it forced the wave's one substantive deviation (`d` 4 → 8) **with a measurement rather than a preference**: the out-of-class ceiling at `d` = 4 is **0.072** against an arm bar of **0.0507**, so *no read could have cleared by > 0.05 at `d` = 4.* ⭐ ***A kill-condition tells you when to stop; a pre-condition tells you not to start.*** ⚠ **And its own caveat, added later: `K0` is REACHABILITY — a collapsed head passes it perfectly (N235).**

**16′.** ⭐⭐ **A CEILING WITHOUT ITS NOISE MODEL IS NOT A NUMBER.** The same family reads **0.072 / 0.272 / 0.695 / 0.982 / 0.992** depending on `(d, draws)`. ⛔ **Quote the noise model or quote nothing** — and note what it implies retroactively: at `d` = 4 the ceiling was **1.4× the arm bar**, so **C2W5's cell was information-starved**, a second and independent reason its arms read 0.

**17′.** ⭐ **A GUARD'S DESIGNED NEGATIVE BELONGS IN THE TEST SUITE, NOT IN THE REPORT.** All four guards here are `pytest`-asserted on a designed negative (`G1`: a 0-step read equals the launder **bit-for-bit**; `G2`: hard assignment gives ratio **0.0 exactly**; `G3`: two blank stores, 11 orders apart; `G4`: `assert_k_matched` raises) ⇒ ⭐ ***"the guard fires" becomes checkable by the next agent instead of quotable by this one.***

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The wave did the disciplined thing first, and the disciplined thing is what caught the defect.** C2W8's charter row is the well lifecycle — over-dig, merge to budget, prune what is useless, route the pruned into a trash region. ⭐ **Before building any of those verbs, the prereg required a CENSUS: measure whether the populations the verbs act on exist at all.** `census.json` computed `stage2_unlock` **mechanically** — `P_mean` **0.020833**, `M_mean` **0.238889**, unlock **TRUE**, kill **FALSE** — and the rule fired **exactly as registered**. ⛔⛔ **And neither leg measured the population it was registered to measure.**

**(2) `P` was a tautology and `M` was a definition.** ⛔ **`P` (live attractors never read) came back at 0.02 while 37 of 48 wells were in fact never read — ~77 %, an order of magnitude ABOVE the 0.05 bar.** The reason is that only **1 of 48** wells passes `is_attractor`, and **36 of those 37 are excluded solely by the capture test**: on two seeds of three **every** well has `capture_radius` exactly **0.0**, so the capture conjunct is identically false — **and because `θ_att` is defined as the deepest well among the non-capturers, zero capturers makes `θ_att` the deepest well in the store, so `depth > θ_att` is unsatisfiable too.** ⇒ **two conjuncts, one cause, `P = 0` by construction.** ⛔ **`M` (mergeable near-duplicates) is worse in a quieter way:** every admitted pair has `payload_dist` **exactly 0.0**, and `R_cert` is **10.3–11.2×** the key spacing, so the geometric leg **refused nothing** ⇒ **`M` ≡ the same-class pair rate, exactly, on all three seeds** — *"a class-incremental stream contains two items of the same class"*, true by construction.

**(3) The engineer shipped the one thing that passed its own designed negatives and declared the rest cut — with the cause, not a budget excuse.** ⭐ **K2 landed: `friction_field.py`, built in C1 and referenced NOWHERE in `clu_system.py`, is finally wired — OFF, bit-identical AND parameter-count-identical, with a hole at a well destroying retrievability and a hole far from every well leaving reads bit-identical.** ⛔ **§2.2–2.7 (merge, prune, K3, K4, restoration, the claim cells) NOT BUILT:** the prune verb would have an empty target set, the merge verb would be **collapse-to-one-well-per-class wearing a certificate costume**, and the claim cells would measure **a store that retrieves at chance.** ⚖ **The Hub ratified the cut and filed the prereg defect against itself.**

**(4) And underneath the gate is a physical result the wave did not set out to find.** The store **digs real wells** (depth 0.7 → 1.7, `λ_min > 0` everywhere, 0.791–8.873) and **cannot be knocked on** (`capture_radius` **exactly 0.000 on 47/48**, self-probe `decode` **at chance**, site drift **0.216–1.473** against a key spacing of **0.14**). ⭐⭐ **The diagnosis is the wave's best sentence, and it is measured, not inferred: C3 locality HOLDS in parameter space — 0 violations over 105 events/seed, the own leg losing EXACTLY the designed decay to float32 — and FAILS in function space, with 78–84 % of writes RAISING the foreign contribution at another item's site and foreign exceeding own on 45 of 48 wells.** ⇒ ***a write touches only its own atom block, but atoms have TAILS; local in parameters is not local in the landscape.*** *Which is also why depth rises while retrieval falls: the "depth" at an item's site is largely not that item's well.*

**(5) One dimension up, the tails win outright — and it is not a capacity problem.** At the CL entry's **binding** `phi_dim ≥ 16` the learned `V_θ` store is **INERT** (depth **2.1e-9 / 6.8e-10 / 0.000**, self-probe **0.000**), for an arithmetic reason filed **before** the cells: atom centres drawn `N(0, 1)` in `dim = d+1` put the nearest of *all* atoms at ~`√dim` while `atom_width` stays 0.3, so the write gradient's `exp(−r²/2s²)` underflows **6.18e-01 → 4.98e-06**. ⭐ **And the atom budget was co-scaled exactly as designed at every dimension (`round(512·√2^d)` = 2 048 / 8 192 / 32 768 / 131 072, verified against the shipped code)** ⇒ ⛔ **the store was NOT starved: the binding constraint is REACH.** *Buying atoms geometrically does not bring the nearest one closer.*

**(6) Meanwhile the other spoke re-priced a null, and produced the sharpest laundering statement the program holds.** Split-CIFAR-10 was a w25 **null** diagnosed as a *feature-space* failure. Three real unsupervised encoders later, the diagnosis is **confirmed**: **0.161 → 0.213 → 0.267 → 0.319**, a **+0.1588 ± 0.0123 paired** lift with zero change to the store or the discipline. ⛔ **And N8 misses as registered: −0.014240 ± 0.003, 0 of 5 seeds positive.** ⭐⭐ **Decomposed, the residual is not the read at all: against its OWN SAME KEYS the settle ties to four decimals on all three strong arms (+0.0003 · −0.0007 · +0.0002) where at PCA φ it was 0.048 BELOW** ⇒ ***in a strong feature space the damped settle IS nearest-key indexing, and the remaining 0.014 is bought by a class-balanced admission policy any ring buffer can run.*** **Sixth consecutive laundering confirmation.**

**(7) Two of the wave's corrections are about words, not numbers — and both would have exported a false mechanism.** ⛔⛔ **`population_eroded_not_attractor` is mislabelled on its own numbers: those wells carry depth 0.4–2.2, not depth → 0, so the registered erosion mechanic (*erosion drives depth to zero ⇒ the well ceases to exist*) does not describe them. They are wells that EXIST and are UNADDRESSABLE at the query scale — the reach condition, a different mechanism with a different fix.** ⛔ **And `cl-encoder`'s banked *"reconstruction bought nothing (−0.006)"* is a READ-OUT ARTIFACT: at the measured read-out `convae` beats `randconv` by +0.054 and clears a threshold registered as a MISS** — ⛔ **which does NOT license the inverse claim; the honest form is that the SIGN of the objective comparison depends on the read-out.**

**The rhyme.** C2W6's was *the wave measured its own premise before claiming its own product.* C2W7's: *the wave fixed what it was told to fix, cleared the bar, and reported that the fix changed nothing.* ⭐⭐ **C2W8 pass 1's is: the wave built the instrument that could stop it, the instrument said GO, and the wave read its own arithmetic and stopped anyway.** *We registered a gate, the gate passed, we checked what its two halves were capable of saying, found the answer was "nothing", cut six sections of our own build, and shipped the one component whose designed negatives fired.*

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices and doctrine this wave earned (continuing C2W7's 15–19)

20. ⭐⭐⭐ **A GATE MUST BE SHOWN SATISFIABLE ON THE SHIPPED RIG *AT REGISTRATION*, CONJUNCT BY CONJUNCT — write down what each leg will read before you run it.** C2W7 taught *check what your instrument can possibly say*; C2W8 shows the same defect **inside the wave's own pre-registration**, twice: a conjunction containing a capture test that was never checked to return non-zero at this operating point, and a merge criterion whose geometric leg was never checked to bind (`R_cert` **10.3–11.2×** the key spacing). ⭐ ***A criterion that cannot say "no" has not been tested; it has been assumed.*** ⛔ **The mechanical requirement now standing: any replacement states, before it runs, the value each conjunct takes on the shipped rig.**
21. ⭐⭐ **A DEFINITION THAT DEPENDS ON THE POPULATION IT IS SCORING CAN CANCEL ITSELF.** `θ_att` := *the deepest well among those that did not capture* is a sensible, measured-not-guessed floor — until **nothing** captures, at which point it silently becomes *the deepest well in the store* and the conjunct it feeds is unsatisfiable for **every** well. ⭐ ***Check the degenerate limit of every data-dependent threshold: what does it become when the set it is defined over is empty, or is everything?***
22. ⭐⭐ **NAME A POPULATION AFTER WHAT WAS MEASURED, NOT AFTER THE MECHANISM YOU EXPECTED.** A bucket called `population_eroded_not_attractor` **will be cited as erosion evidence** by the next reader, who will not open the depths — and the depths (0.4–2.2) say the opposite. ⛔ ***A label is a claim, and it propagates faster than the number under it.***
23. ⭐⭐ **AN INSTRUMENT'S OPERATING CORNER IS PART OF EVERY COMPARISON IT PRODUCES.** `cl-encoder`'s arm-isolation table was measured at read-out defaults its **own** sweep then ranked worst of 28 — and the *sign* of its objective comparison flips at the read-out we actually ship (`convae` −0.006 **below** `randconv` → **+0.054 above**). ⭐ ***An A/B run at an unmeasured corner measures the corner.*** ⛔ **And the repair is symmetric: the correction licenses neither the original claim nor its inverse.**
24. ⭐ **A BACKGROUND *LAUNCHER* COMPLETING IS NOT THE *JOB* COMPLETING — and an unrun check asserted in a review entry is the same defect class as a forced-false conjunct in a prereg.** Both are claims whose truth was assumed from their plausibility; both happened in this wave; both were the Hub's, caught by the Hub, and filed by the Hub against itself. ⭐ ***The quotable evidence for a suite is the summary line plus a HEAD re-check, never the notification; the quotable evidence for a sweep is its positive control's hit count.***

## Ch. 7 (epistemics) — three exemplars and two self-filed anti-exemplars

- ⭐⭐⭐ **AN ENGINEER THAT DID NOT OVERRIDE ITS OWN MECHANICAL GATE — AND DID NOT BUILD BEHIND IT EITHER.** `census.json` says `stage2_unlock = true`; the report says so, in those words, and **cuts six sections of its own scoped build anyway**, giving the cause for each and labelling the decision as *"a scoping decision for the Hub, not for me."* ⭐ ***Reporting a green gate and refusing to spend it is harder than either overriding it or obeying it*** — and it is exactly the C2W7 lesson (a bar that cannot fail) applied by the person it would have paid.
- ⭐⭐ **A WAVE THAT FILED ITS ERRATA *BEFORE* THE CELLS THEY GOVERN.** `ERRATA-C2W8.md` registers the capture-floor arithmetic, the merge criterion's two constants (both from **shipped config**, neither tuned), the census's address dimension **and why**, the label→payload map and the netting definition — **each dated, each before the cell it governs, with `PREREG-C2W8.md` untouched.** ⭐ *An erratum written in advance is an instrument definition; the same text written afterwards is a rationalisation.* ⚠ **And it is the reason the `d = 16` inertness reads as a declared rig limit rather than as a surprise.**
- ⭐⭐ **A SPOKE THAT REPORTED THE FULL SUITE CATCHING TWO BUGS ITS OWN FILE-LEVEL RUNS COULD NOT.** Both were x64 ordering hazards — a zero-copy read-only view, and a float64 field promoting the momentum inside the damping so `lax.scan` rejected the carry — **self-reported with the mechanism and fixed with a function-scoped regression test** (⛔ a module-scoped x64 fixture being itself the N211 hazard). ⭐ *"The K2 flag would otherwise have shipped green on a file-level run and reddened the suite for the next agent"* — **the agent said that about its own work.**
- 🔴 **ANTI-EXEMPLAR 1, THE HUB'S, SELF-FILED: an unrun check asserted as run.** The review entry originally read *"grep-checked across both reports"* — **it had not been**; the claim was inferred from the spokes' dial declarations. ⭐ **The `[C2W6-CLOSE-FINAL]` hazard landed mid-filing, the Hub actually ran the sweep — per-file, via a tool that does not skip gitignored paths, with a POSITIVE CONTROL first — and corrected the entry on the record.** ⭐ *The lesson generalises past sweeps: the defect is not the missing grep, it is the inference from plausibility.*
- 🔴 **ANTI-EXEMPLAR 2, ALSO THE HUB'S, ALSO SELF-FILED: a launcher's exit code read as a job's verdict.** A backgrounded `nohup pytest` returned exit 0 in seconds and fired a "completed" notification while pytest was still running; the Hub read a **3-byte log**, ⭐ **caught it, withheld the suite verdict**, re-armed a terminal-state wait that could not read a crash as silence, and quoted only the real **1443/0** with HEAD re-verified. ⚠ **Two defects of one class in one wave — and the wave's central finding (N250) is a third instance of the same class, in a prereg.**

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The directive was "make the store capture", and the store captured — on both arms, on every leg, on every seed.** Pass 1 measured a store that digs real wells nobody can knock on (`capture_radius` **exactly 0.000 on 47/48**, self-probe `decode` **exactly at chance 3/3**, site drift **0.67–0.93** against a key spacing of 0.14). Pass 2 bounded **how far each atom's influence reaches** and the same frozen census, re-run unchanged, reads: **basins 46/48** (16/16 · 15/16 · 15/16), **median capture radius 0.4297 / 0.2930 / 0.3633 = 2.0–2.9 σ_q**, **`decode` 0.1484 / 0.1641 / 0.1406 = +4.02 / +4.75 / +3.65 SE above chance**, **median site drift 0.00100 / 0.00652 / 0.00150**, `λ_min` at the relaxed site **9.85–55.75**, own-beats-foreign **44/48** (pass 1: 3/48). ⭐ **At IDENTICAL BYTES** — `clu_total_bytes` **360 960**, unchanged; the kernel changed, not the budget. **`gate_pass = true`, mechanically computed, 3/3 seeds, with K7 — the capture instrument's own two-sided validation — built and green FIRST.**

**(2) And the thing the wave exists to buy did not move by a single digit.** ⛔⛔ **Reads landing in no basin: 58 / 62 / 62 of 64 — *digit-identical to pass 1*. Items never read: 15/15/14 of 16, against pass 1's 14/14/14 — marginally WORSE. Read-vs-launder margin −0.354, against pass 1's −0.333.** These are the **Advisor's** numbers, computed from arm A's raw cells because **neither arm's report foregrounded them** (§A29.2). ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***The wells are real; the queries still don't reach them.*** ⛔ **The two halves are one fact and neither is ever quoted alone.**

**(3) The gate could not have told us that — and the spoke that proved it was the spoke it cost.** Arm B (an MLP-class head that **emits** the well parameters in one forward pass) passes **all three legs on all three seeds** while **`n_never_read = 16/16 on every seed** — no read landed in *any* item's basin at all — with read accuracy **0.135** against a launder's **0.729**, margin **−0.594**. G-CAP/G-DEC/G-DRIFT measure retrievability **at the sites**; **nothing in them measures whether a query reaches its site.** ⭐⭐ **Arm B led its own report with that, in its first ten lines, against its own interest** — and self-registered `NO_TIER_II_CLAIM` in the same breath. ⇒ ⛔⛔ **the wave's second defect of the same class, and the class is now doctrine: *a gate that cannot fail on the thing that matters*.** Three instances in one wave: pass 1's **vacuous `M`** (a certificate radius **10.3–11.2×** the key spacing against payload distances **identically 0.0** ⇒ refused nothing) · **monitor #3's never-refusing admission gate** (refusal rate **0.000**, **still open**, still tripping 3/3 on both arms) · **pass 2's blind gate.**

**(4) Then the arm's own 2×2 relocated the wave's own headline — and the engineer published it against its own arm's name.** ⛔ **Compactness is not the lever.** A plain **Gaussian** at the same co-scaled width, with the same site-local init, also clears the gate **3/3** and **decodes better** (**+6.21 / +4.75 / +6.94 SE** against the compact arm's +4.02 / +4.75 / +3.65); the compact kernel wins G-CAP and is **10–20× better on drift**, and costs a little decode. ⛔ **And compact-*without*-site-local-init reproduces pass 1 EXACTLY** — capture 0/16, decode at chance, `λ_min = 0.1000 = 2α` (the bare confinement bowl) on all 16, depth 0.000. ⇒ ⭐⭐ **the load-bearing lever is `atom_site_local_init` + a width co-scaled to the MEASURED key spacing — bounded reach — and *kernel form is a secondary axis*.** ⛔ **No artifact may say "compact atoms made the store capture."** ⭐ **The engineer had registered the opposite prediction in a dated erratum before running, was falsified, promoted the ablation to census strength, and reported it in its first ten lines.**

**(5) The second attribution finding corrects a cause this ledger itself has been carrying.** Pass 1's diagnosis — *a write touches only its own atom block, but atoms have TAILS* — was **right about the symptom and wrong about the cause.** A write that **places** atoms (arm B, one forward pass) gives **foreign > own on 0/48** (pass 1: **45/48**) with foreign median **0.0012–0.0152** against **0.611–1.261**. ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***a 300-step gradient write must MOVE an item's atoms across the ball in order to dig, and every item's travelling atoms are every other item's foreign background.*** **The parameter-space/function-space measurement stands exactly as published; the causal clause is corrected in place, struck and dated, nothing deleted** (N253 · N265 · §A29.4(ii)).

**(6) And the root cause of the half that did not move is not physics at all.** ⭐ **`σ_q = 0.15` against a measured key spacing of 0.138–0.147** — *a noisy query is as far from its own key as its key is from its nearest neighbour* — so the repaired basins (radius 2–3× the spacing) **necessarily overlap**, and the launder itself degrades **1.00 → 0.31** across the stream. ⇒ ⛔ **at this operating point the items are not separable by ANY read.** ⭐⭐ **This converges with the pass-1 CIFAR result (0.161 → 0.319 at strong φ): φ is the binding constraint on the CL rig** — which is why the Head's pass-3 ruling puts the completed gate on a strong-φ rig rather than buying more physics here.

**(7) The uncomfortable corollary, filed by the Advisor as a standing warning before anyone can be pleased about the drift number.** ⚠⚠ **G-DRIFT → 0 means the settled point approaches a deterministic function of the stored key — D2a, table-expressible — the exact configuration intervention §8.2 prohibits, and precisely what the CIFAR arm measured at strong φ (settle = same-keys kNN to ±0.0007).** ⇒ ⛔ **the pass-2 gate as written REWARDS the degenerate configuration, and no leg may treat drift → 0 as a target.** ⚖ **The compliance ruling on `atom_site_local_init` is granted with a mechanical check attached: prove the near-zero drift is an OUTCOME, not an algebraic identity — if the attractor cannot move off the stored key when the write objective demands it, capture was bought by re-entering D2a and the ruling reverses.**

**The rhyme.** C2W8 pass 1's was *the wave built the instrument that could stop it, the instrument said GO, and the wave read its own arithmetic and stopped anyway.* ⭐⭐ **Pass 2's is: the wave fixed the thing it set out to fix, completely and on both arms — and then its own spokes proved that the thing it fixed was not the thing that matters, and that the gate it was scored on could never have said so.** *We repaired the wells, we measured that nobody can still find them, we corrected our own attribution of both the fix and the original defect, and the one number that would have flattered us is the one the Advisor flagged as a prohibited configuration.*

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

What is new: ⭐⭐ **retrievability AT THE SITES and addressability FROM `φ` are now measured separately and move independently** — the first goes 1/48 → 46/48 with decode from chance to +3.65…+4.75 SE; the second does not move at all (58/62/62 of 64, margin −0.354 vs −0.333). ⭐ **The chapter's honest sentence gains a seventh clause:** *…and when we finally gave the wells a basin, the number of queries that found one did not change.* ⚠ **The dials:** `admission` and `isolation` gain measurements (own-beats-foreign 44/48; a placing write removes the interference channel entirely); ⛔ **`lifetimes` and `compute-adaptive reads` gain NOTHING — stated so silence is not read as coverage.** ⛔ **The tier-ii organization dividend is untouched and unmeasured in this pass**, and arm B's gate pass is **claim-barred by construction** (`wells_per_item = 1`, `vocabulary_shared = false`, one-hot ⇒ `NO_TIER_II_CLAIM`).

**Ch. 5 (lattice / scale) — ⚠ one clause of the pass-1 re-pricing is refuted, and the REACH thesis gains a positive demonstration.** ⭐ **"Not capacity-starved, REACH-bound" now has a positive datum at `d = 8`** (bounding reach turns 1/48 into 46/48 at an unchanged atom budget), where pass 1 had only the negative one at `d = 16`. ⛔⛔ **And pass 1's own `ERRATA-C2W8.md` §3 is REFUTED on `atom_local_radius`:** *"it needs per-group localization targets at init, and a φ-addressed stream does not know its addresses until write time"* — **write time is exactly when the address IS known**, and applying the localization **at admission** is both usable and load-bearing. ⛔ **Nothing at `d ≥ 12` was measured; the `d ≥ 16` inertness keeps its scratch-probe provenance and its CANCELLED-NOT-RUN status**, and arm B's emitting write is a **named candidate escape, THEORY-ONLY.**

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices and doctrine this pass earned (continuing pass 1's 20–24)

25. ⭐⭐⭐ **A GATE MUST BE ABLE TO FAIL ON THE THING THE WAVE EXISTS TO MOVE — *"a gate that cannot fail on the thing that matters"*.** Practice 20 said a gate must be shown *satisfiable* conjunct by conjunct; this pass adds the other side. Three instances in one wave: a merge criterion whose geometric leg was 10.3–11.2× too loose to bind · an admission monitor whose refusal rate is **0.000** and **still is** · a capture gate all of whose legs pass while **no read lands in any basin**. ⭐ ***The question to ask when registering a gate is not only "can each leg say no?" but "can the CONJUNCTION say no to the failure I actually care about?"*** ⛔ **The mechanical consequence, already ruled: G-ADDR is registered — two-sided, with designed negatives proving it can fail, pytest-asserted — before anything else is scored, and the arm race stays void until it exists.**
26. ⭐⭐ **AN ARM MUST BE PRICED BY THE ABLATION THAT SEPARATES ITS BUNDLED LEVERS, AND THE ABLATION MUST BE REGISTERED BEFORE IT RUNS.** Arm A bundled two levers and its own registered prediction about which one mattered was **falsified**; a single pilot seed then **over-called the result in the other direction**, and only the census-strength re-run gave the form that survives. ⭐ ***A wave's headline mechanism should be the one its own 2×2 attributes it to — and if you cannot run the 2×2, you do not have the attribution, you have the bundle.*** ⛔ **The failure this prevents is exact and near-miss here: "compact atoms made the store capture" would have shipped as the wave's sentence, and the shipped code's actual lever would have been unnamed.**
27. ⭐⭐ **BEFORE ATTRIBUTING A DEFECT TO A STRUCTURAL PROPERTY, CHECK WHETHER YOUR OWN *OPERATOR* PRODUCES IT.** Pass 1 attributed foreign-atom domination to the atom kernel's tails (a property of the representation). Pass 2 measured the same store with a **placing** write and the domination went to **0/48**. ⇒ ⭐ ***the interference was manufactured by the 300-step gradient write, not by the kernel*** — a *symptom-correct, cause-wrong* diagnosis, corrected in place. ⛔ **The generalisation: an artifact of the optimisation procedure will imitate a property of the architecture, and the cheapest discriminator is a different write, not a better measurement of the same one.**
28. ⭐⭐ **A METRIC THAT GOES TO ZERO BECAUSE THE SYSTEM BECAME DEGENERATE IS NOT A WIN — CHECK EVERY GATE LEG AGAINST THE PROHIBITED CONFIGURATION.** Site drift → 0 reads as a triumph and **is** the D2a table-expressible configuration the programme's own intervention doc prohibits. ⭐ ***Register, with every leg, the degenerate limit that would maximise it, and state in advance why that limit is not what you want.*** ⛔ **And when a designed mechanism could have bought a leg by algebra rather than by physics, the compliance ruling carries a counterfactual check, not an argument.**
29. ⭐ **A COUNT IS ONLY COMPARABLE INSIDE ITS OWN CHECKOUT.** Three modules use `importorskip`/`skipif` and a fresh worktree lacks the cached datasets ⇒ **a 7-test delta at one and the same commit.** ⭐ ***State the checkout with the number*** — and note that this pass's own circulating base figures (1445 shared vs 1438/1436 fresh vs three independent spoke measurements of 1445 in fresh worktrees) **do not reconcile and are filed as a conflict, not resolved.**
30. ⭐ **PARSE + IMPORT IS NOT VERIFICATION OF A HAND-RESOLVED MERGE; A CONFIRMING SUITE BELONGS ON STABLE DISK; AND A `-q` PARTIAL LOG CANNOT CHARACTERISE FAILURES.** All three are the Hub's own, all three self-filed. The first is the sharpest: a mechanical keep-both-sides resolution **whose conflict boundary fell between `@dataclass` and its class line** consumed one decorator and produced **19 suite failures from a single missing line** — **invisible to every check run at merge time.**

## Ch. 7 (epistemics) — four exemplars and three self-filed Hub anti-exemplars

- ⭐⭐⭐ **A SPOKE THAT PROVED ITS OWN GATE PASS WAS UNINFORMATIVE, IN ITS FIRST TEN LINES.** Arm B cleared all three legs on all three seeds — the outcome it was built to produce — and opened its report with *"the pass-2 gate is blind to addressability-from-`φ`, and arm B is the proof"*, with `n_never_read = 16/16` and a launder margin of **−0.594** beside it, and with the recommendation to measure the missing leg **on both arms from data already banked, before adjudicating anything.** ⭐ ***This is the conduct to reinforce: the spoke that had the most to gain from the gate's silence is the one that broke it.***
- ⭐⭐⭐ **AN ENGINEER THAT PUBLISHED THE ABLATION THAT TOOK ITS ARM'S NAME OFF THE RESULT.** Arm A registered its lever-separation predictions in a dated erratum **before** running them, measured that a **plain Gaussian** clears the same gate and **decodes better**, refused to leave the finding at one pilot seed, **promoted it to census strength** — and put *"the reach thesis survives, my attribution of it does not"* in its own report. ⭐ **It also asked the compliance question about its own load-bearing lever rather than deciding it.**
- ⭐⭐ **TWO INDEPENDENT KILL-CONDITIONS BUILT BEFORE THE THING THEY COULD KILL, AND BOTH SURFACED PROPERTIES THAT WEAKEN THEIR OWN ARM'S HEADLINE.** K7 was built and green **first**, in **both** arms separately, against the read-only instrument — and it produced a **positive floor of `tol/expansion-rate`** (one engineer's own prediction of "exactly 0.0" falsified, and *that* is the finding), a **bowl-minimum false positive of ≈`r_hi`**, and a **saturating bound for an isolated well.** ⇒ ⛔ **G-CAP alone cannot carry the verdict; G-DEC does.** ⭐ **A wave that measured its instrument's failure modes on the same day it used the instrument to declare success.**
- ⭐⭐ **A SPOKE THAT REPORTED ITS OWN DEGENERATE FIRST CONFIGURATION IN FULL.** Arm B's config 1 admitted **2/16** and **1/16** items — a gate scored on one or two wells — and it is on the record with its artifacts, its cause and its two designed changes, in an erratum filed **before** the final run, with each seed run **once** and none re-run after its result was seen.
- 🔴 **ANTI-EXEMPLAR 1, THE HUB'S, SELF-FILED: a merge resolution verified by the wrong instrument.** Keeping both sides of a predicted `config.py` conflict consumed a `@dataclass` decorator; `config.py` **parsed**, both groups **imported** and read their defaults, and **19 tests failed**. ⭐ *Parse + import is not verification of a hand-resolved merge; the suite is.*
- 🔴 **ANTI-EXEMPLAR 2, ALSO THE HUB'S: a confirming suite run on volatile disk.** `/private/tmp` was cleaned mid-run; pytest died with `INTERNALERROR`, **no summary line, ~40 minutes lost.** The commits survived only because they were on the `main` ref rather than worktree-local.
- 🔴 **ANTI-EXEMPLAR 3, ALSO THE HUB'S: failures characterised from a `-q` partial log** — *"at least one `F`"* against a true count of **19**. ⭐ *The FAILED names print only in a summary that a crashed run never reaches; use `-rf`.* ⚠ **Three defects of one family — verification shortfall — in one integration session, and all three self-filed, exactly as pass 1's two were.**

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The wave built the leg it was missing, and the leg worked — on the designed negatives first.** Pass 2 ended with a gate that could pass while **no read landed in any basin**. Pass 3's first act was **G-ADDR**: correct-basin (**the basin of the QUERIED item**, not merely *some* basin), never-addressed fraction, and the launder margin — **two-sided, pytest-asserted, and validated before anything was scored on it.** `gate_addr_validated = true` as the AND of **nine** mechanical checks: ⭐ **arm B's banked configuration — which passed all three pass-2 legs — FAILS G-ADDR 3/3** (the designed negative fires); a **planted permutation** scores **A1 = 0.0000** while `any_basin_rate` stays **exactly 1.0000** (⭐ *the defect class isolated: the two runs differ only in the declared target*); an `N1′` narrow-wells negative fails; **three scale controls** hold. ⭐ **The engineer added a positive control and a narrow-wells negative beyond the brief.**

**(2) And then the completed leg retired the evidence the whole diagnosis rested on.** ⛔⛔ **The *"58 / 62 / 62 of 64 reads land in no basin, digit-identical to pass 1"* statistic — the number this ledger, the primer and the charter all called decisive — is computed at the LAUNCH POINT.** `covered = min_j‖q₀ − c_j‖ ≤ ½·min-sep` is evaluated on **q₀** against the codebook, so **at fixed φ and a fixed admitted codebook it is the same number whatever the store does.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **its digit-identity was never evidence about the physics, and `n_never_read` inherits the defect.** ✅ **The replacement, measured on the same cells, is the wave's sentence:** ***BOTH arms address — arm A correct-basin A1 = 0.50 / 0.42 / 0.52 against a threshold of 0.25, arm B 0.93 / 0.98 / 0.88 — and both still fail on A3, the launder margin (launder 0.79–0.90 against a store at ≈0.5). THE STORE CAN ADDRESS AND STILL LOSES TO A ZERO-PARAMETER READ OF ITS OWN KEYS.*** ⚠ **The Hub's own registered prior Q2 predicted A1 = 0.03–0.12; measured 0.42–0.52 — falsified UPWARD by a factor 4–5, and recorded as falsified rather than quietly dropped.** ⛔ **Pass 2's *"arm B never addresses"* is retired with the telemetry that produced it.**

**(3) The second erratum removed the CAUSE, and this one hurts more, because the program had already acted on it.** §A29.5 explained everything by *"σ_q = 0.15 against a key spacing of 0.138–0.147 ⇒ a noisy query is as far from its own key as its key is from its neighbour ⇒ at this operating point the items are not separable by ANY read."* ⛔⛔ **`median_nn_task1` is the nearest-neighbour spacing of a ~200-key SIZING set. The store holds 16 items.** Against the population that actually matters, **σ_q/spacing = 0.32 (MNIST) and 0.19–0.37 (CIFAR)** ⇒ ⭐ **queries are ~3× CLOSER to their own key than to a neighbour — the exact opposite of the stated mechanism.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **the failure is REAL and reproduces; its CAUSE IS NOW OPEN**, registered as a theorist-priced question and explicitly **not assumed**. ⭐⭐ **And the same defect explains two other things the wave had been carrying as findings:** `d_safe` is derived from that same 200-key spacing and applied to a 16-item store ⇒ ⛔ **monitor #3's famous 0.000 refusal rate was ARITHMETIC, not a finding**; and it produced pass 3's own **arm-dependent cue difficulty** (a 30 % spread). ⭐ ***One defect, three consequences, and fixing the population choice fixes all three.***

**(4) The spine fired the branch it had pre-registered — and this is the wave's banked finding.** ⭐⭐ **Branch (b) NO DAYLIGHT: 0 of 9 cells, 3 of 3 arms**, computed by the registered `daylight_verdict` rule, **Hub prior Q6 = 0.70**, **nothing tuned** (store arm, map, dimension and seeds were all fixed by ruling or by measurement *before* the first cell). **|A3b| ≤ 0.047 on 7 of 9 cells** and 8 of 9 sit inside 2 SE ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***the store and an 832-byte table are statistically indistinguishable on held-out stream reads.*** Visible in the raw events: `randconv` seed 0 reads store **[0.6875, 0.5000, 0.2500, 0.1875]** against launder **[0.6875, 0.5000, 0.3125, 0.2500]** — **identical on the first two.** ⭐⭐ **And it is ATTRIBUTABLE, which is exactly what the two precondition spokes were bought for:** the store is **NOT inert at `d = 12` on CIFAR φ (9/9 cells)**, `geometry_go = true` was **verified in advance**, `gate_addr_validated = true` was **verified in advance** ⇒ ⛔ **the null cannot be dismissed as a dead store, a bad substrate or a blind gate.** ⇒ ⭐ **the tier-i thesis, now measured at a THIRD substrate (gym → LM block → CL census), flagged to the B′/shorts track** — ⛔ **flagged, not drafted: the revision is the writer track's and is Head-sequenced.**

**(5) Then the pass inverted its own premise.** The whole point of running at strong φ was *"does the physics add anything once the encoder is not the bottleneck?"* ⛔⛔ **The answer is that on this rig the encoder was never the binding constraint for addressing — and making φ stronger BY THE METRIC THAT DEFINES "STRONG" HERE made the store STRICTLY WORSE at addressing.** Paired on the bit-identical stream: **`simclr − randconv` A1 = −0.1406 ± 0.0508 (0/3 seeds)**, **`simclr − pca` = −0.1276 ± 0.0589 (0/3)**, `randconv − pca` **+0.0130 ± 0.1017 (tied)** — and **the weak PCA-φ reference produced the single best cell in the run** (A1 = 0.3828, the only cell to clear all four legs). ⭐⭐ **Meanwhile the UNFITTED `randconv` — which sees no data, costs 2.9 s against `simclr`'s ≈41 min/seed — clears the geometry GO rule at `simclr`'s own margin with ZERO fit steps.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ ***task accuracy and address geometry are ANTI-CORRELATED on this substrate***, measured on the store rather than on the geometry. ⛔⛔ **"strong φ" may never again be used as one undifferentiated notion, and Add.10 §A29.5's *"φ is the binding constraint on the CL rig"* does not survive.**

**(6) And the gate the Hub designed to score all of this was measured to reward the configuration the program prohibits.** Across all nine cells: ⛔⛔ **Spearman ρ(A1, G-DRIFT ratio) = −0.967** and **ρ(A1, settle↔launder agreement) = +0.933** ⇒ **the completed gate's score is a near-monotone function of settle-collapse.** The mechanism is visible per-arm: on `randconv` (the arm that works) the settle agrees with nearest-key indexing **51.6 %** of the time against a chance of 0.0625 and sits **0.44× spacing** from the launder's key, while on `simclr`/`pca` agreement is **at chance** and the settle lands **~2× spacing** away — ⭐ ***the working arm is the most table-like arm.*** ⛔ **And the per-arm boolean built to catch exactly this (`best_is_also_lowest_drift`) reads False on all three arms, decided by drift ties of 0.004 — it hid a ρ = −0.97 monotone relationship.** ⇒ **the gate is barred from further quotation until hardening lands.**

**(7) The verdict was then re-read by a rule ratified the same day — and the re-read is the honest one, not a rescue.** §A33.1: **every gate leg is MECHANICS or VALUE; mechanics legs are pass/fail at component level; VALUE legs exist ONLY at tier level with the tier's own control.** ⛔ **A launder margin on a COMPONENT gate is a DIAGNOSTIC, never a pass condition — and pass 3's A3 is the last of its kind.** ⇒ ⭐⭐ **the ratified filing form is *MECHANICS: all green. VALUE: untested by design at component level*** — and the earlier reading, ~~*"the completed gate fails on all three arms"*~~, is **superseded, struck and dated, not deleted.** ⚠ **This is not a promotion of the result: the A3 diagnostics are unchanged and negative, and the D2a bar now stands over the mechanics legs themselves.**

**(8) One thing was discharged cleanly, with no hedging.** ⭐ **R3, the compliance counterfactual attached to `atom_site_local_init` at pass 2:** with the lever ON at full strength and atoms placed exactly on the stored key, the shipped write with a displaced target moves the attractor with **`follow = 1.0079`** at arm A's own kernel — the settled point lands **0.0064** from the displaced minimum and **0.3024** from the stored key. ⇒ ⭐⭐ **the near-zero drift is an OUTCOME of the write objective, not an algebraic identity: capture was earned, not pinned. The compliance ruling STANDS, no escalation.** ⚠ **With an honest limit stated by the engineer: at `‖δ‖ = 0.60` the move saturates (`follow = 0.686`) — the write can drag the attractor only a bounded distance, which is a capacity statement, not a pinning one.**

**The rhyme.** Pass 1's was *the wave built the instrument that could stop it, the instrument said GO, and the wave read its own arithmetic and stopped anyway.* Pass 2's was *the wave fixed the thing it set out to fix and then proved that the thing it fixed was not the thing that matters.* ⭐⭐ **Pass 3's is: the wave finally built the leg that could ask the real question, asked it — and discovered that its evidence for the problem was vacuous, its explanation of the problem was measured on the wrong population, its "strong" encoder was the worst one, and its own gate was scoring the configuration the programme forbids. Every one of those four was found by the wave itself, and the pre-registered null it set out to test fired exactly as registered, on a substrate proven in advance to be alive.** *We did not learn that the store works. We learned, with unusual precision, what we had been wrong about — and the one number we had pre-committed to came out exactly where we said it would.*

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 8 (the memory-architecture chapter) — ⭐ verdict UNCHANGED; the chapter gains its sharpest laundering measurement, its first *completed* addressability instrument, and TWO retractions of its own supporting evidence.** `[split — lookup refuted, indexing refuted, control validated-with-scope]` stands. What is new: ⭐⭐ **the tier-i thesis measured at a THIRD substrate — branch (b) NO DAYLIGHT, 0/9 cells, 3/3 arms, |A3b| ≤ 0.047 on 7/9 ⇒ the store and an 832-byte table are statistically indistinguishable on held-out stream reads — and it is ATTRIBUTABLE** (store not inert 9/9; geometry GO and gate validation both verified in advance). ⛔⛔ **Two of the chapter's supporting sentences are retracted:** the *"reads land in no basin, digit-identical"* evidence (**launch-point vacuous**) and the *"σ_q ≈ spacing ⇒ not separable by any read"* cause (**wrong population**). ✅ **The chapter's honest sentence gains an eighth clause:** *…and when we finally measured where the reads SETTLE instead of where they LAUNCH, they were reaching the right well about half the time — and still losing to a copy of their own keys.* ⚠ **The dials:** `admission` gains the corrected monitor-#3 arithmetic (refusal 0.000 was a sizing artifact, not a finding); `isolation` gains the R3 discharge (attractors move when the objective demands it); ⛔ **`lifetimes` and `compute-adaptive reads` gain NOTHING at component level — stated so silence is not read as coverage** (⭐ though the anytime curve returns at tier level as **navigability**, §A33.2(iii)). ⛔ **The tier-ii organization dividend remains unmeasured (§A28.4).**

**Ch. 3 (trash region / trinity) — ⚠ the lifecycle verbs are STILL unbuilt, and they have now been ruled MOOT at this substrate rather than merely deferred.** ⛔ **`mergeable_pairs` still implements pass 1's rule; K9 is registered with two designed refusal negatives and NOTHING CALLS IT; `test_no_merge_verb_exists` still asserts the verb's absence — the next spoke must delete an assertion to build one.** ⚠ **Pass-1 `M` = 0.2333/0.2417/0.2417 remains `vacuous_gate`-tripped and is never quotable as a merge population without that qualifier** (verified travelling this fold). ⭐⭐ **What changes is the frame, not the debt: Add.12 §A34.3 ratifies a THREE-STATE lifecycle — PROTECTED (no decay) ⇄ ACTIVE (designed decay) → TRASH (`γ_φ(q)`/pruned) — with promotion usage-gated (read-hit telemetry + hysteresis) and ⛔ DEMOTION = protected→ACTIVE re-exposure to decay, explicitly NOT the trash region** (trash stays the never-useful/spurious route), plus a monitored protected fraction as the anti-collapse guard. ⇒ **the chapter's verbs re-enter as DESIGN in C2W11, not as resumed C2W8 debt.** ⭐ **And the trash region's C2W8 first use stands** (shipped OFF, bit-identical, parameter-count-identical).

**Ch. 5 (lattice / scale) — ⭐ the REACH thesis's last provenance caveat RETIRES, and the chapter acquires a hard operational ceiling.** ⭐⭐ **`d = 16` inertness is now measured on a CENSUSED cell at a fully honoured 131 072-atom budget** (median fitted depth **5.44e-7**, **0/6** usable wells) with `d = 8` (8 192, median 0.1479) and `d = 12` (32 768, 0.0186) digging ⇒ **N254's scratch-probe provenance caveat is discharged for `d = 16`** and *"not capacity-starved, REACH-bound"* is confirmed where it was previously only probed. ⛔ **`d = 16` is a DECLARED NOT-RUN for the spine and never a null** — the geometry **favours** 16 and the store **cannot dig** there, reported in separate fields on purpose. ⚠⚠ **And the chapter must carry the qualifier the spoke supplied: *"not inert" is not "healthy"* — even at `d = 8` only 3/6 designed writes produced a usable well, and at `d = 12` only 2/6.** ⭐ **`d = 12` is now charter-carried substrate as the operational reach ceiling** (§A34.10).

**Ch. 1 (mode budget / Goldstone) — ⛔ nothing. Stated explicitly so silence is not read as coverage.**

**Ch. 2 (mass-as-allocator) — ⚠ untouched as a verdict, and it acquires its first ratified forward scope in three waves.** ⛔ **No pass-3 cell varied any mass axis** (the census runs `newtonian_learned`; the spine reports no mass result) — stated so no capture, depth, drift or A1 number is read as a mass result. ⭐⭐ **But Add.12 §A34.5 rules the kinetics scoping and it lands squarely on this chapter: the dissipation is a DRAG FORCE `−γp`, so `v̇ = −(1/m)∇V − γv` and mass sets the DAMPING RATIO and inertia (ζ ∝ √m at fixed γ, λ) — light = underdamped (overshoot, longer floor travel), heavy = overdamped (local settle). Mass-blindness holds ONLY for the fully-settled endpoint in a strictly convex well (Prop F1 unchanged).** ⭐ **Ratified lever order: friction first (the measured 14× channel, already plumbed) · mass second · spectral/per-direction mass (per-address mass, shipped C2W7) as the richer selector — `M⁻¹∇V` with a mass TENSOR tilts the roll direction.** ⛔ **And the binding precondition, which is the chapter's own T3 result: the mechanism REQUIRES within-well flat/soft floors, finite-budget reads, or trajectory reads — settled-point kinetic gradients are BITWISE ZERO, so kinetics heads train only through those channels.** **Nomenclature (Def-2): this is INERTIAL `M` throughout; ⛔ spectral μ does not enter.** Status: **`[reframed → doctrine]` unchanged; the forward scope is TASKED (C2W11) as a priced ablation, ⛔ not all-at-once.**

**Ch. 4 (boost / test-time compute) — ⛔ nothing at component level, and ⭐ one ratified return at tier level.** ⛔ **No compute-adaptive read, no anytime protocol, no retry cell, no claim cell ran in this pass.** ⭐ **§A33.2(iii) brings the anytime curve back as the tier-ii VALUE leg *navigability* — answer quality vs READ COMPUTE** — which is the first time this chapter's instrument has been given a role in the joint behaviour rather than as a component metric. ⛔ **Registered, unbuilt, and it measures nothing yet.**

**Ch. 6 (sampler thermodynamics) — untouched.**

**Ch. 7 (epistemics) — ⭐⭐ this is the pass's richest chapter by a wide margin: two ratified self-errata on the program's OWN banked evidence, a pre-registered null that fired and was reported as a finding, three spokes reporting against their own interest, a `BLOCKED` spoke that ran zero cells and caught two Hub errors, and ten self-filed Hub instrument defects.**

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices and doctrine this pass earned (continuing pass 2's 25–30)

31. ⭐⭐⭐ **A STATISTIC COMPUTED AT THE LAUNCH POINT CAN NEVER BE EVIDENCE ABOUT THE SETTLE — AND "DIGIT-IDENTICAL ACROSS CONDITIONS" IS THE SIGNATURE THAT YOU HAVE ONE.** The programme read *"58/62/62, digit-identical between pass 1 and pass 2"* as decisive proof that the physics had not moved. It was **store-invariant by construction**: `covered` is evaluated on `q₀` against the codebook, so the same φ and the same admitted codebook give the same number **whatever the store does.** ⭐ ***When a number is IDENTICAL across an intervention that should have moved it, the first hypothesis is not "the intervention failed" — it is "this quantity cannot see the intervention."*** ⛔ **The mechanical rule: for every telemetry number, state WHERE IN THE TRAJECTORY it is evaluated, and never substitute a launch-side quantity for a settle-side one** (the two differ here by 0.94 vs 0.25–0.375 on the same cells).
32. ⭐⭐⭐ **A RATIO IS ONLY A DIAGNOSIS IF ITS NUMERATOR AND DENOMINATOR DESCRIBE THE SAME POPULATION.** The wave's root-cause sentence divided an absolute query noise by the spacing of a **~200-key sizing set**, and applied the result to a **16-item store**. Against the real population the ratio inverts from ≈1.07 to **0.32** — *"the items are not separable by any read"* becomes *"queries are three times closer to their own key than to a neighbour."* ⭐ ***One population mismatch produced THREE separate wrong conclusions — a retracted mechanism, a monitor whose 0.000 refusal rate was arithmetic, and an arm-dependent cue difficulty — and all three are fixed by one repair.*** ⛔ **Register the POPULATION with every geometric quantity, exactly as this programme already registers the scale.**
33. ⭐⭐ **A GATE LEG IS EITHER MECHANICS OR VALUE, AND IT MUST BE LABELLED WHEN IT IS REGISTERED, NOT WHEN IT IS SCORED.** Mechanics legs are pass/fail at component level; **VALUE legs exist only at tier level with the tier's own control.** ⛔ **A launder margin on a component gate is a DIAGNOSTIC, never a pass condition** — pass 3's A3 is the last of its kind, and the *"failed on all three arms"* verdict it produced is superseded. ⭐ ***This is the mirror of practice 25: a gate that cannot fail on what matters is one defect; a component gate that tries to measure VALUE is the other — it reports a failure it was never entitled to test.*** ⚠ **The discipline that makes this honest rather than convenient: both readings stay on the record with their dates, and the diagnostics are unchanged and still negative.**
34. ⭐⭐ **CHECK WHETHER YOUR GATE'S SCORE IS A MONOTONE FUNCTION OF THE DEGENERATE LIMIT YOU PROHIBIT — AND CHECK IT ACROSS THE WHOLE DESIGN, NOT PER-ARM.** ρ(A1, drift) = **−0.967** across nine cells: the completed gate scored a store higher the closer it got to being a lookup table. ⛔ **The per-arm boolean built to catch exactly this read False on all three arms, decided by 0.004 ties.** ⭐ ***A per-arm boolean is the wrong instrument for a design-level property — report the correlation, not only the flag*** — and ⭐ **the co-occurrence of "best-scoring" with "most table-like" is a property of the GATE, discoverable only by looking across cells that differ in both arm and seed.**
35. ⭐⭐ **PRE-REGISTERING BOTH BRANCHES IS WHAT MAKES A NULL A FINDING — and the compliance test is whether the null was FORESEEN, not whether it was liked.** Branch (b) was registered as **reportable** with a prior of **0.70** *before the first cell*, together with the exact rule that would compute it; the store arm, map, dimension and seeds were fixed by ruling or measurement in advance; **nothing was tuned after a number was seen.** ⭐ ***The non-compliant act would have been tuning branch (b) away, and the record shows the opposite: it is the headline.*** ⭐ **The companion discipline that made the null worth having: two precondition spokes bought ATTRIBUTABILITY** (store not inert 9/9, geometry GO, gate validated) — ⛔ **without them the same numbers would have been uninterpretable.**
36. ⭐ **A MECHANICAL PRECONDITION MUST NAME THE PATH THE SPOKE ACTUALLY WRITES, AND IT MUST BE VERIFIED ON DISK AT REVIEW.** The spine was spawned **in parallel with its own gating spokes** and **refused to run** — exactly as designed — and in doing so caught **two further Hub errors**: an **unfilled base-commit placeholder** in its own task file, and a precondition naming `…/PHI-GEOMETRY.json` where the spoke had written `…/results/PHI-GEOMETRY.json`. ⭐ ***A gate that fires on a file that exists is not a gate***; and ⭐ **a spoke that returns `BLOCKED` with zero cells, zero code and zero git footprint is a success of the protocol, not a wasted spawn.**

## Ch. 7 (epistemics) — the exemplars and the self-filed anti-exemplars

- ⭐⭐⭐ **THE ADVISOR RETRACTED ITS OWN TWO PIECES OF DECISIVE EVIDENCE, IN THE SAME DOCUMENT THAT ADJUDICATED THE WAVE.** §A31.1 and §A31.2 are both filed against the Advisor's own §A29 — *"the evidence I called decisive is a launch-point statistic"* and *"my σ_q ≈ spacing diagnosis is retracted as wrong-population"* — with the never-quotes attached, the replacement sentence supplied, and ⛔ **§A29 deliberately NOT edited in place so the supersession chain stays legible.** ⭐ ***The conclusion was allowed to survive on new evidence while the old evidence was destroyed; that is the distinction most retractions fail to make.***
- ⭐⭐⭐ **TWO SPOKES INDEPENDENTLY FOUND THE POPULATION DEFECT, AND NEITHER WAS LOOKING FOR IT.** wt1 found it re-scoring arm A; wt2 found it building the geometry — **the Hub then confirmed it in code** (`exp_well_lifecycle.py:202`). ⭐ **wt2 went further and reported that its own monitor-#3 diagnosis was *"arithmetically empty"* — the ratio it had been quoting was `d_safe_frac = 0.88` **by construction** and carried zero information.**
- ⭐⭐⭐ **THE SPINE PUBLISHED THE INVERSION THAT DESTROYED ITS OWN PASS'S PREMISE, IN ITS FIRST TEN LINES.** The pass existed to show that the physics adds something once the encoder is not the bottleneck; the spoke measured that the strong encoder is the **worst** arm on every leg, put it as reconciliation item 1, **named the confound that cuts against its own strongest claim** (the 30 % cue spread partially confounds *"randconv is best"*), and then stated why the confound **does not** rescue `simclr` — *an easier cue than `pca` and still below it.* ⭐ **It also flagged that `randconv` fails A1 by ONE READ and called it a seed-count question rather than running more seeds after seeing the result.**
- ⭐⭐ **A SPOKE THAT REPORTED THE INSTRUMENT DEFECT THAT WOULD HAVE FLATTERED ITS OWN RESULT.** wt1 measured that the legacy `own_foreign_site_depth` estimator **over-read arm A's foreign leg by 15–136×** (0.1233 → 0.00091; 0.3532 → 0.02359; 0.3407 → 0.00464), repaired it, pinned it against **all three kernels** with a cross-kernel test — and stated plainly that ⛔ **the gate never read that column, so no pass-2 verdict changes.** ⭐ *A correction that helps the arm, reported with the clause that stops it being used as a win.*
- ⭐⭐ **A `BLOCKED` REPORT WITH ZERO CELLS THAT WAS ONE OF THE WAVE'S MOST USEFUL ARTIFACTS** (pass-3 review): it refused to run, and it caught the Hub's unfilled base commit and the Hub's wrong precondition path.
- ⭐⭐ **PRE-REGISTRATION SCORECARDS PUBLISHED WITH THEIR MISSES, ON EVERY SPOKE.** The spine: **11 hits, 5 misses** — including **G2 FALSIFIED** (its own predicted arm ordering), **G3 badly missed** (*"I registered the mechanism and still got the level wrong"*), **D3 missed at the boolean with the note that the boolean is the wrong instrument**, and **S1b missed because the store dug DEEPER than predicted**. wt2: **8 hits, 3 misses**, including **P2/P2b missed because the address space is far MORE comfortable than predicted** — the miss that became erratum 2. ⭐ **wt1's registered P5 (−0.354 ± 0.05) held exactly, and its ERRATA §3 attribution cell predicted *"A1 returns to 0.5000 ± 0.08"* before running and measured 0.5000 to 4 dp.**
- 🔴 **ANTI-EXEMPLAR 1, THE HUB'S, SELF-FILED: the gate the Hub specified rewarded the configuration the Hub prohibits** (ρ = −0.967), **and the flag the Hub specified to catch it was too weak to fire** (0.004 ties). ⭐ *Both were found by the spoke, not by the Hub, and both are on the record with owners assigned.*
- 🔴 **ANTI-EXEMPLAR 2, THE HUB'S, SELF-FILED: a scale guard that bounded the METRIC and not the VERDICT.** A legal, scale-covariant rescale moved A1 by **+0.0469 — inside the registered 0.05 bound — and flipped the leg False → True.** ⭐ *The guard passed on its own terms while the thing it guarded was not stable.*
- 🔴 **ANTI-EXEMPLAR 3, THE HUB'S, SELF-FILED: a task-file base commit left as `main @ <the Hub will name the integrated base>`, and a precondition naming a path no spoke ever wrote.** ⚠ **Filed beside N260 / N272 — the THIRD consecutive wave in which the Hub's own instrument or integration step was the largest source of defects, and the third in which the Hub filed it against itself.**

# ⟲ C2W8 **CLOSE-FINAL** addendum — the wave repaired the instrument that had scored it, and then found the defect inside its own designed negative (2026-08-10, `doc-curator-c2w8-close-c2w11-fold`, **[C2W8-CLOSE-FINAL]**) — **the exam that rewarded collapse now fails a planted table; the test that proved the exam could fail was built on the statistic the wave had just retired; and one repair moved a knob nobody was looking at**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope.** Sources: the ACCEPTED-MERGED close-out spoke **`c2w8-close-gate-hardening`** (§1–§7 + its six-item reconciliation list, its own `PREREG.md` filed before the code, and `GATE-HARDENING-DONE.json` as described in it), the **`[C2W8-CLOSE-FINAL]`** §10 review entry, charter **ADDENDUM 11 §A31–§A32** and **ADDENDUM 12 §A33–§A34**, and **`PREREG-C2W11.md`** (Amendment 1 §2.1, §4, §6, §8) for what the repairs bind forward.
