> ⛔ **THE HUB WROTE NO ⟲ ADDENDUM FOR THE CLOSE-OUT** (verified per-file before writing, positive-controlled: the last `⟲ C2W8` heading in this file is the **PASS-3 + CLOSE** addendum, whose own scope banner states that the hardening spoke *"HAS RUN NOTHING"*). **This addendum is the close-out's primary ledger record, not a `(curator supplement)` — and its whole reason for existing is that the previous fold correctly declared this material out of scope.**
> ⛔⛔ **SCOPE, BINDING ON EVERY LINE: THE CLOSE-OUT SPOKE AND THE C2W11 HAND-OFF.** ⛔ **NO BANKED ARM WAS RE-RUN** — every consequence below is a **mechanical consequence of a ratified rule or of a repaired instrument**, never a re-score. ⛔ **No C2W11 cell has run**, so nothing below is a C2W11 result. ⛔ **The arm A vs arm B race stays VOID and UNADJUDICATED (§A30.1)** · ⛔ **no tier-ii, full-CLU or I2 verdict, no paper number, no performance/ACC claim** · **every reading `flags.promotable = false`** · ⛔ **depth is not feature importance (§A23.5 ACTIVE)** · ⛔ **wells are never named semantically.**

## ⭐⭐ THE CLOSE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The wave's last act was to repair the instrument that had scored it — and the repair is measured, not asserted.** `gate_hardening_done = true` as the AND of **twelve** items, with **both** mandatory designed negatives **measured on planted constructions**: ⛔⛔ **a table-like store planted at drift/spacing 0.0042 FAILS the repaired two-sided leg and PASSES the retired one-sided rule** (floor **0.0100 = 0.01 × the MEASURED codebook spacing**, derivation registered in the spoke's own prereg **before** the code, sitting **2×** above a banked table-expressible point); and ⭐⭐ **a mutated store leaves `launch_covered` BIT-IDENTICAL at 8/8 → 8/8 while `settle_covered` moves 8/8 → 0/8** — *that single pair is the proof the two are different quantities*, and it is the mechanical explanation of the *"58/62/62 digit-identical"* reading erratum 1 retired. ⇒ ⭐ **§A32.3's quotation bar is DISCHARGED: census numbers are quotable again, with the instrument that produced them named.**

**(2) And the repair immediately proved itself by failing a real cell of ours.** The pass-3 **arm-A seed-0** cell sits at drift/spacing **0.0071 < 0.0100** ⇒ ⛔ **it would FAIL the repaired leg on the D2a side.** ⭐ **That is the intended behaviour and it is worth saying out loud: the repaired census is STRICTLY HARDER than the one every banked C2W8 number was measured on**, so a C2W11 cell that fails there is not a regression in the physics. ⚖ **Recorded, not re-scored; the Hub is asked to confirm the floor before C2W11 scores anything.**

**(3) The sharpest finding of the close-out is a defect inside a test — and the reason it happened is structural, not careless.** G-ADDR's designed negative **N1** (*"arm B's banked configuration MUST FAIL"*) fed **`a2 = 1.0` from arm B's banked `frac_never_read`** — ⛔⛔ **exactly the `covered`-derived launch-point statistic that erratum 1 retired.** ⇒ ***the negative that certified the instrument's falsifiability was built out of the defect the instrument was being repaired for.*** ⭐ **The spoke REWROTE it rather than deleting it, with the truth in the docstring**, and G-ADDR's falsifiability now rests entirely on **planted** constructions (narrow wells · permutation · the drift negative · the scale-guard negative) that run the shipped code. ⭐⭐ **Standing lesson, and it generalises well beyond this gate: a designed negative must rest on a planted construction, never on banked numbers that may themselves carry the defect under repair.**

**(4) One ratified rule, applied in code, moved a banked verdict — and the discipline is in refusing to call that a result.** With **A3 relabelled DIAGNOSTIC in the census code** (`gate_addr_pass = A1 AND A2`; `in_pass_condition: False`), arm B's banked configuration — which failed G-ADDR **only** on A3 — **passes both MECHANICS legs** (settle-side A1 = **0.9297 / 0.9844 / 0.8750**; A2 pass 3/3). ⛔ **This is a mechanical consequence of §A33.1, not a re-scoring and not an adjudication:** no cell was re-run, **the race is VOID (§A30.1)**, and **arm B is claim-barred by `NO_TIER_II_CLAIM` regardless**. ⭐ **§A33.1 is now enforced by the instrument, not only by doctrine** — which is the difference between a rule and a habit.

**(5) The quiet item that will cost someone a day if it is missed: one repair moved a knob nobody was looking at.** Every arm store-config factory recovers the spacing as **`d_safe / d_safe_frac`** ⇒ repairing `d_safe`'s population **also moved each arm's CO-SCALED ATOM WIDTH onto the store population**, whose spacing is **~3× the sizing set's (0.445 vs 0.141)**. ⇒ ⛔⛔ **`atom_width_frac_spacing = 1.5` no longer means what it meant when it was selected, and the banked value is NOT inherited.** ✅ **`d_safe_population = "sizing"` reproduces banked cells bit-exactly and is for reproduction ONLY**; **C2W11 spoke A must RE-SELECT the width against the store population and DECLARE it**, with the census's refuse-at-unselected-width guard making the drift impossible rather than merely discouraged.

**(6) And the wave's own record was corrected twice more, both times by someone other than its author.** ⛔ *"Zero worktrees"* was **true when written and false within hours** — two removed worktrees and the broken ref `refs/remotes/clu-dev/main 2` **came back**, ⭐ **diagnosed from evidence (600/700 permissions and preserved older timestamps against live git's 644/755 ⇒ a RESTORE process, not git)**, and a left-in-place broken ref makes `git merge` die with `fatal: stash failed` **on a clean tree**. ⚠ **And the Hub's own "verified worklist" for the inversion qualifier over-counted by FILENAME matches** — the curator's per-file counts (28 / 10 / 4 / **0**) were ruled canonical, and the file the Hub listed at 4 hits has **zero** concept-uses. ⭐ *Both were found downstream of the person who filed them, and both were filed against the filer.*

**The rhyme.** Pass 1: *the wave built the instrument that could stop it, the instrument said GO, and the wave stopped anyway.* Pass 2: *the wave fixed the thing it set out to fix and proved that the thing it fixed was not the thing that matters.* Pass 3: *the wave finally asked the real question and discovered its evidence was vacuous, its explanation was measured on the wrong population, its "strong" encoder was the worst one, and its own gate scored the configuration the programme forbids.* ⭐⭐ **The close-out's is quieter and it completes the set: the wave went back and repaired the instrument that had produced all of that — and found, in the process, that the test certifying the instrument could fail had itself been built out of the retired statistic. *We did not learn anything new about the store. We made every future number about the store harder to earn, and we can prove the bar moved, because a planted table now fails where it used to pass.***

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices this fold earns (continuing 31–36)

37. ⭐⭐⭐ **A DESIGNED NEGATIVE MUST REST ON A PLANTED CONSTRUCTION, NEVER ON BANKED NUMBERS.** G-ADDR's N1 fed a banked `frac_never_read` into the leg — the exact launch-point statistic under repair — so **the certificate of falsifiability inherited the defect it was certifying against.** ⭐ ***A negative built from banked telemetry can only be as trustworthy as the telemetry, which is precisely what is in question when you are repairing an instrument.*** ⛔ **The mechanical rule: every designed negative constructs its own store/queries and runs the shipped code; if it consumes a recorded number, it is a regression test, not a falsifier.** ⭐ **And rewrite rather than delete: deleting N1 would have removed the falsifiability record along with the defect.**
38. ⭐⭐ **WHEN A SHARED QUANTITY IS RECOVERED BY DIVISION INSIDE OTHER COMPONENTS, REPAIRING IT MOVES EVERY CONSUMER — ENUMERATE THEM BEFORE SHIPPING.** `d_safe / d_safe_frac` was how each arm's factory recovered the spacing, so a **population** fix silently became an **atom-width** change of ~3×. ⭐ ***The repair was right and the propagation was invisible; what makes it safe is that the spoke declared it in code and named the reproduction flag.*** ⛔ **A repair report must say which banked cells stop reproducing and how to reproduce them.**
39. ⭐⭐ **A CLEANLINESS CLAIM IS A STATEMENT ABOUT A MOMENT, NOT A PROPERTY OF THE REPOSITORY.** *"Zero worktrees, no broken refs"* was true when written and false hours later — a **restore process** on this machine brings removed worktrees and refs back (**600/700 permissions and preserved older timestamps** against live git's 644/755 is the signature), and a left-in-place broken ref breaks the next `git merge` with `fatal: stash failed` **on a clean tree**. ⛔ **Check `git worktree list` and `.git/refs/…` at the START of a session, not only at the end.**
40. ⭐ **A PER-FILE `grep` FOR A CONCEPT MUST EXCLUDE FILENAME AND PATH MATCHES.** The Hub's verified worklist listed a file at 4 hits that has **zero** concept-uses — all four were the *filename* `test_cifar_strong_phi.py`. ⭐ ***A "verified" worklist with a phantom entry is worse than an unverified one, because the next reader stops checking.*** ⚠ **Companion, unchanged: directory-level search over `.claude/` is void (gitignored) — sweep per file and positive-control every negative.**

# ⟲ C2W10 addendum — the wave that tested both of its venues before building on them and was beaten at matched bytes on both (2026-08-11, `doc-curator-c2w10-fold`, **[C2W10-CLOSE]**) — **the mechanics shipped, the value question found it had nowhere to be asked, and the one instrument that could have answered a two-wave-old question turned out to have no channel to measure through**

> **⚠ Provenance & scope.** Sources: the four ACCEPTED spoke reports **`c2w10-benchmark-gate` · `c2w10-metro-gate` · `c2w10-lifecycle-mechanics` · `c2w10-i2-usage-erosion`** (plus the earlier `c2w10-benchmark-scout`) and the JSON verdicts they describe, **`PREREG-C2W10.md` AMENDMENTS 3–8**, the **`[C2W10-CLOSE]`** §10 review entry, and charter **ADDENDUM 14 §A39–§A41** (Head-ratified 2026-08-11). Position: **`main @ a47ed20`** = `clu-dev/main` (pushed `168a892..a47ed20`), full suite **1679 passed / 0 failed / 2 deselected** (2726 s), **HEAD verified identical before AND after**, ⛔ `origin` verified frozen at `40c2f31`, **zero C2W10 worktrees**; count arithmetic in one checkout: **1610 selected at `168a892` → 1679, +69 = 34 + 21 + 14.** **Companion edits this fold:** `negative_results.md` (**N294–N303** + a `DECLARED NOT-RUNs — C2W10` block), `future_work.md` (status moves on seven earlier items + nine new boundaries), `HEP_primers.md` (**§11.29, Records 38–39** + a dated update on Record 23). ⟲ protocol respected — **no chapter body edited, no earlier addendum rewritten, nothing deleted.**
> ⛔ **THE HUB WROTE NO ⟲ ADDENDUM FOR C2W10** (verified per-file with a positive control: the last `⟲` heading in this file is the **C2W8 CLOSE-FINAL** addendum; the eight `C2W10` strings that exist in this document are all forward-references inside C2W6/C2W7/C2W8 addenda). **This addendum is C2W10's primary ledger record, not a `(curator supplement)`.**
> ⛔⛔ **SCOPE, BINDING ON EVERY LINE: THE VALUE SURFACE (PREREG-C2W10 §6, V1–V3) IS A DECLARED NOT-RUN FOR WANT OF AN ADMISSIBLE VENUE — NEVER A NULL, IN ANY ARTIFACT, EVER.** ⛔ **No CLU cell of any kind ran in either gate spoke** · ⛔ **no tier-ii, full-CLU, VALUE, I2 or performance verdict exists in this wave** · the lifecycle rig's stream is **synthetic = a MECHANICS instrument, never a claim venue (§A14.8)** · ⛔ **depth is not feature importance (§A23.5 ACTIVE — and now TRAVELLING)** · ⛔ **`M` never appears in the lifecycle build.**
> ⭐⭐ **AND THE FRAMING IS RULED, NOT CHOSEN (§A41.1): TWO FIRED TRIPWIRES ARE A FALLBACK BEING CORRECTLY RETIRED, NOT A VENUE CRISIS** — Track-2's primary (bounded-state sequence modelling) is live on CSF3 and the tier-ii compositional family is live in C2W11.

## ⭐⭐ THE WAVE IN ONE ARC

**(1) The wave's best decision was made before it started: it ran the venue tripwire FIRST.** The scoping Hub registered that **criterion 4 gates the VALUE venue, not the MECHANICS build** — so the wave was robust to the tripwire either way — and then spent ≈4 h of CPU asking whether a **byte-matched exemplar store** already sits at the frontier of the primary benchmark. It does: **SAM-kNN at 0.634 MiB scores 76.9157 % against ARF-100's 78.8139 %**, `b2_margin_pts = −1.8983`, **`criterion4_cleared = FALSE`**, firing against **every** ARF reference computable including Souza's published 77.13 (−0.2143). ⭐ **The mechanism is the part worth carrying: on that stream, storing more makes you worse** — the exemplar ladder runs **76.03 % at L=500 down to 59.75 % at our own byte budget** — because *recency is the hidden regime variable*. ⛔⛔ **And the anti-hobbling rule is what decided the gate: the exemplar arms ran raw AND standardised with the max consumed, worth +4 to +6 points; raw-only would have returned `criterion4_cleared = TRUE` and the wave would have built on an inadmissible venue.**

**(2) The pre-authorised fallback was not argued into place — it was put through the same tripwire, and it failed harder, on three independent criteria.** A k-NN over past windows at **0.634 MiB scores MAE 314.575** against a tuned GBDT's **335.203** carrying **5.44× the bytes** — firing against **all nine** strong references and, crucially, **also on a static 70/30 holdout with no streaming harness at all** (265.47 vs 266.33). ⛔ Criterion 2 fails independently: the best tuned ensemble beats a **one-line weekly naive** by **2.17 %**. ⭐⭐ **And the third kill is the one that does not depend on any baseline being strong: shuffle the stream into random order and the byte-matched store gets BETTER (320.982 → 311.748, −2.9 %) — at the budgets this wave would have used, that stream's ordering carries nothing for a memory to be good at.** *A retention experiment there would have been measuring an effect the venue does not have.*

**(3) So the value question found it had nowhere to be asked — and the honest form of that is a NOT-RUN, not a null.** ⛔⛔ **The VALUE surface is a declared NOT-RUN with an attributable cause**, and `c2w10-value-surface.md` is superseded *as scoped* rather than deleted. ⭐ **Criterion 4 now stands at six confirmations, and the programme's response is to believe its own theorem rather than pay for a seventh: no third tabular stream is shopped, and the absence of one is a RULING.** ⭐⭐ **The question itself is handed forward as a design input, in the C2W5→C2W7 pattern: *"does a persistent store help across compositional streams"* — on a venue where a table does not win by theorem — if C2W11's tier-ii claim lands.**

**(4) Meanwhile the mechanics shipped, and shipped with the discipline the last two waves paid for.** The three-state lifecycle **`PROTECTED ⇄ ACTIVE → TRASH`** landed **7/7 legs**, with `lifecycle_mechanics_done` computed **in code** as the AND over pytest exit statuses, and ⭐ **the kill-conditions committed BEFORE the verbs — visibly, in the commit log (`162fdba`: 32 failed, 1 passed against a stub).** ⭐⭐ **Every designed negative ships with the NAMED MUTATION that makes it fail** — the direct answer to *"a gate that cannot fail on the thing that matters"*, which C2W8 caught three times. **L2's rich-get-richer negative passes** (an early-popular-then-abandoned well demotes and is **not** trashed); **L3 routes never-useful items to the trash keyed on `read_hits`, never on depth**; **L7 OFF is bit-identical and parameter-count-identical**; and ⛔ **L4 is labelled UNEXERCISED**, because the protected population peaked at **3 / 1 / 3** against a cap of 16 — *promotions are undone almost as fast as they are made, 60 against 53, and the register says so rather than counting a green verb as a working one.*

**(5) The I2 re-measurement is the wave's sharpest epistemic object: the arithmetic ran, the answer is INDETERMINATE, and the finding is that the rig could not ask the question at all.** Power was met for the first time (`n_live` 64/64/64), the proxy was **reliable for the first time** (`ICC` +0.280/+0.447/+0.467), and `ρ(U, E_netted)` came back **negative on 3/3 seeds** — and still the registered CONFIRM branch fired **0/3**. ⛔⛔ **Then the load-bearing fact: `exp_persistent_store` runs no outer objective and no optimizer step on store parameters, so the hypothesised mechanism — gradient magnitude ∝ contribution — cannot act at that rig at all.** The deferral that sent the question there had checked the **live-well count** and never the **channel**; the count was in fact adequate. ⇒ ⛔ **a DECLARED NOT-RUN at this rig, never a null**, re-homed to a rig with a live outer objective, with **§A23.5 staying ACTIVE and TRAVELLING.** ⭐ **And the spoke got there first: it registered the never-quote and declared the mechanism a NOT-RUN *before* the close relay ruled either way — the artifact and the ruling agree because the measurement was honest, not because it was told.**

**(6) The instrument lesson is the twin of C2W6's, and it arrived by a refuted pre-registration.** `E_netted`'s split-half reliability is **0.78–0.89** — *excellent* — while the quantity itself is **≈4 float32 ULP**: a netted total log-drop of **4e-7 nats** against **0.58–0.62 nats** of designed decay, a ratio of **6.8e-7**, with more than half the wells showing netted *increases*. ⭐⭐ ***A stable ruler pointed at nothing is still a stable ruler.*** The proposed rule — **reliability AND magnitude against the designed channel; ≤1e-6 of the channel ⇒ UNDEFINED** — is filed **PROPOSED, Advisor-facing, not adopted**, with two concrete consequences: **float64 amplitudes** for any future undesigned-erosion cell, and a corrected detection floor of **0.272 / 0.265 / 0.272**.

**(7) And three defects were filed by the people they cost.** The analyst who owned the Metro gate found **its own pre-registered protocol leaking up to 23 h of future labels**, implemented the embargo, and reported that the leak was **asymmetric in the direction of firing its own tripwire** (+10.9 % to a k-NN, −0.3 % to GBDT). The engineer who owned the lifecycle found that **the numbers relayed to it for the I1 leg were the GUARD-OFF baseline**, resolved it from the raw artifact **before** pinning the test, and in doing so gave a standing charter claim (*"zero post-guard violations"*) **its first evidence — recomputed as 0/0/0** from artifacts where the field was `null`. And the Hub filed **two defects of its own registration** against itself: an ambiguous 2-SE wording, and a CONFIRM bar that needed **ρ ≈ −0.47** at the very `n` the same document called sufficient.

**The rhyme.** C2W8 pass 1: *the wave built the instrument that could stop it, the instrument said GO, and the wave stopped anyway.* C2W8's close: *the wave repaired the instrument that had scored it and found the defect inside its own designed negative.* ⭐⭐ **C2W10 completes a different arc — the cheap one: *the wave checked, for two hours, whether the room it was about to build in had anything to build for. It did not. So it built the machinery, banked the two measurements that closed a whole track, and refused to shop for a third room.*** ⛔ **We learned nothing new about whether a persistent store is worth anything. We learned, at a fiftieth of the price, that neither of the two best-documented places to ask could have told us.**

## Chapter-by-chapter deltas

**Ch. 3 (trash region / trinity) — ⭐⭐ THE FIRST SUBSTANTIVE MOVEMENT IN THIS CHAPTER IN THREE WAVES, and it is mechanics only.** The three-state lifecycle **`PROTECTED ⇄ ACTIVE → TRASH`** exists, is green on 7/7 legs, and ships with its designed negatives and their can-fail mutations: **promotion usage-gated with hysteresis** (`d_dwell > window` enforced at construction — below that inequality the burst negative is *arithmetically incapable of failing*), **demotion = re-exposure to designed decay and ⛔ NEVER the trash region** (the rich-get-richer negative is the standing test), **trash reserved for the never-useful/spurious and keyed on item-id `read_hits`, ⛔ never on depth**, a bounded protected fraction with a new `protected_saturation` monitor row, and **γ_φ(q)'s first experimental use ON.** ⛔ **What the chapter does NOT gain: any evidence that the lifecycle is worth anything** — no VALUE cell exists, the substrate is synthetic, **L4 is UNEXERCISED**, and part of the trashed population is *"unreachable"* rather than *"useless"* (**48–55 % of reads land in no basin at all**). ⚠ **Merge, prune-by-depth and K9 remain UNBUILT.**

**Ch. 8 (memory architecture) — ⭐ the verdict is UNCHANGED; the chapter gains a closed route and a rule about where it may shop.** `[split — lookup refuted, indexing refuted, control validated-with-scope]` stands, and **no measurement in the chapter moves.** What is new is a **boundary**: the *non-stationary streaming prediction with regime revisits* family is, **by measurement on both shortlisted candidates**, metric-native at matched bytes — the sixth confirmation of criterion 4 — so the chapter's laundering story gains two real-data instances that were bought for ≈5 h of CPU rather than a wave. ⚠ **The dials:** `lifetimes` gains **mechanics** (the lifecycle verbs, netting as a build requirement) and ⛔ **no value**; `admission`, `isolation` and `compute-adaptive reads` gain **NOTHING** — stated so silence is not read as coverage.

**Ch. 7 (epistemics) — ⭐⭐ the richest chapter of the wave, and every item in it was self-filed.** A pre-registered *protocol* claim falsified by its own author mid-run (the 24-h leak); a pre-registered *"this will be noise"* refuted into the wave's instrument rule; a relayed number caught as belonging to the wrong arm **before** it was pinned into a test; a Hub filing two defects in its own registration; and a spoke declaring a mechanism a NOT-RUN **against its own task's framing** before any ruling existed. ⭐ **Plus a calibration datum worth keeping: on the one number nobody had published, the analyst's pre-registered 0.25 beat the Hub's 0.55.**

**Ch. 1, Ch. 2, Ch. 4, Ch. 5, Ch. 6 — ⛔ nothing measured. Stated explicitly so silence is not read as coverage.** ⚠ For Ch. 2 specifically: **Nomenclature (Def-2) — no mass of either kind enters this wave**; the lifecycle build states `⛔ M never appears`, and *depth* throughout is a **fitted well depth**, ⛔ **never feature importance**.

## Gap-list updates

- ⭐⭐ **CLOSED AS A ROUTE (not as a question):** *"find a real, drift-annotated streaming benchmark where a persistent store can show value"* — ⛔ **closed by ruling after six confirmations**, with the two new ones measured here. **The question re-opens only in compositional territory.**
- ⭐ **DISCHARGED:** *net the designed decay before any erosion claim* (C2W6 fold-2's build requirement) — **shipped, raw AND netted on every curve**; and *item-id keys in per-well telemetry* — **shipped**.
- ⚠ **RECURRED:** *a usage-vs-erosion decision rule that can confirm its own hypothesis* — a new rule, the same structural failure, now with the Hub's own arithmetic attached (**ρ ≈ −0.47 needed at n = 64**).
- ⛔ **NEWLY UNHOMED:** *I2's mechanism* — it has never been measured through a live channel, at any `n`, in any wave, and the caveat that depends on it **travels** until it is.
- ⚠ **NEW AND UNOWNED:** float64 store amplitudes for any undesigned-erosion cell · settle-side crediting for the usage proxy · which reading of §A20.6 the trash criterion takes · whether `d_demote` should exceed the usage burst gap · the one-line depth-endogeneity check on `U`.

## ⭐⭐ Standing practices this fold earns (continuing 37–40)

41. ⭐⭐⭐ **TEST THE VENUE BEFORE YOU BUILD IN IT — AND GIVE THE TRIVIAL COMPETITOR EVERY ADVANTAGE THE RULES ALLOW.** Two tripwires, ≈5 h of CPU total, closed a whole track and saved two lifecycle builds. ⛔ **The ordering is the practice:** discovering a venue is inadmissible *after* the build is the expensive path and it is the one that is free to avoid. ⭐ **And the anti-hobbling half is not a formality — it INVERTED the first verdict:** raw-only features would have returned `criterion4_cleared = TRUE`.
42. ⭐⭐⭐ **MEASURE HEADROOM AGAINST THE STRONGEST TRIVIAL RULE, NOT AGAINST PERSISTENCE — AND SHUFFLE THE STREAM.** On Metro those two readings are **41.6 %** (pass) and **2.17 %** (fail); both are true and only the second is informative. ⭐ **And the shuffle is the check that depends on no baseline at all: *a venue where destroying the temporal order does not degrade a byte-matched store cannot host a retention claim, whatever the tripwire says.*** It costs 20 s of CPU. ⚠ **Both are PROPOSED practices, not yet adopted.**
43. ⭐⭐ **A PROTOCOL CLAIM IN A PRE-REGISTRATION IS A HYPOTHESIS ABOUT THE PROTOCOL, AND IT IS FALSIFIABLE LIKE ANY OTHER.** *"Test-then-train is leak-free here"* was written down, believed, and **wrong**: at an h-step horizon plain prequential hands a continuously-updated learner up to **h−1** hours of future labels, worth **+10.9 %** to a small exemplar store and **−0.3 %** to a periodically-refit GBDT. ⭐ ***The leak was asymmetric in the direction of the analyst's own tripwire, and the analyst closed it anyway.*** ⛔ **Mechanical form: compute the embargo index `A(t)` and publish the leak-delta table for any h-step-ahead stream.**
44. ⭐⭐⭐ **RELIABILITY IS NECESSARY BUT NOT SUFFICIENT — CHECK MAGNITUDE AGAINST THE DESIGNED CHANNEL.** A proxy split-halved at **0.78–0.89** while being **four float32 ULP** of round-off, because a deterministic, item-specific rounding residual reproduces perfectly. ⭐ ***Repeatability tells you the instrument is stable; it never tells you it is pointed at anything.*** ⛔ **The pair to hold together: C2W6's `ICC ≤ 0 ⇒ UNDEFINED` and C2W10's `≤1e-6 of the designed channel ⇒ UNDEFINED`.** ⚠ **PROPOSED, Advisor-owned.**
45. ⭐⭐ **A DEFERRAL MUST NAME THE MECHANISM'S CHANNEL, NOT ONLY ITS SAMPLE SIZE.** *"Defer until there are enough live wells"* was satisfied to the digit — and the destination rig had **no outer objective and no optimizer step on store parameters**, so the mechanism could not act there at any `n`. ⇒ ⛔ **the condition becomes *"enough wells AND a live channel"***, and the caveat that depends on the answer **travels with the question** instead of expiring where it was parked. ⭐ ***An unmeasurable question deferred to a rig that cannot measure it looks exactly like an answered one, two waves later.***
46. ⭐⭐ **SET THE DECISION THRESHOLD AND THE SAMPLE SIZE JOINTLY, AND SAY WHICH QUANTITY THE INTERVAL MUST EXCLUDE.** At the power precondition's own floor the registered CONFIRM bar needed **ρ ≈ −0.47** — near-unreachable at the very `n` the same prereg called sufficient — and *"ρ ≤ −0.2 beyond 2 SE"* admitted two readings that differ by **2/3 seeds**. ⭐ **Both were filed by the Hub against its own registration, which is the only reason INDETERMINATE reads as a result rather than as a shortfall.**
47. ⭐⭐ **RE-DERIVE AN INHERITED NUMBER FROM THE RAW ARTIFACT BEFORE PINNING IT INTO A TEST.** The figures relayed as the I1 arm were the **guard-OFF baseline** (27/40/70 with rates 0.593/0.050/0.043; the I1 arm is **44/62/59 with 6/0/0**), and a standing charter claim of *"zero post-guard violations"* turned out to have **`null` in every artifact** until this wave recomputed it as **0/0/0**. ⭐ ***A claim with no artifact behind it survives indefinitely, because nothing ever asks it for one.***

The C2W10 and C2W8-close addenda above are untouched.** ⚠ **This addendum is written against a wave the Hub closed as MECHANICS** (charter Add.15 §A43.1) **and a campaign the Head closed the same day** (Add.16). ⛔ **Coverage caveat, stated up front: this is a PARTIAL fold.** Five of the seven C2-close debts (the pilot-ttt merge + its three rulings · the post-merge suite · the `d_addr` probe · the spoke-B §2 contradiction fix · the `[C2W11]` §10 close entry + `clu-dev` push) are **outside the four documents this curator may edit and outside its tool set** — they are named in the report and remain **OPEN and Hub-owned**.

## The one-paragraph version

C2W11 asked charter §A33.2's tier-ii question on a substrate repaired in three measured ways, and **the question turned out to be unaskable at `d_addr = 4` — not lost, unaskable.** The ceiling on **any** placement organizer is **0.3290 ± 0.0074** (spoke B, an exact linear-assignment optimum) and the launch points' own decodability is **0.3449 ± 0.0175** (spoke C, a different instrument, a blind spoke) — **both below the bar for both arms**, so the swap compares nothing. In the same wave the store was **exonerated**: at **0.8621 ± 0.0036** exact-set under oracle addressing, with a **zero-parameter** read, the store, the settle and the payload composition all work the moment addressing is correct. ⭐ **The program's blocker chain therefore moved for the first time in five waves** — write-side organization → capture (fixed, C2W8) → **the launch head's assertion, and a read that throws away the continuous launch coordinate.**

## Standing practices added this wave (continuing the numbering)

48. ⭐⭐⭐ **A GATE LEG MUST BE SCORED ON AN ARM CAPABLE OF PRODUCING BOTH OUTCOMES.** Fourth instance of one class in four waves — vacuous `M` (*could not fail*) · the blind gate (*could not fail on what mattered*) · the D2a-rewarding drift flag (*rewarded the prohibited configuration*) · **a circular vacuous K5 (*could not pass*)**. ⭐ ***The corollary is the half that generalizes: a leg built to police one question must not silently police a different one.***
49. ⭐⭐⭐ **SELECT AN OPERATING POINT ON A REGISTERED ARITHMETIC CONDITION, NEVER ON THE SCORE THE POINT WILL BE JUDGED BY.** The reach repair swept `payload_radius` to `‖v_j‖ / capture ≤ 0.75` — **fixed before the sweep** — and scored K5 **once** at the result. ⭐ ***That is the difference between a repair and a fishing trip, and it is the reason the resulting K5 abstain is quotable at all.***
50. ⭐⭐ **GENERALIZE A GATE PATCH BEFORE APPLYING IT — the K5-shaped fix would have missed K3 and K4**, which were vacuous by the same mechanism and would have kept passing silently. ⭐ ***A per-leg `discriminated` flag costs one field and catches the class; a special case catches one instance.***
51. ⭐⭐ **A COORDINATION FILE ONLY PREVENTS THE FAILURES IT ENUMERATES.** The frozen-interfaces file froze the budget grid and the K-leg keys and **not the V-leg launch keys** — the same hole, one field over, and it survived only because an unrelated stop meant no swap was ever computed (N311). ⭐ ***Freezing is not a property of the file; it is a property of each field in it.***
